### [1] [Mitigating Video Hallucinations via Attention-Guided Representation Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19728)
*Zhixin Cai,Ruoqi Yuan,Xinchen Yang,Zhuofan Chen,Jun Bai,Chuantao Yin,Wenge Rong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架ARP-CD，通过在表示空间构建对比负样本，缓解视频大语言模型的幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频大语言模型（Video LLMs）常生成缺乏视频依据的响应。对比解码（CD）可缓解此类幻觉，但其效果取决于负分支如何削弱视觉证据。现有方法依赖预定义的扰动或基于问题重建的掩码，对生成答案时模型视觉依赖的控制能力有限。

Method: ARP-CD为免训练框架，直接在表示空间构建对比负样本。其利用答案预测位置分配给视觉token的注意力质量，引导语言模型输入视觉隐状态的有界梯度更新；该更新降低视觉注意力，产生负输入，并将其预测与原始分支的预测在生成全程进行对比。

Result: 在四种视频大语言模型与四个基准上的实验表明，ARP-CD在多数模型-基准组合中优于代表性免训练基线，在减少幻觉的同时提升通用视频理解能力。进一步分析显示，注意力引导的扰动方向提升了负样本质量，且所得负样本能为纠正原始预测提供有效的对比参考。

Conclusion: ARP-CD通过注意力引导的表示扰动构建高质量对比负样本，是一种有效的免训练幻觉缓解方案，并揭示了注意力引导扰动方向对负样本质量的关键作用。

Abstract: Video large language models (Video LLMs) often generate responses unsupported by the input video. Contrastive decoding (CD) can mitigate these hallucinations, but its effectiveness depends on how the negative branch weakens visual evidence. Existing methods rely on predefined perturbations or masking guided by question reconstruction, offering limited control over the model's visual reliance during answer generation. We introduce Attention-guided Representation Perturbation for Contrastive Decoding (ARP-CD), a training-free framework for constructing contrastive negatives directly in representation space. ARP-CD uses the attention mass assigned to visual tokens at the answer-prediction position to guide bounded gradient updates of the language model's input visual hidden states. These updates reduce visual attention and produce a negative input, whose predictions are contrasted with those of the original branch throughout generation. Experiments across four Video LLMs and four benchmarks show that ARP-CD outperforms representative training-free baselines in most model--benchmark settings, reducing hallucinations while improving general video understanding. Further analyses show that attention-guided perturbation directions improve negative quality and that the resulting negatives provide effective contrastive references for correcting the original predictions.

</details>


### [2] [ActICL: Post-Training for Agentic Multimodal In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19475)
*Yanshu Li,Ruixiang Tang,Junyi Jessy Li,Elias Stengel-Eskin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ActICL，将多模态 ICL 的示例检索建模为工具增强的智能体推理过程，让 MLLM 学会自主判断信息缺口并渐进式检索、筛选与精炼上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态 ICL 性能高度依赖所用示例，示例检索是核心问题。现有方法多为固定评分一次性检索全部示例，易引入冗余与噪声；已有的上下文感知检索虽支持增量检索，但均未让 MLLM 自己学会判断需要哪些新示例并自主执行渐进式检索。

Method: 训练一个 MLLM 智能体，通过工具增强推理获取并使用 ICL 上下文。智能体在迭代推理与工具调用中分析自身信息缺口，据此进行针对性检索得到初始候选；再用已有上下文过滤候选、精炼上下文，或迭代发起新一轮检索；当判断上下文充分或达到资源上限且满足用户请求时，将上下文交给作答模型（智能体自身或另一 MLLM）。采用两阶段后训练联合优化智能体的“获取”与“作答”能力。

Result: 在 7 个基准、3 个 MLLM 上，ActICL 以自适应示例数量相比标准相似度检索平均提升 11.6 个百分点，相比最强已有基线提升 4.4 个百分点。其获取的上下文对前沿模型同样有效：GPT-5.6-Sol 进行 4-shot ICL 时，在三个基准上平均比标准检索提升 3.5 个百分点。

Conclusion: ActICL 通过让 MLLM 智能体自主进行缺口驱动的渐进式检索与上下文精炼，有效缓解固定一次性检索的冗余与噪声问题，并在多种模型与基准上显著超越相似度检索及最强基线，且其上下文具备良好的可迁移性。

Abstract: Multimodal in-context learning (ICL) is an important capability of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Its performance is closely tied to the demonstrations it uses, making demonstration retrieval a core problem for ICL. Common methods use fixed scoring methods to retrieve all the demonstrations at once, which may introduce redundancy and noise. Context-aware retrieval methods have emerged to address this problem by allowing incremental retrieval from a growing context. However, none of them lets the MLLM learn how to judge what new demonstrations it needs and then carry out progressive retrieval on its own. To this end, we introduce ActICL, which trains an MLLM agent to acquire and use an ICL context via tool-augmented reasoning. An ActICL agent analyzes its own information gaps during iterative reasoning and tool-use actions. Based on these gaps, it performs targeted retrieval to acquire a set of initial candidates. It can then use the existing context to further filter the candidates and refine the context, or it can iteratively perform a new retrieval. When the agent judges that the context is sufficient for the task, or when the resource limit is reached with the user request met, it sends the acquired context to an answering model, either the agent itself or another MLLM. We use a two-stage post-training procedure that jointly optimizes the agent's acquisition and answering. With an adaptive number of demonstrations, ActICL improves average performance by 11.6 percentage points over standard similarity-based retrieval across seven benchmarks and three MLLMs, and by 4.4 points compared to the strongest prior baseline. The contexts acquired by ActICL agents can also be used effectively by frontier models. When GPT-5.6-Sol performs 4-shot ICL, ActICL's contexts improve its performance by 3.5 points over standard retrieval, averaged over three benchmarks.

</details>


### [3] [VLX-Go: Robust Embodied Tracking through Multi-Stage Learning and On-Policy Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19361)
*Kyusong Lee,Heting Ying,Tiancheng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出视觉—语言路点策略模型，通过视觉语言对齐、多模态推理中期训练与跟踪专门化三阶段训练，将干扰物场景下的人物跟随成功率从41.54%提升至55.80%，并借助在策略精修进一步提升至58.22%，同时降低碰撞率1.99个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身视觉跟踪中，跟随单一孤立目标较简单，但在存在干扰物（distractors）时跟踪指定目标仍十分困难。现有方法从单目标跟踪切换到干扰物跟踪时成功率显著下降，因此需要关注人物跟随机器人在共享空间中导航时的鲁棒性问题。

Method: 提出一种视觉—语言路点策略（vision–language waypoint policy），核心假设是视觉与语言表征应在学习跟踪特定运动之前先完成对齐。策略组合了互补的冻结视觉编码器、多尺度时序上下文、Transformer骨干网络以及用于机器人中心路点预测的轻量级头部。训练分为三个阶段：视觉—语言对齐、多模态推理中期训练，以及在19,532条oracle回合上的跟踪专门化。此外还探索了利用过滤后的oracle标签对策略访问状态进行在策略（on-policy）精修。

Result: 完整多阶段训练方案将干扰物跟踪成功率从早期OmTrackVLA模型的41.54%提升至55.80%；在策略精修进一步带来2.42个百分点的提升，达到58.22%，同时碰撞率降低1.99个百分点。最终策略在所有对比方法中取得了最高的干扰物跟踪与歧义场景跟踪成功率。

Conclusion: 结果支持分阶段多模态学习是解决干扰物下人物跟踪问题的有前景方向，并表明在共享空间中开展在策略精修值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Embodied visual tracking requires a robot to identify a designated target from visual observations and control its own motion to follow that target through an environment. Following an isolated person is a comparatively simple demonstration of embodied visual tracking, but following the intended person among distractors remains challenging. Existing methods exhibit a substantial drop in success from single-target to distractor tracking, motivating a focus on the shared spaces that person-following robots must navigate. We present \method{}, a vision--language waypoint policy built around the hypothesis that visual and language representations should be aligned before tracking-specific motion is learned. The policy combines complementary frozen visual encoders, multiscale temporal context, a transformer backbone, and a lightweight head for robot-centric waypoint prediction. Its training progresses through vision--language alignment, multimodal reasoning mid-training, and tracking specialization on 19,532 oracle episodes. Compared with 41.54\% distractor-tracking success for our earlier OmTrackVLA model, the complete multi-stage recipe achieves 55.80\%. We then explore on-policy refinement using filtered oracle labels for states visited by the policy. This preliminary study adds 2.42 percentage points of distractor-tracking success, reaching 58.22\%, while reducing collision rate by 1.99 pts. The final policy records the highest distractor- and ambiguous-tracking success rates among the compared methods. These results support staged multimodal learning as a promising direction for tracking among distractors and motivate further study of on-policy refinement for person following in shared spaces.

</details>


### [4] [Towards a Data-Centric Understanding of Spatial Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19108)
*Mahdieh Mallahnezhad,Masoud Jafaripour,Mohamadali Tofigh,Sheila Schoepp,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对面向空间推理任务的多模态大模型（MLLM）微调方法进行综述，聚焦从监督学习到强化学习的数据驱动路径，并重点讨论数据集构建、数据模态与标注、以及模型评估，进而提出未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM已广泛应用于具身智能与机器人视觉任务，但其在空间推理与感知方面存在明显不足；后训练（post-training）虽能改善这些能力，却缺乏从数据驱动角度的系统综述。

Method: 综述性研究：梳理从监督微调到强化学习的预训练MLLM空间推理微调方法，围绕数据集构建、数据模态与标注、评估方式三大主题展开；将研究分为数据集设计、数据集构建与评估三类，并归纳固定空间标注的四类构建方法：规则与模板、LLM提示、程序化生成、人在回路（human-in-the-loop）。

Result: 归纳出四类固定空间标注构建方法（规则与模板、LLM提示、程序化生成、人在回路）；发现评估方法差异显著，体现在打分方式、容差指标与基准类型的不同。

Conclusion: 指出未来研究机会：统一动态标注、高效空间推理、评估标准化、扩展空间范围以及以人为中心的空间交互。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have been widely applied in embodied agents and robotics for vision tasks. However, MLLMs suffer from limitations in spatial reasoning and perception. Post-training techniques have shown promise for improving these capabilities; yet, there are limited data-driven surveys of such approaches. In this survey, we consider approaches to fine-tuning pretrained MLLMs for spatial reasoning tasks, ranging from supervised to reinforcement learning, with an emphasis on the datasets used. We investigate themes of dataset construction, data modality and annotation, and evaluation of the resulting models. Based on these themes, we categorize research into three groups: dataset design, dataset construction and evaluation. We find four main methods for constructing fixed spatial annotations: rules and templates, LLM prompting, procedural generation, and human-in-the-loop. We also observe substantial variation in evaluation approaches, including different scoring methods, tolerance metrics, and benchmark types. Finally, we identify opportunities for future research in unified dynamic annotation, efficient spatial reasoning, standardization of evaluation, expanded spatial range, and human-centered spatial interactions.

</details>


### [5] [OneLA: Learning Many Worlds from Latent Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20160)
*Ziyu Zhu,Zhaoxu Sun,Fan Jiang,Chiyu Wang,Junfeng Ni,Mu Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OneLA 提出用共享正交字典与统一预测目标从多领域视频中学习连续隐动作，作为跨领域世界模型的共享控制接口，无需任务特定设计或跨域对齐损失，在动作读出与动作循环迁移上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建跨领域世界模型需要统一控制空间，但各领域动作接口差异大且动作标注稀缺。从视频直接学习隐动作可免去显式动作监督，然而跨领域外观、具身形态与动力学差异会把领域特定视觉线索与可迁移的转移结构纠缠在一起，使共享隐动作难以学习。

Method: OneLA 通过共享正交字典与共同预测目标学习连续隐动作，隐动作预训练阶段不使用任务特定设计或跨域对齐损失。这些隐动作构成世界模型的共享条件接口，并用轻量适配器将各领域原生控制指令映射到同一空间。世界模型以隐动作与当前视觉状态为条件，预测依赖上下文的转移，从而实现可控 rollout。

Result: 跨领域的动作读出得到改善，动作循环迁移旋转误差较最强基线降低 24.8%。联合训练对比显示，真实场景生成的 Human Preference Score v3（HPSv3）提升 20.5%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该框架为从多样视频中学习、并通过隐动作空间支持灵活控制的通用世界模型提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Learning world models across diverse domains calls for a unified control space, yet ac- tion interfaces vary across domains and action annotations are often scarce. Latent ac- tions learned directly from videos offer a promising way to establish such a space without explicit action supervision. However, learning shared latent actions across domains re- mains challenging, as variations in appearance, embodiment, and dynamics can entangle domain-specific visual cues with transferable transition structure. To this end, we intro- duce OneLA, a simple framework that learns continuous latent actions through a shared orthogonal dictionary and a common predictive objective, without task-specific designs or cross-domain alignment losses during latent action pretraining. These latent actions pro- vide a shared conditioning interface for world models, with lightweight adapters mapping native control commands into the same space. Conditioned on latent actions and the cur- rent visual state, the world model predicts context-dependent transitions for controllable rollout. OneLA improves action readout across domains and reduces action cycle-transfer rotation error by 24.8% over the strongest evaluated baseline. A joint-training comparison further shows a 20.5% improvement in Human Preference Score v3 (HPSv3) for real- scene generation. These results suggest a path toward general-purpose world models that learn from diverse videos and support flexible control through latent action space.

</details>


### [6] [StreamErase: Towards Ultra-Fast Streaming Video Erasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20137)
*Zhuobai Dong,Xiaobing Tu,Xiantao Zhang,Jinkui Ren,Yinggui Wang,Yue Ma,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamErase 是一种流式视频物体擦除方法，通过将双向基础模型的擦除能力蒸馏到单向流式擦除器，实现单卡 26 FPS、亚秒级延迟、无限长视频的实时交互式擦除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频物体擦除方法延迟极高（每视频数分钟）且采用非因果处理，无法支持实时交互；同时长时长视频擦除面临域间隙、误差累积与算力随上下文增长等难题。

Method: 先用 mask 输入增强 text-to-video 模型以生成高质量擦除与背景合成视频（离线双向）；再渐进式地把该能力迁移到高效的单向流式擦除器。针对长时长挑战，设计带 attention sink 的滑窗因果注意力，并结合训练期的 self-rollout（含 attention sink）与 KV cache rolling，在固定上下文窗口下模拟推理外推，实现恒定速度的任意长视频生成。同时提出 LongErase 专用基准。

Result: 在单 GPU 上达到亚秒级延迟、最高 26 FPS 的流式生成；在 LongErase 基准上优于现有视频物体擦除模型，并在真实场景中泛化良好，独有地支持无限长流式擦除。

Conclusion: StreamErase 打通了从双向基础模型到单向流式擦除器的能力迁移，解决了长时长、误差累积与恒定算力的三重矛盾，使用户可输入 mask 并实时看到擦除结果，带来真正交互式体验。

Abstract: Current video object removal methods suffer from prohibitive latency (minutes per video) and non-causal processing that prevents real-time interaction. We present StreamErase, enabling sub-second latency with up to 26 FPS streaming generation on a single GPU. Our approach first augments a text-to-video model with mask input to generate high-quality videos that erase target objects and synthesize coherent backgrounds, but does not perform inference on the fly. As such, we progressively transfer the erasing capability from a powerful bidirectional foundation model to an efficient unidirectional streaming eraser, enabling long-horizon erasing without sacrificing visual fidelity. Several key challenges arise when generating videos over long, potentially infinite horizons: (1) bridging the domain gap from training on finite length and extrapolating to long horizons, (2) sustaining high quality by preventing error accumulation, and (3) maintaining fast inference, without increasing computational cost as the context grows. To address these challenges, we carefully designed sliding-window causal attention with attention sinks. By incorporating self-rollout with attention sinks and KV cache rolling during training, we simulate inference-time extrapolations with a fixed context window, enabling constant-speed generation of arbitrarily long videos. Finally, we establish a dedicated benchmark, LongErase, for streaming long-video object removal. Our model achieves strong performance compared to existing video object erasing models and generalizes well to real-world video scenarios, uniquely enabling infinite-length streaming. With StreamErase, users can input a mask and see the erasing results unfold in real-time, delivering a truly interactive experience.

</details>


### [7] [When Pairwise Preferences Cycle: A Reliability-Anchored Preference Walk for Video GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20089)
*Xinrui Lin,Fan Li,Shumin Wang,Dehong Kong,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Reliability-Anchored Preference Walk (RAPW)，一种图感知的优势聚合方法，用于视频生成模型强化学习中的成对偏好反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成对偏好反馈已成为视频生成模型强化学习广泛采用的监督信号，比逐点标量评分更可靠。然而，朴素基于平均胜率的成对奖励聚合会丢弃底层比较结构，独立处理每个 rollout，可能为某些 rollout 引入不可靠的优势估计，影响强化学习优化。

Method: 作者为成对评估器下的每个 GRPO 组定义偏好图，节点为采样的 rollout，边编码成对偏好。通过审计 6,976 个在线比较图，发现循环偏好普遍存在且贯穿训练。基于此提出 RAPW：一种图感知的优势聚合方法，通过可靠性引导的偏好游走，将胜率的全局偏好信号与循环偏好图中的可靠关系证据相结合。在与评估器、比较协议、组大小、提示调度、查询预算相匹配的条件下，RAPW 不需要额外的评估器查询。

Result: 在受控对比、迁移设置和盲人评估中，RAPW 均优于 Pairwise-GRPO。

Conclusion: RAPW 通过利用循环偏好图的结构信息，提供更可靠的图感知优势聚合，在不增加评估器查询成本的前提下改进了视频生成模型的强化学习效果。

Abstract: Pairwise preference feedback has become a widely adopted supervision signal for reinforcement learning in video generation models, as pairwise comparisons provide a more reliable alternative than pointwise scalar ratings for evaluating the quality of generated videos. Specifically, we define a preference graph for each GRPO group under a pairwise evaluator, where nodes represent sampled rollouts and edges encode pairwise preferences. Auditing 6,976 on-policy comparison graphs, we find that cyclic preferences are prevalent and persist throughout training. Naive pairwise reward aggregation based on average win rates discards the underlying comparison structures and treats each rollout independently, which may introduce unreliable advantage estimates for certain rollouts and consequently affect reinforcement learning optimization. Therefore, we propose the \emph{Reliability-Anchored Preference Walk} (\ours{}), a graph-aware advantage aggregation method that strengthens the global preference signal from win rates with reliable relational evidence from cyclic preference graphs through a reliability-guided preference walk. Under matched evaluator, comparison protocol, group size, prompt schedule, and query budget, \ours{} requires no additional evaluator queries beyond the observed comparisons within each rollout group and improves over Pairwise-GRPO across controlled comparisons, transfer settings, and blind human evaluations.

</details>


### [8] [Feasibility of a Patient-Specific Digital Twin for Ventriculoperitoneal Shunt Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20074)
*Kritsanavis Chongsrid,Vich Yindeedej,Supaporn Konmun,Xulei Yang,Si Yong Yeo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 基于术前CT构建的数字孪生为脑室分流提供个体化入点与深度规划，使徒手置管准确率大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分流失败最常见原因是导管梗阻，而Keen点徒手置管时导管尖端仅约十分之一能游离于脑脊液中，因此需要在不改变手术操作方式的前提下，通过个体化选择入点与深度提高脑室内导管长度与置管准确度。

Method: 构建数字孪生：由术前CT以确定性几何重建颅骨、头皮、耳与脑室；在Keen点周边所有入点中搜索满足安全约束、且脑室内长度最长的垂直轨迹；将规划结果以距耳的两次标尺测量值和两个深度值返回。随后在11例连续成人中按IDEAL 2a阶段徒手执行该规划。

Result: 11例中10例达到Hayhurst 1级置管，历史对照为213例中22例（p = 9×10^-9）；入点与角度偏差中位数分别为2.5 mm和5.9度；脑室内导管较Keen点平均多1.4 cm；无术中并发症。

Conclusion: 手术技术本身未改变，获益来自按患者个体化选择入点与深度；该规划是否能降低翻修率留待下一阶段验证。

Abstract: Catheter obstruction is the commonest cause of shunt failure; freehand insertion at Keen's point leaves the tip free in cerebrospinal fluid in only one case in ten. We built a digital twin that reconstructs skull, scalp, ear and ventricles from preoperative CT by deterministic geometry, searches every entry around Keen's point for the perpendicular trajectory with the longest intraventricular length under safety constraints, and returns the plan as two ruler measurements from the ear and two depths. Executed freehand in 11 consecutive adults (IDEAL Stage 2a), it achieved Hayhurst Grade 1 placement in 10 of 11 against 22 of 213 historically (p = 9 x 10^-9), median entry and angular deviations 2.5 mm and 5.9 degrees, 1.4 cm more intraventricular catheter than Keen's point, and no intraoperative complication. The technique was unchanged; the gain came from choosing entry and depth per patient. Whether it lowers revision is the next stage's hypothesis.

</details>


### [9] [Instruction Mining From Images: Constructing a Synthetic Dataset for Multimodal Learning ILSP DCVLR Competition Submission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19932)
*Eleni E. Kasoura,Dimitrios Damianos,Georgios Paraskevopoulos,Vassilios Katsouros*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一套紧凑可复现的图像指令-响应对合成与筛选流水线，用于DCVLR，基于Qwen2.5-VL-7B Instruct生成并用奖励模型筛选高质量样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为DCVLR提供高质量、图像条件化的指令-响应对数据，现有数据合成方法需兼顾可复现性、紧凑性与质量可控性。

Method: 以Cauldron集合为源数据，采用Magpie风格指令与响应合成，用Qwen2.5-VL-7B Instruct在系统提示控制下生成图像条件化指令；流程包括指令合成、净化、打标签，并基于DeBERTa-v3奖励模型评分过滤，保留正分样本。

Result: 奖励分数分布的中位数约1.53、均值约1.75，表明多数生成指令质量良好；所得数据集在数学、空间与社会推理领域呈现多样化指令。

Conclusion: 该流水线能以奖励模型筛选有效提升生成指令质量，产出多领域覆盖的图像指令数据，适用于DCVLR训练。

Abstract: This work presents a compact and reproducible pipeline for synthesizing and curating image-based instruction-response pairs for DCVLR. We draw from the Cauldron collection and use Magpie-style instruction and response synthesis with Qwen2.5-VL-7B Instruct to create concise, image-conditioned instruction pairs. The pipeline consists of instruction synthesis, conditioned on input images and controlled via a system prompt, sanitization, tagging, and filtering of the instructions based on the score obtained by a reward model (DeBERTa-v3). We retain samples with positive scores to maximize the quality of the generated instructions. We observe that score distributions have a median and mean reward score of ~ 1.53 and ~ 1.75 respectively, indicating that most generated instructions are of good quality. The resulting dataset contains diverse instructions in the math, spatial, and social reasoning domains.

</details>


### [10] [VascuSR: Iterative Anatomy-Aware Diffusion for CT Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20509)
*Hairong Ni,Jiahe Huang,Minghao Guo,Wojciech Matusik,Rose Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VascuSR：解剖感知迭代扩散框架，通过显式3D血管表示与轻量解剖控制器引导CT超分，显著提升血管分割与3D结构保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式超分在严重下采样的CT图像中易产生视觉逼真但解剖上无依据的血管细节（如血管缺失、虚假血管或错误连接），其根源在于高频信息无法由低分辨率输入唯一确定，故需引入解剖先验约束以恢复可信血管结构。

Method: 提出VascuSR迭代式解剖感知扩散框架，将图像重建与显式3D血管引导相结合：(1)采用显式3D血管表示捕捉跨切片的结构信息，并让轻量3D生成精炼器专注于血管结构而非复杂CT外观；(2)设计轻量解剖控制器，将精炼后的结构注入扩散解码器以引导逐切片超分，并通过迭代反馈从每个新生成的SR体更新引导。

Result: 在重新标注的MSD Task08与HVM数据集上，VascuSR在分割精度（Dice、clDice）及3D血管结构（连通性、平均中心线距离）上优于插值与学习型超分基线，图像质量与SR主干相当。相比学习型基线，类平均连通域数量在两个数据集上均降低20%以上，MSD上平均中心线距离降低10%以上。

Conclusion: 显式3D血管引导与迭代反馈机制能有效抑制生成式CT超分中的血管幻觉，在保持图像质量的同时提升下游血管分析的解剖可信度，为解剖感知超分提供可行范式。

Abstract: Generative super-resolution (SR) can recover sharp detail in computed tomography (CT) images from severely downsampled observations, but the missing high-frequency content is not uniquely determined by the low-resolution input. Consequently, visually plausible reconstructions may contain anatomically unsupported vascular detail, such as missing, spurious, or incorrectly connected vessels. To overcome this challenge, we introduce VascuSR, an iterative anatomy-aware diffusion framework that combines image reconstruction with explicit 3D vascular guidance. First, to provide richer anatomical guidance, we use an explicit 3D vascular representation that captures structural information beyond individual slices. This representation also simplifies vascular refinement by allowing a lightweight 3D generative refiner to focus on vascular structure rather than complex CT appearance. Second, to guide slice-wise SR, a lightweight anatomy controller injects the refined structure into the diffusion decoder, while iterative feedback updates this guidance from each new SR volume. On re-annotated MSD Task08 and HVM datasets, VascuSR outperforms interpolation and learning-based SR baselines in segmentation accuracy (Dice and clDice) and 3D vascular structure (connectivity and mean centerline distance), while maintaining image quality comparable to its SR backbone. Compared with learning-based baselines, VascuSR reduces class-averaged connected-component counts by over 20% on both datasets and mean centerline distance by over 10% on MSD.

</details>


### [11] [Hybrid supervised instance segmentation by learning label noise suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20412)
*Linwei Chen,Ying Fu,Shaodi You,Hongzhe Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合监督实例分割框架，利用廉价边界框标签与少量精确像素级标签，通过两个辅助模型抑制标签噪声并生成高质量伪像素级标签，在PASCAL VOC 2012和Cityscapes上以更低标注成本取得有竞争力性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全监督实例分割依赖大规模像素级标注，获取成本高、耗时长；弱监督与半监督方法虽使用边界框、图像级标签或无标签样本降低标注成本，但性能损失较大。因此需要一种既能显著节省标注成本、又不明显牺牲精度的方案。

Method: 提出一个可混合使用经济型边界框标签与精确像素级标签的流程。设计两个辅助模型：一个用于抑制前景与背景之间的误标注，另一个用于抑制实例误标注带来的噪声，从而从边界框标签中获得较准确的伪像素级标签用于训练。此外引入类别感知空间注意力模块、类别约束模块、实例约束模块以及自学习训练策略，以提升伪标签精度。

Result: 在PASCAL VOC 2012和Cityscapes数据集上的实验表明，该方法在显著降低标注成本的同时，能达到具有竞争力的实例分割性能。

Conclusion: 通过噪声抑制与多约束模块生成的伪像素级标签，可在混合监督下有效替代大量像素级标注，实现标注成本与分割精度之间的良好权衡。

Abstract: To reach top accuracy, current fully supervised instance segmentation methods severely rely on large-scale pixel-wise labeled datasets. They are usually expensive and time-consuming to obtain. Though weakly or semi-supervised methods utilize cheap bounding box labeled, image-level labeled or unlabeled samples to save the labeling cost, their performance is largely sacrificed. To save labeling cost without losing much performance, in this paper, we present a pipeline that can utilize economical bounding box labels and accurate pixel-wise labels in a hybrid way. Specifically, we design two ancillary models to learn label noise suppression and obtain accurate pseudo pixel-wise labels from bounding box labels for training. One is designed to suppress mislabeling between foreground and background, and the other is designed to suppress noise from mislabeling of instances. Moreover, we exploit category-aware spatial attention module, category constraint module, instance constraint module, and self-learning training approach to improve the accuracy of pseudo labels. Experiments on the PASCAL VOC 2012 and the Cityscapes datasets show that our method can achieve competitive performance with much less labeling cost.

</details>


### [12] [Remember, Revisit, Revise: Scaffold Memory for Long-Horizon 3D Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19674)
*Soumick Sarker,Mengmi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出SMORE框架，为冻结的几何基础模型配备可修正的外部记忆，通过关联"观察者在何处"与"观察到什么"实现长时序推理中的存储、检索、验证与修正，在八种模型、五个数据集上一致提升位姿估计、深度估计、点云重建、SLAM与新视角合成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何基础模型从视频序列估计相机位姿与三维场景结构，但在长时序下保持一致的建图仍然困难：前馈模型将长序列切成独立定长窗口并对齐预测，导致位姿与重建误差跨窗口累积；流式模型虽能保留历史信息指导未来预测，却无法在后续观测揭示错误时修正早期估计。

Method: SMORE（Scaffold MemOry for Revision）为冻结的几何基础模型加装可修正的外部记忆。受生物海马体联想记忆（将空间情境与感官体验相链接）启发，SMORE将观察者所在位置与其观察内容相关联，使过往观测能够被存储、检索、与新证据比对验证，并在检测到不一致时进行修正。

Result: 在五个数据集、八种几何模型上评估，覆盖相机位姿估计、多视角深度估计、三维点云重建、SLAM与新视角合成。对所有模型均一致改善长序列位姿估计；多视角深度误差最多降低58%；三维点云重建的Chamfer距离最多降低43%；长时序SLAM平均轨迹误差低至2.9 cm。保留修正后的位姿与观测进一步提升了大规模三维场景重建的保真度与空间一致性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，可修正的外部记忆是一种灵活接口，能将预训练几何模型从固定上下文预测扩展至长视觉序列上的迭代式、可纠错推理。

Abstract: Geometry foundation models estimate camera poses and 3D scene structure from video sequences, but maintaining consistent reconstructions over long horizons remains challenging. Feed-forward models process long sequences as independent, fixed-length frame windows and align their predictions, causing pose and reconstruction errors to accumulate across windows. Streaming models retain information from past frames to inform future predictions, but cannot revise earlier estimates when later observations reveal errors. To address this, we introduce Scaffold MemOry for Revision (SMORE), a framework equipping frozen geometry foundation models with a revisable external memory for long-horizon inference. Inspired by hippocampal associative memory in biological brains, which links spatial context with sensory experience, SMORE associates where an observer is with what it observes, enabling past observations to be stored, retrieved, verified against new evidence, and revised when inconsistencies are detected. We evaluate SMORE with eight competitive geometry models on five datasets spanning camera pose estimation, multi-view depth estimation, 3D point-cloud reconstruction, simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM), and novel view synthesis. Across all evaluated models, SMORE consistently improves long-sequence pose estimation, reduces multi-view depth error by up to 58%, reduces Chamfer distance for 3D point-cloud reconstruction by up to 43%, and achieves average trajectory error as low as 2.9 cm in long-horizon SLAM. By retaining corrected poses and observations, SMORE further improves the fidelity and spatial consistency of large-scale 3D scene reconstruction. These results establish revisable external memory as a flexible interface for extending pretrained geometry models beyond fixed-context prediction toward iterative, error-correcting inference over long visual sequences.

</details>


### [13] [Decoupled Drift-Diffusion World Models for Consistent Future Frame Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19212)
*Anton Arapin,Aditya Singh,Yixin Wang,Jun Gao,Bernadette Bucher*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出D3WM世界模型，通过联合预测确定性下一帧与异方差不确定性图，用不确定性图引导条件扩散过程，在机器人操作与相机运动任务上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉世界模型预测未来帧时，场景演化既包含确定性变化（如机器人平台上的受控运动），也包含随机性变化（如被操控物体或先前遮挡区域的渲染）。作者的核心洞见是：这些随机性变化的空间位置本身是可学习的，因此可以将确定性与随机性变化在空间上解耦处理。

Method: D3WM用Transformer以高斯负对数似然为目标联合回归确定性下一状态预测和异方差aleatoric不确定性空间图；学得的不确定性图作为条件扩散模型的结构化空间门控——确定性区域锚定到预测状态，不确定性区域（如去遮挡、动态纹理）由扩散过程采样。整体框架表述为方差门控随机微分方程，将确定性漂移与随机扩散分离。

Result: 在两类不同动作空间的任务——机器人桌面操作和相机运动——上，D3WM均相比有竞争力的基线取得改进结果。

Conclusion: 空间不确定性是可学习的，利用其作为门控信号解耦确定性与随机性动态，为世界模型提供了一种有效的方差门控扩散建模范式。

Abstract: World models of visual information predict future image frames conditioned on given action sequences. This predicted scene evolution is driven by deterministic changes such as controlled motion on a robot platform, as well as stochastic changes such as rendering of manipulated objects or previously occluded regions. Our key insight is that the spatial location of these stochastic changes is itself learnable. We leverage this insight by jointly regressing both the deterministic next-state prediction and the spatial uncertainty map, then using the uncertainty map to guide a conditional diffusion process. We propose Decoupled Drift-Diffusion World Model (D3WM), which uses a transformer trained with a Gaussian negative-log-likelihood objective to produce both the deterministic next-state prediction and a spatial map of heteroscedastic aleatoric uncertainty. The learned uncertainty map then provides a structured spatial gate for a conditional diffusion model: deterministic regions are anchored to the predicted state, while uncertain regions, such as disocclusions or dynamic textures, are sampled by the diffusion process. We frame this decomposition under a variance-gated stochastic differential equation that separates deterministic drift from stochastic diffusion. D3WM shows improved results over competitive baselines in two distinct types of action spaces: robotic tabletop manipulation and camera motion.

</details>


### [14] [MonoMIP: Multi-Height Inverse Perspective Mapping for Monocular 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19071)
*Emir Ulurak,Furkan Kıraç*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MonoMIP双解码器框架，通过多高度逆透视映射构建高度条件化的BEV特征，为单目3D检测引入互补的几何推理，在KITTI和Waymo上超越现有单目方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目3D检测方法依赖深度引导的Transformer，但完全在透视图像平面中操作，空间关系受透视前缩（foreshortening）影响而被扭曲，缺乏对3D几何关系的直接建模。

Method: 提出MonoMIP双解码器框架：在K个高度平面（从地面到车顶）执行多高度逆透视映射（IPM），生成有序堆叠的高度条件化度量BEV特征，抑制地面以上目标的错误投影并显式呈现跨平面的深度相关外观变化；采用多尺度可变形注意力的BEV解码器按查询聚合多高度证据，同时由深度引导的3D解码器提供透视空间预测作为互补。

Result: 在无额外训练数据的条件下，KITTI验证集Car类取得25.84% Moderate AP3D|R40，官方测试集取得21.66%，在KITTI与Waymo基准上均优于近期发表的单目方法。

Conclusion: 通过多高度IPM构建的BEV表征可有效补充透视空间的几何推理，双解码器结构在保持数据效率的同时提升了单目3D目标检测精度。

Abstract: Monocular 3D object detection methods increasingly rely on depth-guided transformers, but they operate entirely in the perspective image plane where spatial relationships are distorted by foreshortening. We propose MonoMIP, a dual-decoder framework that adds complementary 3D geometric reasoning through Bird's-Eye View (BEV) features constructed via Multi-Height Inverse Perspective Mapping. We perform IPM at K elevation planes spanning ground to rooftop, producing an ordered stack of height-conditioned metric BEV features that reduces above-ground misprojection and exposes depth-dependent appearance variation across planes. A BEV decoder with multi-scale deformable attention aggregates this multi-height evidence per query, while a depth-guided 3D decoder provides complementary perspective-space predictions. Without extra training data, MonoMIP attains 25.84% Moderate AP3D|R40 on KITTI val and 21.66% on the official test set for Car, outperforming recent published monocular methods on both the KITTI and Waymo benchmarks.

</details>


### [15] [How Far Can Coding Agents Go in Visual Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19025)
*Hengguang Zhou,Yuanhao Ban,Jiaqi Feng,Xiaohuan Pei,Justin Cui,Cho-Jui Hsieh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将"通用编码智能体通过可执行程序构造图像"作为图像生成的新范式，发现其在结构与精确视觉约束上表现优异、在感观丰富度上受限，并进一步提出"可执行代码作为外部视觉思维语言"及"内部视觉思维语言"假说，实验表明编码智能体在绘图前已拥有连贯一致的内在世界表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代图像生成主要依赖专用生成模型，图像构造过程被隐含在学习参数中，不可解释、不可检查。作者希望探索一种替代路径：让通用编码智能体以可执行程序的方式显式地构造图像，并借此研究代码作为一种结构化、可执行的视觉表征，以及智能体是否具备共享的内部视觉表征。

Method: 1) 在自建基准 AgentDrawBench（200 个提示）上开展零样本绘图评测，考察结构保持、精确视觉约束满足与感知丰富度；2) 通过绘图接口变化考察表现力差异，并对"从人类演示中提取的程序化技能"在多种艺术媒介上的迁移做定性重建研究；3) 将代码视为外部视觉思维语言，在视觉问答任务中检验重建的视觉代码所保留的信息量；4) 通过跨 harness 绘图、由独立单像素查询拼装的图像，以及移除可见绘图后跨视角的房间布局保持实验，检验"内部视觉思维语言"假说。

Result: 编码智能体在结构保持与精确视觉约束上表现出色，在组合准确性比照片级真实感更重要的领域达到有竞争力的水平，但感知丰富度仍有限；视觉表现力随绘图接口而变；程序化技能可支持不同艺术媒介。在视觉问答中，重建的视觉代码保留了大量信息，部分领域准确率甚至高于原图。跨 harness 绘图与单像素独立查询拼装的图像呈现相近的布局与色调；智能体在可见绘图被移除后仍能保持自生成的房间布局跨视角一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明编码智能体在 harness 介入与生成开始之前，就已在其"想象"中维持一个一致且连贯的世界，支持共享的、组合式结构化的内部视觉思维语言假说；为以可执行程序为媒介、可检查可解释的图像生成提供了新方向。

Abstract: Modern image generation largely relies on specialized generative models that leave the process of image construction implicit in learned parameters. We explore an alternative: general-purpose coding agents that construct images through executable programs. Our study of zero-shot drawing on AgentDrawBench, a 200-prompt benchmark, reveals a distinctive capability profile. Agents excel at preserving structure and satisfying precise visual constraints, achieving competitive results in domains where compositional accuracy matters more than photorealistic appearance, while remaining limited in perceptual richness. Their visual expressiveness varies with the drawing interface, and a qualitative study on reconstruction shows how procedural skills extracted from human demonstrations support different artistic media. We then examine code as an external visual language of thought: a structured, executable representation that agents can construct and inspect. We test it in visual question answering task and find reconstructed visual code retains substantial information for question answering and, in some domains, supports higher accuracy than the original image. These abilities raise a further question: do agents draw on a shared internal representation of scene content beyond a particular drawing program? We explore an internal visual language-of-thought hypothesis, in which a shared, compositionally structured representation supports visual operations across drawing languages. Cross-harness drawings and images assembled from independent single-pixel queries exhibit related layouts and tones. Agents also maintain self-generated room arrangements across viewpoints after visible drawings are removed. These results demonstrate that the coding agent would maintain a consistent and coherent world inside its imagine even before the harness gets involved and the generation starts.

</details>


### [16] [PhyMetric: Diagnosing Physical Plausibility in Text-to-Video Generation via Scene-Level QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19823)
*Siwei Meng,Xuerou Cai,Rui Hu,Ping Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PhyMetric，一个场景级物理合理性诊断基准，将文本到视频生成的物理评估转化为可验证的、场景锚定的问答任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 T2V 物理合理性评测多依赖整体可信度打分或宽泛质量维度，无法明确"测的是哪条物理规律"以及"模型为何失败"，评估结果不透明且难以定位问题。

Method: 构建含 1,115 个场景、覆盖 6 大领域 50 个物理类别、12 个评估维度的基准，配 8,920 道答案均衡的二元问题；每场景配 8 个针对性 yes/no 问题与结构化物理先验（支配定律、预期时序、可观测特征、常见失败模式）；评测时由 VLM 判官基于先验在问题感知采样帧上作答，并按场景、维度、物理类别、模型层级聚合正确率。

Result: 在 4 个开源 T2V 模型与 3 个 VLM 判官上实验：若用单一笼统合理性提问替代针对性二元问题，判官准确率降至随机水平；结构化物理先验提供互补信号，可提升评分与人类判断的一致性，且效果随 VLM 判官能力不同而变化。

Conclusion: PhyMetric 将物理合理性评估从不可解释的整体打分转变为可解释的场景级细粒度诊断，验证了场景化二元问答是评估协议的关键组成，结构化先验可增强人机对齐。

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) generation models can produce visually realistic videos that nevertheless violate the physical consequences implied by the prompt and scene. Existing benchmarks largely rely on holistic plausibility scores or broad quality dimensions, leaving it unclear which physical behavior is being tested and why a model fails. We introduce PhyMetric, a scene-level diagnostic benchmark that recasts physical plausibility evaluation as verifiable, scene-grounded question answering. PhyMetric contains 1{,}115 scenes spanning 50 physical categories across six domains, paired with 8{,}920 answer-balanced binary questions over 12 evaluation dimensions. Each scene is associated with eight targeted yes/no questions and a structured physical prior specifying the governing law, expected temporal sequence, observable signatures, and common failure modes. At evaluation time, a vision-language model (VLM) judge answers each question from question-aware sampled frames conditioned on the prior, and correctness is hierarchically aggregated into scene, dimension, physical category, and model scores. Experiments on four open-source T2V models and three VLM judges show that scene-specific binary QA is a critical component of the protocol: when each targeted question is replaced by a single generic plausibility query, evaluator accuracy drops to the chance level. Structured physical priors offer a complementary signal that improves human alignment of the resulting scores, with effects that vary across VLM judges of different capacity. These findings establish PhyMetric as a benchmark that turns physical plausibility evaluation from opaque holistic scoring into interpretable, scene-level inspection. Our project page is available at: https://mengsiwei.github.io/PhyMetric/.

</details>


### [17] [Q-MAGIC: Quad-only Mesh Autoregressive Generation via Singularity-Aware Circular Relative Offset Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19777)
*Yuming Zhao,Zezeng Li,Weimin Wang,Xiang Wu,Na Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Q-MAGIC自回归模型生成纯四边形网格，通过奇点感知序列化与循环相对偏移词元化，实现更紧凑序列并提升几何保真度与网格质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归模型在三角网格生成中已证明有效，但四边形网格因面拓扑约束更严格而难以生成：模型不仅要能把四边形面序列化为合法词元序列，还需在反序列化时支持无歧义的连通性重建。

Method: 1) 为四边形网格设计定制序列化方案，明确下一面至少需要两个新顶点，且受严格连线约束；2) 提出奇点感知序列化，优先从奇点出发进行连线，使遍历更连贯并缩短序列长度；3) 提出循环相对偏移词元化，主要以局部相对偏移编码几何；4) 基于该表示，用自回归Transformer通过下一词元预测学习四边形网格分布。

Result: 大量实验表明，Q-MAGIC相比当前最优方法能生成更紧凑的序列，并持续提升几何保真度与网格质量。

Conclusion: Q-MAGIC通过面向四边形拓扑的序列化与词元化设计，将四边形网格生成统一为自回归下一词元预测问题，在序列紧凑性与生成质量上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Autoregressive models have proven effective for triangular mesh generation by transforming it into predicting the next face, converting the non-differentiable connectivity prediction into a sequence of token predictions. However, generating quad-only meshes remains challenging because quad faces impose stricter topological constraints than triangles. The model must not only serialize quad faces into a valid token sequence, but also support unambiguous connectivity reconstruction during de-serialization. To address this, we propose Q-MAGIC, a novel autoregressive model for quad mesh generation. We first introduce a tailored serialization scheme for quad meshes, which requires at least two new vertices for the next face and is less flexible than triangles due to strict wiring constraints. To meet these requirements and reduce serialization length, we propose a singularity-aware serialization that prioritizes wiring from singular points, leading to more coherent traversals. Additionally, we propose a circular relative offset tokenization that encodes geometry mainly via local relative offsets. Built upon this representation, an autoregressive Transformer learns the underlying distribution of quad meshes via next-token prediction, enabling effective quad mesh generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Q-MAGIC produces more compact sequences while consistently improving geometric fidelity and mesh quality compared to state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [18] [Physics-Shaped, Not Physical: Auditing Physics-Guided Underwater Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19763)
*Mochamad Rizal Fauzan,Irgi Surya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 对代表性物理引导水下图像复原网络 PhysGuide-Net 的受控审计表明：其"物理"模块带来的收益源于物理形状的归纳偏置而非真实物理约束——满数据下无零样本增益却增加 3.4 倍 FLOPs，小数据下虽有效但预测的透射率不具物理量性质，且模块效应随退化严重程度符号反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理引导网络通过预测透射率与后向散射并反演成像模型来复原水下图像，但鲜有工作验证这些物理量是否真正发挥作用。作者希望以严格控制实验的方式审计这类设计的"物理"是否名副其实，从而厘清其性能来源。

Method: 以 PhysGuide-Net 为对象开展四组受控实验：(1) 在 UIEB 上做五随机种子消融，并在完整 LSUI（4279 对）与 AUIED3K（3000 对）上零样本评估；(2) 数据效率研究，设置容量匹配但去除成像模型的对照；(3) 在 Sea-thru 上将预测图与实测距离做验证；(4) 检查单次运行消融的可重复性并核查损失消融中的实现缺陷。

Result: 满 800 张训练图时物理模块无零样本增益（PSNR 平均差最大 0.16 dB，95% 区间均含零），FLOPs 增至 3.4 倍；80 张图时有帮助（UIEB +0.65 dB，AUIED3K +1.48 dB，AUIED3K 种子间标准差 1.3→0.5 dB），但校正后单独不显著，且容量匹配对照复现不出该增益（低 0.8–1.0 dB）。预测透射率不具备物理量行为：与距离的相关性不高于异图距离图（合并差 +0.02，暗通道先验为 −0.06），Beer-Lambert 拟合仅解释 0.4% 方差；模块效应在数据集间随退化严重程度变号。

Conclusion: PhysGuide-Net 的收益来自物理形状的归纳偏置而非物理本身。单次运行消融可能反号，且报告的实现缺陷会使损失消融结论失效，提示该领域需更严格的审计范式。

Abstract: Physics-guided networks for underwater image restoration predict transmission and backscatter and invert the image-formation model, yet rarely test whether this physics does any work. We audit one representative design, PhysGuide-Net, with four controlled experiments: five-seed ablations on UIEB with zero-shot evaluation on the complete LSUI (4279 pairs) and AUIED3K (3000 pairs) sets; a data-efficiency study with a capacity-matched control that removes the formation model; and a validation of the predicted maps against measured range on Sea-thru. With all 800 training images the physics module gives no zero-shot gain (mean PSNR differences of at most 0.16 dB; all 95% intervals include zero) at 3.4x the FLOPs. With 80 images it helps more (+0.65 dB on UIEB, +1.48 dB on AUIED3K; seed-to-seed s.d. on AUIED3K from 1.3 to 0.5 dB), although not individually significant after correction, and the control does not reproduce this (0.8-1.0 dB lower). Yet the predicted transmission does not behave like a physical quantity: its correlation with range is no stronger than for the range map of a different image (pooled difference +0.02, versus -0.06 for the dark-channel prior), and a Beer-Lambert fit explains 0.4% of its variance. The effect of the module also changes sign with degradation severity between datasets. The benefit thus comes from a physics-shaped inductive bias, not from physics. We further show that single-run ablations can reverse sign and document implementation defects that invalidate loss ablations.

</details>


### [19] [Generative World Simulation with DAO and JING](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19726)
*XGEN Team,Guohao Cai,Haoxuan Che,Frank Fan,Chenran Huang,Junjia Huang,Jiayi Jiang,Yanheng Li,Leon Liu,Shaoteng Liu,Xi Liu,Feng Nie,Zhefan Rao,An Wang,Hanwen Wang,Pengxiang Yan,Jieming Yu,Qian Zha,Wentao Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出耦合持久共享世界状态与连续第一人称视听生成的"生成式世界模拟"框架，由可计算世界引擎DAO与自我中心交互体验模型JING构成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式世界生成多为单次、情景式，难以在持续交互中保留历史后果并面向不同观察者维持一致性共享状态；本文旨在构建可长期持久、随行动演化、超越单一观察者的交互世界。

Method: 框架包含两部分：DAO维护角色、物体、空间及其关系的状态，按交互规则与世界时间推进世界并保留过往行动结果；DAO从演化状态导出观察者特定条件，JING结合该条件、角色/物体/场景引用、观察历史与当前动作，生成每个观察者的视听体验；用户通过视角控制与文本指令探索、操作物体并与角色交流，行动同时影响共享状态与后续体验。

Result: 形成"世界状态演化—体验生成"的连续交互闭环，支持人类与智能体的持续交互，实现保留历史、随行动演化、持久存在的交互世界。

Conclusion: 该框架将世界状态演化与体验生成在连续交互回路中连接，为构建具有持久性、历史性与可演化性的交互世界提供了一种新途径。

Abstract: We present a Generative World Simulation framework that couples persistent shared world state with continuous first-person audiovisual generation. The framework integrates DAO, a computable world engine, and JING, an Egocentric Interactive Experience Model, to support sustained interaction for humans and agents. DAO maintains the states of characters, objects, and spaces and their relationships, advancing the world according to interaction rules and world time while preserving the consequences of past actions. From this evolving state, DAO derives observer-specific conditions for JING, which combines these conditions with character, object, and scene references, observation history, and current actions to generate each observer's audiovisual experience. Users can explore environments, manipulate objects, and communicate with characters through viewpoint control and text instructions, with their actions shaping both the shared state and subsequent experiences. By connecting world-state evolution with experience generation in a continuous interaction loop, the framework offers an approach to building interactive worlds that retain their history, evolve through action, and persist beyond any individual observer.

</details>


### [20] [SceneAE: Scene Generation and Reconstruction in Queryable 3D-Anchored Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19708)
*Shuofeng Sun,Wenzhao Zheng,Yunpeng Zhang,Yi Wu,Linqing Zhao,Haibin Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出SceneAE，将场景表示为紧凑、非结构化的3D锚定隐空间（稀疏隐点云），每个隐点结合显式3D位置与隐式内容特征。该表示直接描述场景本身而非特定视角下的外观，因而同时适用于重建与生成，并在生成质量、计算成本与相机可控性上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期视频世界模型越来越多地采用3D记忆机制，将历史观测整合为轻量且持久的场景表示。作者由此提出一个互补性问题：既然3D表示可作为记忆已观测场景的有效机制，能否也直接用作重建与生成场景的隐空间？

Method: 将场景表示为3D锚定隐空间（SceneAE）：隐点由显式3D位置与隐式内容特征构成，空间上锚定以描述局部场景区域，形成稀疏隐点云。区别于传统视频VAE隐变量（描述场景在特定视角下如何呈现），该表示直接描述场景本身。重建时将持久3D表示投影并由不同目标相机解码为RGB-D观测，无需为每条新相机轨迹重新生成视频；生成时利用几何-内容分离，先生成相机控制的3D几何，再以其为条件合成内容。

Result: 实验表明，该3D隐空间在生成质量、计算成本与相机可控性方面均取得更优表现。

Conclusion: 通过几何-内容分离的3D锚定隐空间，可减少相机与外观的纠缠，提升相机可控性，并统一支持重建与生成，为视频世界模型提供了一种高效、可复用的场景表示范式。

Abstract: Recent video world models have increasingly adopted 3D memory mechanisms to integrate historical observations into a lightweight and persistent scene representation. This naturally raises a complementary question: if 3D representations can serve as an effective mechanism for memorizing observed scenes, can they also be directly used as the latent space for reconstructing and generating scenes? Motivated by this idea, we represent a scene as a compact and unstructured 3D-anchored latent space (\textbf{SceneAE}). Each latent combines an
  explicit 3D position with an implicit content feature and is spatially grounded to describe its local scene region, forming a
  sparse latent point cloud. Unlike conventional video-VAE latents that describe how a scene appears from particular viewpoints, our
  representation directly describes the scene itself, making it naturally applicable to both reconstruction and generation. For
  reconstruction, once the scene has been generated, our persistent 3D representation can be projected and decoded into RGB-D observations by different target cameras, without the need to regenerate a video for each new camera trajectory.
  For generation, we leverage the geometry-content separation to first generate camera-controlled 3D geometry and
  then synthesize content conditioned on it, reducing camera-appearance entanglement and improving camera
  controllability. Experiments show that this 3D latent achieves superior generation quality, computational cost, and camera controllability.

</details>


### [21] [MicroSDF: Microfacet-Driven Hybrid Neural SDFs for Mixed-Reflectance Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19681)
*Lejia Ye,Yuhua Xu,Yulan Guo,Lian Xu,Xiaohu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MicroSDF，一个基于微表面理论的神经隐式框架，通过微表面引导的几何建模、双分支颜色建模和检测引导的颜色混合策略，统一处理反射与非反射表面的高保真三维重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境中的三维重建面临反射与非反射表面共存的挑战，两类表面对建模有不同需求。现有方法通常将两类表面分开处理，导致泛化性受限且物理合理性不足，因此需要一个能统一处理二者的框架。

Method: MicroSDF包含三项核心创新：(i) 微表面引导的几何模型，从SDF中提取宏观与微表面多尺度法线，并用微表面法线一致性损失进行约束以保证表面朝向的物理合理性；(ii) 增强的双分支颜色模型，镜面分支利用微表面法线建模高频反射，基础分支采用反射方向（而非观察方向）以更好地建模漫反射和低频镜面分量；(iii) 检测引导的颜色混合策略，基于反射先验自适应融合颜色输出，替代隐式学习的混合权重。并配以定制的多阶段优化方案。

Result: 在DTU、Shiny Blender、Ref-NeRF和DeepVoxels数据集上的大量实验表明，MicroSDF在反射与非反射表面上均实现了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: MicroSDF为物理驱动的神经重建开辟了新方向，实现了跨反射与非反射表面的鲁棒且高保真重建。

Abstract: Accurate 3D reconstruction in real-world environments remains a significant challenge due to the coexistence of reflective and non-reflective surfaces, which pose distinct modeling demands. Existing methods often treat these surface types separately, limiting their generalizability and physical plausibility. To bridge this gap, we propose MicroSDF, a novel neural implicit framework that facilitates geometry and reflectance modeling through microfacet theory. Our approach incorporates three core innovations: (i) a microfacet-guided geometry model that extracts multi-scale surface normals (macroscopic and microfacet) from a signed distance field (SDF), regularized by a proposed microfacet normal consistency loss to enforce physically plausible surface orientations; (ii) an enhanced dual-branch color model, where the specular branch leverages the microfacet normals to model high-frequency reflectance, and the vanilla branch, unlike prior works, uses reflection direction (instead of viewing direction) to better model diffuse and low-frequency specular components; and (iii) a detection-guided color blending strategy that adaptively fuses the color outputs based on reflection priors, providing more physically intuitive blending than implicitly learned blending weights. Combined with a tailored multi-stage optimization scheme, the proposed MicroSDF achieves robust and high-fidelity reconstruction across reflective and non-reflective surfaces. Extensive experiments on DTU, Shiny Blender, Ref-NeRF, and DeepVoxels datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, establishing a new direction for physically grounded neural reconstruction.

</details>


### [22] [SPECTRA-VLA: Base-Fine Hyperspectral Token Transfer at Frozen Vision-Language-Action Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19651)
*Chih-Chung Hsu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPECTRA-VLA 通过"自身令牌加性残差"注入高光谱信息，在冻结视觉编码器与动作解码器前提下提升类别平衡准确率与外部探针复用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱成像能区分 RGB 相似目标，但预训练视觉-语言-动作模型缺乏可用高光谱状态，且其解码器冻结，无法靠重训适配。因此需要一种不改动编码器与解码器的原生宽度光谱升级方案。

Method: 学习连续残差，经由每个目标令牌自身的归一化伪彩色参考进行富集；用投影、范数控制与验证集校准注入来约束改动幅度。对比"自身令牌相加"与"池化替换"两种注入方式，并进行分组测试与类别匹配光谱交换实验。

Result: 86 次留出采集上类别平衡准确率由 0.812 升至 0.883；相同残差与尺度下，自身令牌相加使外部类别探针在冻结解码器 logits 上的复用率从 0.590 提至 0.701，图像不相交识别相近。五项分组测试在连续与后验编码状态下恢复类内光谱形状 R²=0.802/0.337；类别匹配光谱交换显示自身令牌组合使目标特定恢复增量约翻倍。

Conclusion: 光谱富集与接收令牌保持需在预训练接口处协同作用，才能实现冻结解码器可用的高光谱状态升级。

Abstract: Hyperspectral imaging distinguishes RGB-similar objects, but a pretrained vision-language-action model needs a state its frozen decoder can use. SPECTRA-VLA provides a native-width spectral upgrade without retraining the visual encoder or action decoder. A learned continuous residual enriches each target token through its own normalized false-color reference; projection, norm control and validation-calibrated injection regulate the change. Across 86 held-out acquisitions, category balanced accuracy rises from 0.812 to 0.883. With identical residuals and scale, own-token addition improves reuse of an external category-derived probe on frozen decoder logits: 0.701 versus 0.590 with pooled replacement, at similar image-disjoint recognition. Five grouped tests covering the same 86 acquisitions recover within-category spectral shape with $R^2=0.802/0.337$ from continuous and posterior-code states. Category-matched spectral swaps further show that own-token composition approximately doubles the target-specific recovery increment after frozen decoding. Spectral enrichment and receiving-token preservation thus work together at the pretrained interface.

</details>


### [23] [Mapping Arctic Polynyas from Sea Ice Concentration Data Using Hierarchical Partitioning and Differentiated Region Growing: Sensitivity to Threshold and Product Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19647)
*Tianyu Zhang,Haiyi Ren,Zhilun Zhang,Yang Hong,Chunlei Mi,Xiao Cheng,Fengming Hui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出基于分层分割、连通域分析与差异化区域生长的日海冰浓度制图框架，用于提取北极沿岸冰间湖；结果显示绝对范围强烈依赖阈值与产品配置，而时间演变特征相对稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 北极沿岸冰间湖因海岸几何复杂、冰况空间异质，基于海冰浓度（SIC）数据的精确刻画仍具挑战，且绝对范围对SIC产品、阈值与分类规则高度敏感，长期监测结果可比性不足。

Method: 构建日尺度SIC制图框架，融合分层分割（hierarchical partitioning）、连通域分析（connected-component analysis）与针对冰间湖和开阔水区的差异化区域生长（differentiated region growing）；应用于2002/03–2024/25年9月至次年5月冰季；开展63景目视精度评估、阈值敏感性实验（75%–90%）与多产品（6.25/12.5/25 km）对比。

Result: 目视评估平均总体精度96.74%，平均kappa系数0.93；相对于75%阈值，季节范围积分在80%、85%、90%阈值下分别相差13.48%、32.47%、65.04%，但年际结构仍可辨识；阈值敏感性呈强区域与季节差异，中央北冰洋最强，圣劳伦斯湾很弱，1月后泛北极曲线分离加大。80%阈值下产品间MAPE：6.25与25 km为68.19%，12.5与25 km为11.47%，区域差异显著。

Conclusion: 可复现的长期冰间湖监测必须固定并明确记录SIC产品、阈值与空间分类规则的组合；即便绝对范围差异较大，宽尺度时间演变模式仍具参考价值。

Abstract: Accurately delineating Arctic coastal polynyas from sea ice concentration data remains challenging because of complex coastal geometry and spatially heterogeneous ice conditions. We develop a daily sea ice concentration (SIC) mapping framework based on hierarchical partitioning, connected-component analysis, and differentiated region growing for polynya and open-water regions. Applied to September-May ice seasons from 2002/03 to 2024/25, the framework achieves a mean overall accuracy of 96.74% and a mean kappa coefficient of 0.93 in the 63-scene visual assessment. Sensitivity experiments reveal a consistent pattern: the absolute magnitude of SIC-defined polynya extent is strongly configuration-dependent, whereas major temporal features are comparatively more stable. Relative to a 75% upper SIC threshold, seasonal extent integrals differ by 13.48%, 32.47%, and 65.04% at thresholds of 80%, 85%, and 90%, respectively, while the broad interannual structure remains recognizable. The response is also strongly regional and seasonal, with the largest threshold sensitivity in the Central Arctic Ocean, very weak sensitivity in the Gulf of St. Lawrence, and greater pan-Arctic curve separation from January onward. At an 80% threshold, product-comparison MAPE is 68.19% between the 6.25 and 25 km products and 11.47% between the 12.5 and 25 km products under their respective comparison periods and reference definitions. Regional product disagreement is likewise heterogeneous. Together, these results show that reproducible long-term polynya monitoring requires a fixed and explicitly documented combination of SIC product, threshold, and spatial classification rules, while broad temporal patterns can remain informative even when absolute extent differs substantially.

</details>


### [24] [Frame Evidence:A Benchmark for Reference Frames and Visual Evidence in Multi-Image Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19564)
*Chunxi Luo,Qishuai Ding,Xingchen Peng,Hengwei Ye,Ke Zhang,Wenxin Lian,Tianyi Lu,Chunlong Yu,Zhenya Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出Frame Evidence基准，用于评估空间回答在参考系变化与视觉证据变化下是否持续正确，揭示仅看题目准确率会掩盖正确性的不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间答案依赖于语言中指定的参考系及其支撑视觉证据。仅用题目准确率会掩盖当参考系或视觉证据发生变化时正确性是否仍然保持的问题。

Method: 构建Frame Evidence基准，包含320个匹配三元组、960道中文多选题，来自实拍与渲染场景。一类比较固定图像上的三种有依据参考系；另一类将标注为证据不足的图像删除与保持答案不变的图像添加配对。采用联合指标，要求在三帧及配对证据变体上均回答正确。在288道留出问题上评估20种配置。

Result: GPT三样本设置达到50.00%题目准确率，但最佳三帧组准确率仅为5/48（10.42%），由GPT两样本达到。少样本提示与任务特定适配提升了题目准确率，但未持续提升联合正确性。输入控制显示图像在何处支持匹配成功，重复执行揭示相似分数下存在答案不稳定性。

Conclusion: 仅报告题目准确率不足以衡量空间推理的稳健性；需采用跨参考系与配对证据变体的联合指标，以揭示正确性保持与答案稳定性问题。

Abstract: A spatial answer depends on the reference frame specified in language and its supporting visual evidence. Item accuracy alone obscures whether correctness persists when either changes. We introduce Frame Evidence, a benchmark of 320 matched triplets and 960 Chinese multiple-choice questions from captured and rendered scenes. One family compares three grounded reference frames over fixed images. The other pairs image deletion annotated as insufficient with answer-preserving addition. Joint metrics require correct answers across all three frames and paired evidence variants. Across 20 configurations on 288 held-out questions, GPT three-shot reaches 50.00\% item accuracy, but the best three-frame group accuracy is 5/48 (10.42\%), reached by GPT two-shot. Few-shot prompting and task-specific adaptation improve item accuracy without consistently improving joint correctness. Input controls show where images support matched success, while repeated executions reveal answer instability beneath similar scores.

</details>


### [25] [WinARDet: Compressed Window Auto-Regression for Long-Horizon Vision-Language Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19419)
*Chenxu Wang,Hangyi Kuang,Bo-Wen Yin,Yuxuan Li,Quan-Sheng Zeng,Yang Zhang,Xu Guo,Xiang Li,Qibin Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出压缩窗口自回归机制WinAR，将长答案序列切分为生成窗口并压缩历史窗口为隐token，缓解密集场景下MLLM检测器的框重复循环问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM检测器将视觉语言定位建模为自回归坐标生成，但在密集场景中易陷入框重复循环，持续输出相同边界框直至解码预算耗尽。作者将其归因于历史答案注意力劫持：全历史自回归下，答案历史中重复出现的坐标token吸引过度注意力，挤占用于关注新视觉证据的注意力汇。

Method: 提出压缩窗口自回归（WinAR）：将长答案序列划分为连续生成窗口，仅在当前窗口内保留完整token级自回归，同时把每个历史窗口压缩为固定数量的隐token用以条件化后续解码，从而减少对原始历史坐标的重复暴露，又保留多目标生成所需的跨窗口依赖。

Result: 在RSDense（每图200-2,000个目标）上，WinARDet将RexOmni的F1（IoU=0.5）从32.16提升至39.36，冗余率从35.48%降至13.00%，并可泛化至并行框解码与多边形生成等不同解码策略与输出格式。

Conclusion: WinAR为跨场景密度的长程视觉语言定位提供了实用范式，有效抑制重复生成并提升密集场景检测性能。

Abstract: MLLM-based detectors formulate vision-language grounding as autoregressive coordinate generation, yet in dense scenes, they often collapse into a box repetition loop, repeatedly emitting identical boxes until the decoding budget is exhausted. We trace this failure to historical-answer attention takeover: Under full-history autoregression, repeated coordinate tokens in the growing answer history attract disproportionate attention, crowding out the attention sinks needed to attend to new visual evidence. % To break this loop, in this paper, we propose \textbf{Compressed Window Auto-Regression} (WinAR). This new mechanism partitions a long answer sequence into consecutive generation windows and restricts full token-level autoregression to the current window only while compressing each previous window into a fixed set of latent tokens that condition subsequent decoding. This design reduces the repeated exposure to raw historical coordinates that triggers the attention takeover, yet preserves cross-window dependencies essential for coherent multi-object generation. On RSDense, which contains 200-2,000 objects per image, our model WinARDet improves RexOmni F1-score (IoU=0.5) from 32.16 to 39.36 over the baseline, while reducing the redundancy rate from 35.48% to 13.00%. WinARDet also generalizes across decoding strategies and output formats, including parallel box decoding and polygon generation, providing a practical paradigm for long-horizon vision-language grounding across scene densities.

</details>


### [26] [Improving Category-Aware Active Domain Adaptation via Uncertainty-Guided Region Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19803)
*My Tra Nguyen Huynh,Viet Cuong Ta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于区域（region-based）的主动域适应样本选择方法，通过最大化正流形覆盖并引入不确定性分数，缓解逐点选择策略的敏感性与冗余性问题，从而提升域适应模型的类别均衡性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动域适应旨在降低源域与目标域标注成本并提升无监督域适应性能。然而，仅以平均精度优化适应模型会导致部分困难类别性能下降，而现有的逐点（point-wise）选择策略存在敏感性与冗余性问题，易使采集函数过拟合。

Method: 1）指出基于影响函数的逐点选择在敏感性与冗余性上的缺陷；2）提出基于区域的选择策略，通过最大化正流形覆盖（positive manifold coverage）保证适应模型性能提升；3）在选择机制中引入不确定性分数，以学习更优的目标域决策边界。

Result: 在多个域适应基准上的综合实验表明，所提方法优于首个类别感知（category-aware）主动域适应基线以及现有各类主动域适应方法。代码开源于：https://github.com/nhtm2702/WHO IS NEXT。

Conclusion: 区域化样本选择结合不确定性度量，可有效克服逐点主动域适应选择策略的过拟合问题，在降低标注成本的同时实现对困难类别的性能保障，验证了该类方法的有效性与通用性。

Abstract: Active domain adaptation researches focus on alleviating the annotation cost, which comprises source data and target data labelling, as well as enhancing the performance of unsupervised domain adaptation. However, optimizing the adaptation model based on the average accuracy can lead to the drop in performance of some difficult categories. To overcome this issue, a point-wise selection strategy built upon the influence function can be used to improve domain adaptation performance. In our work, we first address the sensitive and redundant aspects of point-wise selection, which can lead to overfitting of the acquisition function. We thus introduce a region-based approach that ensures the improvement of the adaptation model by maximizing a positive manifold coverage. An uncertainty score is further introduced to the selection mechanism to learn a better decision boundary for the target domain. In comprehensive experiments, our proposed method surpasses the first category-aware active domain adaptation baseline as well as existing active domain adaptation methods across multiple domain adaptation benchmarks. Our implementation is available at: https://github.com/nhtm2702/WHO IS NEXT.

</details>


### [27] [StateCraft: Augmenting Video World Models with Abstract-State Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19795)
*Lorenzo Venturoli,Nedko Savov,Xi Wang,Marc Pollefeys,Luc Van Gool*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 StateCraft，将持久可更新的抽象状态与视觉状态解耦，用语言模型+视频扩散模型演化动态世界，在生成式界面基准上实现长程一致性与可编辑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型需在长程交互中保持一致性，必须追踪历史动作及其对世界的影响。但模型每次只能生成世界的部分观测，单靠记忆过去的观测不足，因为世界在动作序列后可能已改变，过去观测不再反映当前状态。

Method: 将持久、可更新的抽象状态与视觉状态解耦：抽象状态跟踪实体随世界演化的变化，并用于条件化表示当前视图的视觉状态。提出 StateCraft 架构，将语言模型与视频扩散模型配对以演化动态世界。同时构建无需人工或智能体监督的数据采集流程，可规模化生成密集标注且具信息量的轨迹，并设计衡量生成质量、记忆与可控性的基准。

Result: 在生成式界面基准（挑战在于单次动作可能改变当前不可见部分的状态）上，方法超越现有最先进基线；在长轨迹中保持高自一致性并具备强生成质量；抽象状态显式可编辑，生成能跟随训练数据中未出现过的状态编辑；3D 概念验证实验（动作改变未观测房间的光照）表明该范式亦可应用于空间环境。

Conclusion: 解耦抽象状态与视觉状态的范式能有效支撑长程一致的视频世界建模，显式状态带来可编辑性与泛化到未观测状态的能力，并具备推广到空间环境的潜力。

Abstract: Video world models aim to simulate complex environments with realistic appearance and dynamics. Maintaining consistency over long interactions requires tracking past actions and understanding how those actions affect the environment. Video world models can only generate partial observations of the entire world at a time. Remembering past observations alone may not be sufficient in every scenario, as they may no longer reflect the world after a sequence of actions. We introduce a paradigm that decouples a persistent, updatable abstract state from the visual state. The abstract state keeps track of the entities' changes as the world evolves, and is used to condition the visual state, which represents the current view. We propose StateCraft, a novel architecture that pairs a language model with a video diffusion model to evolve the dynamic world. We evaluate our approach on generative interfaces, which pose a substantial challenge because a single action may change parts of the state that are not currently visible. To support training and evaluation, we develop a data collection pipeline that produces informative, densely annotated trajectories at scale without human or agent supervision, together with a benchmark measuring generation quality, memory, and controllability. On this benchmark, our approach improves surpasses the state-of-the-art baselines. We demonstrate that StateCraft maintains high self-consistency and strong generation quality over long trajectories. Because the abstract state is explicit, it can be edited directly, and generation follows edits to states never observed in the data. Finally, a proof-of-concept 3D experiment, in which actions change the illumination of an unobserved room, shows that the paradigm can also be applied to spatial environments. Project page: https://insait-institute.github.io/StateCraft/.

</details>


### [28] [Adaptive Observation Control for Active Visual Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19624)
*Dikai Li,Weijia Wang,Yuan-Gen Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将固定步数的自适应视觉识别器（AdaptiveNN-DeiT-S）扩展为可控观察接口，使模型能按输入自适应地调整每次注视的视野尺度与注视次数，并通过标签监督的停止头实现早停。实验表明该接口在细粒度、ImageNet-1K及ImageNet-A/R分类划分上保持竞争力，同时支持无重训的阈值化精度-计算权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉识别模型通常在固定视野与固定计算预算下处理图像，但不同图像往往需要不同的尺度与空间证据量。主动视觉虽可通过先观察粗场景再选择信息性注视点来适应，但公开的AdaptiveNN-DeiT-S流水线仍将视野与观察次数固定，缺乏对二者的输入自适应控制。

Method: 在固定步数识别器基础上引入可控观察接口：(1) 预测每次注视的尺度以设定其视野；(2) 对每个选中裁剪区域用可选的尺度门控凹视（scale-gated foveation）与残差高分辨率细节令牌分支进行细化；(3) 使用标签监督的停止头实现依赖输入的早停。

Result: 在细粒度基准、完整ImageNet-1K及ImageNet-A/R衍生的分类划分上，该接口与固定步数基线保持竞争力，并暴露可控观察预算；同一检查点即可实现阈值控制的精度-计算权衡，无需按阈值重训。在序列ImageNet-1K上，学习到的停止规则优于同检查点的固定步数截断，以2.45/4个注视步维持81.9% top-1，并在更低成本下超过三步截断。各基准上，结合尺度控制与细节令牌融合的变体在测试的组件叠加非退出变体中精度最高。

Conclusion: 可控观察接口可在不牺牲精度的前提下，使主动视觉识别按输入自适应调整视野尺度与注视次数，并借助学习到的停止规则实现更优的精度-计算权衡。

Abstract: Visual recognition models usually process a fixed view under a fixed computation budget, although images often benefit from different scales and amounts of spatial evidence. Active vision can adapt by observing a coarse scene and then selecting informative fixations, but the public AdaptiveNN-DeiT-S pipeline still fixes both field of view and observation count. We extend this fixed-step recognizer with a controllable observation interface that adapts both the field of view and the number of fixations to the input. Specifically, the model predicts a per-fixation scale that sets its field of view, refines each selected crop with optional scale-gated foveation and a residual high-resolution detail-token branch, and uses label-grounded stop heads for input-dependent early termination. Experiments across fine-grained benchmarks, full ImageNet-1K, and ImageNet-A/R-derived classification splits show that the interface remains competitive with fixed-step references while exposing a controllable observation budget. The same checkpoint enables threshold-controlled accuracy-compute trade-offs without per-threshold retraining. On sequential ImageNet-1K, the learned stop rule outperforms fixed-step truncation of the same checkpoint, sustaining 81.9% top-1 using 2.45 of the four focus steps and exceeding three-step truncation at lower cost. On each benchmark, a variant combining scale control and detail-token fusion achieves the highest accuracy among the tested component-added non-exit variants. Our code is available at https://github.com/GZHU-DVL/LI-Dikai.

</details>


### [29] [Learning Visual Speech Representations via Cross-Modal Distillation and Joint Face-Lip Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19559)
*Jing-Xuan Zhang,Genshun Wan,Jianqing Gao,Jia Pan,Yumei Zhang,Lichen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FLARE：跨模态蒸馏+人脸-唇部双流建模的鲁棒唇读框架，在LRS2/LRS3/WildVSR上WER分别为11.7%/13.2%/34.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语音线索本身存在固有歧义，且对头部姿态和视频分辨率敏感，导致唇读任务仍然困难；需要更强的视觉语音表征来提升鲁棒性。

Method: 以Whisper语义丰富的表征作为声学教师信号，经随机投影量化器转为离散蒸馏目标，用掩码预测目标进行预训练；采用双流架构联合处理唇部ROI与全脸输入，唇流捕捉细粒度发音动作，脸流编码更广的面部动态；预训练时引入辅助音频以促进跨模态对齐，并施加模态dropout以强化纯视觉表征；下游唇读时将FLARE作为视觉编码器，搭配轻量Transformer解码器或基于LLM的解码器。

Result: 在LRS2、LRS3及域外WildVSR基准上取得较强唇读性能，词错误率分别为11.7%、13.2%和34.3%；渐进式设计研究验证了各主要组件的贡献；分析表明联合人脸-唇部建模在视频分辨率退化和大头部姿态变化下提升鲁棒性，并产生更具音素判别力的视觉表征。

Conclusion: FLARE通过跨模态蒸馏与联合人脸-唇部建模，有效提升唇读的鲁棒性与表征判别力，兼顾域内和域外场景，代码与预训练模型已开源。

Abstract: Lipreading remains challenging due to the inherent ambiguity of visual speech cues and their sensitivity to head pose and video resolution. In this work, we propose FLARE, a framework for robust visual speech representation learning via cross-modal distillation and joint face-lip modeling. FLARE leverages semantically rich Whisper representations as the acoustic teacher signal, converting them into discrete distillation targets through a random-projection quantizer and pretraining the model with a masked prediction objective. To capture complementary visual information, FLARE employs a dual-stream architecture that jointly processes lip region-of-interest (ROI) and full-face inputs, where the lip stream captures finegrained articulatory motion and the face stream encodes broader facial dynamics. During pretraining, auxiliary audio input is incorporated to facilitate cross-modal alignment, and modality dropout is applied to encourage robust visual-only representations. For downstream lipreading, FLARE serves as a visual encoder coupled with either a lightweight Transformer decoder or an LLM-based decoder. Evaluated on LRS2, LRS3, and the out-of-domain WildVSR benchmark, FLARE achieves strong lipreading performance, with word error rates of 11.7%, 13.2%, and 34.3%, respectively. A progressive design study confirms the contribution of each major component. Further analyses demonstrate that joint face-lip modeling improves robustness under degraded video resolution and large head pose variation, while yielding more phoneme-discriminative visual representations. Code and pretrained models are available at https://github.com/jxzhanggg/FLARE.

</details>


### [30] [Reducing the Gap Between Spatial and Space-Filling Curve Neighbors in 3D Point Cloud Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19530)
*Konan Sasaki,Junya Komori,Kazuhiro Hotta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种损失函数以缓解空间填充曲线(SFC)邻近关系与原始欧氏空间不一致带来的间隙问题，并将其用于3D点云分割，在室内外数据集上均优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于空间填充曲线(SFC)的3D点云最近邻搜索方法轻量且精度高，但因SFC固有性质，沿SFC的邻近关系与原始欧氏空间中的邻近关系之间存在间隙(gap)，影响特征学习的准确性。

Method: 设计一种损失函数以降低该间隙的影响：计算沿SFC相邻点之间的距离，并训练模型优化那些在原始3D空间中相距较远的点对之间的特征相似度。将该方法集成到高性能3D点云分割模型中，在三个数据集上进行评估。

Result: 在室内和室外点云数据集上，所提方法均优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 通过损失函数约束SFC序列邻近但欧氏空间远离的点对，可有效缓解SFC邻近关系与欧氏空间邻近关系间的间隙，提升3D点云分割性能。

Abstract: As a lightweight and highly accurate method for nearest-neighbor search in 3D point clouds, approaches based on space-filling curves (SFC) have been proposed. However, due to the inherent properties of SFC, a gap arises between proximity relationships along the SFC and those in the original Euclidean space. In this study, we propose a loss function that reduces the influence of this gap. Specifically, we compute the distances between adjacent points along the SFC and train the model to optimize feature similarity between pairs of points that are distant from each other in the original 3D space. We incorporate the proposed method into a high-performing 3D point cloud segmentation model and evaluate its effectiveness on three datasets. The results demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms conventional methods on both indoor and outdoor point cloud datasets.

</details>


### [31] [Spectral World Renderer: Unified 3D Hyperspectral Unmixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19520)
*Chia-Ming Lee,Yu-Jou Hsiao,Ming-Ching Chang,Xin Li,Yu-Lun Liu,Chih-Chung Hsu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Spectral World Renderer (SWR)，将3D世界生成与高光谱解混统一，实现渲染中解混，并发布带精确标注的 SWR-Bench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机及基础模型只能识别3D场景"有什么"，无法判断"由什么材料构成"，同色表面可能材料不同；现有高光谱解混在3D中或作用于合成后的渲染图像，或绑定于缓慢的隐式场，且缺乏已知组成的多视角数据来验证结果。

Method: 用编码智能体构建可执行的3D世界并以实测光谱渲染，使每张高光谱图像自带精确标签与亚像素比例；在渲染过程中解混：将紧凑3D表示中的每个高斯解码为材料丰度后再合成，一次渲染即得光谱与材料图。

Result: SWR-Bench 提供8个室内外世界可验证分解；无材料标签时在全部8个世界中材料识别最优，在真实采集上平均PSNR最佳，训练速度比解混基线快3.7–12.9倍，渲染34.9个带标签视角/秒，并能区分同色材料。

Conclusion: SWR 统一了世界生成与高光谱解混，支持按材料名、点击像素或参考光谱查询任意表面，验证了渲染中解混的有效性与效率。

Abstract: Cameras and the foundation models built on them recognize what a 3D scene contains, but not what it is made of: surfaces of the same colour can be different materials. Hyperspectral unmixing explains each spectrum as a mixture of material signatures, yet in 3D it is applied to rendered images after the views are composited, or tied to slow implicit fields, and no multi-view data with known composition exist to check the result. We present the Spectral World Renderer (SWR), which unifies world generation and unmixing. A coding agent builds executable 3D worlds that are rendered with measured spectra, so every hyperspectral image comes with exact labels and sub-pixel proportions; the resulting SWR-Bench provides eight indoor and outdoor worlds in which decompositions can be verified. SWR then unmixes inside rendering: each Gaussian of a compact 3D representation is decoded into material abundances before compositing, so one render yields spectra and material maps, and any surface can be queried by material name, a clicked pixel or a reference spectrum. Without material labels, SWR identifies materials best in all eight worlds, has the best mean PSNR on real captures, trains 3.7-12.9x faster than unmixing baselines, renders 34.9 labelled views/s and separates same-coloured materials.

</details>


### [32] [Learning to Select Features with Manifold Deep Unfolding PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19436)
*Jingjing Liu,Pei Tai,Xianchao Xiu,Wanquan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出流形深度展开特征选择网络MDUFS-Net及其增强版MDUFS-Net+，将ℓ2,0范数融入PCA并将ADMM迭代算法展开为端到端可训练网络，实现自动参数学习与流形优化，在无监督特征选择与聚类任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督特征选择在图像处理与模式识别中意义重大，但现有基于模型的方法依赖人工调参，而深度学习方法通常需要大量训练数据，二者均存在明显局限，因此需要一种兼顾自动参数学习与低数据依赖的高效方法。

Method: 首先将ℓ2,0范数引入经典PCA框架；随后将基于ADMM的迭代算法展开为端到端可训练网络，从而实现参数的自动学习；此外设计流形优化策略以提升数值鲁棒性；在此基础上进一步引入ℓ0范数，将MDUFS-Net扩展为更具判别性特征表示的增强变体MDUFS-Net+。

Result: 大量实验验证表明，所提出的MDUFS-Net与MDUFS-Net+在无监督特征选择任务上优于当前最先进方法，并在聚类任务中展现出良好性能。

Conclusion: 通过模型驱动与深度展开的结合，MDUFS-Net有效缓解了超参数人工调优与大数据依赖问题，并在UFS及聚类任务上取得优越表现，代码已开源。

Abstract: Unsupervised feature selection (UFS) is of great significance in image processing and pattern recognition. Nevertheless, existing model-based methods often rely on manually tuned hyperparameters, while deep learning-based methods generally require massive training data. To address these issues, this paper proposes an efficient manifold deep unfolding feature selection network (MDUFS-Net). Technically, we first incorporate the $\ell_{2,0}$-norm into the classic principal component analysis (PCA) framework, then unfold the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM)-based iterative algorithm into an end-to-end trainable network, thereby enabling automatic parameter learning. Moreover, a manifold optimization strategy is developed to improve the numerical robustness. On this basis, we further extend MDUFS-Net into an enhanced variant called MDUFS-Net+ by introducing the $\ell_{0}$-norm for more discriminative feature representations. Extensive experiments validate that our proposed MDUFS-Net and MDUFS-Net+ outperform state-of-the-art methods in the UFS task, with promising performance in clustering. Our code is available at https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/MDUFS.

</details>


### [33] [Kandinsky S2V 1.0: Audio-Driven Avatar Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19290)
*Nikolay Semenov,Artem Riabinin,Sergey Bassenko,Anna Averchenkova,Julia Agafonova,Anastasia Aliaskina,Kirill Kozlov,Lev Novitskiy,Nikita Samsonov,Nikolai Gerasimenko,Denis Dimitrov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Kandinsky S2V 1.0语音到视频模型家族（Lite 2B与Pro 21B），实现可控音频驱动人物动画，支持长视频生成；配合π-Flow蒸馏变体实现5.4倍加速，并开源代码与权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向音频驱动人物动画（说话人脸/半身视频）任务，现有开源方案在长时视频生成、唇音同步、多模态条件控制以及推理效率方面仍存在不足，且缺少可部署的开放模型。论文旨在提供一个从参考图像、语音波形和文本提示出发、可生成可控长视频的开放语音到视频模型家族，并推动该方向的开放研究与部署。

Method: 架构上从单一Qwen2.5-Omni模型同时提取文本与音频条件，并引入参考感知的三尺度视觉记忆以支撑长视频生成。训练基于Kandinsky 5.0 Video基础模型，采用混合参考-记忆布局与面积自适应人脸损失（area-adaptive face loss）。训练数据为117万条单说话人片段，经主动说话人检测筛选，字幕以英语和俄语直接生成。此外对Lite与Pro均提供π-Flow蒸馏变体，Pro蒸馏模型无需无分类器引导，仅用16步采样。

Result: 与最强开源基线及两个专有商业模型的并排人工评测显示，Kandinsky S2V 1.0 Pro平均表现相当或更优，并在唇同步上更受偏好；在常见语音到视频基准上，Lite与Pro基于五项指标的均值排名均位列最优模型之列。蒸馏版Pro在几乎所有报告指标上匹配或超越基础模型，同时实现5.4倍端到端加速（16步采样、无需CFG）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可控、长时、可高效部署的开放语音到视频模型家族，并以MIT许可发布代码、模型检查点与diffusers集成，以促进语音到视频生成的开放研究与落地应用。

Abstract: We present Kandinsky S2V 1.0, an open family of speech-to-video models for controllable audio-driven human animation, comprising Kandinsky S2V 1.0 Lite (2B parameters) and Kandinsky S2V 1.0 Pro (21B parameters). From a single reference image, a speech waveform, and a text prompt, the models generate long-form 25 FPS video in which lip motion, expression, pose, and gesture follow the speech. The architecture draws text and audio conditioning from one Qwen2.5-Omni model and adds a reference-aware three-scale visual memory. Training starts from the Kandinsky 5.0 Video foundation models [1] and combines mixed reference-memory layouts with an area-adaptive face loss. The training corpus contains 1.17M single-speaker clips selected by active-speaker detection, with captions generated directly in English and Russian. A side-by-side human evaluation against the strongest open-source baseline and two proprietary commercial models shows that Kandinsky S2V 1.0 Pro performs comparably to or better than these models on average and is also preferred for lip synchronization. On common speech to video benchmarks, Kandinsky S2V 1.0 Lite and Kandinsky S2V 1.0 Pro rank among the top performing models according to mean rank over five reported metrics. Beyond the base models, we provide π-Flow distilled variants of both Kandinsky S2V 1.0 Lite and Kandinsky S2V 1.0 Pro. The distilled Kandinsky S2V 1.0 Pro model generates video in 16 sampling steps without classifier-free guidance, achieving a 5.4 times end-to-end speedup over the base model, while matching or exceeding it on nearly all reported benchmark metrics. To accelerate open research and deployment in speech-to-video generation, we release the code, model checkpoints, and diffusers integration under the MIT license.

</details>


### [34] [A Gradient-Regularized Deep Learning Method for Restoring Image in Myopic Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19218)
*Kebin Contreras,Luis Toscano-Palomino,Rafael Torres,Jorge Bacca*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于U-Net的深度学习方法，通过在图像上预补偿由二阶Zernike多项式Z_2^0建模的离焦像差，使固定距离下的近视者可获得更清晰的场景感知，并在多数据集上取得稳定PSNR提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近视因眼球球面离焦像差而降低对精细空间细节的感知质量。作者希望不依赖光学矫正手段，而是通过计算方式改造图像内容，主动抵消离焦模糊，从而在固定观看距离下提升近视者的视觉清晰度。

Method: 将离焦像差用二阶Zernike多项式Z_2^0建模，训练神经网络学习其逆变换。采用U-Net架构，损失函数为重建误差最小化与基于ℓ_1正则化的空间差分结构保持项相结合（梯度正则化）。模型仅在KITTI数据集上训练，在DIV2K与ImageNet上测试以验证跨分布泛化能力。

Result: 在Z_2^0模拟的近视条件下，基线视觉退化明显，各数据集与不同严重程度下PSNR为19.47–27.42 dB。所提梯度正则化方法取得一致改进，PSNR增益达1.48–7.34 dB，且感知指标较经典基线显著降低（更优）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过学习式图像预补偿可有效对抗近视离焦像差，且从KITTI到DIV2K、ImageNet的泛化性良好。该方法为面向视觉缺陷的计算成像显示校正提供了可行途径，但摘要未给出具体感知指标数值及主观验证细节。

Abstract: Myopic vision degrades the quality of human vision due to spherical eye defocus aberrations that impair the perception of fine spatial details. To address this limitation, this work proposes a deep learning-based approach to computationally design transformed images that allow individuals with myopia in fixed distance to perceive scenes with enhanced clarity. The method trains a neural network to counteract defocus aberrations modeled using the second-order Zernike polynomial $Z_2^0$. The approach employs a U-Net architecture trained with a loss function combining reconstruction error minimization with spatial difference structural preservation through $\ell_1$ regularization. Training is carried out exclusively on the KITTI dataset, while testing is performed on DIV2K and ImageNet datasets, demonstrating generalization across diverse image distributions. Under myopic conditions modeled by $Z_2^0$, baseline performance shows visual degradation with PSNR values ranging from 19.47 to 27.42 dB across datasets and severity levels. The proposed gradient-regularized method achieves consistent improvements, delivering PSNR gains ranging from 1.48 to 7.34 dB and significantly lower perceptual compared to classical baselines.

</details>


### [35] [RTAformer: Relation-Transition-Aware Transformer for Video Scene Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20091)
*Jaeyeon Heo,Geuntaek Lim,Yukyung Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出RTAformer，通过分别建模实体持续性与关系变化、注入校准的相邻帧关系查询差异并采用跨帧实例对齐训练，缓解“关系语义惯性”问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频场景图生成需同时刻画物体身份持续性及关系突变。现有方法跨帧聚合信息以利用时序上下文，但过度强调一致性会掩盖关系变化，导致关系已改变模型仍保留旧预测，即“关系语义惯性”。

Method: 提出单阶段框架Relation-Transition-Aware Transformer（RTAformer），将实体持续性与关系变化分开建模：向关系查询注入校准后的相邻帧差异以引导谓词更新，并在训练中进行跨帧实例对齐以减轻跨帧表征漂移。

Result: 在Action Genome数据集上，召回率与平均召回率优于现有方法；消融实验验证各组件贡献；在匹配骨干网络下，关系转换帧上的提升大于非转换帧。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模关系转变并对齐跨帧实例表征，可有效克服关系语义惯性，提升视频场景图生成性能，尤其利于关系突变帧。

Abstract: Video scene graph generation (VidSGG) aims to capture objects and their evolving relationships in videos. This requires accounting for both the persistence of object identities and abrupt changes in relationships. Existing methods aggregate information across frames to exploit temporal context, but an emphasis on consistency can obscure these changes. As a result, a model may retain its previous prediction even after the underlying relation has changed. We refer to this failure mode as relational semantic inertia. To address this issue, we propose Relation-Transition-Aware Transformer (RTAformer), a one-stage framework that separately models entity persistence and relation changes.
