It injects calibrated differences between adjacent-frame relation queries to guide predicate updates, while cross-frame instance alignment during training helps reduce cross-frame representational drift. Experiments on Action Genome show improvements in recall and mean recall over existing methods, with ablation studies supporting the contributions of the proposed components and comparisons using matched backbones showing larger gains on relation-transition frames than on non-transition frames.

</details>


### [36] [GeoCloak: An Agentic Framework for Semantics-Preserving Geolocation Privacy via Peripheral Clue Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20055)
*Yixin Wu,Mingjie Li,Yun Shen,Michael Backes,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GeoCloak，一个基于视觉-地理位置不对称性的智能体框架，通过语义编辑抑制周边地理线索并结合对抗扰动，在保持图像效用的同时显著降低LVLM的城市级地理定位准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）使单张无元数据照片的地理定位变得高度准确且易得，带来严重隐私风险：黑盒攻击者即可推断用户位置。现有防护几乎只依赖对抗扰动，会引入可感知视觉伪影并损害图像效用，亟需兼顾隐私与效用的保护方法。

Method: 作者发现"视觉-地理不对称性"：决定地理定位的线索（如路牌）往往在视觉上处于边缘，却主导位置推断。基于此提出GeoCloak智能体框架：作为线索感知规划器，分析图像识别地理相关线索，针对性下发语义编辑以直接抑制边缘线索，并自主配置对抗扰动进一步压制残余地理特征，全过程在迭代的效用感知反馈下进行。

Result: 在覆盖108个国家、576个城市的四个数据集上，针对五个LVLM和三种推断方法评估。GeoCloak将城市级准确率从41.2%降至9.9%，约为最优基线的一半，同时在定性和定量上均实现了明显更优的效用保持。

Conclusion: GeoCloak以语义保持的方式实现了实用的地理定位隐私保护，在强隐私效果与高图像效用之间取得更佳平衡，为用户控制其分享图像中无意嵌入的位置信息提供了新途径。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of large vision language models (LVLMs) has made geolocation inference from images highly accurate and accessible. This poses serious privacy risks: a passive adversary with black-box LVLM access can infer a user's location from a single photo with no metadata. Existing protection methods rely almost exclusively on adversarial perturbation, which introduces perceptible visual artifacts and utility degradation. We identify the visual-geolocation asymmetry: geolocation-decisive clues, such as street signs, tend to be visually peripheral but dominate location inference. Exploiting this asymmetry, we propose GeoCloak, an agentic framework for semantics-preserving geolocation privacy protection. It acts as a clue-aware planner that analyzes each image to identify geolocation-relevant clues, dispatches targeted semantic edits to directly suppress peripheral clues, and autonomously configures adversarial perturbation to further suppress residual geolocation features, all under iterative utility-aware feedback. We evaluate GeoCloak on four diverse datasets spanning 108 countries and 576 cities against five LVLMs and three inference methods. GeoCloak reduces overall city-level accuracy from 41.2% to 9.9%, approximately half of the best-performing baseline, while achieving substantially superior utility preservation both qualitatively and quantitatively. We hope this work takes a meaningful step toward practical, semantics-preserving geolocation privacy protection, giving users greater control over the location information inadvertently embedded in their shared images.

</details>


### [37] [Speculative Sampling in Interactive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20556)
*Yubo Huang,Yuxuan Zhang,Jiaming Liu,Sirui Zhao,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Speculative Sampling 策略，利用大双向窗口建模质量高的特点，通过拼接噪声 latent 扩展建模窗口并缓存预测结果复用，实现低延迟交互式视频生成，同时打破分块边界导致的等待时间下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式视频生成通常依赖小双向分块以降低响应延迟，但初步实验表明更大的分块能提升视频质量并更好地利用 GPU 并行性，且采样时间仅小幅增加。因此需要一种策略兼顾大窗口的建模能力与小分块的低延迟交互。

Method: 提出 Speculative Sampling：通过在窗口后拼接噪声 latent 作为 padding 来扩展建模窗口，并缓存所生成的预测结果，在条件不变时复用；预测结果增量式交付，未提交的未来部分保持可替换。

Result: 延迟分析表明该方法消除了由分块边界施加的等待时间下限，使优化重点从缩小分块尺寸转向加速每块采样；双向窗口大小由此成为联合优化视觉质量与采样时间的决策变量，而非低延迟交互的必要约束。

Conclusion: 该策略为在交互式流式系统中部署大型双向模型提供了路径，无需复杂的流式后训练即可实现高质量、低延迟的交互式视频生成。

Abstract: Interactive video generation commonly relies on small bidirectional chunks to reduce response latency. Yet our preliminary experiments show that larger chunks improve video quality and better utilize GPU parallelism, allowing more latents to be sampled with only a modest increase in sampling time. Motivated by these observations, we propose Speculative Sampling, a strategy that combines the modeling power of large bidirectional windows with the low-latency interaction of small ones. It expands the modeling window by appending noise latents as padding and caches the resulting predictions for reuse when the condition remains unchanged. Predictions are delivered incrementally, while the uncommitted future remains replaceable. Our latency analysis shows that this removes the waiting-time lower bound imposed by chunk boundaries, shifting the optimization focus from reducing chunk size to accelerating per-chunk sampling. Bidirectional window size thus becomes a decision variable for jointly optimizing visual quality and sampling time, rather than a necessary constraint on low-latency interaction. This opens a path to serving large bidirectional models in interactive streaming systems without complex streaming post-training.

</details>


### [38] [Where Should the Gaussians Go? Reusable Label-Aware Allocation for Budget-Constrained Gaussian Splatting of Cinematic CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20397)
*Bowen Xiang,Dingjie Su,Jon S. Heiselman,Michael I. Miga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种可复用的、标签感知的高斯位置分配前端，将患者CT、标签与传递函数映射为2mm体素密度并抽取固定预算B个高斯位置，无需逐患者优化；神经先验与七参数VACF封闭解在20例保留患者上使血管掩膜PSNR较均匀分配提升4.5-7.1dB，接近逐患者优化分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在CT电影级渲染的3D高斯泼溅中，解剖标签有助于保留血管，但表示仍需逐患者优化，致密化决定高斯位置，而实时显示又限制高斯数量。论文聚焦其中一环：在固定预算B下决定高斯应放置于何处，以避免逐患者优化位置分配的开销。

Method: 提出可复用、标签感知的分配前端：输入患者CT、标签与传递函数，输出2mm单元上的密度并精确抽取B个位置，其余高斯属性仍逐患者拟合。前端从逐患者优化场景中一次性学习，实现为群体训练的神经先验和可解释的七参数可见性感知封闭形式（VACF）。

Result: 在20例保留患者、10K-100K高斯、相同属性拟合条件下，两者较均匀分配使血管掩膜PSNR提升4.5-7.1dB，较剪枝的稠密体素对齐模型提升1.6-3.3dB。神经先验在各预算下与逐患者优化分配无显著差异，VACF在血管上与其相差0.6dB以内；19例外部患者上神经先验同样匹配，VACF相差0.7dB以内。作为位置优化初始化，VACF分配在10K高斯时血管上优于逐患者致密化，25K时优化118s后的血管PSNR高于致密化248s。

Conclusion: 标签感知的可复用位置分配前端可有效替代逐患者致密化来分配固定高斯预算，神经先验几乎达到逐患者优化分配的质量，VACF以可解释闭式形式接近该水平并可作为更优初始化。在渲染预设改变时，VACF可迁移到新颜色与光照但不能迁移到无标签传递函数，神经先验则呈相反模式。

Abstract: Anatomical labels help 3D Gaussian splatting preserve vessels in cinematic renderings of CT, but the representation is still found by optimising each patient's scene, where densification decides where Gaussians go, while real-time displays cap their number. We address one part of this process: deciding where a fixed budget of B Gaussians should be placed. We propose a reusable, labelaware allocation front end that maps a patient's CT, labels and transfer function to a density over 2mm cells and draws exactly B positions from it without per-patient optimisation; the other Gaussian attributes are still fitted per patient. The front end is learned once from per-patient optimised scenes and implemented as a population-trained neural prior and an interpretable seven-parameter visibility-aware closed form (VACF). With identical attribute fitting on 20 held-out patients at 10K-100K Gaussians, both raise vessel-masked PSNR over uniform allocation by 4.5-7.1dB and over pruning a dense voxel-aligned model by 1.6-3.3dB. The neural prior does not differ significantly from per-patient optimised allocation at any budget, and VACF is within 0.6dB of it on vessels; on 19 external patients the neural prior again matches it on vessels and VACF is within 0.7dB. As an initialisation for position optimisation, VACF allocation beats per-patient densification on vessels at 10K Gaussians and, at 25K, reaches a higher vessel PSNR after 118s of optimisation than densification after 248s. Under altered rendering presets, VACF transfers to new colours and lighting but not to a label-free transfer function, whereas the neural prior shows the opposite pattern.

</details>


### [39] [STP-GUI: Spatially-Aware Token Pruning for Efficient Web-GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20390)
*Shuo Wang,Sifan Zhou,Xiaobo Wang,Tianyu Fu,Chenxu Zhao,Anyang Su,Hanning Wang,Ping Jiang,Minghui Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出STP-GUI，一种面向GUI智能体的查询引导、空间感知token剪枝框架，在冻结VLM主干下用策略学习优化轻量选择器，于激进token预算下保持细粒度指令相关评分并做空间连贯的窗口级选择，在Online-Mind2Web上21%保留率下保住77.7%全量性能，延迟降31%、FLOPs降57%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web-GUI智能体依赖截图接地，高分辨率截图与多轮历史带来过多视觉token，推理开销大；现有面向自然图像的剪枝方法多按重要性分数独立选token，会打碎识别按钮、图标、超链接等小GUI元素所需的局部视觉结构。

Method: STP-GUI：查询引导的token剪枝框架。在激进token缩减下，兼顾细粒度、指令条件下的token评分，并进行空间连贯的窗口级选择；通过策略学习优化一个轻量选择器，VLM主干保持冻结。

Result: 在Online-Mind2Web上，21% token保留率时保住77.7%的全token性能，推理延迟降低31%，FLOPs降低57%；在Android in the Wild上的结果进一步表明方法可迁移到移动GUI环境。

Conclusion: 空间感知、窗口级、查询引导的剪枝能在低token预算下为GUI智能体取得更优的效率-性能权衡，并具备跨Web与移动GUI环境的迁移性。

Abstract: With the rapid development of vision-language models (VLMs), Web-GUI agents have achieved promising performance in interacting with real-world interfaces through screenshot-based grounding. However, high-resolution screenshots and multi-turn histories introduce excessive visual tokens, causing substantial inference overhead. Existing token pruning methods, mainly designed for natural images, often select tokens independently based on importance scores, which can fragment the local visual structures required for recognizing small GUI elements such as buttons, icons, and hyperlinks. In this work, we propose STP-GUI (Spatially-aware Token Pruning for GUI agents), a query-guided token pruning framework that preserves fine-grained instruction-conditioned token scoring while performing spatially coherent window-level selection under aggressive token reduction. A lightweight selector is optimized through policy learning while the VLM backbone remains frozen. Experiments on Online-Mind2Web show that \papername achieves a favorable efficiency-performance tradeoff under low token budgets. At 21% token retention, it preserves 77.7% of full-token performance while reducing inference latency by 31% and FLOPs by 57%. Results on Android in the Wild further suggest that the method transfers to mobile GUI environments.

</details>


### [40] [Look Before You Restore: Learning to Predict Before Acting for Agentic Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20384)
*Xu Zhang,Jun Wan,Lefei Zhang,Liangpei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PreActIR 智能体图像修复框架，用工具条件世界模型在执行前预测工具后果，减少无效调用并提升修复质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合退化图像修复智能体只能"先执行后评估"：失败的调用浪费算力，有害输出直到过晚才被发现。因此需要在执行前预测工具后果，避免无效与有害调用。

Method: PreActIR 对每个候选工具使用工具条件世界模型，预测残差退化、图像质量、保真度的变化，以及有害风险和预测不确定性；智能体执行排名最高的候选，独立验证器在执行后决定提交或回滚，随后智能体基于观测状态重新规划。框架还审计性能瓶颈来自选择器还是候选空间，并将针对性工具适配作为扩展候选空间的可控手段。

Result: 在固定候选池、验证器和预算并采用带参考标注退化状态的受控评测中，PreActIR 相比反应式"先执行后评估"试错，真实工具调用减少 42.5%，被拒绝调用减少 63.4%，Y-PSNR 提升 0.92 dB。

Conclusion: 在执行前预测工具后果可显著提升混合退化图像修复智能体的效率与效果，独立验证加回滚机制保障输出安全，且对选择器与候选空间的瓶颈分析及工具适配为进一步扩展能力提供方向。

Abstract: Agents for mixed-degradation image restoration typically learn what a tool does to an image only after running it: failed calls waste computation and harmful outputs go unnoticed until too late. We propose PreActIR, an agentic restoration framework that predicts tool consequences before execution. For each candidate tool, a tool-conditioned world model forecasts changes in residual degradation, image quality, and fidelity, together with the risk of harm and the uncertainty of the prediction. The agent executes the top-ranked candidate, and an independent verifier commits or rolls back its output before the agent replans from the observed state. PreActIR also audits whether performance is limited by the selector or by the candidate space, and studies targeted tool adaptation as a controlled way to expand that space. In a controlled evaluation that fixes the candidate pool, verifier, and budget and employs reference-annotated degradation states, PreActIR uses 42.5% fewer real tool calls and 63.4% fewer rejected calls than reactive, execute-then-evaluate trial and error, while improving Y-PSNR by 0.92 dB.

</details>


### [41] [ReferFlow: Beyond Final-Target Localization for Complex Referring Expressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20352)
*Tianyi Gao,Han Fang,Xin Wei,Yaoyao Zhong,Zhaoyan Pan,Weijie Zhu,Hongbo Sun,Zhiyu Lin,Kaicheng Yang,Jinglin Xu,Ye Yuan,Hao Li,Hao Sun,Jingmin Xin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出轨迹级指代理解（REC）新设定并构建 ReferFlow-Bench，同时提出 ReferFlow 方法实现多步中间视觉指代的连续定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 REC 基准多聚焦最终目标定位，而复杂查询常包含依赖先前定位结果的中间视觉指代，仅评测最终结果导致对定位过程缺乏考察。

Method: 将单条复杂查询构建为有序定位步骤序列，在 ReferFlow-Bench 中以 Refinement、Shift、Reset、Mixture 四类轨迹刻画区域转移；提出 ReferFlow，将交错的文本—隐空间轨迹生成与坐标解码解耦，包括以连续隐变量表示对象索引状态并施加视觉与空间监督的有状态区域隐空间推理，以及通过四轮几何约束精修、以轨迹前缀为条件并行恢复全部坐标块的轨迹感知并行解码，并采用 Grounding-Aware GRPO 促进跨步一致定位与自适应轨迹生成。

Result: 现有模型在连续步骤中难以一致地定位中间视觉指代；ReferFlow 在通用 REC 基准与 ReferFlow-Bench 上均取得领先性能。

Conclusion: 轨迹级 REC 设定及 ReferFlow 方法可有效建模复杂查询中的中间指代依赖，提升多步定位一致性并保持通用 REC 性能。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models have made significant progress in referring expression comprehension (REC), yet most REC benchmarks still focus on final-target localization. Complex queries often involve intermediate visual references with different dependencies on prior grounding, but final-only evaluation leaves this process unexamined. To address this gap, we formulate trajectory-level REC, in which models receive an ordered sequence of grounding steps constructed from a single complex query and localize the target regions associated with each step. We instantiate this setting in ReferFlow-Bench, which characterizes structured region transitions through four trajectory types, namely Refinement, Shift, Reset, and Mixture. Experiments show that current models struggle to consistently ground intermediate visual references across successive steps. We further propose ReferFlow, which decouples interleaved text-latent trajectory generation from coordinate decoding. Specifically, Stateful Region Latent Reasoning represents each target region as an object-indexed state composed of continuous latents, with explicit visual and spatial supervision to preserve region information for subsequent grounding. Trajectory-Aware Parallel Decoding then recovers all coordinate blocks in parallel through four geometry-constrained refinement rounds, with each block conditioned on its corresponding trajectory prefix, thereby reducing within-box sequential dependencies and eliminating cross-box autoregression. We further apply Grounding-Aware GRPO to promote consistent grounding across steps and learn adaptive trajectory generation from complex REC queries. ReferFlow achieves leading performance on general REC benchmarks and ReferFlow-Bench.

</details>


### [42] [DreamHMR: From Video Generation to Human Motion Modeling via Structural Focusing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20124)
*Zhengjie Xu,Ruihao Gong,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamHMR 将视频生成预训练先验迁移到人体网格恢复任务，采用全局到局部策略，实现完整身体与手部重建，在多个数据集和遮挡场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从视频中准确重建身体运动与精细手部关节仍然困难，尤其在真实场景中普遍存在遮挡和部分可见问题。视频生成预训练提供了丰富的时空先验，但其侧重视觉合成，与显式人体几何之间存在表征鸿沟。

Method: 提出 DreamHMR 通用框架，采用全局到局部策略。全局身体表征迁移（Global Body Representation Transfer）训练视频骨干预测完整人体渲染，将其原生预测任务转化为学习表面对应与姿态几何的手段；局部关节特征增强（Local Joint Feature Enhancement）将上下文估计与可靠的局部证据结合，恢复精细的身体与手部细节，同时抑制遮挡物与背景干扰。

Result: 在五个数据集、四种视频骨干上验证了跨骨干族的强重建性能。统一评测协议下，最佳模型在 3DPW、EMDB-1、RICH 的所有报告指标上超越现有最佳方法，身体 MPJPE 分别降低 4.2%、4.7%、6.1%；在 BEDLAM 和 EgoBody 留出序列的更具挑战性遮挡设置中，整体身体与手部 MPJPE 分别降低 7.0% 和 10.0%。

Conclusion: DreamHMR 有效弥合了视频生成先验与人体几何之间的鸿沟，为遮挡场景下的全身与手部重建提供了通用且性能领先的解决方案，代码将公开。

Abstract: Human mesh recovery (HMR) provides a geometric foundation for understanding human motion, but accurately reconstructing both body motion and fine hand articulation from videos remains challenging, especially in real-world scenes where occlusion and partial visibility are common. Video generation pretraining offers rich spatial and temporal priors for this task, but its focus on visual synthesis leaves a gap between these representations and explicit human geometry. We present DreamHMR, a general framework that adapts video generation priors for complete body-and-hand reconstruction. It follows a global-to-local strategy, first grounding video representations in human geometry and then refining body and hand detail with reliable local evidence. Global Body Representation Transfer trains the video backbone to predict complete human renderings, turning its native prediction task into a means of learning surface correspondence and posed geometry. Local Joint Feature Enhancement combines contextual estimates with reliable local evidence to recover fine body and hand detail, while limiting interference from occluders and background. Experiments on five datasets with four video backbones demonstrate strong reconstruction performance across backbone families. Under a unified evaluation protocol, our best model outperforms the best existing methods on all reported metrics of 3DPW, EMDB-1, and RICH, reducing body MPJPE by 4.2%, 4.7%, and 6.1%, respectively. In more challenging occlusion settings on held-out BEDLAM and EgoBody sequences, DreamHMR reduces overall body and hand MPJPE by 7.0% and 10.0%, respectively. Our code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [43] [Action Jets: Learning the Local Shape of the Action-conditioned Data Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19007)
*Anton Arapin,Chahyon Ku,Bernadette Bucher*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Action Jet，一种利用低维动作可达帧位于低维流形上的性质，通过估计该流形的局部泰勒展开形状（至三阶），实现单次推理即可评估大量候选动作的规划器，速度提升超过300倍，且短期预测精度更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型维度高、计算昂贵，限制了规划器可评估的动作候选数量。作者发现短期规划只需当前状态的局部信息，因此这种高计算代价是可以避免的。

Method: 对于固定状态，变化低维动作（相机移动或末端执行器指令）可达的帧位于低维流形上。作者通过一个网络直接估计该流形的局部形状：该网络预测每个图像区域在每单位各动作轴上的移动量，由生成器采样数据监督；再用一个在轨迹数据上拟合的校正头，将流形区域形状估计到三阶泰勒多项式精度。这个估计泰勒多项式的网络称为 Action Jet，其有效性半径可由闭式求解。通过遍历该局部流形形状的近似，每个状态只需推理一次即可应对任意数量的候选动作。

Result: 单次提取为十六个候选动作打分，比使用任一源生成器快300倍以上，且随规划器评估候选数增加差距进一步扩大。在真实7-DoF操作基准 BridgeData V2 上与最先进的动作条件生成器 IRASim 和 WorldGym 对比，在 DL3DV 场景上与 FrameCrafter 对比，并参照新视角生成器 DepthSplat 和 LagerNVS。由于仅切向量来自生成器、其余项来自数据，Action Jet 在短时域上预测留出帧的精度也高于提供其切向量的 IRASim，且计算量低100倍以上。

Conclusion: 通过利用短期规划所需的局部低维流形结构并以泰勒多项式近似，Action Jet 在保持甚至超越生成器预测精度的同时，大幅降低计算成本，使规划器能以极高效率评估大量动作候选。

Abstract: World models are a powerful means of reasoning and planning, but many of these action-conditioned predictive models are high dimensional and computationally expensive. These challenges limit the number of action candidates that can be evaluated by a planner. We show that this cost is avoidable for planners over short horizons, which only require local information with respect to the current state. For a fixed state, the frames reachable by varying a low-dimensional action (a camera movement or an end-effector command) lie on a low-dimensional manifold. We estimate the local shape of that manifold directly via a network trained to estimate how each image region moves per unit of each action axis supervised by data collected from samples by the generator. A correction head fit to trajectory data estimates the shape of this manifold region up to a third-order Taylor polynomial. We call this network estimating the Taylor polynomial describing the manifold shape an Action Jet and compute its radius of validity in closed form. By traversing this approximation of the local manifold shape, we only have to perform inference once per state for any number of candidate actions. Scoring sixteen candidates from a single extraction is over 300x faster than with either source generator, and this gap widens with the number of candidates a planner evaluates. We measure against state-of-the-art action-conditioned generators, IRASim and WorldGym on BridgeData V2, a real 7-DoF manipulation benchmark, and FrameCrafter on DL3DV scenes, where we chart from the novel-view generators DepthSplat and LagerNVS. Because only the tangent comes from the generator and every other term from the data, the Action Jet also predicts held-out frames more accurately than IRASim, the generator that supplied its tangent, at short horizons and at over 100x less compute.

</details>


### [44] [DeltaStreamer: Towards Human-Like Agentic Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19849)
*Boyuan Sun,Deshui Miao,Wenzhao Gao,Shaoyong Jia,Shaohui Jiao,Chi Liu,Yan Shu,Derek Li,Bryan Dai,Shengsheng Qian,Qibin Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeltaStreamer是一个受人类观察、记忆与探究方式启发的流式视频理解智能体框架，通过“变化感知记忆 + 查询驱动规划 + 定向证据回放”实现持续观察与主动调查的结合，在OVO-Bench和StreamingBench上分别取得66.07%和59.53%的总分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解要求系统在尚不知道未来查询需要哪些细节时就保留证据。现有方法在压缩视频历史时往往模糊事件转换、遗漏相关细节，且无法恢复缺失的视觉证据。

Method: 提出DeltaStreamer智能体框架：查询到来前持续观察视频流，将视觉变化显式记录进结构化记忆，保留事件如何演进而非仅记录当前内容；查询到来时，由查询驱动规划器识别所需证据，引导记忆检索与定向回放；通过重访历史帧，恢复观察阶段被省略的视觉细节，使答案基于留存描述之外的证据。

Result: 在OVO-Bench和StreamingBench上相比代表性流式视频理解方法取得提升，总分分别为66.07%和59.53%。

Conclusion: DeltaStreamer将连续观察与针对每个问题的主动调查相连接，通过变化感知记忆与查询驱动的证据回放，缓解了压缩历史导致的信息丢失与不可恢复问题，验证了该范式在流式视频理解任务上的有效性。

Abstract: We propose DeltaStreamer, a novel agentic framework for streaming video understanding inspired by how humans observe, remember, and investigate. Streaming video understanding requires a system to retain evidence before knowing which details future queries will need. Existing approaches often obscure event transitions and omit relevant details when compressing video history, and cannot recover missing visual evidence. DeltaStreamer addresses these limitations by combining change-aware memory with query-driven planning and targeted evidence gathering with replay. Before a query arrives, it continuously observes the stream and explicitly records visual changes in structured memory, preserving how events unfold rather than only what is present. When a query arrives, a query-driven planner identifies the required evidence and guides memory retrieval and targeted replay. By revisiting historical frames, the agent can recover visual details omitted during observation and ground its answer in evidence beyond the retained descriptions. This connects continuous observation with active investigation tailored to each question. Experiments on OVO-Bench and StreamingBench demonstrate improvements over representative streaming video understanding methods, with overall scores of 66.07% and 59.53%, respectively.
  Project page: https://bbbbchan.github.io/DeltaStreamer/

</details>


### [45] [LiFi: LiDAR Generation from Multi-View Images via Geometric and Semantic Collaborative Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19786)
*Sizhuo Zhou,Xiaosong Jia,Fanrui Zhang,Qifeng Li,Junjie Li,Zirui Wang,Yukang Feng,Yu Hong,Shaofeng Zhang,Wenlong Liao,Tao He,Juyong Zhang,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 LiFi 框架，从多视角相机图像生成在视觉与几何上对齐的 LiDAR 场景，通过几何与语义双分支引导潜在扩散过程，并引入双分支平衡的无分类器引导（CFG）策略，实验证明其生成质量优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从多视角图像生成 LiDAR 点云在可控数据合成、跨模态仿真和感知任务中具有价值，但现有方法并非专为此任务设计，生成质量有限，且通常仅利用单目图像的语义线索，缺乏几何对齐与多视角信息。

Method: 提出 LiFi 专用框架：以潜在扩散模型为核心，通过几何与语义两条互补分支引导生成。几何分支借助反向采样算法与不确定性感知几何编码器（结合 DepthAnything3）从图像构建场景特征；语义分支通过跨视角交互模块提供高层语义表示；并设计双分支平衡的无分类器引导（CFG）策略，在增强条件生成能力的同时保持两分支的独立学习与协同可控性。

Result: 大量实验表明，LiFi 在生成高保真 LiDAR 场景方面优于现有最先进方法。代码将在 https://github.com/szzhou88/LiFi 公开。

Conclusion: LiFi 是面向多视角图像到 LiDAR 生成的专用双分支扩散框架，通过几何-语义互补引导与平衡 CFG 策略实现了高质量的视觉与几何对齐生成效果。

Abstract: Generating LiDAR point clouds from multi-view camera images is a valuable task with applications in controllable data synthesis, cross-modal simulation and various perception tasks. However, most existing methods are not specifically designed for this task, and the quality of their generated LiDAR remains limited. Moreover, these methods typically focus only on semantic cues in images or generate LiDAR from a monocular camera image. In this work, we introduce LiFi (LiDAR From images), a dedicated framework for generating visually and geometrically aligned LiDAR scenes from multi-view camera images. LiFi guides the latent diffusion process through two complementary branches: geometry and semantics. The geometry branch constructs scene features from images through a reverse sampling algorithm and an uncertainty-aware geometric encoder combined with DepthAnything3, whereas the semantic branch provides high-level semantic representations via a cross-view interaction module. We further introduce a dual-branch balanced classifier-free guidance (CFG) strategy, which enhances the model's conditional generation capability while preserving the independent learning and collaborative controllability of the two branches. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LiFi outperforms state-of-the-art methods in generating high-fidelity LiDAR scenes. The code will be publicly available at: https://github.com/szzhou88/LiFi.

</details>


### [46] [FPV-CRASH: A Dataset for Offline Crash Moment Localization in Drone First-Person-View Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19785)
*Jifan Zhang,Tingyu Wang,Qian Liu,Zhedong Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FPV-CRASH数据集与固定协议基准，用于无人机第一人称视角(FPV)视频中的坠机时刻定位，并给出热力图参考模型，将MAE从1.12 s降至0.56 s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有坠机视频数据集多面向监控或行车记录仪画面，其标注体系与任务定义不适用于无人机机载FPV视频中"主要物理撞击"时刻的时间定位，导致该任务缺乏专用数据与评测规范。

Method: 构建FPV-CRASH数据集：1,052段无人机FPV片段（526坠落/526正常），每个坠落片段含单帧坠机时刻参考标注，并为每段配四条由采样帧与已知标签自动生成的英文描述；正常片段无时间戳目标，仅提供训练期背景监督。描述用于辅助的文生视频检索任务，定位推理仅用视频。进一步提出基于热力图的参考模型，从稀疏采样帧估计连续坠机时刻。评测采用固定、非源不交的片段级协议。

Result: 参考模型将MAE由最强固定时序先验基线的1.12 s降至0.56 s（相对降低50%），中位绝对误差(MedAE)为0.33 s；0.5 s内定位成功的片段比例由37.14%提升至65.71%。

Conclusion: FPV-CRASH填补了无人机FPV坠机时刻定位的数据与评测空白；发布内容含标注、可用源视频标识与URL、切片元数据、固定划分列表及预处理代码，可在不重新分发源视频的前提下重建可报告部分；投稿时所有源汇编URL均可访问。

Abstract: Crash Moment Localization aims to localize the major physical impact that interrupts controlled flight in an onboard first-person-view (FPV) video recorded by a drone, thereby providing a temporal reference for retrospective crash analysis in aerial robotics. However, existing crash-video datasets predominantly target surveillance or dash-camera footage, and their annotation schemes and task formulations are not designed for temporal localization of the major physical impact in UAV onboard FPV videos. To address this gap, we present FPV-CRASH, a dataset and fixed-protocol reference evaluation for offline Crash Moment Localization in drone FPV videos. FPV-CRASH contains 1,052 drone FPV clips (526 Crash and 526 Normal); each Crash clip has a single-frame reference annotation for the Crash Moment, and every clip is paired with four English captions automatically generated from sampled frames and the known Crash/Normal label. Normal clips carry no timestamp target and provide training-time background supervision. The captions support an auxiliary Text-to-Video Retrieval task, whereas localization inference remains video-only. We further provide a heatmap-based reference model that estimates a continuous Crash Moment timestamp from sparsely sampled frames. Under the fixed, non-source-disjoint clip-level protocol, the reference model reduces MAE from 1.12 s for the strongest fixed timing-prior baseline to 0.56 s (a 50% relative reduction) and achieves a median absolute error (MedAE) of 0.33 s; the fraction of clips localized within 0.5 s rises from 37.14% to 65.71%. The release includes annotations, available source video identifiers and URLs, clipping metadata, fixed split lists, and preprocessing code so that the reportable portion can be reconstructed without redistributing source footage. At submission time, all source-compilation URLs remained accessible.

</details>


### [47] [HyperLoop: Hyperspectral-Assisted Loop Closure Detection in 3D-SLAM for Planetary Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19742)
*Annie Bhalla,Dennis Rotondi,Riccardo Giubilato,Kai O. Arras,Wolfgang Stuerzl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperLoop，首个将高光谱成像(HSI)集成到3DGS-SLAM回环检测模块的框架，在行星类地环境中通过光谱描述子缓解视觉重复地形的感知歧义；但在600-860 nm红-近红外范围内的实验表明HSI融合增益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行星漫游车自主导航面临视觉重复、缺乏纹理的地形，基于3DGS的SLAM虽有稠密建图能力，但依赖RGB外观描述子，在感知歧义（物理不同位置视觉相同）下退化，这是地外环境的常见失效模式。作者希望引入材质级光谱信息补充RGB特征，提升回环检测对光照变化和视觉重复的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出HyperLoop框架，将HSI接入3DGS-SLAM的回环闭合模块。设计两类HSI描述子：(1) Bag-of-Spectral-Words (BoSW)，用TF-IDF加权直方图编码光谱材质分布；(2) PCA-K VLAD (PCAK-VLAD)，捕获PCA压缩光谱特征的一阶残差统计。与SIFT-VLAD、NetVLAD两类RGB描述子在Strict-AND与Weighted两种融合策略下对比。数据集为DLR的Moon-Mars Outdoor Test Site (MMOTS)自采集数据，包含同步RGB-D与高光谱序列，共四条行星类比路线（总长65.5 m）。

Result: 关键发现：传感器红-近红外波段(600-860 nm)对岩石地形的判别力有限。最优HSI融合(SIFT-VLAD + BoSW, Strict-AND)达到5.04 cm ATE RMSE，与仅用SIFT-VLAD (4.95 cm)相差仅1.8%。相对NetVLAD (8.81 cm)的性能提升归因于SIFT-VLAD本身，而非HSI融合。

Conclusion: 600-860 nm范围不足以提供行星SLAM所需的光谱判别信息，未来应改用SWIR传感器，以捕获该波段缺失的诊断性矿物吸收特征，从而真正发挥高光谱回环检测的优势。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation of planetary rovers is challenged by visually repetitive, featureless terrain. SLAM systems based on 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) offer dense mapping but rely on RGB appearance descriptors that degrade under perceptual aliasing - a common failure mode in extraterrestrial environments where physically distinct locations appear visually identical. This paper presents HyperLoop, the first framework integrating hyperspectral imaging (HSI) into the loop closure module of a 3DGS-based SLAM system. Hyperspectral descriptors encode material-level spectral signatures complementing RGB features, providing robustness to illumination changes and visual repetitiveness. Two HSI descriptor families are introduced: Bag-of-Spectral-Words (BoSW), encoding spectral material distributions via TF-IDF weighted histograms, and PCA-K VLAD (PCAK-VLAD), capturing first-order residual statistics of PCA-compressed spectral signatures. Both are evaluated with SIFT-VLAD and NetVLAD RGB descriptors under Strict-AND and Weighted fusion strategies on a custom dataset from the Moon-Mars Outdoor Test Site (MMOTS) at DLR, comprising synchronized RGB-D and hyperspectral sequences across four planetary analog traverses (65.5 m total). A key finding is that the sensor's Red-NIR range (600-860 nm) provides limited discriminability for rock terrain: the best HSI fusion (SIFT-VLAD + BoSW, Strict-AND) achieves 5.04 cm ATE RMSE, within 1.8% of SIFT-VLAD alone (4.95 cm). The gain over NetVLAD (8.81 cm) is attributable to SIFT-VLAD, not HSI fusion. This motivates future adoption of SWIR sensors capturing diagnostic mineral absorption features absent from the 600-860 nm range for planetary SLAM missions.

</details>


### [48] [No Way To Steal My Face: Proactive Defense Against Identity-Preserving Personalized Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19717)
*Lizhi Xiong,Jun Li,Ziqiang Li,Weiwei Jiang,Zhangjie Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IDGuardian：首个通用、模型无关的人脸身份保护框架，可同时防御基于训练与免训练的人脸个性化生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型个性化（数字头像、虚拟试穿）依赖敏感人脸参考图，引发隐私担忧；现有防御仅针对基于训练的方法，难以泛化到免训练方法，因其身份整合范式存在本质差异。

Method: 将个性化过程抽象为身份提取与身份注入两个阶段，施加精心设计的对抗扰动同时干扰两阶段：退化外部编码器提取的身份特征，并构建对抗性概念桥梁，使生成轨迹偏离目标身份。

Result: 在多种个性化流程与模型架构上有效保护身份，且对后处理、自适应攻击和跨数据集泛化保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: IDGuardian是通用且模型无关的身份保护框架，能统一防御基于训练与免训练的个性化方法，代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in diffusion models have enabled high-fidelity, identity-preserving image generation for personalized applications such as digital avatars and virtual try-on systems. However, their reliance on sensitive facial reference images raises growing privacy concerns. Existing defense mechanisms are primarily designed for training-based personalization and struggle to generalize to emerging training-free approaches, due to fundamental differences in their identity integration paradigms. To bridge this gap, we propose IDGuardian-the first generalizable and model-agnostic identity protection framework capable of defending against both training-based and training-free personalization methods. IDGuardian abstracts the personalization process into two critical stages: identity extraction and identity injection. It then introduces crafted adversarial perturbations to simultaneously disrupt both stages. Specifically, it degrades the identity features extracted by external encoders and establishes an adversarial conceptual bridge that misdirects the generative trajectory away from the target identity. Extensive experiments show that IDGuardian effectively protects identity across various personalization pipelines and model architectures, while remaining robust to post-processing, adaptive attacks, and cross-dataset generalization. Our code is available at https://github.com/OpenAscent-L/IDGuardian.git.

</details>


### [49] [What Breaks in Multi-Image Schema Application? Factorial Oracle Interventions Reveal an Instance-Information Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19611)
*Yiliu Xu,Xinjin Li,Yu Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过2×2 Oracle析因设计在多图像模式应用任务中定位瓶颈：主要可恢复缺陷在于上游有序实例恢复，而非下游模式使用与组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多图像视觉语言模型虽可处理多图上下文，但聚合准确率无法揭示多图像推理流程中具体在哪个环节失败，因此需要一个可控任务与干预设计来定位瓶颈。

Method: 构建受控的模式应用任务：模型须在16图序列中定位两个目标字符、识别其视觉属性、查阅条目专属视觉码本，并组成两个有序非词码名。采用条目内2×2析因Oracle设计：Oracle-A提供经核验的文本模式，Oracle-B提供经核验的有序目标外观表征，两者干预均保留原始图像与最终问题。共72个新条目、1188条模型响应；另设检索诊断任务。

Result: Oracle-B使严格精确准确率提升54.6个百分点（95%置信区间46.3–63.0），并比Oracle-A高60.6个百分点（Holm校正p≤.0002）。辅助检索诊断在移除码名生成后仍仅达49.1%的有序外观准确率。同时提供两种Oracle时，所有模型准确率达100%。

Conclusion: 多图像推理中主要的可恢复性能缺陷位于上游的有序实例恢复，而非下游的模式使用与组合；该结论由Oracle干预的显著差异与检索诊断的低上限共同支持。

Abstract: Modern vision-language models can accept multi-image contexts, but aggregate accuracy rarely reveals where a multi-image reasoning pipeline fails. We study a controlled schema-application task in which a model must locate two target characters in a 16-image sequence, identify their visual attributes, consult an item-specific visual codebook, and compose two ordered nonce code names. We introduce a within-item 2-by-2 factorial Oracle design: Oracle-A supplies a verified textual schema, Oracle-B supplies a verified ordered target-appearance representation, and both interventions retain the original images and final question. Across 72 new items and 1,188 analyzed model responses, Oracle-B increased strict exact accuracy by 54.6 percentage points (95% confidence interval: 46.3 to 63.0) and outperformed Oracle-A by 60.6 points (Holm-adjusted p-value no greater than .0002). An auxiliary retrieval diagnostic reached only 49.1% ordered-appearance accuracy even after removing code-name generation. Providing both Oracle sources yielded 100% accuracy across all models. These results localize the dominant recoverable deficit to upstream ordered instance recovery rather than primarily to downstream schema use and composition.

</details>


### [50] [LED-BENCH: A Benchmark for Large-format Engineering Drawing Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19571)
*Ke Li,Tianyi Wang,Enning You,Chaoqun Wan,Zhen Huang,Shuxian Liang,Xian-Sheng Hua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出LED-BENCH，一个面向大尺寸、信息密集工程图纸的多学科基准，用于评测多模态大模型的细粒度理解与复杂推理能力。评测11个代表性模型发现，最强模型平均准确率仅52.65%，证据整合类问题表现最差，说明该任务仍是持续挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实工程图纸通常是大尺寸、信息密集的，回答实际工程问题需要细粒度理解工程信息，并跨区域、跨视图对对象、关系与证据进行复杂推理。现有基准多聚焦局部区域、单一视图、特定要素或较受限的视觉设定，缺乏对真实大尺寸工程图纸理解的系统性评测，因此需要构建新的基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建LED-BENCH多学科基准，覆盖机械、建筑、交通三大工程领域，任务形式聚焦大尺寸工程图纸上的细粒度理解与复杂推理（包括需整合更广范围证据的问题）。基于该基准对11个代表性多模态大语言模型进行广泛评测，其中包含8个专有模型与3个开源模型。

Result: 评测显示任务难度显著：GPT-5.6 Sol取得52.65%的平均准确率；模型性能在不同工程领域之间存在差异；所有被评测模型在需要更广证据整合的问题上表现更差。

Conclusion: 对大尺寸工程图纸的细粒度理解、证据整合与复杂推理，仍是当前多模态模型持续面临的挑战，LED-BENCH为这一方向提供了系统性评测平台并揭示出明显的性能缺口。

Abstract: Real-world engineering drawings are often large-format and information-dense. Answering practical engineering questions therefore requires both fine-grained understanding of engineering information and complex reasoning over objects, relations, and evidence distributed across different regions or views. Existing benchmarks cover a range of engineering-drawing understanding tasks involving local regions, individual views, specific elements, or more constrained visual settings. However, systematic evaluation of understanding over realistic, large-format engineering drawings remains limited. To address this gap, we introduce LED-BENCH, a multidisciplinary benchmark designed to evaluate fine-grained understanding and complex reasoning over large-format engineering drawings, spanning the mechanical, architectural, and transportation domains. Extensive evaluation of 11 representative multimodal large language models, including 8 proprietary models and 3 open-source models, reveals substantial remaining difficulty: GPT-5.6 Sol achieves 52.65% average accuracy, performance varies across engineering domains, and all evaluated models perform worse on questions requiring broader evidence integration. These results highlight fine-grained understanding, evidence integration, and complex reasoning over large-format engineering drawings as persistent challenges for current multimodal models.

</details>


### [51] [Cross-Scenario Industrial Defect Detection via Domain-Robust Training and Metric-Aligned Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19540)
*Dikai Li,Weijia Wang,Yuan-Gen Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向IDA 2026 Track 1的跨场景工业板缺陷检测方法，包含四个互补模块，在测试集上取得Track 1得分0.564、筛选得分0.696（单张RTX 4090）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业板自动缺陷检测需在光照、表面污染物、产线条件等跨场景变化下保持高可靠性，模型须在未见场景中完成缺陷的检测、定位与分类，同时尽量减少清洁板上的误报；因此该任务本质上是一个域泛化问题。

Method: 方法由四个模块组成：（1）跨场景鲁棒训练，引入跨场景鲁棒归一化与DSU式统计扰动结合渐进随机卷积；（2）小缺陷感知学习，采用NWD与无参数空间注意力；（3）分数校准与推理阈值选择，使用VFL学习目标；（4）指标对齐的检查点选择，采用面向Track 1代理指标的定制SWAD变体。

Result: 在测试数据集上，模型取得Track 1得分0.564与筛选得分0.696，实验基于单张RTX 4090 GPU完成。代码已开源于 https://github.com/GZHU-DVL/Dikai。

Conclusion: 四个互补模块的组合有效应对了跨场景缺陷检测中的域泛化挑战，在单卡条件下取得具有竞争力的成绩，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Abstract: Automated defect detection of industrial boards must remain highly reliable under cross-scenario variations in lighting, surface contaminants, and production line conditions. This makes cross-scenario defect detection a domain generalization problem, wherein models are required to detect, localize, and classify defects across unseen scenarios while minimizing false alarms on clean boards. For the IDA 2026 Track 1 challenge, we propose a cross-scenario defect detection method that includes four complementary modules: cross-scenario robust training, small-defect-aware learning, score calibration and inference threshold selection, and metric-aligned checkpoint selection. These four modules respectively introduce cross-scenario robust normalization and DSU-style statistic perturbation with progressive random convolution, NWD and parameter-free spatial attention, the VFL learning objective, and a custom SWAD variant driven by the Track 1-oriented proxy. Experimental results demonstrate that our model achieves a Track 1 score of 0.564 with a screening score of 0.696 on the test dataset on a single RTX 4090 GPU. The code is available at https://github.com/GZHU-DVL/Dikai.

</details>


### [52] [HyrCap: Hybrid Rank-Calibration of Action Proposals for Temporal Event Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19526)
*Rui Chen,Xingyu Chen,Pengxin Xu,Kai Chen,Junzhi Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以提案为中心的查询式时序动作检测框架HyrCap，通过混合特征与排序校准提升提案可靠性与语义理解，并给出分阶段评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长未裁剪视频的时序动作检测存在定位不准、提案质量低的问题，尤其在稀疏标注与边界模糊情况下。传统TAD流程缺乏对提案定位质量、跨表征支持及提案间关系的显式建模，且可靠提案是连接时序定位、事件标注与语义理解的关键。

Method: HyrCap将密集查询预测视为过完备的时序事件假设空间；"Hybrid"融合互补视频特征与不同定位线索；"Rank-Calibration"调整提案排序置信度以反映定位可靠性。具体包含跨表征共识校准（Cross-Representation Consensus Calibration）、定位质量估计（Localization Quality Estimation）和提案冗余抑制（Proposal Redundancy Suppression）三个模块；并利用校准后提案作为LLM语义理解的输入。

Result: 在公开基准上，HyrCap在多个指标上达到最先进水平，并提供更细粒度的查询式事件分割与标注范式。分阶段评测协议可分别评估事件定位、片段级语义理解及二者结合的事件理解性能。

Conclusion: HyrCap以提案为中心统一了时序定位、事件标注与语义理解，提升了提案排序可靠性和定位质量，为查询式事件分割与标注提供了更细粒度的范式与评测协议。

Abstract: Temporal action detection in long untrimmed videos suffers from inaccurate localization and low-quality proposals, especially with sparse annotations and ambiguous boundaries. For open-ended video understanding and LLM-driven event analysis, reliable proposals connect temporal localization, event annotation, and semantic understanding. However, conventional temporal action detection (TAD) pipelines lack explicit modeling of proposal localization quality, cross-representation support, and inter-proposal relations. We propose Hybrid Rank-Calibration of Action Proposals (HyrCap), a proposal-centric query-based TAD framework for temporal event understanding. Hybrid combines complementary video features with different localization cues, while Rank-Calibration adjusts proposal ranking confidence to reflect localization reliability and candidate competition. HyrCap treats dense query-based predictions as an over-complete temporal event hypothesis space. Cross-Representation Consensus Calibration, Localization Quality Estimation, and Proposal Redundancy Suppression improve proposal ranking reliability, localization quality, and candidate discrimination. Inspired by LLMs' capabilities in fine-grained semantic recognition, event description, and reasoning, we introduce HyrCap for Semantic Action Understanding, using calibrated proposals for standard TAD and as inputs to LLM-based semantic understanding. We define a staged evaluation protocol to separately measure event localization, segment-level semantic understanding, and combined event understanding performance. Experiments on public benchmarks show that HyrCap achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple metrics and provides a finer-grained paradigm and evaluation protocol for query-based event segmentation and annotation.

</details>


### [53] [Robust Real-Time Monocular Human Pose Estimation with Insole-Based Sensor Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19494)
*Liang Yu,Tao Zhu,Xiangqing Lu,Wenjian Luo,Liming Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用智能鞋垫的足底压力与加速度传感，弥补单目人体姿态估计在下肢遮挡或短暂视觉缺失时的不足，并构建同步多模态数据集与部件解耦时序融合框架，在三种实时视觉主干上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目视觉姿态估计精度虽高，但在下肢遮挡或短暂出框等视觉信息不完整的常见场景下，难以重建物理合理的下肢运动与可靠的足地接触。作者希望借助非侵入、可穿戴的足部传感器解决这些视觉固有限制。

Method: 首先采集同步多模态数据集；提出部件解耦时序融合框架：将足部感知特征融合进下肢分支以补偿不完整视觉证据，并用轻量协调器在上、下肢时序表示间交换信息以保持全身一致性；在基于2D关键点、3D landmark和SMPL人体网格恢复的三种代表性实时视觉主干上进行评估。

Result: 定量实验表明，引入简单足部传感器相比纯视觉基线可稳定提升姿态精度、运动平滑度与步态相关测量指标，且在下肢遮挡和短暂视觉丢失条件下增益最显著。

Conclusion: 非侵入式足部传感在相机观测暂时不可用时，能提供关键且互补的物理可观测性，是单目人体姿态估计的有效补充。

Abstract: Real-time monocular human pose estimation is an important sensing component for human-centered intelligent systems. While recent camera-based methods have achieved impressive accuracy, fundamental challenges persist, particularly when visual observations are incomplete, such as during lower-body occlusion or short out-of-frame intervals. In these challenging but common scenarios, vision-only systems often struggle to reconstruct physically plausible lower-limb motion and reliable foot-ground contact. Due to the highly unobtrusive and wearable nature of smart insoles, this paper investigates whether simple plantar pressure and acceleration sensors can effectively resolve these visually induced limitations. To systematically investigate this, we first collect a synchronized multimodal dataset and then propose a part-decoupled temporal fusion framework. The proposed model fuses foot-sensing features into the lower-body branch to compensate for incomplete visual evidence, while a lightweight coordinator exchanges information between the upper- and lower-body temporal representations to maintain whole-body consistency. The framework is evaluated with three representative real-time visual backbones based on 2D keypoints, 3D landmarks, and SMPL-based human mesh recovery. We evaluate our framework on three representative real-time visual backbones. Quantitative experiments demonstrate that incorporating simple foot sensors consistently improves pose accuracy, motion smoothness, and gait-related measurements over vision-only baselines, with the most significant gains observed under occlusion and short visual-dropout conditions. Ultimately, our results demonstrate that unobtrusive foot sensing provides vital and complementary physical observability when camera observations are temporarily unavailable.

</details>


### [54] [SonoWorld: Towards Interactive Pose-Controllable Ultrasound World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19327)
*Zefan Yang,Zheyuan Zhang,Boris Maihe,Yikang Liu,Hang Yu,Ankush Mukherjee,Xiao Chen,Pingkun Yan,Terrence Chen,Shanhui Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SonoWorld——首个主要从自由手临床视频学习、具备因果性与6-DoF位姿可控的超声世界模型，为机器人超声提供可交互的策略学习与评估环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人超声可标准化图像采集并降低操作者负担与变异性，但策略学习受限于缺乏真实可控的仿真环境。超声世界建模困难：每帧仅捕获3D解剖的一个截面，探头微小移动即引起大幅外观变化。现有方法依赖机器人跟踪位姿与隐空间预测，无法直接生成交互控制所需的观测。

Method: 三阶段训练：1）在约690万帧无标注多器官超声帧上预训练潜在动作条件视频扩散模型；2）用少量EM传感器跟踪的甲状腺数据集，将学到的视频动力学锚定到度量探头位姿；3）采用分布匹配蒸馏，实现因果、长时程自回归生成，并在推理时选择性缓存驱逐token。整体从无标注临床视频学习视频动力学，并以少量EM跟踪自由手扫描实现6-DoF控制接地。

Result: 在留出跟踪扫描上以合成动作评估可控性，指标包括位姿跟随保真度、往返一致性与轴特异控制，并通过用户研究验证。在基于模型的动作判别中，SonoWorld在多达74.8%的试验中正确地将目标导向探头运动排序于等幅度反向运动之上。

Conclusion: SonoWorld是首个主要从自由手临床视频学习、因果且位姿可控的超声世界模型，为机器人超声数据采集与策略评估提供数据驱动的交互式环境。

Abstract: Robotic ultrasound can standardize image acquisition and reduce operator workload and variability, but policy learning remains limited by the lack of realistic, controllable simulation environments. While controllable world models have advanced rapidly in natural-image domains, ultrasound world modeling remains challenging because each frame captures only a cross-section of unobserved 3D anatomy, causing small probe motions to induce large appearance changes. Existing approaches rely on robot-tracked poses and latent-space prediction, but do not directly generate the observations required for interactive control. We propose SonoWorld, a causal ultrasound world model that learns video dynamics from unlabeled clinical videos and grounds them to 6-DoF control using a small set of freehand clinical scans tracked with an EM sensor. SonoWorld is trained in three stages: pretraining a latent-action-conditioned video diffusion model on around 6.9 million unlabeled multi-organ ultrasound frames; grounding the learned video dynamics to metric probe poses with a small EM-tracked thyroid dataset; and applying distribution matching distillation to enable causal, long-horizon autoregressive generation with selectively caching eviction tokens at inference time. We evaluate controllability through pose-following fidelity, round-trip consistency, and axis-specific control under synthetic actions on held-out tracked scans, and further validate it with a user study. In model-based action discrimination, SonoWorld correctly ranks the target-directed probe motion over an equal-magnitude opposite motion in up to 74.8% of trials. To our knowledge, SonoWorld is the first causal, pose-controllable ultrasound world model learned primarily from freehand clinical videos, providing a data-driven interactive environment for robotic ultrasound data acquisition and policy evaluation.

</details>


### [55] [Data-free Multistep Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19305)
*Ngoc Duy Tran,Kien Do,Duc Thanh Nguyen,Truyen Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出渐进式多步无数据蒸馏PMDD，为每个学生分布分配专属适配网络（不增加内存开销），配合分阶段unforget损失，单模型支持多推理预算，在ImageNet 64x64两步达到FID 1.99，零样本COCO 512x512达FID 7.76，训练算力较DMDv2减少4-5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步扩散蒸馏在小数据集上表现良好，但在高分辨率或复杂文本到图像场景下不足；扩展到多步蒸馏通常依赖训练数据，且存在早期步退化、不同采样预算下输出不一致、计算成本高等问题。作者指出这些缺陷源于"同时训练"：单一适配网络需同时逼近不同学生分布的多个score函数，导致分布匹配有偏、优化不稳定。

Method: 提出渐进式多步无数据蒸馏（PMDD）：为每个学生分布分配专属的适配网络，同时通过设计避免维护多个适配网络带来的显存开销；并引入分阶段unforget损失以保留早期步的生成能力，从而降低学生模型的优化偏差。

Result: 在每个多步预算下均持平或超越无数据少步基线；ImageNet 64x64两步达到FID 1.99，优于其512-NFE教师模型（2.32）；零样本COCO 512x512达到FID 7.76；支持三种推理预算时训练算力比无数据DMDv2少4-5倍。

Conclusion: PMDD通过按学生分布分解训练、分阶段保留早期步生成，缓解了同时训练带来的分布匹配偏差，实现更快收敛，并以单一统一模型支持多种推理预算，在无数据少步蒸馏中取得更优的精度与算力效率。

Abstract: One-step diffusion distillation achieves strong performance on small datasets but remains insufficient for high-resolution or complex text-to-image settings. Extending to multistep distillation often requires training data, suffers from early-step degradation, inconsistent outputs across sampling budgets, and incurs high computational cost. We identify that these limitations stem from simultaneous training, where a single adapted network approximates multiple score functions of different student distributions at once, causing biased distribution matching and unstable optimization. Therefore, we propose Progressive Multistep Data-free Distillation (PMDD), which assigns a dedicated adapted network per student distribution while avoiding the memory overhead of maintaining multiple adapted networks. Combined with a stage-wise unforget loss to preserve earlier-step generations, PMDD reduces optimization bias in the student, provides faster convergence, and a single unified model that supports multiple inference budgets. Extensive experiments show that PMDD matches or outperforms data-free few-step baselines at every multistep budget, reaches FID 1.99 on ImageNet 64x64 in two steps, below its 512-NFE teacher (2.32), and achieves FID 7.76 on zero-shot COCO 512x512, while requiring 4-5x less training compute than data-free DMDv2 to support three inference budgets.

</details>


### [56] [Agronomic Characterization and Time-Series UAV Phenotyping to Support Field Pea Breeding in Iowa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19154)
*Yanben Shen,Joscif Raigne,Asheesh Singh,Arti Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 评估17份引种材料和7个加拿大品种的农艺与产量表现，并利用无人机时序冠层性状进行高通量表型分析，发现冠层面积是与产量最相关的性状，可加速中西部豌豆育种。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紫花豌豆在美国中西部（尤其是爱荷华州）种植体系中有潜在价值，但缺乏对多样化种质资源的适应性和产量评价，也缺乏高效的表型选择手段来支撑当地育种。

Method: 在6个站点-年份下评价17份PI引种材料与7个加拿大品种的农艺性状和籽粒产量；提取无人机（UAV）时序冠层性状，并分析其与多个农艺性状及产量的相关性，同时比较遗传力与变异系数等选择可靠性指标。

Result: PI引种材料在物候和冠层性状上表现出遗传多样性，成熟期在75–95 DAP之间；加拿大优良品种在爱荷华条件下产量潜力高，两年平均1961 kg/ha。在无人机衍生的冠层特征、植被指数及人工测量性状中，时序冠层面积与产量相关性最高（r = 0.63），且选择可靠性优于产量本身（H2 = 0.87，CV = 13.46%）。

Conclusion: 研究识别出可用于豌豆育种的有用无人机衍生性状，证明整合多样种质、优良商业品种与时序无人机表型分析的价值；将引种材料的遗传变异与高产商业品种的产量潜力结合，可为中西部培育本地适应、高产紫花豌豆品种提供育种材料与表型策略。

Abstract: Field pea has gained interest in the Midwest U.S. and has the potential to benefit Iowa cropping systems. This study evaluated 17 accessions and 7 Canadian varieties for agronomic and seed yield performance. This study found that PI accession trials exhibit genetic diversity in phenology and canopy traits, whereas the Canadian elite varieties demonstrated great yield potential under Iowa conditions. The accession genotypes matured between 75 DAP (late June) and 95 DAP (late July) across six site-years. The top-yielding Canadian varieties averaged 1961 kg/ha across two site-years. To comprehensively assess field pea performance, this study extracted time-series Unmanned Aerial Vehicle(UAV)-derived traits, assessed UAV-derived traits, and evaluated their correlations with multiple agronomic traits and yield. The results found that UAV-derived time-series canopy area was the most yield-relevant trait (r = 0.63) among all UAV-derived canopy features, vegetation indices, and manually collected measurements. Compared with yield, UAV-derived canopy area showed higher selection reliability (H2 = 0.87, CV = 13.46%), making it suitable for phenotypic selection and accelerating Midwest field pea breeding programs. This study identifies useful UAV-derived features for pea breeding and demonstrates the value of integrating diverse germplasm, elite commercial varieties, and time-series UAV phenotyping. Combining genetic variation from the accession trial with the yield potential of high-performing commercial varieties provides valuable breeding materials and phenotyping strategies for developing locally adapted, high-yielding field pea varieties for future production in the Midwest.

</details>


### [57] [Reliability-Aware Multimodal Sentiment Prediction Under Local Modality Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20543)
*Minghao Kong,Deng Yao,Xi Zhang,Ying Gao,Jiurun Chen,Zirong Liu,Rongjie Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文研究对齐CMU-MOSEI特征上的局部缺失问题，提出可靠性感知网络RM-Net（保留时间位置、学习token表示缺失、输出受观测质量门控的有界模态强度），以连续片段双视图掩码训练，并与LoRA微调的BERT文本专家融合（融合权重仅依赖可见内容token比例）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态情感模型通常在完整话语上评估，而真实流式场景中文本、音频或视频常出现连续片段缺失，模型在局部缺失下的可靠性缺乏系统研究。

Method: 在CMU-MOSEI对齐特征上：RM-Net保留原始时间位置、以学习token表示未观测位置、输出有界且受观测质量门控的逐模态强度贡献，采用连续片段双视图掩码训练；将5个RM-Net种子与LoRA适配的BERT文本专家融合，融合权重仅由可见内容token比例决定；架构、检查点与融合选择均在验证集完成；解释性分析采用八模态子集的精确Shapley归因与位置级遮挡；另构建可审计的词级对齐流水线（不参与预测模型）。

Result: 在留出集上，融合模型干净输入macro-F1为0.655，40%混合缺失下为0.634；双视图平均较辅助头基线高0.024（95%视频簇bootstrap区间[0.003, 0.045]）。解释性上，排序靠前位置使预测类别概率下降快于随机位置（验证集AUC差0.113）。

Conclusion: RM-Net结合质量门控与双视图掩码训练可提升局部缺失下的多模态情感鲁棒性与可解释性；但因该划分在开发中被查看，上述结果并非盲测，需谨慎解读。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment models are usually evaluated on complete utterances, yet streams often lose a contiguous span of text, audio, or video. We study local missingness on aligned CMU-MOSEI features. Our reliability-aware network (RM-Net) keeps the original temporal positions, represents unobserved positions with a learned token, outputs bounded per-modality intensity contributions gated by observation quality, and is trained with dual-view masking of contiguous segments. We fuse five RM-Net seeds with a LoRA-adapted BERT text expert, using a fusion weight that depends only on the visible fraction of content tokens. Architecture, checkpoint, and fusion choices were made on the validation split. On the held-out split, the fused model reaches a macro-F1 of 0.655 on clean inputs and 0.634 under 40\% mixed missingness; its two-view average exceeds the auxiliary-head baseline by 0.024 (95\% video-cluster bootstrap interval [0.003, 0.045]). Because this split was inspected during development, these numbers are not a blind test. For explanations, exact Shapley attributions over the eight modality subsets and position-level occlusion show that top-ranked positions reduce the predicted-class probability faster than random positions (AUC gap 0.113 on validation). We also describe an auditable word-level alignment pipeline for raw clips; its outputs do not feed the predictive models.

</details>


### [58] [OLiVES: An Outdoor Low-Light Video Benchmark for Enhancement and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20501)
*Yini Li,Joanne Lin,David Blackham,David Bull,Nantheera Anantrasirichai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了OLiVES，一个面向户外动态场景的低光照视频基准数据集，同时支持低光照视频增强（LLVE）和视频目标分割（VOS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光照视频处理因高质量真值数据稀缺而研究不足。现有公开数据集存在运动多样性受限、场景覆盖不足、退化程度弱、依赖事件等辅助模态、低光输入与真值不对齐等问题，且通常只针对增强或场景理解单一任务，而二者应联合考虑。

Method: 构建OLiVES数据集：采用专业电影级相机拍摄户外动态场景，涵盖多样自然纹理、动物、遮挡、动态活动和特写场景，提供超过25,000对对齐的正常光-低光帧对及超过46,000条VOS标注，并在其上对当前最优的LLVE与VOS方法进行基准评测。

Result: OLiVES是首个使用专业电影级相机采集的户外低光照视频数据集；实验表明在其上训练可同时提升LLVE与VOS两个任务的性能。

Conclusion: OLiVES填补了户外动态低光视频数据的空白，支持增强与理解任务的联合评测，为低光照视频处理研究提供了高质量基准。

Abstract: Low-light video processing remains an underexplored problem in computer vision, primarily due to the scarcity of high-quality ground-truth data. Existing public datasets exhibit several limitations, including restricted motion diversity, limited scene coverage, weak degradations, reliance on auxiliary modalities such as event data, and misalignment between low-light inputs and ground truth. Furthermore, current datasets typically address either low-light enhancement or scene understanding, whereas these tasks should be considered jointly. We introduce \textbf{OLiVES}, a benchmark dataset for outdoor low-light videos with dynamic scenes, designed to support both low-light video enhancement (LLVE) and video object segmentation (VOS). The dataset addresses key content gaps by incorporating a diverse range of natural textures, animals, occlusions, dynamic activities, and close-up scenes. To the best of our knowledge, OLiVES is the first outdoor low-light video dataset captured using professional cinema-grade cameras. It provides over 25,000 aligned normal- and low-light frame pairs, along with over 46,000 VOS annotations. We benchmark state-of-the-art LLVE and VOS methods on OLiVES and show that training on it improves performance on both tasks. The OLiVES project page is available at: https://anonymous-OLiVES.github.io/.

</details>


### [59] [RepForcing: Representation-Space Initialization for Autoregressive Video Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20111)
*Siming Fu,Zheming Fu,Lianrui Mu,Haoyang Huang,Chaojie Gu,Ruizhe He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Representation Forcing（RepForcing），用表示空间监督学习少步因果初始化，替代中间教师轨迹回归，可提升少步视频扩散蒸馏质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将预训练视频扩散模型蒸馏为少步自回归生成器通常结合学生初始化与分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）。中间AR教师可为ODE初始化提供因果兼容目标，但仅有因果兼容目标不足以保证短采样调度下的高质量生成。作者分析直接潜回归偏好的后验均值预测，发现因果ODE初始化虽缓解模糊，但仍无法恢复细节。

Method: 提出Representation Forcing，通过表示空间监督学习少步因果初始化，而非回归中间教师轨迹。学生以teacher forcing训练，利用冻结视频扩散Transformer的归一化特征比较干净预测与训练目标。该阶段复用双向模型为AR教师训练生成的合成视频，并替代教师训练与因果ODE蒸馏，无需额外生成因果ODE轨迹；后续非对称DMD流程保持不变。

Result: 四步采样下，RepForcing在DMD前比基于中间教师的初始化更好地保持生成质量。经DMD后，四步chunk-wise模型相对已报告的因果ODE基线将VBench Total从84.04提升至84.66（+0.62分），VisionReward从6.326提升至10.074（+59.25%）。

Conclusion: 表示空间监督可作为少步因果初始化的有效替代方案，优于中间教师轨迹回归，并在结合非对称DMD后显著提升少步自回归视频生成的总体质量与奖励指标。

Abstract: Distilling pretrained video diffusion models into few-step autoregressive (AR) generators typically combines student initialization with distribution matching distillation (DMD). An intermediate AR teacher can provide causally compatible targets for ODE initialization. However, causally compatible targets alone do not ensure high-quality generation under a short sampling schedule. We analyze the posterior-mean predictions favored by direct latent regression and empirically find that causal ODE initialization alleviates blur but still leaves fine detail unresolved. To address this limitation, we propose Representation Forcing, which learns few-step causal initialization through representation-space supervision rather than intermediate-teacher trajectory regression. The student is trained with teacher forcing by comparing clean predictions and training targets through normalized features of a frozen video diffusion transformer. This phase reuses the synthetic videos generated by a bidirectional model for AR teacher training and replaces both teacher training and causal ODE distillation, without generating additional causal ODE trajectories. The subsequent asymmetric DMD procedure remains unchanged. Empirical results show that, at four sampling steps, RepForcing better preserves generation quality than intermediate-teacher-based initialization before DMD. After DMD, our four-step chunk-wise model improves VBench Total from 84.04 to 84.66 (+0.62 points) and VisionReward from 6.326 to 10.074 (+59.25%) over the reported causal-ODE-based baseline.

</details>


### [60] [Quantifying Annotation Agreement in Object Detection with Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19912)
*Kirill Prokhorov,Alexandr A. Kalinin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于最优传输（OT）的目标检测标注一致性度量，替代传统二值化重叠指标，可捕捉分级一致性变化并区分阈值化指标得分相同的标注对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型验证依赖被视为真值的标注测试集，但标注可能含噪或存在歧义。目标检测中不同来源在目标是否存在、框位置或类别标签上可能不一致。标准一致性指标通常在计算汇总分数前对框重叠做二值化，掩盖了标注集间不一致的程度与结构。

Method: 研究一种基于最优传输（Optimal Transport, OT）的目标检测标注一致性度量：不对边界框重叠做二值化，而是对匹配与未匹配的标注框赋予分级代价；并结合理论分析与真实及合成扰动标注上的实验进行验证。

Result: OT能够捕捉一致性的分级变化，并区分那些经过阈值化的指标给出相同得分的标注对。

Conclusion: OT为目标检测提供了更丰富的一致性度量，尤其适用于包含大量小目标或歧义目标的领域；此类一致性估计可在标签含噪或冲突时支持更可靠的验证。

Abstract: Reliable validation of machine learning models is a crucial step in designing robust and trustworthy systems. Yet validation relies on annotated test sets treated as ground truth, even though annotations can be noisy or ambiguous. In object detection, sources may disagree on object presence, box location, or class label. Standard agreement metrics usually binarize box overlap before computing a summary score, obscuring the degree and structure of disagreement between annotation sets. Here, we study an Optimal Transport (OT)-based metric for measuring agreement between object detection annotations. Instead of binarizing bounding box overlap, this metric assigns graded costs to matched and unmatched labeled boxes. Through theoretical analysis and experiments on real and synthetically perturbed annotations, we show that OT captures graded changes in agreement and distinguishes annotation pairs that thresholded metrics score identically. Our results suggest that OT provides a richer measure of annotation agreement in object detection, especially for domains with many small or ambiguous objects. Such agreement estimates can support more reliable validation when labels are noisy or conflicting.

</details>


### [61] [CoDy: Codec Dynamics as Complementary Priors for Unsupervised Temporal Action Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19880)
*Tianyu Yan,Qingyi Yang,Qin Yang,Xufei Guo,Yuan Tang,Rui Wang,Yixue Hao,Long Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoDy，一个利用视频编解码器动态（GOP量化模式、运动矢量与残差）作为视觉补充信息的无监督时序动作定位框架，通过置信度感知共识聚类生成伪标签，并将编解码动态转为类别无关边界先验指导边界学习与提案精化，在THUMOS14和ActivityNet v1.3上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督时序动作定位方法仅从RGB与光流表征推导监督信号，导致类别分配噪声大、时序边界不准确；作者发现编解码器中的GOP量化模式保留类别判别性动态，且运动矢量与残差在动作转换处响应显著，这一特性此前被忽视。

Method: CoDy融合编解码增强视图与RGB、光流视图，采用置信度感知共识聚类生成可靠软伪标签；将编解码动态转化为类别无关的边界先验，用于监督视觉边界学习，并指导提案初始化与迭代精化。编解码分支仅在训练阶段使用，推理时不引入额外编解码端计算。

Result: 在THUMOS14与ActivityNet v1.3上的大量实验表明，CoDy持续优于现有无监督时序动作定位方法，达到当前最优的定位性能。

Conclusion: 编解码信号可作为有效的无标注先验，用于联合推断"发生何种动作"与"何时发生"，为无监督时序动作定位提供了新的信息维度与解决方案。

Abstract: Unsupervised temporal action localization (UTAL) aims to identify action categories and localize their temporal boundaries without annotation supervision. Existing methods typically derive supervision for both tasks solely from RGB and optical-flow representations, often resulting in noisy category assignments and inaccurate temporal boundaries. In this work, we reveal an overlooked property of video codecs: group-of-pictures quantization patterns preserve category-discriminative dynamics, while motion-vector and residual variations exhibit pronounced responses around action transitions. Based on this insight, we propose CoDy, a codec dynamics-guided framework that exploits the codec as complementary information to the visual view for UTAL. CoDy integrates a codec-enhanced view with RGB and flow through confidence-aware consensus clustering, producing reliable soft pseudo-labels. It further converts codec dynamics into a class-agnostic boundary prior, which supervises visual boundary learning and guides proposal initialization and iterative refinement. The codec branches are only used during the training stage and introduce no extra codec-side computation at inference. Extensive experiments on THUMOS14 and ActivityNet v1.3 demonstrate that CoDy consistently outperforms existing UTAL methods and achieves state-of-the-art localization performance. These results establish codec signals as effective annotation-free priors for jointly inferring which actions occur and when they occur.

</details>


### [62] [A Valid Watermark Is Not a Valid Identity: Spatial-Spectral Adversarial Face Evasion with Watermark Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20278)
*Jefferson David Rodriguez Chivata,Davide Ghiani,Simone Maurizio La Cava,Marco Micheletto,Giulia Orrù,Gian Luca Marcialis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在黑盒水印设置下、无需访问水印组件的"保水印人脸逃逸攻击"（PGD-L2），通过空间与频谱保持项引导对抗扰动，在人脸识别中被误判的同时保持水印可恢复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人脸水印可支持生物识别流程的可追溯性与认证，但仅靠来源认证或篡改检测无法保证生物识别身份本身的可靠性。作者旨在揭示"水印有效但生物识别不可靠"这一结构性缺口。

Method: 提出 PGD-L2 攻击，在无监督黑盒水印条件下工作：以对抗扰动降低人脸识别相似度，同时引入空间保持项和频谱保持项，限制像素级失真与高频变化，从而避免破坏隐藏的水印线索。评测使用两类水印方案、两个人脸识别模型和两个数据集。

Result: 在操作阈值下逃逸率超过 92%；水印恢复率对 StegaFormer 保持 93% 以上，对 FaceSigns 保持 99.7% 以上；在 epsilon_2=2.0 时视觉质量 PSNR 高于 47dB。

Conclusion: 结果暴露了基于水印的人脸认证的结构性缺陷：人脸图像可以同时保持水印有效和视觉保真，却在生物识别层面变得不可靠。

Abstract: Facial image watermarking can support traceability and certification in biometric pipelines, but provenance or manipulation detection alone does not guarantee reliable biometric identity. We introduce a watermark-preserving face evasion attack that targets this gap under a black-box watermarking setting, without access to the watermarking components. The proposed PGD-L_2 attack guides adversarial perturbations with spatial and spectral preservation terms to reduce face-recognition similarity while limiting pixel-level distortion and high-frequency changes that may interfere with hidden watermark cues. Evaluated on controlled facial images with two watermarking schemes, two face-recognition models, and two datasets, the attack achieves evasion rates above 92% at operational thresholds while preserving watermark recovery above 93% for StegaFormer and above 99.7% for FaceSigns, with visual quality above 47dB PSNR at epsilon_2=2.0. These results expose a structural gap in watermark-based facial certification: a face image can remain watermark-valid and visually faithful while becoming biometrically unreliable.

</details>


### [63] [Attention-Based Spatio-Temporal Approach for Fire and Smoke Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20141)
*Alireza Mirrashid,Mohammad Khoshbin,Ali Atghaei,Hassan Shahbazi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种结合空间与时间特征的三部分级联式烟雾与火焰检测方法，并引入Transformer模块提升精度，可用于户外场景下的工作场所安全监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 烟雾与火焰是各类工业场所的重要威胁。常用的红外面热传感器与烟雾传感器无法用于户外场景，因此需要基于视觉的检测方法。同时，烟雾与火焰检测本质上是时空（spatiotemporal）问题，必须采用时空解决方案。

Method: 提出一种同时利用空间特征与时间特征的烟雾和火焰检测方法，模型由三个主要部分组成，各部分逐级降低前一部分的误差，以获得鲁棒的最终性能；并引入Transformer模块以提高模型精度。

Result: 实验结果显示出所提方法在解决烟雾与火焰检测问题上的良好性能，表明该框架可有效应对该类时空检测任务。

Conclusion: 该方法可作为提升工作场所安全性的有效手段，适用于户外烟雾与火焰检测场景。

Abstract: In various industries, smoke and fire are two of the most important threats in the workplace. One of the common methods for detecting smoke and fire is the use of infrared thermal and smoke sensors, which cannot be used in outdoor applications. Therefore, the use of vision-based methods seems necessary. The problem of smoke and fire detection is spatiotemporal and requires spatiotemporal solutions. This paper presents a method that uses spatial features along with temporal-based features to detect smoke and fire in the scene. It consists of three main parts; the task of each part is to reduce the error of the previous part so that the final model has a robust performance. This method also uses transformer modules to increase the accuracy of the model. The results of our model show the proper performance of the proposed approach in solving the problem of smoke and fire detection and can be used to increase workplace safety.

</details>


### [64] [InfiniSplat v2: Analyzing and Improving Feed-Forward 3DGS from Any Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20341)
*Yongzhen Hu,Hao Yu,Jiawei Wang,Xianming Zeng,Jianyun Xu,Xingliang Liu,Ruizhen Hu,Xiaowei Zhou,Hujun Bao,Sida Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 InfiniSplat v2，通过冻结几何分支解耦几何与属性预测、训练时用真值相机位姿对齐高斯、以及大角度目标视角监督，显著提升前馈高斯重建在新视角合成中的渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈高斯重建可直接从输入图像构建 3D 场景用于新视角渲染，但从预测几何中学习高斯属性、并在大视角变化下实现高质量渲染仍具挑战性，论文旨在分析高斯属性学习的局限性并加以改进。

Method: 引入 InfiniSplat v2，从架构与训练两方面改进：以冻结的几何分支解耦几何预测与属性预测；训练阶段利用真值相机位姿对齐预测高斯；采用大角度目标视角监督。

Result: 在六个数据集上，同一训练模型的前馈输出在姿态条件与无姿态设定下均取得所评估方法中最优的平均 LPIPS 与 DISTS；姿态条件设定下，平均 LPIPS 与 DISTS 较最强基线分别降低 25.8% 与 32.9%。此外发现仅靠图像重建监督学习到的透明度会抑制几何不一致的点。

Conclusion: 解耦几何与属性学习并结合位姿对齐和大角度监督，可有效提升前馈高斯重建的渲染质量，且重建监督下的透明度学习具备抑制几何不一致点的作用。

Abstract: Feed-forward Gaussian reconstruction builds 3D scenes directly from input images for novel view rendering, but learning Gaussian attributes from predicted geometry for high-quality rendering under large viewpoint changes remains challenging. We analyze limitations in Gaussian attribute learning and introduce InfiniSplat v2, which improves rendering quality through architectural and training changes. Our method decouples geometry and attribute prediction with a frozen geometry branch, aligns predicted Gaussians using ground-truth camera poses during training, and adopts large-angle target view supervision. Across six datasets, the feed-forward outputs of the same trained model achieve state-of-the-art mean LPIPS and DISTS among the evaluated methods in both pose-conditioned and pose-free settings. In the pose-conditioned setting, mean LPIPS and DISTS decrease by 25.8% and 32.9%, respectively, compared with the strongest evaluated baselines. We further find that opacity learned with only image reconstruction supervision suppresses geometrically inconsistent points. Project page: https://zju3dv.github.io/InfiniSplat2/

</details>


### [65] [Read, Parse, Describe: Unified Document Parsing with Visual Element Description Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20335)
*Yuheng Chen,Yufan Chen,Zhuojun Cai,Ruiping Liu,Junwei Zheng,Jiale Wei,Di Wen,Weijia Fan,Kunyu Peng,Jiaming Zhang,Rainer Stiefelhagen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出统一文档解析数据集 Read-Parse-Describe（RPD），要求模型对页面图像输出含边界框、语义标签、阅读顺序、文本内容与视觉元素替代文本的有序布局元素列表；并设计即插即用的空间感知增强器，实验表明现有端到端 VLM 与流水线解析器难以同时恢复阅读顺序与视觉语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档理解系统通常按布局分析、光学文本识别、阅读顺序预测和图像描述等独立组件分别评测，这种割裂导致科学图表、图注与视觉语义之间联系薄弱，也无法衡量模型能否生成结构忠实且语义可访问的单一页面级解析结果。

Method: 构建统一数据集 RPD：给定页面图像，模型需输出有序布局元素列表，包含边界框、语义标签、阅读顺序索引、识别文本内容，以及视觉元素的替代文本描述。数据集含 2,402 篇文档、18,163 个连续页面、125,218 个布局元素和 11,338 个视觉实例。同时提出即插即用的空间感知增强器，复用多层视觉特征并向视觉-语言 token 注入网格感知空间引导，以实现布局感知生成。

Result: 在端到端 VLM 与流水线解析器上的实验显示，现有系统在同时恢复阅读顺序与视觉语义方面仍存在困难，而所提增强模型取得了较强的联合性能。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了从孤立的文档解析模块走向联合读取、解析与描述复杂文档的统一模型的必要性，数据集与代码将公开于 https://github.com/yufanchen96/RPD。

Abstract: Document understanding systems are typically evaluated as separate components via layout analysis, optical text recognition, reading-order prediction, and image captioning. This separation leaves scientific figures, captions, and visual semantics weakly connected, and fails to measure whether a model can produce a single page-level parse that is both structurally faithful and semantically accessible. We introduce Read-Parse-Describe, a unified dataset for document parsing with visual element description generation. Given a page image, models are required to output an ordered list of layout elements containing bounding boxes, semantic labels, reading-order indices, recognized textual content, and alternative text descriptions for visual elements. The benchmark is built from 2,402 documents, comprising 18,163 continuous pages, 125,218 layout elements, and 11,338 visual instances. Besides, we further propose a plug-and-play space-awareness enhancer that reuses multi-layer visual features and injects grid-aware spatial guidance into vision-language tokens for layout-aware generation. Experiments across end-to-end VLMs and pipeline parsers show that existing systems still struggle to jointly recover reading order and visual semantics, while our enhanced model achieves strong joint performance. These results highlight the need to move beyond isolated document parsing modules toward unified models that jointly read, parse, and describe complex documents. The new dataset and code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/yufanchen96/RPD

</details>


### [66] [Beyond Static Cues: Unified Dynamic Detection of MLLM Backdoors via Dual-Flow Trajectory Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20319)
*Yinzhen Huang,Jiahe Xie,Yitong Sun,Ruochen Zhang,Xingxing Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 EVO-Detect，将多模态大模型后门检测从静态观测转向动态输出演化，通过监控早期解码阶段的 Attention Flow 与 Logits Flow 两条通道构建样本级指标，在多种攻击族上显著优于 BYE、TCAP 等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 广泛部署于安全关键领域，但易受后门攻击。现有防御从视觉补丁线索发展到无监督内部注意力统计，仍受限于静态快照与攻击特定启发式；多模态触发器设计空间巨大（文本注入、亚感知噪声、跨模态协同触发），未知攻击类型下这些刚性先验易被绕过。

Method: 将诊断范式由静态观测转为动态输出演化。观察到成功攻击必然劫持解码轨迹：生成中扭曲内部模态贡献，同时在输入扰动下维持异常输出不变性。据此提出 EVO-Detect 统一检测框架，在早期解码步监控内部信号动态演化：注意力分支（Attention Flow）度量图文注意力分配随早期生成的演化；logits 分支（Logits Flow）通过干预式消融量化输出分布对模态扰动的敏感性。将轨迹转为紧凑样本级指标，并以负对照签名归一化，降低自然扰动被误判为后门证据的概率。

Result: 大量评估显示，该方法在多种攻击族上显著优于 BYE、TCAP 等最先进基线。

Conclusion: 以动态解码轨迹（注意力流与 logits 流）为核心的检测范式可统一应对文本、视觉及跨模态后门，克服静态先验与攻击特定启发式的局限，为 MLLM 后门防御提供更鲁棒的方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in safety-critical domains, yet their susceptibility to backdoor attacks remains a formidable challenge. While existing defenses have evolved from visual-centric patch cues to unsupervised internal attention statistics, they are fundamentally constrained by a reliance on static snapshots and attack-specific heuristics. Given the intricate interplay between dual modalities, the vast design space for multimodal triggers allows diverse threats, such as textual injections, sub-perceptual noises, and coordinated cross-modal triggers, to easily circumvent such rigid priors, particularly when attack types are unknown in real-world applications. In this paper, we rethink backdoor detection by shifting the diagnostic paradigm from static observations to dynamic output evolution. We observe that a successful attack inevitably hijacks the model's decoding trajectory, skewing internal modal contributions during generation while maintaining an abnormal output invariance despite input perturbations. To capture this behavior, we propose EVO-Detect, a unified detection framework that monitors the dynamic evolution of internal signals during early decoding steps through two complementary channels: Attention Flow and Logits Flow. The attention branch measures how image-text attention allocation evolves during early generation, whereas the logits branch quantifies the sensitivity of the output distribution to modal perturbations through interventional ablations. We then convert these trajectories into compact sample-level metrics and normalize them against negative-control signatures so that natural perturbations are less likely to be mistaken for backdoor evidence. Extensive evaluations show that our approach substantially outperforms state-of-the-art baselines such as BYE and TCAP across diverse attack families.

</details>


### [67] [Event Cameras versus Frame Cameras for Synthetic-Aperture Imaging: A Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20309)
*Jakob Hinterholzer,Mohamed Youssef,Oliver Bimber*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过仿真对比事件相机与帧相机在合成孔径成像（SAI）中去遮挡的性能，发现事件相机在高速明亮场景下优势显著，但在低速或低照度场景下无明显优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成孔径成像（SAI）可沿相机路径整合多视角以去除树叶等遮挡物，但此前主要基于帧相机。作者希望探究事件相机是否对SAI具有优势。

Method: 使用密集渲染的Blender序列，分别构建符合EMVA 1288噪声模型的工业全局快门相机的帧图像，以及按Sony IMX636参数化的v2e事件流。帧图像用现有航空光学切片渲染器整合；事件则采用新路径，将每个事件投影到其自身插值位姿下的焦平面，无需分箱成帧。在三次主要运行及额外暗光高速测试中比较两种传感器。

Result: 25 m/s明亮场景下帧相机仅记录三帧且无法去遮挡，事件相机可重建目标（即使事件率控制器丢弃大部分事件）；5 lux、25 m/s时事件相机也无法分辨目标；5 lux、5 m/s时两者重建质量相近。高速下受限事件流与三帧数据量相同时仍显示目标；1 m/s下在匹配数据预算时事件相机无重建质量优势。事件相机数据率取决于场景，在最快最亮、最需要时最高。

Conclusion: 事件相机在高速明亮场景下对SAI去遮挡具有优势，但在低速或低照度场景下与帧相机相比无明显优势；其数据率随场景变化且在最需要的场景中最高。

Abstract: Synthetic-aperture imaging (SAI) removes occluders such as foliage by integrating many views of a scene along a camera path. It has so far been used mainly with frame cameras. We ask whether an event camera has advantages for SAI, and answer the question in simulation, where both sensors see exactly the same scene and the same camera movement. A densely rendered Blender sequence is turned into frames with an EMVA~1288 noise model of an industrial global-shutter camera, and into an event stream with the v2e simulator parameterized like a Sony IMX636. The frames are integrated with the existing airborne optical sectioning renderer. For the events we add a new integration path that projects every single event onto the focal plane at its own interpolated pose, so no events have to be binned into frames. We compare the two sensors in three main runs and an additional dark high-speed test. In a bright scene at 25,m/s the frame camera records only three images on the path and cannot remove the occluder, while the event camera reconstructs the target, even after an event-rate controller drops most of its events to fit the camera link. At the same speed in a 5,lux scene the event camera no longer resolves the target either. In a 5,lux scene at 5,m/s both sensors reconstruct the target at similar quality. We also compare the data one traversal of the aperture costs. At high speed the capped event stream still shows the target at the same data volume as the three frames. At 1,m/s we compare both cameras at two matched data budgets, and there the event camera has no advantage over the frame camera in reconstruction quality. The data rate of the event camera depends on the scene and is highest exactly in the fast bright scenes where it is needed most.

</details>


### [68] [Contrastive uncertainty learning for robust multi-structure segmentation in lower-pelvic MR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20251)
*Bilal Sidiqi,Yipei Wang,Wen Yan,Shaheer U. Saeed,Daniel C. Alexander,Yipeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出对比不确定性学习（CUL），用预测不确定性直接指导表征学习，在3D前列腺MRI多结构分割中保持Dice不变的同时显著降低表面距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前列腺MRI解剖结构边界常存在区域模糊性；依赖全局重叠损失（如Dice）的自动分割方法对表面偏差不敏感，导致模糊区域出现较大误差。已有不确定性方法多将不确定性作为事后质量指标或预测过滤器，未用于表征学习。

Method: 提出对比不确定性学习（CUL），一个监督框架，用预测不确定性直接引导表征学习。基于Monte Carlo Dropout获得体素级不确定性，并据此构造嵌入几何：低不确定且预测正确的特征作为稳定体素被拉近，高不确定或误分类特征被主动排斥，从而防止困难边界的特征坍缩。在589例扫描的3D前列腺MRI多结构分割任务（8个解剖结构）上评估，骨干为ViT+UperNet解码器。

Result: 平均Dice为83.38%，与基线83.35%相当；8个结构中有6个的表面距离显著下降（p<0.001）。困难病例分析显示改善与退化病例间存在22倍不对称（18.8% vs 0.9%）。消融表明：移除不确定性引导的样本选择会消除移行区的改善，将对比目标替换为边界损失还会额外消除膀胱的改善。

Conclusion: 不确定性引导的表征学习是降低安全关键医学分割中极端误差风险的实用机制；不确定性引导采样与对比目标二者缺一不可。

Abstract: Boundary delineation of anatomical structures in prostate MRI is often compromised by regional ambiguity. Automated segmentation methods relying on global overlap loss functions tend to be insensitive to surface deviations, leading to significant errors in ambiguous regions. Most existing uncertainty-aware methods treat uncertainty as a post-hoc quality indicator or prediction filter. We propose Contrastive Uncertainty Learning (CUL), a supervised framework that uses predictive uncertainty to directly guide representation learning. Using Monte Carlo Dropout, CUL identifies voxel-wise uncertainty and structures the embedding geometry accordingly: low-uncertainty, correctly predicted features form stable voxels while high-uncertainty or misclassified features are actively repelled, preventing feature collapse at difficult boundaries. We evaluate CUL on a 589-scan 3D prostate MRI multi-structure segmentation task (8 anatomical structures) using a ViT with UperNet decoder architecture. Average Dice ($83.38\%$) remains comparable to the baseline ($83.35\%$), while surface distance decreases significantly ($p<0.001$) across 6 of 8 anatomical structures. A hard-case analysis reveals a $22{\times}$ asymmetry between significantly improved and degraded cases ($18.8\%$ vs $0.9\%$). Ablation studies show that removing uncertainty-guided sample selection eliminates improvements for the transition zone, while replacing the contrastive objective with a boundary loss additionally removes bladder improvements, demonstrating that both uncertainty-guided sampling and the contrastive objective are essential for the observed gains. These findings demonstrate that the proposed uncertainty-guided representation learning is a useful mechanism for reducing the risk of extreme errors in safety-critical medical segmentation

</details>


### [69] [PAWA: Panoramic Action-conditioned World Modeling for Multiple Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20240)
*Siqi Yang,Yimu Wang,Ziqi Cai,Chengxuan Zhu,Shuchen Weng,Erwin Wu,Zhaofei Yu,Kaipeng Zhang,Boxin Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PAWA，一个用于动作条件化全景多人世界建模的数据集与框架，配套双塔模型联合预测POV与全景视频，并引入二次相位玩家编码，在POV预测质量上超越现有最先进多人世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人世界模型需预测多个玩家独立动作对共享环境的影响，包括玩家视野之外的区域变化。POV视频只能记录变化进入视野后的信息，而全景视频能记录各方向环境，适合作为世界状态的中间表示。但学习该任务需要同步的POV与全景视频及动作数据，现有数据集在Minecraft中均不提供这一组合。

Method: 构建PAWA数据集，将每个玩家的POV视频与世界稳定化的全景视频及对齐动作配对；采用分布式流水线，将CPU游戏采集与GPU渲染解耦，可扩展至64个以上同时在线玩家，并支持在不重放游戏过程的情况下按新观测配置重新渲染已录制的会话。在此基础上提出双塔模型，联合预测POV与全景视频，并通过跨注意力耦合两个流，对齐共享世界坐标系中观测同一方向的token；同时引入二次相位玩家编码，在相同嵌入维度下提供比现有旋转编码更多的玩家编码。

Result: 实验表明，PAWA在POV预测质量上优于现有最先进的多人世界模型，同时还能生成高保真的全景预测。数据集与采集流水线已开源。
