Conclusion: PAWA为多人世界建模提供了可扩展的数据集与采集框架，验证了POV与全景联合建模及高效玩家编码的有效性，开源资源可支持后续研究。

Abstract: A multiplayer world model must predict how the independent actions of several players change a shared environment, including changes outside a player's current field of view. A point-of-view (POV) video captures such changes only after they enter its field of view, whereas panoramic video records the surroundings in every viewing direction and has served as an intermediate representation of the world state in recent world models. Learning this task therefore requires synchronized POV and panoramic videos of interacting players together with their actions, yet no existing dataset provides this combination in Minecraft, an established testbed in which players act independently in a shared, modifiable 3D world. We present PAWA, a dataset and framework for action-conditioned panoramic multiplayer world modeling. The dataset pairs each player's POV video with world-stabilized panoramic video and aligned actions. Its distributed pipeline decouples CPU gameplay capture from GPU rendering, scales beyond 64 simultaneous players, and re-renders each recorded session under new observation configurations without replaying the gameplay. On this dataset, we develop a dual-tower model that jointly predicts POV and panoramic video and couples the two streams through cross-attention that aligns tokens observing the same direction in a shared world frame. We further introduce a quadratic-phase player encoding that provides many more player codes than existing rotary codes at the same embedding dimension. Experiments show that PAWA outperforms state-of-the-art multiplayer world models in POV prediction quality while also producing high-fidelity panoramic predictions. The dataset and collection pipeline are open source to support further research on multiplayer world models.

</details>


### [70] [Continual pretraining Prostate MRI ViT Encoder for task alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20217)
*Bilal A. Sidiqi,Yipei Wang,Shaheer U. Saeed,Shiqi Huang,Daniel C. Alexander,Yipeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对MRI基础模型预训练与下游任务对齐不足的问题，探索在前列腺分割任务上用LoRA对预训练ViT编码器进行持续预训练，并用CKA进行逐层表征分析。结果显示6个下游任务中3个性能提升、其余相当，且分割任务上持续预训练特征与微调编码器更相似，与性能提升相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MRI基础模型研究多聚焦于预训练策略及其在多下游任务上的表现，较少关注预训练目标与下游目标之间的任务对齐。语言模型中两阶段天然对齐，而视觉应用中这种对齐并不明显，因此需要探索增强对齐的方法。

Method: 在前列腺分割任务上，使用低秩适配（LoRA）对预训练ViT编码器进行持续预训练，以增强任务对齐；同时采用中心核对齐（CKA）进行逐层表征分析，评估表征偏移及其与任务结果的相关性。

Result: 实验表明，在6个下游任务中有3个性能提升，其余任务结果相当。特征分析显示，对于分割任务，持续预训练编码器特征比原始预训练编码器更接近微调编码器，且该相似性与观察到的性能提升相关。

Conclusion: 持续预训练结合LoRA可改善任务对齐并提升部分下游性能，CKA表征分析为理解预训练与微调之间的表征偏移及其与任务表现的关系提供了有效手段。

Abstract: Building foundation models for MRI has emerged as a promising direction for advancing medical image analysis and improving accessibility of task specific models. Existing approaches primarily focus on effective pretraining strategies and evaluating their performance across diverse downstream tasks. While these methods show performance gains, limited attention has been given to understanding task alignment between pretraining and downstream objectives. Unlike language models, where the two stages are naturally aligned, such alignment is less evident in vision based applications. Motivated by this gap, we explore continual pretraining. Specifically, continually pretraining a pretrained ViT encoder using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) on a prostate segmentation task to enhance task alignment and downstream performance. We further perform layer-wise representational analysis using Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) to assess representational shifts and their correlation with task outcomes. Our experiments show improved performance in 3 of 6 downstream tasks and comparable results in the others. Our Feature analysis reveals that, for segmentation tasks, continually pretrained encoder features exhibit greater similarity to those of the finetuned encoder than the original pretrained encoder, correlating with observed performance gains.

</details>


### [71] [BluePixel: Toward Native 4K Agentic Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19882)
*Shanyan Guan,Shiyu Li,Xin Li,Zhizhou Chen,Yunzhe Xu,Yuehao Liu,Ruixue Shen,Shiju Zhang,Min Jin,Qiang Liu,Junbo Liu,Penggang Shi,Sheng Sun,Xiao Wang,Yong Zhang,Zhongang Qi,Wei Li,Chao Ma,Ying Tai,Yanhao Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BluePixel 是首个公开完整训练配方的原生 4K 指令驱动图像编辑系统，由 4K 编辑模型与 BluePixel Agent 智能体框架组成，实现单卡 8.7 秒完成 4K 编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生 4K 指令驱动图像编辑虽已实现，但其训练方法未公开，从头预训练超出多数团队的能力范围；且任务本身存在三重难点：单一端到端编辑器无法做精确参数化调整、难以匹敌专用专家模型、也无法理解用户实际写出的模糊指令。作者据此主张 4K 编辑是系统问题而非单模型问题。

Method: 1) 渐进式 VAE 适配，将 1K 预训练编辑器转为原生 4K，使 4K 训练成本与 1K 相当；2) 通过持续学习吸收长尾垂类编辑技能；3) 用强化学习精调美学—保真度权衡；4) 通过源锚定目标投影（SATP）蒸馏，将 50 步去噪压缩至 4 步；5) 智能体框架 BluePixel Agent 将用户指令分解为专家操作与通用操作，用与训练同源的奖励模型验证每次结果，并在有界次数内重试。

Result: SATP 蒸馏后单张昆仑芯 P800 XPU 上 8.7 秒完成 4K 编辑，同时保持任务成功率与背景一致性。在含 3,305 个原生 4K 案例的 VINS-4KEval++ 上，BluePixel 在七个模型中排名第二，非编辑区域一致性仅次于 GPT Image 2。

Conclusion: 将 4K 编辑视为系统级问题、以「编辑模型 + 智能体框架」耦合的设计可行且高效：既公开了完整训练配方，又在原生 4K 基准上取得领先表现，验证了低成本 4K 训练与可控化编辑的实践路径。

Abstract: Instruction-driven image editing has reached native 4K, yet how such editors are trained remains undisclosed, and pretraining one from scratch is beyond most teams. The task itself compounds the difficulty: a single end-to-end editor cannot make precise parametric adjustments, match specialized experts, or interpret the vague instructions users actually write. We argue that 4K editing is a system problem rather than a single-model problem, and present BluePixel, which couples a native-4K editing model with an agent harness; the complete training recipe is disclosed in this paper. The model makes 4K training practical: it turns a 1K-pretrained editor into a native-4K one through progressive VAE adaptation that keeps 4K training as affordable as 1K, absorbs a long tail of vertical editing skills through continual learning, and refines the aesthetic-fidelity trade-off with reinforcement learning. Distillation via source-anchored target projection (SATP) then compresses 50 denoising steps to 4, completing a 4K edit in 8.7 s on a single Kunlunxin P800 XPU while preserving task success rate and background consistency. The harness, BluePixel Agent, makes editing controllable: it decomposes user instructions into specialist and general operations, verifies every result with the same reward model that guided training, and retries within bounded attempts. On VINS-4KEval++ (3,305 native-4K cases), BluePixel ranks second among seven models, behind only GPT Image 2 in non-edited-region consistency.

</details>


### [72] [Quantifying privacy risks in foundation model representations of brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20494)
*Imen Bajar,Héber Hwang Arcolezi,Antoine Boutet,Carole Frindel,Mohamed Maouche*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究冻结的脑MRI预训练表征在隐私保护方面的风险，发现即便未使用身份识别目标训练，释放的嵌入仍可被用于高精度患者重识别（MIRIAD上rank-1达99%）和敏感属性推断（记录性别预测平衡准确率达90.9%–95.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练表征被日益用于在不暴露原始医疗影像的前提下共享和复用信息，但这些表征究竟保留了多少患者特异信息尚不清楚。作者旨在系统评估脑MRI预训练嵌入的隐私泄露程度。

Method: 选取五个冻结的预训练编码器，覆盖不同架构、预训练目标和训练域，在OASIS-2和MIRIAD两个纵向T1加权数据集上评估。采用访问释放嵌入、对应公开编码器及辅助MRI扫描的攻击者模型，开展患者关联（patient linkage）和敏感属性推断两类攻击实验。

Result: 在评估的各表征中观察到大量受试者特异信息：最高风险的embedding在MIRIAD上rank-1患者关联率达99%，在OASIS-2上超过90%；同时释放的嵌入保留了人口统计学属性信息，记录性别预测在OASIS-2上平衡准确率达90.9%，在MIRIAD上达95.5%。

Conclusion: 仅用学习到的嵌入替换原始脑MRI本身并不能消除患者关联或属性推断风险，因此单靠嵌入化不足以保障匿名性，需要额外的隐私保护措施。

Abstract: Pretrained representations are increasingly used to share and reuse information from medical images without exposing the original scans, but it is unclear how much patient-specific information these representations retain. We investigate this question for brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) by evaluating five frozen pretrained encoders spanning different architectures, pretraining objectives, and training domains on two longitudinal T1-weighted datasets, OASIS-2 and MIRIAD. Under an attacker model with access to released embeddings, the corresponding public encoder, and auxiliary MRI scans, we evaluate patient linkage and sensitive-attribute inference. Across the evaluated representations, we observe substantial subject-specific information: rank-1 patient linkage reaches up to 99\% on MIRIAD and exceeds 90\% on OASIS-2 for the highest-risk representation, despite the absence of an identification objective during encoder training. We further show that the released embeddings retain information about a demographic attribute, with recorded-sex prediction reaching balanced accuracy of 90.9\% on OASIS-2 and 95.5\% on MIRIAD. Overall, our results demonstrate that replacing raw brain MRI with learned embeddings does not, by itself, eliminate patient-linkage or attribute-inference risk, and is therefore insufficient on its own to establish anonymity.

</details>


### [73] [Infant Fingerprints: Quality Assessment & Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20432)
*João L. H. Dall Agnol,Dalcimar Casanova,Rodrigo Minetto,Luiz F. P. Southier,Marcelo Teixeira,Marcelo Filipak,Anil K. Jain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对婴幼儿指纹识别中因生理特征与采集难题导致的质量评估标准失配问题，在覆盖新生儿至三个月六个年龄组的纵向数据集上评估了十种指纹质量评估方法，并提出年龄特异性参数调优流程。结果表明，调优后的参数配置在多数方法与年龄组中优于默认配置，可提升质量引导的图库筛选效果与识别可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 婴幼儿指纹识别受两类因素阻碍：一是生理特征，如脊线宽度与间距较小；二是采集挑战，包括非自主运动、皮肤残留和指尖压力不均。这些因素使原本为成人设计的指纹质量标准失效，因为其假设的特征分布不适用于婴幼儿。因此需要针对婴幼儿群体重新评估与校准现有质量评估方法。

Method: 研究在跨六个年龄组（新生儿至三个月）的纵向数据集上评估十种指纹质量评估方法，并设计参数调优流程以揭示年龄特异性指纹特征，从而对具有领域依赖假设的方法进行重新校准。随后通过识别实验比较默认配置与调优配置，评估质量引导的图库选择的有效性，并进一步考察模板融合策略与潜指纹识别技术。

Result: 调优参数集在大多数方法和年龄组中优于默认配置，说明对现有框架进行针对性重校准可提升图库效用。同时，通过模板融合策略与潜指纹识别技术，识别出表现最佳方法所能达到的最优匹配率。

Conclusion: 对现有指纹质量评估框架进行年龄特异性参数重校准，可有效改善婴幼儿指纹的图库效用，并推动更可靠的婴幼儿指纹识别。

Abstract: Infant fingerprint recognition is hindered by a combination of physiological characteristics, such as small ridge width and spacing, and acquisition challenges that include involuntary motion, skin residue, and uneven fingertip pressure. These factors undermine fingerprint quality standards originally designed for adults, which assume feature distributions that do not hold for infants. To address this gap, this paper evaluates ten fingerprint quality assessment methods on a longitudinal dataset that spans six age groups from newborns to three months, alongside a parameter tuning routine that uncovers age-specific fingerprint characteristics to recalibrate methods with domain-dependent assumptions. Both default and tuned configurations are compared through recognition experiments assessing the effectiveness of quality-guided gallery selection. Template-fusion strategies and latent fingerprint recognition technologies are further examined to identify the best possible matching rates achievable by the top-performing method. Results show that tuned parameter sets outperform default configurations for the majority of methods and age groups, indicating that targeted recalibration of existing frameworks improves gallery utility and contributes to more reliable infant fingerprint recognition.

</details>


### [74] [Dataset-Level Regularization for Joint-Embedding Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20158)
*Konstantinos Almpanakis,Anna Kreshuk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"批外正则化"(out-of-batch regularization)：在CPU内存中维护数据集级状态，用全局构造的逐实例目标替代小批量估计，使正则化几何不依赖批大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督联合嵌入学习通过正则化防止表示坍缩，但多数方法仅在当前小批量内计算正则化，导致小批量时分布估计偏差大、正则化几何随批大小波动。

Method: 在CPU内存中维护数据集级状态，形成"锚点"(anchors)，在小批量训练时使用全局构造的逐实例目标进行正则化；将该策略分别应用于对比式方法SimCLR与非对比式方法SIGReg。

Result: 在自然图像与生物医学数据集上，小批量下锚点比传统小批量估计更准确地逼近全数据集正则化梯度；在图像分类、受控不平衡下的少数类性能、实例检索与持续自监督预训练任务上均相对批内目标有一致提升，且批量越小增益越大。

Conclusion: 数据集级正则化可与小批量优化解耦，批大小由此成为纯优化选择，不再影响正则化器的几何形态。

Abstract: Self-supervised joint embedding learning aligns representations of augmented views while regularizing their distribution to prevent collapse. Regularization aims to shape the overall representation distribution, but most joint embedding methods restrict its computation to the active minibatch. We propose out-of-batch regularization, which maintains a dataset-level state in CPU memory and uses globally constructed per-instance targets during minibatch training. Evaluated on natural images and biomedical datasets, these anchors approximate the full-dataset regularization gradient more faithfully than conventional minibatch estimation at small batch sizes. Applications to contrastive (SimCLR) and non-contrastive (SIGReg) approaches show consistent improvement over the corresponding in-batch objectives for image classification, minority-class performance under controlled imbalance, instance retrieval, and continued self-supervised pretraining, with the largest gains at small batch sizes. These results suggest that dataset-level regularization can be decoupled from minibatch optimization, making batch size an optimization choice that does not affect the geometry of the regularizer.

</details>


### [75] [OmniPoser: Flexible Human Motion Recovery in the Wild with Masked Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19857)
*Minghao Liu,Tutian Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniPoser：一种统一框架，可从异构稀疏可穿戴传感器输入的任意子集恢复全身SMPL运动，单步ODE推理，性能媲美SOTA且超过700 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏可穿戴传感器恢复3D人体运动面临真实跟踪信号固有的噪声、遮挡和不稳定性。现有方法受限于固定传感器配置；基于扩散模型的方法计算开销大，难以实时部署。因此需要一个能适应任意异构传感器组合、且推理高效的通用框架。

Method: 提出跨模态掩码机制与掩码条件编码器（Masked Conditioning Encoder），使单一架构能通过掩码设计融合多种传感器配置（3D关键点、稀疏IMU旋转、头戴式6-DoF位姿及其任意子集）。采用解耦双流生成骨干，通过条件流匹配（Conditional Flow Matching）生成完整运动，推理时仅需一次ODE积分步骤。

Result: 在三种设置、七个基准（包括野外数据集Nymeria和Ego-Exo4D）上，OmniPoser性能与SOTA方法相当，同时运行速度超过700 FPS。

Conclusion: OmniPoser通过跨模态掩码与条件流匹配的结合，实现了对异构稀疏传感器输入的通用、实时全身运动恢复，兼顾灵活性与高效性。

Abstract: Recovering 3D human motion from sparse wearable sensors is challenging due to the inherent noise, occlusion, and instability of real-world tracking signals. Existing approaches are restricted to fixed sensor configurations and, when built on diffusion models, are computationally prohibitive for real-time deployment. We propose OmniPoser, a universal framework that recovers full-body SMPL motion from a flexible combination of heterogeneous inputs, such as 3D keypoints, sparse IMU rotations, head-mounted 6-DoF poses, or any subset thereof, within a single model. OmniPoser introduces a cross-modal masking mechanism paired with a Masked Conditioning Encoder, enabling one architecture to incorporate diverse sensor configurations through a masked design. A decoupled dual-stream generative backbone generates complete motion via Conditional Flow Matching, requiring only a single ODE integration step at inference. Across three settings and seven benchmarks, including in-the-wild Nymeria and Ego-Exo4D datasets, OmniPoser performs on par with state-of-the-art methods while running at over 700 FPS. The project page is available at https://jamesbridgewater51.github.io/omniposer-eccv/.

</details>


### [76] [VisibilitySplat: Ray-Traced Occlusion for Relighting with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19847)
*Sven Kluge,Oliver Staadt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文在3DGS逆渲染中引入显式可见性建模，通过稀疏visibility oracle提升重光照与材质-光照解耦效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准3DGS光栅化管线缺乏显式可见性建模，导致逆渲染中重光照和材质-光照解耦不佳。

Method: 在标准3DGS管线中加入稀疏visibility oracle，用BVH加速的point-based ray tracer估计高斯原语的透射率加权遮挡，稀疏查询于逆渲染目标中；着色采用PBR微表面BRDF与低分辨率HDR环境贴图。

Result: 在TensoIR合成数据集上，NVS、albedo重建和重光照性能有竞争力并优于多种NeRF、tensor和3DGS基线。消融显示显式可见性建模对重光照和材质-光照解耦贡献重要，而纯PBR监督有利于NVS外观拟合。

Conclusion: 稀疏point-based可见性查询可有效补充光栅化splatting用于逆渲染，同时保留3DGS生态的实用优势。

Abstract: We address inverse rendering with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) by introducing explicit visibility modeling for relighting as well as material and light decomposition. Our approach augments the standard rasterized 3DGS pipeline with a sparse visibility oracle that estimates transmittance-weighted occlusion for Gaussian primitives from sampled lighting directions. This oracle is implemented using a point-based ray tracer accelerated by a bounding volume hierarchy (BVH) and queried sparsely within the inverse-rendering objectives, while the original 3DGS loop is retained for training efficiency, interactive visualization, and tooling compatibility. Shading follows a physically based rendering (PBR) formulation with a principled microfacet bidirectional reflectance distribution function (BRDF) and low-resolution high-dynamic-range (HDR) environment maps. We evaluate our method on the TensoIR synthetic dataset against baselines based on neural radiance fields (NeRFs), tensor representations, and 3DGS. The results show competitive performance and improvements over several earlier baselines for novel-view synthesis (NVS), albedo reconstruction, and relighting. Ablations indicate that explicit visibility modeling is an important contributor to relighting and material-illumination disentanglement, while pure PBR supervision can favor appearance fitting in NVS. Overall, the findings suggest that sparse, point-based visibility queries effectively complement rasterized splatting for inverse rendering while preserving the practical advantages of the 3DGS ecosystem.

</details>


### [77] [Self-Conditioning Enables Deep Sequential Reasoning in Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19628)
*Zeyang Zhang,Linxiang Gao,Meiqi Gu,Xingyan Chen,Minrui Luo,Kaifeng Lyu,Tianxing He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 从电路复杂度视角揭示：标准流匹配采样器的轨迹可由常数轮并行模型评估模拟，额外采样步数无法提升常数深度主干模型的表达能力；而自条件（self-conditioning）可打破该瓶颈，使常数深度主干模型达到与空间有界图灵机等价的表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作通过图像生成来完成视觉推理任务，直觉上迭代采样器（如流匹配）的每个采样步可对应一个推理步，因而适合该任务。本文旨在检验并纠正这一直觉，探明迭代采样步数究竟能否真正增强模型的推理能力。

Method: 采用电路复杂度理论分析：在模型输出满足一定温和正则性条件下，证明标准流匹配采样器的轨迹可由常数轮顺序的并行模型评估模拟；进一步以自条件为变量，进行受控实验，在多种视觉推理任务上对比标准流匹配与自条件流匹配的准确率和推理深度，并给出理论刻画。

Result: 理论上证明标准流匹配下增加采样步数不能提升常数深度主干模型在标准Transformer复杂度模型下的表达能力；自条件流匹配在多种视觉推理任务上取得更高准确率与更深推理深度，且随采样步数增加性能持续提升；并证明带自条件时常数深度主干可达到与空间有界图灵机等价的表达能力。

Conclusion: 迭代采样步数本身并不自动等价于推理深度，关键在于采样步之间传递的信息量。由此提出视觉推理的设计原则：在采样步之间携带更多信息，使额外采样步数转化为更深的推理。

Abstract: Recent work has explored visual reasoning through image generation, where models solve visual-reasoning problems by directly generating solution images. Intuitively, iterative samplers such as flow matching are well suited to this task, since each sampling step may resemble one reasoning step. However, in this work, from a circuit complexity perspective, we show that this intuition is misleading. We prove that as long as the model outputs satisfy certain mild regularity conditions, the trajectory of the vanilla flow-matching sampler can be simulated by a constant number of sequential rounds of parallel model evaluations. Consequently, additional sampling steps cannot increase the expressive power of a constant-depth backbone model under the standard complexity-theoretic model of transformers. We then reveal that self-conditioning, which conditions the model on the previous step's estimate of the final image, plays a previously unrecognized role in removing this bottleneck. Controlled experiments on diverse visual-reasoning tasks show that flow matching with self-conditioning achieves higher accuracy and greater reasoning depth than vanilla flow matching, and its performance improves with more sampling steps. Theoretically, we prove that with self-conditioning, a constant-depth backbone can achieve expressive power equivalent to that of space-bounded Turing machines. Our study suggests a design principle for future visual reasoning research: carry more information between sampling steps, so that additional steps translate into deeper reasoning.

</details>


### [78] [MagicGarment: Benchmarking Single-Image Standalone 3D Garment Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19623)
*Jia Chen,Ruyang Zhao,Jun Bai,Chao Huang,Saishang Zhong,Xinrong Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MagicGarment基准（1,518个专业资产，覆盖17类复合服装类型，含多模态标注与统一评测协议），并基于预训练图到3D骨干构建服装感知基线（GSR与FTR-LoRA两级适配），验证了基准有效性与适配方法的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图3D重建进展迅速，但独立服装（standalone garments）因薄壳几何、开放边界与层叠结构而仍具挑战；现有数据集与评测协议难以支撑该场景的系统性评估。

Method: 构建MagicGarment基准：1,518个专业制作资产、17类复合服装类型、对齐的多模态标注、统一评测协议；并在预训练image-to-3D骨干上进行两级服装感知适配：Grounded Semantic Refinement（GSR）提供面向服装的视觉条件，Flow-Time-Route LoRA（FTR-LoRA）利用流时间步、token路由与专家负载信息适配冻结的3D生成骨干。

Result: 实验证明了MagicGarment作为基准的实用价值，以及服装感知适配方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为单图独立服装3D重建提供了系统性评测基准与可参考的服装感知基线，推动该方向的可比较研究。

Abstract: Single-image 3D reconstruction has advanced rapidly, yet standalone garments remain challenging due to thin-shell geometry, open boundaries, and layered structures. Existing datasets and evaluation protocols provide limited support for systematic evaluation in this setting. We introduce MagicGarment, a benchmark for single-image standalone 3D garment reconstruction with 1,518 professionally constructed assets across 17 composite garment types, aligned multimodal annotations, and a unified evaluation protocol. We further establish a garment-aware reference baseline by adapting a pretrained image-to-3D backbone at two levels: Grounded Semantic Refinement (GSR) provides garment-focused visual conditioning, while Flow-Time-Route LoRA (FTR-LoRA) adapts the frozen 3D generative backbone using flow-timestep, token-routing, and expert-load information. Experiments demonstrate MagicGarment's benchmark utility and the effectiveness of garment-aware adaptation. Project page: https://1019538157.github.io/MagicGarment/.

</details>


### [79] [MMKG-R1: Constructing Multimodal Knowledge Graphs via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19341)
*Zongyu Wu,Yilong Wang,Xiaochen Wang,Minhua Lin,Yuan Zhong,Fenglong Ma,Xiang Zhang,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MMKG-R1，一种以MMKG质量驱动的强化学习框架，将多模态大模型训练为多模态知识构建器，通过质量感知奖励直接评估抽取与组装质量，从而提升下游问答准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态知识图谱（MMKG）支撑多模态Graph RAG，其效果高度依赖MMKG质量。然而现有方法构建MMKG时缺乏对图质量的显式指导；基于强化学习的方法仅依赖最终答案正确性，只能为MMKG质量提供间接反馈。因此需要直接面向MMKG质量的训练信号。

Method: 提出MMKG-R1，一个MMKG质量驱动的强化学习框架，用于将MLLM训练为多模态知识构建器。其核心是引入多种质量感知奖励，直接评估两个层面的质量：一是从单个知识源抽取信息的质量，二是组装后的MMKG的质量；并将这些奖励用于指导RL训练。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，MMKG-R1构建的MMKG能够提升下游问答任务的准确率。

Conclusion: 以MMKG质量为导向的显式奖励设计可有效指导强化学习训练，使MLLM构建出更高质量的MMKG，并带来下游问答性能提升。

Abstract: Multimodal knowledge graphs (MMKGs) organize information across different modalities to support multimodal Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), which aims to improve the performance of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). The effectiveness of these multimodal Graph RAG methods largely depends on the quality of the constructed MMKGs. However, most existing methods construct MMKGs without explicit guidance on graph quality, while reinforcement learning (RL)-based methods that rely on final-answer correctness may provide only indirect feedback on MMKG quality. To address these limitations, we propose MMKG-R1, a novel MMKG-quality-driven RL framework for training an MLLM as a multimodal knowledge constructor. To be specific, MMKG-R1 introduces multiple quality-aware rewards to directly evaluate both the quality of information extracted from individual knowledge sources and the quality of the assembled MMKG. These rewards are then used to guide the RL training. Experiments across multiple datasets show that MMKG-R1 constructs MMKGs that improve downstream question-answering accuracy.

</details>


### [80] [Long-horizon Video Generation with Slot Memory Addressed By Region Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19257)
*Eren Cetin,Julius Erbach,Vukasin Bozic,Hyojun Go,Zixiang Zhao,Nikolai Kalischek,Federico Tombari,Konrad Schindler,Dominik Narnhofer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SMART 提出一种以区域 token 寻址的槽记忆机制，在恒定存储与每步开销下实现长时程视频生成中的空间寻址召回，超越训练时域仍保持高保真记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程视频生成对记忆有两点相互冲突的需求：记忆须有界以支持无限生成，同时当相机返回某地点时须能恢复该处曾观测到的内容，无论时间间隔多久。基于重建的记忆虽具空间召回能力，却需增长式存储并依赖估计的三维结构；循环记忆虽保持恒定状态，但其保留由时间递归支配，旧观测会随时间衰减。

Method: 提出 SMART：一种由区域 token 寻址的槽记忆。内容存于恒定大小的槽递归中，写入由观测相机寻址，读取由渲染相机查询；不构建显式三维表示，也不维护增长式缓存。每个读操作额外给出置信度估计，用于调制 chunk-causal 扩散 transformer，使其在检索内容可信时选择性采信，否则依赖生成先验。

Result: 在 Memory Maze 与 Minecraft 的长轨迹上，SMART 在远超其训练时域的情况下仍保持高保真召回，且使用恒定内存与每步开销。

Conclusion: SMART 通过将召回条件从时间新近性转为视图间几何关系，兼顾了有界存储与空间寻址召回，为长时程视频生成提供了一种无需显式三维重建、存储与计算开销恒定的记忆方案。

Abstract: Memory for long-horizon video generation must satisfy two competing requirements: it must remain bounded so generation can continue indefinitely, while still recovering what was observed at a place when the camera returns, regardless of how long ago it was seen. Reconstruction-based memories provide spatial recall by caching and reprojecting explicit geometry, but require growing storage and depend on estimated 3D structure. Recurrent memories keep a constant-size state, but retention is governed by temporal recurrence, causing old observations to fade with time. We introduce SMART, a slot memory addressed by region tokens, which combines bounded storage with spatially addressed recall. SMART stores content in a constant-size slot recurrence whose writes are addressed by the observing camera and whose reads are queried by the rendering camera, without constructing an explicit 3D representation or maintaining a growing cache. Consequently, recall is conditioned on inter-view geometry rather than on recency. Each read additionally provides a confidence estimate that conditions a chunk-causal diffusion transformer, allowing it to selectively trust retrieved content while relying on its generative prior elsewhere. On long trajectories in Memory Maze and Minecraft, SMART maintains high-fidelity recall far beyond its training horizon while using constant memory and per-step cost.

</details>


### [81] [Why Do VLMs Fail at Remote Physiological Measurement? Rethinking with Physiological Grounding and Numerical Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19052)
*Xiao Yang,Zekun Zhang,Yuxuan Fan,Duoxun Tang,Ziyang Liu,Dengbo He,Kaishun Wu,Jiyao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VITAL，一种面向视觉语言模型（VLM）的视觉-生理token对齐框架，用于视频远程生理测量（RPM），在提升跨域心率估计算法的同时保持通用视觉理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RPM方法依赖任务专用模型，难以与VLM的多模态理解与交互能力融合。作者指出VLM用于RPM存在两大瓶颈：通用视觉语义表征丢失细粒度生理线索；自回归生成中的离散token预测与连续数值测量本质错位。

Method: 提出VITAL框架：保留原始视觉通路以维持语义理解，并引入独立的生理通路，联合建模语义与生理信息；同时提出结构化数值对齐方法，通过增强数值关系建模提升生成式心率估计的可靠性。

Result: 大量实验表明，VITAL在多个rPPG数据集上提升了跨域估计性能，同时保持VLM的通用视觉理解能力，验证了其作为统一视觉-生理模型的有效性。

Conclusion: VITAL通过视觉-生理token对齐，将RPM与VLM能力有效统一，兼顾生理测量精度与通用视觉任务，代码将开源。

Abstract: Video-based remote physiological measurement (RPM) has broad applications, while Vision-Language Models (VLMs) provide a promising opportunity to enable end-to-end physiological monitoring for future human-aware intelligent systems. However, existing RPM methods rely on task-specific models and cannot be seamlessly integrated with the multimodal understanding and interaction capabilities of VLMs. We revisit RPM with VLMs and provide empirical evidence for two major bottlenecks: generic visual semantic representations fail to preserve fine-grained physiological cues; while discrete token prediction in autoregressive generation is inherently misaligned with continuous numerical measurement. To address these challenges, we propose VITAL (Visual-physiological Token Alignment for Language Models), a visual-physiological token alignment framework for RPM and preserving general vision tasks. VITAL retains the original visual pathway for semantic understanding and introduces an independent physiological pathway to jointly model semantic and physiological information. Moreover, we propose a structured numerical alignment approach to improve the reliability of generative heart rate estimation by enhancing numerical relationship modeling. Extensive experiments show that VITAL improves cross-domain estimation performance on multiple rPPG datasets while preserving the general visual understanding capability of VLMs, demonstrating its effectiveness as a unified visual-physiological model. Code will be released.

</details>


### [82] [Holi-Spatial 360: Generative Reconstruction from Sparse Panoramas for Spatial Rendering and Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19502)
*Yuanyuan Gao,Zhirui Liu,Yuning Gong,Yifei Liu,Yifan Zhan,Kuixuan Jiao,Xiaolong Zhou,Yifan Yang,Hongjie Zhang,Xue Yang,Dan Xu,Zhihang Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Holi-Spatial 360 框架，将房产平台的多房间全景图转化为带标注的 3D 环境，构建包含 1K+ 3DGS 场景、10 万+ 实例标注和 200 万空间问答对的数据集 Holi360 及配套基准，实验证明在稀疏全景输入下重建与标注质量优越，且微调 VLM 可显著提升空间推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间智能需要兼具照片级真实感与语义标注的可扩展 3D 环境。现有室内数据集以房间尺度采集为主，依赖昂贵的 LiDAR。在线房产平台虽拥有丰富的多房间住宅全景图，但视点稀疏、纹理贫乏区域使重建严重欠约束，难以直接利用。

Method: 提出 Holi-Spatial 360 生成式重建与数据策管框架。核心为 Correspondence-Guided Geometric Optimization，将前馈重建先验转化为可靠的跨视角约束，构建一致的 3D 高斯泼溅（3DGS）表征作为 3D 空间记忆；随后适配单图与视频先验以恢复外观并补全连续轨迹，同时保留已观测内容。重建场景支持任意 RGB-D 渲染，并配合自动化策管流程，融合端到端 3D 定位与基于 VLM 的验证。

Result: 构建了 Holi360 数据集，包含 1K+ 多房间 3DGS 场景、100K+ 实例标注和 2M 空间问答对，并提出 Holi360 基准以评估 VLM 在真实多房间室内环境中的空间理解。实验表明在稀疏全景输入下重建与策管质量优越，微调 VLM 后空间推理能力显著提升。

Conclusion: 该工作通过生成式重建与自动化策管，将稀疏全景图转化为大规模标注多房间 3D 环境，为空间智能研究提供了可扩展的数据与评测基础，并验证了其对 VLM 空间推理的有效增益。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence requires scalable 3D environments that are both photorealistic and semantically grounded, yet existing indoor datasets are dominated by room-scale captures and costly LiDAR acquisition. Online real-estate platforms offer abundant panoramas of diverse multi-room homes, but sparse viewpoints and texture-poor regions make reconstruction severely under-constrained. We introduce Holi-Spatial 360, a generative reconstruction and data curation framework for converting these panoramas into annotated multi-room 3D environments. Because reconstruction quality bounds subsequent generation, our Correspondence-Guided Geometric Optimization converts feed-forward reconstruction priors into reliable cross-view constraints and builds a coherent 3D Gaussian Splatting representation as 3D spatial memory. Adapted single-image and video priors then restore appearance and complete continuous trajectories while preserving observed content. The reconstructed scenes support arbitrary RGB-D rendering and an automated curation pipeline that combines end-to-end 3D grounding with VLM-based verification. Holi360 contains over 1K multi-room 3DGS scenes, more than 100K instance annotations, and 2M spatial question-answer pairs. We also introduce the Holi360 benchmark to evaluate the spatial understanding of VLMs in real-world, multi-room indoor environments. Experiments demonstrate superior reconstruction and curation quality under sparse panoramic inputs, while fine-tuning VLMs on Holi360 substantially improves spatial reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [83] [DeBoneDiT: Depth-Driven Conditional Bridge Diffusion Transformers for Bone Suppression in Chest X-Ray Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19466)
*Yifei Sun,Fenglei Fan,Junhao Jia,Wenming Deng,Hongxia Xu,Changmiao Wang,Ruiquan Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DeBoneDiT：一种深度驱动条件桥扩散Transformer，用于高效高分辨率胸片去骨；并发布目前最大的公开配对去骨数据集SZCH-X-Rays（741对CXR与DES软组织图像），在去骨性能与计算开销上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸片（CXR）是肺部疾病诊断的主要手段，但骨骼结构重叠常遮挡关键影像征象，增加诊断不确定性。双能减影（DES）是临床去骨金标准，但依赖专用硬件且辐射剂量更高，难以普及。近期扩散生成模型虽有潜力，却受限于：潜空间压缩偏重重建、先验导致域间差距、以及高质量配对数据稀缺。

Method: 1) 深度自编码器（DAE）：结构感知视觉分词器用于潜空间压缩，引入源自预训练Depth Anything V2编码器的多层级深度特征损失，兼顾视觉保真度与结构一致性。2) 将去骨建模为源条件布朗桥扩散过程，以CXR同时作为扩散先验与结构引导，缩小源域到目标域的差距并保持解剖合理性。3) 构建并公开SZCH-X-Rays数据集，含741对CXR与DES软组织图像配对。

Result: 大量实验与下游评估表明，DeBoneDiT在去骨效果上更优，同时计算开销更低。

Conclusion: DeBoneDiT通过深度驱动条件桥扩散Transformer与结构感知潜压缩，有效实现高分辨率胸片去骨，并以新公开的大规模配对数据集推动该方向研究。

Abstract: Chest X-Ray (CXR) is a primary modality for diagnosing pulmonary diseases, yet the overlap of bony structures often obscures critical radiographic findings, increasing the risk of diagnostic uncertainty. While Dual-Energy Subtraction (DES) is the clinical benchmark for bone suppression, its widespread adoption is primarily hindered by specialized hardware requirements and increased radiation exposure. Recently, diffusion-based generative models have shown promise for this task, yet they are still constrained by reconstruction-biased latent compression, prior-induced domain gap, and a scarcity of high-quality paired datasets. To address these challenges, we propose DeBoneDiT, a depth-driven conditional bridge diffusion Transformer architecture for efficient high-resolution bone suppression. We first introduce Depth Auto-Encoder (DAE), a structure-aware vision tokenizer for latent compression. By incorporating a multi-level depth feature loss derived from the pretrained Depth Anything V2 encoder, DAE preserves both visual fidelity and structural consistency. Second, we formulate bone suppression as a source-conditioned Brownian bridge diffusion process, leveraging CXRs as both the diffusion prior and structural guidance to narrow the source-to-target domain gap and maintain anatomical plausibility. Furthermore, we have constructed and released SZCH-X-Rays, the largest publicly available high-quality paired dataset for bone suppression to date, comprising 741 pairs of CXR and DES soft tissue images. Extensive experiments and downstream evaluation demonstrate that DeBoneDiT achieves superior bone suppression performance with reduced computational overhead.

</details>


### [84] [VideoHarness-SRSI: Structured Recursive Self-Improvement for Video Understanding Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19424)
*Zihao Lin,Haoyu Dong,Haobo Xu,Chi-Chih Chang,Xuying Ning,Yinglong Xia,Shuang Yang,Zhuokai Zhao,Xinying Song,Dongfang Liu,Lifu Huang,Yang Song,Qifan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM系统中"可执行脚手架"(harness)的递归自进化问题，在智能体式视频理解任务中提出VideoHarness-BRSI框架，识别出三类失效模式，并进一步提出证据治理框架VideoHarness-SRSI，在四个基准上显著提升泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM系统能力不仅取决于模型参数，还取决于组织信息获取、工具使用、推理与记忆的可执行脚手架。递归式脚手架进化可自动化其设计，但在有限、含噪且被反复复用的反馈下，如何可靠地发现可泛化的系统仍不清晰。作者选择智能体式视频理解作为研究场景，因其多模态证据丰富、机制耦合紧密，使脚手架优化尤为困难。

Method: 1) 提出VideoHarness-BRSI：围绕冻结的视觉语言模型递归自进化视频脚手架。2) 分析4000余个进化候选，识别三类相互关联的失效模式：验证过拟合、搜索预算稀释、早期经验陷阱，并追溯其共同根源——同一份不完美的验证证据同时驱动脚手架修改与提议者的搜索策略。3) 提出VideoHarness-SRSI证据治理框架：每个搜索决策依赖可迁移、充分且先于搜索承诺收集的证据；限制提议者仅使用无内容反馈；用带插桩的预热实验引导探索；在隔离的probe-race分支中开发机制，再进行因子组合与联合精炼。

Result: 在四个视频理解基准上，VideoHarness-SRSI相较VideoHarness-BRSI将等权平均留出集得分相对提升6.2%，并将平均验证-测试差距缩小37.9%。

Conclusion: 可靠的脚手架进化取决于搜索如何被组织与如何被信息引导，而不仅取决于其优化验证性能的能力。

Abstract: LLM system capabilities depend not only on model parameters but also on the executable harnesses that organize information acquisition, tool use, reasoning, and memory. Recursive harness evolution can automate their design, but how it can reliably discover generalizable systems from limited, noisy, and repeatedly reused feedback remains unclear. We study this question in agentic video understanding, where rich multimodal evidence and coupled mechanisms make harness optimization challenging. We first introduce VideoHarness-BRSI, a recursive self-improvement framework that evolves video harnesses around a frozen vision-language model. Analyzing over 4,000 evolved candidates, we find it competitive with hand-engineered systems but identify three interconnected failure modes: validation overfitting, search-budget dilution, and the early-experience trap. We trace all three to a common root: the same imperfect validation evidence drives both harness revisions and the proposer's search strategy. We therefore propose VideoHarness-SRSI, an evidence-governed framework in which every search decision rests on evidence that is transferable, sufficient, and gathered before the search commits. It restricts the proposer to content-free feedback, informs exploration with instrumented warm-up experiments, and develops mechanisms in isolated probe-race branches before factorial composition and joint refinement. Across four video understanding benchmarks, VideoHarness-SRSI improves the equally weighted mean held-out score by 6.2% relative to VideoHarness-BRSI and reduces the mean validation-test gap by 37.9%. Reliable harness evolution thus depends on how search is organized and informed, not only on how well it optimizes validation performance.

</details>


### [85] [The Impact of Detector Architecture, Model Scale, and Scene Density on Multi-Pedestrian Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19369)
*Pedro Manoel Herminio Alves,Dalton Cézane Gomes Valadares,Cláudio de Souza Baptista*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统对比 YOLO11、RT-DETR、YOLO26 三类检测器与 ByteTrack、BoT-SORT 两种跟踪器在 MOT17、MOT20、SOMPT22 上的多行人跟踪表现，考察检测器架构、模型规模、场景密度与推理方式的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频行人跟踪在监控与场景分析中很重要，但在拥挤、频繁遮挡与快速运动场景下难度显著增加；现有研究缺乏对检测器架构、模型规模、场景密度与推理机制如何影响跟踪性能的系统性分析。

Method: 在 MOT17、MOT20、SOMPT22 基准序列上，将 YOLO11、RT-DETR、YOLO26 检测器家族与 ByteTrack、BoT-SORT 跟踪器组合进行对比实验；针对 YOLO26 额外比较基于 NMS 的传统重叠过滤与端到端推理两种机制，并以 HOTA 等指标评估跟踪质量与计算成本。

Result: RT-DETR 家族在三个基准、两种跟踪器下均取得最高平均 HOTA 分数，在 MOT20 上优势更明显，但计算开销更高；在 MOT17 与 SOMPT22 上，YOLO26 变体（尤其搭配 NMS 的 YOLO26-L）在质量与速度之间取得了较好的折中；YOLO26 的端到端推理模式相较 NMS 机制并未带来一致的跟踪增益。

Conclusion: 研究结果为依据应用优先级（精度优先或速度优先）选择检测器与跟踪器提供了实用指南。

Abstract: Pedestrian tracking in video is important for applications such as surveillance and scene analysis, but it becomes more challenging in crowded scenarios with frequent occlusions and rapid motion. This paper analyzes how detector architecture, model scale, scene density, and inference regime affect the tracking of multiple pedestrians. We compare the YOLO11, RT-DETR, and YOLO26 detector families with the ByteTrack and BoT-SORT trackers on sequences from the MOT17, MOT20, and SOMPT22 benchmarks. For YOLO26, we also compare traditional overlap filtering via Non-Maximum Suppression (NMS) with end-to-end inference. The RT-DETR family achieved the highest mean scores on the HOTA tracking quality metric across all three benchmarks with both trackers, with a more pronounced advantage on MOT20, albeit at a higher computational cost. On MOT17 and SOMPT22, YOLO26 variants, especially YOLO26-L with NMS, offered favorable combinations of quality and speed compared with RT-DETR. In the YOLO26 inference comparison, the end-to-end mode did not yield consistent tracking gains over the NMS-based regime. Taken together, these results provide a practical guide for selecting detectors and trackers for this task based on application priorities.

</details>


### [86] [Discriminative Score Function: Turning Pretrained Models into Functional Generative Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19743)
*Junhoo Lee,Hyeonjin Kim,Sangbum Han,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出判别式评分函数（DSF），从预训练判别模型的损失梯度几何中提取图像空间的生成场，无需训练或任务特定目标即可将图像推向模型一致的视觉区域，实现无条件生成、条件引导、编辑、修复与解释，并可用于零样本量化校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判别模型通常以分类、检测或表示学习等任务特定目标训练，但其实际效用取决于能否泛化到同域未见样本，这暗示模型可能隐含保留了数据流形的痕迹。作者希望探究：无需任务特定引导，这种痕迹能否被显式地暴露为生成信号。

Method: 提出判别式评分函数（DSF），从预训练判别模型的损失梯度几何中提取图像空间的生成场。DSF 提供一种免训练（training-free）的更新规则，在不重新训练、不使用任务特定目标的情况下，将图像移向与模型一致的视觉区域。方法应用于多种预训练判别模型，包括 ResNet 分类器、DETR 检测器和 DINOv2 ViT 编码器。

Result: DSF 能够从噪声生成无条件图像，并支持条件引导、编辑、修复和解释。当真实图像或标签不可用时，DSF 生成的样本可作为有效的零样本量化校准数据，其保留特征几何的效果优于无数据（data-free）校准基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明，判别式训练会留下一种可用于合成与校准的生成性功能信号，即判别模型中蕴含可被提取的隐式生成能力。

Abstract: Discriminative models are trained with task-specific objectives such as classification, detection, or representation learning, yet their practical utility depends on generalizing beyond the training task to unseen samples from the same domain. This suggests that a discriminative model may retain an implicit trace of the data manifold beyond its task labels. We ask whether this trace can be exposed as a generative signal without task-specific guidance. We introduce the Discriminative Score Function (DSF), which extracts an image-space generative field from the loss-gradient geometry of a pretrained discriminative model. DSF provides a training-free update rule that moves images toward model-consistent visual regions without retraining or task-specific targets. We apply DSF to diverse pretrained discriminative models, including ResNet classifiers, DETR detectors, and DINOv2 ViT encoders. DSF generates unconditional images from noise and supports conditional guidance, editing, inpainting, and explanation through the same field. DSF-generated samples also provide effective zero-shot quantization calibration when real images or labels are unavailable, preserving feature geometry better than data-free calibration baselines. These results show that discriminative training leaves a generative functional signal for synthesis and calibration. Project page: https://junhoo.me/dsf.

</details>


### [87] [Why 3D Reconstruction Breaks Down on Under-Ice AUV Video, and How to Predict It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19993)
*Ana-Ştefania Kogălniceanu,Klára Orbán,Ashwin Nedungadi,Stefan Lüdtke,Kuderna-Iulian Benţa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文测试了四类三维重建方法在同一南极冰下AUV视频（Icefin，Kamb Ice Stream）上的表现，全部失败且失败模式各异，并证明其共同根源是"相机基线/场景深度比"过小，进而提出可在数分钟内预警此类失败的检测系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冰下AUV视频的三维重建结果一直不准确，此前无法判断问题出在重建方法本身还是采集系统固有缺陷。本文旨在厘清失败原因，避免盲目消耗大量算力。

Method: 在同一段南极冰下AUV视频上对比四种方法：经典特征匹配SfM、可微光度splatting、体深度融合、无位姿前馈基础模型；并分析各失败模式与采集条件（相机基线-场景深度比）的一致性，据此设计失败检测系统。

Result: 四种方法全部失败且方式不同：几乎无配准、光度指标正常但几何破碎、持续性覆盖伪影、跨块尺度不稳定；这些失败均与小相机基线/场景深度比这一采集条件一致。

Conclusion: 失败根源在于采集系统固有的小基线-深度比，而非单一方法的缺陷；所提出的检测系统可在数分钟（而非数小时）内预警该类失败，从而节省算力。

Abstract: Reconstruction of 3D structures using under-ice AUV video footage has been inaccurate, although it is not certain whether this is due to shortcomings in the methods or an inherent deficiency of the capture system. We test four different models for reconstruction on the same Antarctic under-ice AUV video (Icefin, Kamb Ice Stream): classical feature matching SfM, differentiable photometric splatting, volumetric depth fusion, and a pose-free feed-forward foundation model. All four fail, each in a distinct way: near-total registration yielding almost no structure, geometrically broken output despite healthy photometric metrics, a persistent coverage artifact, and unstable cross-chunk scale. We show that all four are consistent with the same capture regime, a small camera-baseline-to-scene-depth ratio. Following our analysis, we propose a detection system that flags this type of failure in minutes, rather than hours of wasted compute.

</details>


### [88] [LAMP: 3D Source-Aware Lighting Estimation at Multiple Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19266)
*Yanqiao Wang,Jin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LAMP 利用视觉语言模型，从单张 RGB 图像与查询点出发，实现点条件化、源感知的光照推理：构建光源清单、排序贡献源并预测 3D 查询点到光源方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光照估计方法主要恢复用于渲染的光照分布，但往往隐含处理特定场景位置处各物理光源的贡献，未能显式建立局部光照与其物理来源之间的对应关系。

Method: 提出 LAMP 框架：在带标记查询点的单张 RGB 图像上进行点条件化、源感知的光照推理；构建活跃光源共享清单，为每个查询点对贡献源排序，并预测 3D 查询点到光源方向。使用程序化控制的室内场景进行监督，复用独立渲染的光源响应以导出跨光照配置的贡献目标，并由光源与查询点位置获得精确方向。

Result: 在 Infinigen 测试集上，微调 Qwen3-VL-8B 将主光源 3D PCK@0.5m 从 0% 提升至 70.6%，在正确定位的光源中 94.5% 的方向误差在 12.5 度以内。结合预测稠密 RGB 辐照度的轻量网络后，LAMP 达到与先前光照估计方法相当的探针渲染质量，同时额外实现单个物理光源的 3D 定位与局部光照的显式归因。

Conclusion: LAMP 通过视觉语言模型将局部光照与物理光源显式关联，在保持竞争性渲染质量的同时，实现了光源 3D 定位与光照归因，弥补了传统光照估计方法在物理源贡献显式化方面的不足。

Abstract: Existing illumination estimation methods primarily recover lighting distributions for rendering, but typically leave the contributions of individual physical light sources at specific scene locations implicit. We address this gap by leveraging the scene understanding capabilities of vision-language models (VLMs) to connect local illumination with its physical sources. We introduce LAMP, a framework for point-conditioned, source-aware illumination reasoning from a single RGB image with marked query points. LAMP constructs a shared inventory of active light sources, ranks contributing sources for each query, and predicts their 3D query-to-source directions. We supervise these relationships using procedurally controlled indoor scenes, reusing independently rendered source responses to derive contribution targets across lighting configurations and obtaining exact directions from source and query positions. On the Infinigen test set, fine-tuning Qwen3-VL-8B increases dominant-source 3D PCK@0.5m from 0% to 70.6%, with 94.5% of directions within 12.5 degrees of ground truth among correctly localized sources. Combined with a lightweight network predicting dense RGB irradiance, LAMP achieves probe-rendering quality comparable to prior lighting-estimation methods while additionally localizing individual physical sources in 3D and explicitly attributing local illumination to them.

</details>


### [89] [CORE: Contrast-and-Reflect for Self-Supervised Prompt Optimization in Anomaly Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19610)
*Haoxiang Rao,Fang Zhao,Zhao Wang,Weijia Li,Chenyang Si,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 CORE，一个基于大型多模态模型（LMM）的成本高效、免训练提示优化框架，统一了零样本与单样本工业异常合成，通过异常上下文锚定与自监督对比反思机制生成逼真缺陷，提升下游异常检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业异常检测受限于异常样本稀缺，高保真异常合成对提升下游性能至关重要。然而现有方法要么依赖稀缺的真实异常参考或辅助数据，要么缺乏在开放集场景下生成真实缺陷所需的复杂推理与迭代优化能力。

Method: CORE 包含两个核心模块：1）异常上下文锚定（Anomaly Context Grounding），将稀疏用户上下文转化为缺陷感知的生成上下文，并实例化为结构化异常提示，将正常区域保持与异常特定变化解耦，实现可控且忠实的缺陷生成；2）自监督对比-反思（contrast-and-reflect）机制，自主迭代优化提示。整个框架无需任务特定训练，可同时支持零样本和单样本设置。

Result: 在 MVTec-AD 和 VisA 数据集上的大量实验表明，CORE 能合成逼真且多样的异常，并在下游异常检测性能上带来显著提升。其模型无关、即插即用的范式可无缝集成多种 LMM，便于适应未见过的开放集场景。

Conclusion: CORE 通过免训练的提示优化与自监督反思机制，有效解决了异常合成中的数据依赖与开放集泛化难题，实现了跨模型、可插拔的通用异常合成方案。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection (AD) is bottlenecked by the scarcity of anomalous samples, making high-fidelity anomaly synthesis crucial for improving downstream performance. However, existing methods either rely on scarce real anomaly references or auxiliary data, while lacking complex reasoning and iterative optimization capabilities for generating realistic defects in open-set scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose CORE, a cost-efficient and training-free prompt optimization framework based on Large Multimodal Models (LMMs), which unifies zero-shot and one-shot anomaly synthesis. CORE first introduces an Anomaly Context Grounding module to transform sparse user context into defect-aware generation context, which is subsequently instantiated as structured anomaly prompts that disentangle normal-region preservation from anomaly-specific variation, enabling controllable and faithful defect generation. Furthermore, CORE autonomously refines prompts through a self-supervised contrast-and-reflect mechanism. This design empowers general-purpose LMMs to synthesize realistic anomalies under both zero-shot and one-shot settings without task-specific training. Extensive experiments on MVTec-AD and VisA demonstrate that CORE synthesizes realistic and diverse anomalies, yielding superior improvements in downstream AD performance. Furthermore, its model-agnostic and plug-and-play paradigm enables effortless integration with diverse LMMs, facilitating adaptation to unseen open-set scenarios.

</details>


### [90] [SaTPO: Text-to-Image Safety Alignment via Transition Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19563)
*Ziyi Fang,Jingzhi Li,Xiaojun Jia,Xingxing Wei,Wenqi Ren,Haijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Safety Transition Preference Optimization (SaTPO)，通过在去噪过程中进行逐步对齐来改进文本到图像扩散模型的安全对齐，在保持生成质量的同时降低有害内容生成，并构建了 CoProPV 数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像扩散模型的安全对齐通常基于完整图像层面的偏好，这种粗粒度信号将多维生成因素压缩，可能引起超出安全需求的行为偏移，损害有用生成能力。因此需要一种更精细的对齐方法来平衡安全性与生成质量。

Method: 提出 SaTPO，在去噪过程中实施逐步对齐。利用不安全提示与其安全改写在同一父状态和噪声实现下自然构成紧密耦合偏好对的特性，将优化锚定于相同父状态，消除历史轨迹分歧，使安全适配局限于增量对齐。同时引入多尺度漂移约束和良性回放，限制行为偏移并保留普通生成能力。此外构建了经视觉验证的 CoProPV 数据集。

Result: 在 Stable Diffusion v1.5 和 SDXL 上的实验表明安全-质量权衡得到改善。与 AlignGuard 相比，在 Inappropriate Image Prompts 上 PerspectiveVision 不安全检测率分别从 12.70% 降至 6.00%，从 15.04% 降至 9.30%；FID 分别从 32.28 改善至 25.95，从 36.50 改善至 27.51。

Conclusion: SaTPO 通过在去噪步级别进行偏好优化，有效提升了扩散模型的安全对齐效果，同时更好地保持了生成质量和语义一致性。

Abstract: Safety alignment of text-to-image diffusion models aims to suppress harmful content without compromising useful generation capabilities. However, preferences over completed images compress multidimensional generation factors into a coarse signal, potentially driving changes beyond those required for safety. To address this, we propose Safety Transition Preference Optimization (SaTPO), which enforces step-wise alignment during denoising. SaTPO leverages the insight that an unsafe prompt and its safe rewrite naturally form a tightly coupled preference pair when conditioned on a shared parent state and noise realization. By anchoring optimization on identical parent states, SaTPO eliminates historical trajectory divergence, confining safety adaptation to an incremental alignment while preserving task-relevant semantics. Multi-scale drift constraints and benign replay further limit behavioral deviation and preserve ordinary generation. We additionally construct CoProPV, a refined dataset ensuring visually verified contrasts. Experiments on Stable Diffusion v1.5 and SDXL demonstrate improved safety-quality trade-offs. Compared with AlignGuard, PerspectiveVision unsafe-detection rates on Inappropriate Image Prompts decrease from 12.70% to 6.00% and from 15.04% to 9.30%, respectively, while FID improves from 32.28 to 25.95 and from 36.50 to 27.51. Warning: this paper includes potentially offensive content.

</details>


### [91] [TextVerifier: Seeing Beyond Recognition to Verify Chinese Text Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19551)
*Yiyang Chen,Guo-Hua Wang,Yaoli Liu,Qing-Guo Chen,Zhao Xu,Weihua Luo,Xiujun Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文将生成图像中的中文文字渲染验证形式化为“目标字符条件判断”问题，提出基于IDS输入的验证器TEXTVERIFIER，并构建首个逐字符基准TEXTRENDERBENCH-ZH，验证器F1达0.844，用作Flow-GRPO奖励还能进一步减少渲染错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像模型生成的汉字常出现多笔、缺笔或部件畸变等局部渲染缺陷，而OCR与视觉语言模型以识别为目标，对这类结构偏差不敏感，无法可靠判别渲染是否正确，因此需要专门的能力与评测手段。

Method: 将文字渲染验证定义为给定目标字符与图像裁剪区域，判断该裁剪是否为该字符的正确渲染。TEXTVERIFIER向文本塔输入字符的表意文字描述序列（IDS）而非单字符符号，并在12.7M裁剪-IDS对以及从文本区域修复模型训练轨迹中采样的“同字符、不同质量”无标签硬对上训练。

Result: TEXTVERIFIER取得F1=0.844，显著高于OCR置信度（0.526）、通用VLM（0.329）与TextPecker（0.590）；作为逐字符奖励用于Flow-GRPO后训练可进一步降低渲染错误。

Conclusion: 将字符结构表示（IDS）与目标条件验证结合，能有效检测并纠正生成中文文字的细粒度渲染错误，且可作为奖励信号反哺生成模型优化。

Abstract: Text-to-image models increasingly generate readable Chinese text that still contains local rendering defects, such as a character with an extra or missing stroke or a malformed component. Such errors are invisible to OCR and vision-language models, which are trained for recognition and are therefore invariant to the very structural deviations that define a rendering error. We formalize text rendering verification as a target-conditioned problem: given a target character and an image crop, decide whether the crop is a correct rendering of that character. Our verifier TEXTVERIFIER feeds the text tower a character's Ideographic Description Sequence (IDS) rather than a single-character symbol, and is trained on 12.7M crop-IDS pairs plus label-free "same-character, different-quality" hard pairs sampled from the training trajectory of a text-region inpainting model. To evaluate this ability we build TEXTRENDERBENCH-ZH, the first per-character benchmark for generated Chinese text, annotating position, identity, and correctness for 7,358 characters. TEXTVERIFIER reaches an F1 of 0.844, far above OCR confidence (0.526), a general VLM (0.329), and TextPecker (0.590). Used as a per-character reward for Flow-GRPO post-training, it further reduces rendering errors, showing that its score can both detect and correct errors.

</details>


### [92] [RDCompGS: Rate-Distortion Optimization for Compression of 3D Gaussian Splatting with Image Codecs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19538)
*Huiping Ren,Shuai Wan,Luqian Bai,Zhang Chen,Fuzheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RDCompGS，一种面向后续编码的3DGS压缩方法，通过将可微码率估计纳入率失真优化并调节率失真权衡，在三个基准数据集上相比H.265/HEVC和JPEG XL分别实现平均37.84%和49.31%的BD-Rate降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）虽能实现高质量实时新视角合成，但存储与传输开销大。将高斯属性映射到2D表示可借助成熟图像编解码器高效压缩，然而现有方法在优化时对渲染质量相近但编码码率差异显著的高斯表示同等对待，忽略了后续压缩代价，导致压缩后的率失真（R-D）性能受限。

Method: 提出RDCompGS，实现高斯表示针对后续编码的整体率失真优化：将可微的编码码率估计与渲染失真联合纳入R-D优化；并设计码率约束调整模型，用于在不同压缩条件下调控率失真权衡。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上进行大量实验，RDCompGS在H.265/HEVC和JPEG XL下分别取得平均37.84%和49.31%的Bjøntegaard Delta Rate（BD-Rate）降低；且估计码率在不同压缩条件下与实际比特率具有强相关性。

Conclusion: RDCompGS通过将码率估计融入率失真优化并自适应调节约束，有效提升了3DGS在标准图像编解码器下的压缩效率，验证了面向编码进行联合优化的重要性。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables high-quality, real-time novel view synthesis but requires significant storage and transmission resources. Mapping Gaussian attributes to 2D representations enables efficient compression using mature image codecs. However, Gaussian representations that achieve comparable rendering quality are generally treated similarly during optimization, despite substantial differences in coding rate, leading to limited rate-distortion (R-D) performance after compression. To address this limitation, we propose RDCompGS, a 3DGS compression method that enables overall R-D optimization of Gaussian representations with respect to subsequent coding. A differentiable estimate of the coding rate is incorporated into the R-D optimization with rendering distortion. The rate constraint adjustment model further regulates the R-D trade-off across different compression conditions. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that RDCompGS achieves average Bjøntegaard Delta Rate reductions of 37.84% and 49.31% with H.265/HEVC and JPEG XL, respectively. The estimated rate also shows a strong correlation with the actual bit-rate under different compression conditions.

</details>


### [93] [NavAble: A Large-Scale Dataset and Synthetic Data Generation Pipeline for Blind Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19330)
*Hochul Hwang,Jahir Sadik Monon,Soowan Yang,Shiven Umeshbhai Patel,Anh Nhat Hong Nguyen,Kien Nguyen,Keshav Garg,Dylan Gage,Duretti Hordofaa,Anshu Anjna,Eshed Ohn-Bar,Donghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NavAble是一个面向盲人及低视力用户辅助导航的大规模数据集，覆盖11类无障碍关键物体，结合真实图像与合成数据，并提供可扩展的生成管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模视觉数据集中无障碍关键物体（如可听行人信号、开门按钮、扶手）严重不足，已有相关数据集存在类别覆盖有限、标注不一致、多样性差等问题，难以支撑盲人及低视力用户的辅助导航感知需求。

Method: 构建NavAble数据集：整合41K精选开源图像与8K新采集密集标注真实图像；开发合成数据生成管线，基于高质量3D资产在37种环境、多样风格与视角下渲染452K帧，覆盖565个资产并附带丰富真值标注；管线结合自动网络图像爬取、VLM过滤、基于SAM的3D资产生成与便捷相机轨迹配置，降低人工成本。

Result: 在NavAble上微调现有分割模型可实现可靠的无障碍物体分割（留出测试集mIoU>80%）；合成数据增强相较仅用真实数据微调最多提升+3.5 mIoU；在两个移动辅助机器人的第一视角数据上验证了真实导航场景中感知可靠性的提升。

Conclusion: NavAble为盲人及低视力导航感知建立了可扩展的数据基础，直接缓解数据稀缺与具身差距问题，数据集与管线已公开。

Abstract: Reliable recognition of accessibility-critical objects (e.g., audible pedestrian signals, door-activation buttons, and handrails) is essential for assistive navigation technologies that support safe, independent mobility for blind and low-vision (BLV) users. Yet these categories are underrepresented in existing large-scale vision datasets, and the few datasets that include them suffer from limited class coverage, inconsistent annotations, and poor diversity. We introduce NavAble, a large-scale dataset spanning 11 accessibility-critical object classes, combining 41K curated open-source images with 8K newly collected, densely annotated real-world images. To extend scale and diversity, we develop a synthetic data generation pipeline that renders high-fidelity 3D assets across 37 environments under diverse styles and viewpoints, yielding 452K frames with rich ground-truth annotations from 565 assets. The pipeline minimizes manual effort by combining automated web image crawling, VLM-based filtering, SAM 3D-based asset generation, and convenient camera trajectory configuration, enabling large-scale data generation at a diversity unattainable through manual collection. Experiments show that fine-tuning existing segmentation models on the NavAble Dataset achieves reliable accessibility-object segmentation (>80% mIoU on our held-out test set). We also demonstrate the effectiveness of synthetic data augmentation, which yields up to +3.5 mIoU over real-world-only fine-tuning. Beyond benchmark evaluation, validation on egocentric data from two mobility-assistive robots demonstrates improved perception reliability in real-world navigation scenarios. Together, NavAble establishes a scalable foundation for BLV navigation perception, directly addressing the data scarcity and embodiment gaps. The dataset and pipeline are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/NavAble/NeurIPS_2026_BLV.

</details>


### [94] [Into the LidarVerse: A Real-Time Unified LiDAR World Model Across Tasks and Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19201)
*Henry Che,Xuanchi Ren,Sanja Fidler,Shenlong Wang,Laura Leal-Taixé,Shengyu Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LidarVerse 是一个实时统一的 LiDAR 世界模型，将重建式与生成式仿真范式统一到一个条件生成框架中，支持多任务、多传感器配置，并在单张 H100 上实现 11 FPS 的实时长时序推演。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LiDAR 仿真器主要分为两类互补范式：重建式方法能保留捕获场景的几何结构，但需要针对每个场景进行冗长的优化；生成式方法可跨数据摊销学习，但缺乏长时序推演所需的一致性。作者希望将两者优势结合，构建一个统一模型。

Method: 1) 通用 tokenizer 将 LiDAR 距离序列映射到共享隐空间；2) 双向 teacher 学习统一的条件接口，可基于 LiDAR 前缀、HD-Map、持久空间缓存以及可选的文本进行条件建模；3) 通过改变可用的场景证据，使同一模型分别充当新世界生成器、点云预测器或可泛化的观测场景重仿真器；4) 将 teacher 蒸馏为四步自回归因果 student，并维护滚动时序 K/V 缓存与世界对齐空间缓存。

Result: 在全部三种评估协议的大多数指标上达到 SOTA；tokenizer 支持 32、64、128 线 LiDAR，并能泛化到未见过的扫描模式；在单张 H100 GPU 上实现 11 FPS 的实时推理与持久长时序推演。

Conclusion: 生成式与重建式 LiDAR 仿真可以在同一个模型内实现，为未来闭环传感器仿真提供了核心能力。

Abstract: We present LidarVerse, a real-time unified LiDAR world model for sensor simulation across tasks and sensors. Existing LiDAR simulators largely follow one of two complementary paradigms: reconstruction-based methods preserve the geometry of a captured scene but require lengthy per-scene optimization, whereas generative methods amortize learning across data but lack the consistency needed for long-horizon rollouts. LidarVerse brings both paradigms into a single, unified conditional generative framework. A universal tokenizer maps LiDAR range sequences into a shared latent space, where a bidirectional teacher learns a common interface for conditioning on a LiDAR prefix, an HD-Map, a persistent spatial cache, and optionally, text. Varying the available scene evidence turns the same model into a generator of new worlds, a point-cloud forecaster, or a generalizable resimulator of observed scenes without per-scene optimization. We distill the teacher into a four-step autoregressive causal student and maintain both a rolling temporal K/V cache and a world-aligned spatial cache, enabling persistent long-horizon rollouts with real-time inference at 11 FPS on a single H100 GPU. LidarVerse achieves state-of-the-art performance on most reported metrics across all three evaluation protocols, while its tokenizer supports 32-, 64-, and 128-beam LiDAR and generalizes to an unseen scanning pattern. These results demonstrate that generative and reconstruction-grounded LiDAR simulation can be realized within one model, providing the core capabilities for future closed-loop sensor simulation.

</details>


### [95] [Object Pose from 2-D to 3-D Point and Line Correspondences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19734)
*Thai Quynh Phong,Radu Horaud,Adnan Yassine,Pham Dinh Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于对偶数四元数表示相机与三维物体间旋转和平移的优化估计方法，构造二次误差函数并用信赖域法最小化，实验证明其在图像噪声和匹配误差下具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从点和/或线对应关系估计相机与三维物体之间的旋转和平移是标定与位姿估计的核心问题，已有方法（牛顿法、Faugeras-Toscani线性法、Levenberg-Marquardt非线性法）在噪声与匹配误差下表现有待改进。

Method: 用对偶数四元数（dual number quaternion）统一表示旋转和平移，从而构造出二次形式的误差函数；采用信赖域（trust-region）优化方法最小化该误差函数，并详细讨论其计算实现细节。

Result: 信赖域方法在性能上优于广泛使用的牛顿法、用于相机标定的Faugeras-Toscani线性法以及Levenberg-Marquardt非线性优化方法；实验结果显示该方法对图像噪声与匹配误差具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 以对偶数四元数表示位姿可使误差函数呈二次型，配合信赖域优化可获得优于多种经典方法的估计精度与鲁棒性。

Abstract: In this paper we present a method for optimally estimating the rotation and translation between a camera and a 3-D object from point and/or line correspondences. First we devise an error function and second we show how to minimize this error function. The quadratic nature of this function is made possible by representing rotation and translation with a dual number quaternion. We provide a detailed account of the computational aspects of a trust-region optimization method. This method compares favourably with Newton's method which has extensively been used to solve the problem at hand, with Faugeras-Toscani linear method [6] for calibrating a camera, and with the Levenberg-Marquardt non-linear optimization method. Finally we present some experimental results which demonstrate the robustness of our method with respect to image noise and matching errors.

</details>


### [96] [A Diagnostic Study of World-Space Human Recovery Under High Camera Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19729)
*Hao-Jen Chien,Wei-Lun Chao,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出单目世界空间人体恢复方法在包含快速相机运动的视频上存在漂移问题，提出K-Pop压力测试并推导出闭式尺度校正方法予以解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目世界空间人体恢复方法在常规基准上表现优异，但这些基准很少包含快速相机运动。作者引入K-Pop压力测试（结合快速大幅度相机运动与密集多人编舞的广播舞蹈视频），发现领先方法虽能生成合理的逐帧网格，但其世界空间轨迹严重偏离表演者的舞台位置，因此需要系统诊断并解决该漂移问题。

Method: 通过系统诊断表明相机估计与人体估计各自保持自洽，漂移在两者以不匹配的尺度结合时出现——两者各自在自身尺度下被恢复，尺度不匹配会把相机运动转化为随相机位移增大的人体漂移。作者推导出一个闭式校正，估计该尺度不匹配并对所有表演者施加统一的单一尺度，从而消除漂移，同时保持他们在同一共享世界坐标系中的相对位置。该方法无需重新训练，可与现有后端配合使用。

Result: 闭式校正在K-Pop压力测试视频上消除了世界空间漂移，同时保持多表演者的相对位置；与物理约束结合后，可获得稳定的世界空间重建。该结果支持编舞建模、场景编辑、数据生成与虚拟重拍等应用。

Conclusion: 相机与人体估计的尺度不匹配是快速相机运动下世界空间人体轨迹漂移的根源，统一尺度校正可无需重训练地修复该问题，从而实现稳定的世界空间重建并拓展其下游应用。

Abstract: Monocular world-space human recovery methods excel on current benchmarks, but these rarely include fast camera motion. We introduce the K-Pop stress test, broadcast dance videos that combine fast, large camera motion with dense multi-person choreography. Leading methods produce plausible per-frame meshes on these videos, yet their world-space trajectories drift far from the performers' stage positions. Through a systematic diagnosis, we show that the camera and human estimates each remain self-consistent, and that the drift appears when they are combined at mismatched scales. Each is recovered at its own scale, so a mismatch between the two can turn camera motion into human drift that grows with camera displacement and becomes severe in broadcast dance videos. We derive a closed-form correction that estimates this mismatch and applies one scale to all performers, removing the drift while keeping their relative positions in one shared world frame. It requires no retraining and works with existing backends. Combined with physical constraints, the correction yields stable world-space reconstruction for choreography modeling, scene editing, data generation, and virtual reshooting.

</details>


### [97] [The Gap Is in the Geometry: A Convergent Meta-Analysis of Multi-Facet Aerial and Remote Sensing Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19102)
*Georgios Angelidis,Aggelos Psiris,Maria Vakalopoulou,Georgios Th. Papadopoulos,Giorgos Sfikas,Iraklis Varlamis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过四项证据层级的元分析，论证当前航空与遥感视频基准以“多选问答+准确率”评估多模态大模型的做法测量了错误的维度，并提出五项可证伪原则与报告卡 AVU-Card。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空与遥感视频评估正沿用网络视频的范式：在抽样帧上对 MLLM 出多选题并报告准确率。作者认为该范式测的不是真正的航空视频理解能力，且缺乏与人类/随机基线的对照，因此需要系统性证据来重新界定评估方向。

Method: 对大量已发表结果进行汇聚式元分析，并按四个证据层级组织，使结论在证据一致时才能上升为“立场”。分析维度包括：参数规模效应、采集条件（高度、视角、目标尺寸、时间跨度）的受控对比、视频输入与人类/随机基线的组合情况、从“回答”到“行动”的闭环导航能力、航空与地面空间基准及跨视角几何任务的表现差。

Result: 参数规模每增加十倍仅带来中位数 +3.4 个准确率点的提升，远低于至少 19 点的人类差距；采集条件占主导，19 组同论文内对比中 318 次评估里有 315 次在条件变难时准确率下降；语料库中没有任何航空基准同时具备视频输入与逐任务的人类和随机基线；能力从“回答”到闭环导航“行动”出现崩塌；同一批 MLLM 在航空基准上得分高于地面空间基准，但在跨视角几何研究上得分更低。

Conclusion: 基于这些发现推导出五项可证伪原则，并提出 AVU-Card（航空视频理解报告卡）作为航空视频基准的规范性报告工具。

Abstract: Aerial and remote sensing video is increasingly evaluated like web video, by posing multiple-choice questions to multimodal large language models (MLLMs) over sampled frames and reporting accuracy. This paper argues that this recipe measures the wrong aspect, and supports the claim with a convergent meta-analysis of a broad set of published results, organized in four evidence tiers, in which a claim becomes a position only when collected evidence agrees. Parameter scale exhibits a median of +3.4 accuracy points per tenfold increase on aerial benchmarks, far below a human gap of at least 19 points, whereas acquisition conditions control perception, as 315 of 318 evaluations in 19 within-paper contrasts lose accuracy when conditions such as altitude, view, object size or time horizon become harder. No aerial benchmark in the corpus combines video input with per-task human and random baselines, competence collapses from answering to acting in closed-loop navigation, and the same MLLMs score higher on the aerial benchmark than on ground spatial ones, but lower on studies that test cross-view geometry. From these findings the current study derives five falsifiable principles and proposes the AVU-Card, a reporting card for aerial video benchmarks

</details>


### [98] [WorldMemBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Memory of Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19063)
*Nan Zhou,Jianxiong Gao,Qiuyu Wang,Wen Wang,Jiahao Wang,Yufeng Yuan,Shuaiting Li,Hao Ouyang,Yujun Shen,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 WorldMemBench，一个用于系统评估视频世界模型中"世界记忆"能力的基准，覆盖 260 个测试用例，从静态记忆与动态记忆两个维度进行评测，并揭示现有模型静态保持与动态演化能力不匹配的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着视频模型支持更长、更真实的交互体验，保持与历史一致的世界状态变得至关重要，这要求模型既能保留已观察到的内容，又能保证世界随时间连贯演化。然而"世界记忆"这一关键能力此前缺乏系统性的评估手段，因此需要构建专门的基准来与视觉保真度、视频时长等指标一同衡量。

Method: 提出 WorldMemBench 基准，包含 260 个测试用例。它通过静态记忆（人物身份与外观、物体外观与几何、环境内容与几何的保持）和动态记忆（事件进展与状态持续性）两方面进行评估。采用受控的相机离开与多次重访轨迹，将静态内容与固定初始参考对比，将动态事件与期望状态转移对比；结合外观与几何指标及事件特定评估，并将视频质量与相机跟随精度作为补充诊断指标。

Result: 对九个代表性模型的评估表明：强的静态保持能力并不意味着可靠的动态状态演化；多次重访时，记忆在首次返回之后会出现退化。人类研究进一步验证了自动评估与人类判断之间的一致性。

Conclusion: WorldMemBench 为诊断记忆失效、指导持久且时间连贯的视频世界模型开发提供了系统化的测试平台。

Abstract: As video models enable longer and more realistic interactive experiences, maintaining a world consistent with its history becomes essential. This requires both preserving previously observed content and ensuring coherent world evolution over time. World memory is a critical capability to evaluate alongside visual fidelity and video duration. We introduce WorldMemBench, a benchmark for systematically evaluating world memory in video world models. Across 260 test cases, it assesses static memory through the retention of human identity and appearance, object appearance and geometry, and environmental content and geometry, and dynamic memory through event progression and state persistence. Using controlled camera departures and multi-revisit trajectories, the benchmark evaluates static content against fixed initial references and dynamic events against expected state transitions. It combines appearance and geometry metrics with event-specific assessments, and reports video quality and camera-following accuracy as complementary diagnostics. Evaluation of nine representative models shows that strong static retention does not imply reliable dynamic state evolution, while repeated revisits reveal memory degradation beyond the first return. Human studies support the alignment between our automatic evaluations and human judgments. WorldMemBench provides a systematic testbed for diagnosing memory failures and guiding the development of persistent, temporally coherent video worlds.

</details>


### [99] [VisualWorldBench: A Fine-Grained Multi-Task Benchmark for Evaluating Visual World Knowledge in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20083)
*Chen Chen,Zheming Liang,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VisualWorldBench，一个基于 21,291 个叶节点类别分类体系、覆盖 8 大视觉领域与 4 类互补任务的多任务基准，揭示多模态大模型在细粒度视觉世界知识上的能力缺口，并发布 50K 训练资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉世界知识基准存在两点不足：一是类别覆盖狭窄，仅涉及少数领域中的少量类别，难以匹配真实世界视觉实体的开放规模；二是任务构建不够严谨，模型可借助粗粒度线索、模糊关联或排除法作答，无法真正检验其对细粒度实体的精确概念掌握。

Method: 构建基于层级分类体系的多任务基准 VisualWorldBench，包含 8 个视觉领域、21,291 个叶节点类别，共 32,541 个评测实例，真值标签覆盖 15,479 个类别；设计四类互补任务——闭集识别、对比选择、精确定位与开放世界命名，覆盖不同知识检索方向与答案开放度；多数题目由分类学近邻类别构成，强制进行细粒度实体区分。同时构建覆盖 50K+ 类别的训练资源 VisualWorldBench-50K。

Result: 最强的闭源模型在 VisualWorldBench 上平均准确率仅为 80.4%，暴露出传统基准掩盖的能力缺口；在 VisualWorldBench-50K 上微调可在该基准上取得超过 21 个百分点的提升，并一致迁移至公开的细粒度识别与视觉知识基准。

Conclusion: VisualWorldBench 通过大规模分类体系与多任务设计，提供了更严格、更全面的视觉世界知识评测手段，同时配套训练资源可显著提升 MLLM 的细粒度视觉知识能力，具备良好迁移性。

Abstract: Visual world knowledge is a core capability of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), underpinning knowledge-intensive visual question answering and fine-grained visual reasoning. However, existing benchmarks remain limited in two key respects. First, their category coverage is narrow: most benchmarks span only a small number of categories across a few domains, falling far short of the open-ended scale of real-world visual entities. Second, their task construction is often insufficiently rigorous: models can answer correctly using coarse-grained cues, vague associations, or elimination strategies without truly mastering precise concepts, making it difficult to accurately assess their knowledge of fine-grained entities. We introduce VisualWorldBench, a taxonomy-grounded multi-task benchmark built on a hierarchical taxonomy of 21,291 leaf-node classes across 8 visual domains, with 32,541 evaluation instances whose ground-truth labels cover 15,479 categories across four complementary tasks: closed-set recognition, contrastive selection, precise localization, and open-world naming, covering different directions of knowledge retrieval and varying degrees of answer openness to comprehensively evaluate visual world knowledge. Most questions are constructed from taxonomically proximate categories, forcing precise discrimination among fine-grained entities. Experiments reveal that the strongest closed-source model achieves only 80.4% average accuracy, exposing capability gaps hidden under conventional benchmarks. We further construct VisualWorldBench-50K, a training resource covering 50K+ categories. Fine-tuning on VisualWorldBench-50K yields over 21 percentage points improvement on VisualWorldBench, with consistent transfer to public fine-grained recognition and visual knowledge benchmarks.

</details>


### [100] [GDBA-Net: A Gated Dual-Branch Adaptation of SAM2 for Robust Cross-Domain Nuclei Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20073)
*Biplab Poudel,Betserai Albert Magarire,Anish Kumar Shah,Jiacheng Xie,Dong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GDBA-Net：以门控双分支适配器（GDBA）微调冻结 SAM2 编码器，融合语义表示与频域结构先验，仅 4.3M 可训练参数即实现细胞核分割的域内 SOTA 与跨域泛化提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞核分割受形态多变、边界微弱、核拥挤及染色/制片/成像造成的显著域偏移影响；现有深度模型与基础模型在训练分布匹配时表现良好，但跨异构病理域泛化能力有限。目标是构建参数高效、标注友好且跨域稳健的分割框架。

Method: 提出参数高效框架 GDBA-Net，核心为 Gated Dual-Branch Adapter（GDBA）：在冻结的 SAM2 编码器上引入适配器，将预训练语义表示与显式频域结构先验（frequency-derived structural priors）相结合；两路互补表示分别经 Grid MLP 分支独立变换，再以样本自适应通道级门控（sample-adaptive channel-wise gating）动态融合，选择性强化与核轮廓相关的结构线索。可训练参数仅 4.3M。

Result: 在六个多样化细胞核分割基准上，于全监督、跨数据集及 5-shot 适配三种协议下，取得域内 SOTA 表现并一致优于对比方法（CNN、Transformer 及基础模型类方法）的跨域泛化。消融实验证实增益源于语义-频率互补设计与自适应门控的协同，而非模型容量增加。

Conclusion: 通过统一可迁移语义表示与显式结构先验，GDBA-Net 为细胞核分割中的域变异问题提供了稳健且标注高效的解决方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: Accurate nuclei segmentation underpins quantitative computational pathology, yet remains challenging due to morphological variability, weak boundaries, nuclear crowding, and pronounced domain shifts arising from differences in tissue preparation, staining, and acquisition. While deep learning and recent foundation models have improved segmentation performance under matched distributions with the original training data, their generalization across heterogeneous histopathology domains remains limited. We introduce GDBA-Net, a parameter-efficient nuclei segmentation framework built around the proposed Gated Dual-Branch Adapter (GDBA). GDBA-Net adapts a frozen SAM2 encoder by integrating pretrained semantic representations with explicit frequency-derived structural priors. The complementary representations are independently transformed through Grid MLP branches and dynamically integrated using sample-adaptive channel-wise gating, selectively emphasizing structural cues relevant to nuclear delineation. Despite using only 4.3 million trainable parameters, GDBA-Net achieves state-of-the-art in-dataset performance and consistently superior cross-dataset generalization across six diverse nuclei segmentation benchmarks under fully supervised, cross-dataset, and five-shot adaptation protocols, outperforming competing CNN-, transformer-, and foundation model based methods. Comprehensive ablations confirm that these gains arise from the coordinated design of semantic-frequency complementarity and adaptive gating rather than increased model capacity. By uniting transferable semantic representations with explicit structural priors, GDBA-Net provides a robust, annotation-efficient solution to domain variability in nuclei segmentation. https://github.com/biplabpoudel25/GDBA_Net

</details>


### [101] [GLoD-LVSM: Learning Geometry-Grounded Continuous-LoD Scene Representations for Large View Synthesis Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20254)
*Weikai Lin,Nicholas Milef,Carl S Marshall,Grant Kaijuin Yang,Todd Keeler,Yuhao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GLoD-LVSM，一种面向大视图合成模型（LVSMs）的紧凑、几何基础的场景表示，通过连续细节层次学习与几何引导注意力压缩场景token，在保持渲染质量的同时显著降低存储与渲染开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVSMs能从稀疏视图快速重建并渲染新视图，但存在token冗余，导致不必要的存储和渲染成本。现有场景压缩方案在渲染质量与token数量之间的权衡不够理想。

Method: 1) 学习连续场来初始化可变数量的场景anchor以匹配目标预算，而非固定anchor初始化；2) 通过condenser将场景信息聚合到anchor中，场与condenser端到端训练；3) condenser推断参考token与anchor几何，利用geometry-grounded attention引导压缩，提升渲染质量并支持解码时的frustum culling。

Result: 相比现有LVSM场景压缩基线，取得最佳的渲染质量与场景token数量权衡。相比未压缩基础模型，token数减少33.4%，渲染加速1.23倍且无质量损失；在放宽质量损失预算下，减少达85.5%，渲染加速3.00倍。分析表明视图合成监督带来涌现的场景深度和自组织anchor，其贴合表面并按局部纹理复杂度自适应密度。该场景表示还支持场景语义理解。

Conclusion: GLoD-LVSM提供紧凑、几何基础的连续LOD场景表示，有效压缩LVSM场景token并加速渲染，同时具备涌现的几何特性与语义理解潜力，可拓展至更广泛的下游任务。

Abstract: Large view synthesis models (LVSMs) reconstruct scenes from sparse views and render novel views quickly, but they incur unnecessary storage and rendering costs due to token redundancy. We present GLoD-LVSM, a compact, geometry-grounded scene representation for LVSMs with continuous levels of detail. First, instead of learning a fixed set of scene anchor initializations, GLoD-LVSM learns a continuous field that initializes a flexible number of anchors to match the desired budget. A condenser then aggregates scene information into these anchors, with both the field and condenser trained end-to-end. Second, the condenser infers reference-token and anchor geometry to guide compression through geometry-grounded attention, improving rendering quality and enabling frustum culling during decoding. Compared with existing LVSM scene-compression baselines, GLoD-LVSM achieves the best trade-off between rendering quality and scene token count. Compared with the uncompressed base model, GLoD-LVSM reduces the scene-token count by 33.4% and accelerates rendering by $1.23\times$ with no quality loss. Under a relaxed quality-loss budget, the reduction increases to 85.5%, with $3.00\times$ rendering speedup. Further analysis reveals that view-synthesis supervision yields emergent scene depth and self-organized anchors that align with surfaces and adapt their density to local texture complexity. Beyond rendering, we further show that our scene representation also supports scene-semantic understanding, highlighting its potential for broader downstream tasks.

</details>


### [102] [Glance Forcing: Making Bidirectional Diffusion Autoregressive at a Glance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20220)
*Zhuobai Dong,Junchao Yi,Hu Jian Guo,Linjie Li,Rui Zhao,Alex Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Glance Forcing轻量蒸馏框架，将去噪轨迹解耦为慢/快两个专家，每步用阶段特异目标训练，教师保持冻结。仅用单条PF-ODE轨迹即将VBench总分从84.04提升至84.22，轨迹采集成本从数万GPU时降至约1 A100 GPU时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归生成是实时因果视频合成与视频世界模型的基石。现有主流做法是把双向扩散模型蒸馏为因果少步学生，但需离线PF-ODE轨迹，成本高达数万GPU时和数TB存储，转换代价过高。作者希望将此过程显著高效化，并探究将轨迹监督压缩到单样本（最严格设定）时为何会出现严重的质量崩塌。

Method: 将质量崩塌归因于教师轨迹几何：高噪声区（决定语义布局与运动）与低噪声区（决定精细纹理）具有根本不同的曲率和优化行为，单一整体学生无法同时拟合二者，导致严重表征干扰。据此提出Glance Forcing：把去噪轨迹解耦为互补的慢专家与快专家，各自由量身定制的阶段特异目标引导，预训练教师完全冻结。

Result: 仅使用一条PF-ODE轨迹，Glance Forcing即超越完全训练的SOTA基线：VBench总分由84.04升至84.22；轨迹采集成本从数千GPU时降至约1 A100 GPU时；在动态程度、VisionReward和指令遵循上提升更显著；人类评分者相对强基线也一致偏好本方法。

Conclusion: 解耦还揭示少步蒸馏的通用机制：PF-ODE蒸馏主要“拉直”少步轨迹几何，而随后的分布匹配阶段独立决定分布质量。该方法在极低轨迹预算下实现高效、高质量的因果视频蒸馏。

Abstract: Autoregressive generation underlies real-time, causal video synthesis and is the backbone of video world models. Today's dominant approach distills a bidirectional diffusion model into a causal few-step student, but existing pipelines make this conversion prohibitively expensive, requiring offline Probability Flow ODE} (PF-ODE) trajectories that cost tens of thousands of GPU-hours and terabytes of storage. We show it can be made dramatically more efficient. Pushing this efficiency to its extreme, we reduce trajectory supervision to a single sample, the strictest setting possible, and find that it causes severe quality collapse. We trace this to the teacher's trajectory geometry: the high-noise regime, governing semantic layout and motion, and the low-noise regime, governing fine texture exhibit fundamentally different curvature and optimization behavior, so a single monolithic student cannot jointly fit both, causing severe representation interference. Motivated by this, we propose Glance Forcing, a lightweight distillation framework that decouples the denoising trajectory into two complementary slow and fast experts, each guided by a tailored, stage-specific objective, while keeping the pretrained teacher entirely frozen. This decoupling further reveals a general mechanism underlying few-step distillation: PF-ODE distillation mainly straightens the few-step trajectory geometry, while the subsequent distribution-matching stage independently governs distributional quality. Using only one PF-ODE trajectory, Glance Forcing already surpasses the fully-trained state-of-the-art baseline, raising the VBench Total score from 84.04 to 84.22 while cutting trajectory-collection cost from thousands of GPU-hours to about one A100-GPU-hour, with markedly larger gains in dynamic degree, VisionReward, and instruction-following, and consistent preference from human raters over strong baselines.

</details>


### [103] [SAM3RL: Memory Control via Reinforcement Learning for Visual Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19931)
*Tomáš Čížek,Matej Straka,Illia Volkov,Matěj Kripner,Ervin Macić,Jiri Matas,Klara Janouskova,Martin Schmid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将记忆库管理建模为序列决策问题，提出用轻量级强化学习控制器（SAM3RL/SAM2RL）自适应决定哪些帧入记忆库，在12个基准上多数取得SOTA且无额外运行时开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM 3/SAM 2 追踪器的记忆库采用简单的先进先出（FIFO）规则存储历史帧信息。现有改进方法依赖手工设计的记忆更新规则，各针对单一失效模式（如干扰物、遮挡、目标运动），但其增益难以完整迁移到更强的 SAM 3 骨干上，说明这些规则缺乏通用性。

Method: 将记忆控制形式化为序列决策任务，提出 SAM3RL：在 SAM 3 追踪器上增加一个轻量级记忆控制器，通过强化学习训练，自主决定哪些帧进入记忆库以最大化追踪性能。同一方法应用于 SAM 2 得到 SAM2RL，以验证跨模型代际的通用性。

Result: SAM3RL 在 12 个追踪与视频目标分割基准中的 10 个上刷新 SOTA，优于所有手工规则且无运行时开销；SAM2RL 在 12 个基准中的 9 个上超过手工规则。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的记忆控制策略比针对特定失效模式的手工规则更通用、更有效，且能跨模型代际（SAM 2 与 SAM 3）迁移，同时保持轻量、无额外推理开销。

Abstract: Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM 3) is a foundation model for image and video segmentation that sets the state of the art in visual object tracking. Its tracker, inherited from SAM 2, stores information from past frames in a memory bank, managed by a simple First-In-First-Out rule, allowing temporal consistency in complex videos. Several recent methods improve SAM 2 with hand-crafted memory update rules, each targeting a specific failure mode, such as distractors, occlusions, or object motion. However, their gains transfer only partially to the stronger SAM 3 backbone. We instead present a more general, data-driven approach that frames memory control as a sequential decision-making task.
