SAM3RL is an extension of the SAM 3 tracker with a lightweight memory controller, trained by reinforcement learning, that decides which frames enter the memory bank in order to maximize tracking performance. SAM3RL sets a new state of the art on 10 of 12 tracking and video object segmentation benchmarks, outperforming all hand-crafted rules without any runtime overhead. The same approach applied to SAM 2 gives SAM2RL, which outperforms hand-crafted rules on 9 of the 12 benchmarks, showing the generality of our method across model generations.

</details>


### [104] [Multimodal Medical Image Parsing with Task-Specific Low-Rank Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19901)
*Taha Sabir,Alberto Montero Molina,Thinh Quang Ly,Veronika Dmitrenko,Mohammad Yaqub,Tausifa Jan Saleem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文面向FLARE 2026 Task 3，提出基于Qwen3.5-9B的任务特定适配系统：共享冻结4-bit主干+按问题选择的LoRA适配器，覆盖6类任务族与7种成像模态，在内部留出集与官方隐藏验证集上均取得竞争性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像解析涉及多任务（分类、多标签分类、计数、检测、回归、报告生成）与多模态，单一视觉-语言模型难以用统一训练配置满足各任务需求；因此需要研究任务特定的适配策略，而非"一刀切"的微调。

Method: 以Qwen3.5-9B为基础，采用共享且冻结的4-bit量化主干，外挂LoRA适配器，并根据输入问题选择对应适配器。针对不同任务定制适配器秩（rank）与提示词，对稀有分类目标进行重平衡，并在回归训练中补充皮肤科分类样本（数据增强/辅助监督）。

Result: 内部固定留出集：分类数据集宏平均平衡准确率0.7791；多标签分类数据集宏平均micro-F1 0.6286；计数MAE 11.73；检测数据集宏平均F1 0.8914；回归数据集宏平均MAE 9.78；报告生成CRIMSON分数0.8204。官方1703例隐藏验证集：分类平衡准确率0.8496，多标签sample-averaged F1 0.5338，回归MAE 14.5950。最终容器30.1 GB，含模型加载平均每例3.1秒，重复验证运行输出完全一致。

Conclusion: 任务特定适配器配置与图像预算在共享医学视觉-语言模型中具有明显收益，且收益因任务而异；验证了在单一共享模型内采用任务特定适配策略的可行性与稳定性。

Abstract: Adapting a single vision-language model to medical image parsing requires training choices that reflect the demands of each task. We study task-specific adaptation with Qwen3.5-9B for FLARE 2026 Task 3, covering six task families and seven imaging modalities. Our system couples a shared, frozen 4-bit backbone with LoRA adapters selected from the input question. We customize adapter ranks and prompts, rebalance rare classification targets, and supplement regression training with dermatology classification examples. On the fixed internal holdout, the task-specific adapters achieve a dataset-macro balanced accuracy of 0.7791 for classification, a dataset-macro micro-F1 of 0.6286 for multi-label classification, a counting mean absolute error (MAE) of 11.73, a dataset-macro detection F1 of 0.8914, a dataset-macro regression MAE of 9.78, and a report-generation CRIMSON score of 0.8204. On the organizer's 1,703-example hidden-validation set, the submitted system achieves 0.8496 classification balanced accuracy, 0.5338 sample-averaged multi-label F1, and 14.5950 regression MAE. The final 30.1 GB container averages 3.1 seconds per case, including model loading, and produces identical outputs across repeated verification runs. The benefits vary across tasks, supporting the use of task-specific adapter configurations and image budgets within a shared medical vision-language model. Code is available at https://github.com/TahaSabir0/FLARE26-Hexapus.

</details>


### [105] [Feed-Forward Multi-View Feature Coding for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20590)
*Seungmi Choi,Ji-Hyoun Choi,Suhyun Jo,Tae-Gwon Park,Hui Yong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MFGS 提出一种端到端前馈高斯泼溅场景编码框架，直接压缩多视图特征而非显式高斯表示，实现高压缩率、低计算量与可调渲染预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3DGS的“先生成后压缩”流程需先生成完整高斯表示再分别编码几何与外观属性，常含不可微操作，导致编解码器设计复杂、难以利用跨属性冗余，限制率失真性能。

Method: MFGS从输入图像提取多视图特征并编码为紧凑比特流，通过端到端率失真训练学习联合表示几何与外观的紧凑特征，接收端从解码特征预测高斯属性；并引入重要性引导的高斯选择，使接收端可在同一比特流下调整渲染所用高斯数量。

Result: 在RE10K、ACID和DL3DV上，MFGS相较“先生成后压缩”流程在BD-rate指标下实现99.76–99.86%的比特率节省，总GMACs降低52–55%，端到端加速约12倍。

Conclusion: MFGS以端到端多视图特征编码替代显式高斯表示压缩，统一几何与外观表示，兼顾率失真性能、计算效率与渲染预算灵活性。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) methods enable efficient reconstruction of 3D scenes from sparse multi-view images in a single forward pass, without scene-specific optimization. However, existing feed-forward scene-coding pipelines first generate a complete Gaussian representation and then encode its geometry and appearance attributes separately, often involving non-differentiable operations. The resulting 'generate-then-compress' pipeline requires complex attribute-specific codec designs and limits rate-distortion performance by restricting the exploitation of cross-attribute redundancy. We propose multi-view feature coding for Gaussian splatting (MFGS), an end-to-end feed-forward framework for scene coding. Instead of compressing an explicit Gaussian representation, MFGS extracts multi-view features from the input images and encodes them into a compact bitstream. Through end-to-end rate-distortion training, MFGS learns compact multi-view features that jointly represent geometry and appearance, enabling the receiver to predict Gaussian attributes from the decoded features. We further introduce importance-guided Gaussian selection to accommodate different rendering budgets, allowing the receiver to adjust the number of Gaussians used for rendering from the same bitstream. Across RE10K, ACID, and DL3DV, MFGS achieves 99.76-99.86% bitrate savings over the generate-then-compress pipeline, as measured by BD-rate, while reducing total GMACs by 52-55% and providing an approximately 12 times end-to-end speedup.

</details>


### [106] [Player-1: Learning to Play from Synthetic Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20584)
*Mingyu Ouyang,Yiran Wang,Siyuan Hu,Xingyilang Yin,Huiqiang Sun,Guangzhi Wang,Junfu Pu,Cheng Cheng,Honghao Chen,Ying Shan,Mike Zheng Shou,Hwee Tou Ng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Player-1后训练框架，通过合成经验（Player-1-Gym十六个环境+PPO强化学习）在演示之外学习游戏控制，显著超越监督初始化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用代理在屏幕理解与键鼠操作上表现强劲，但游戏因其视觉丰富、机制多样仍具挑战性，是迈向通用数字控制的重要下一步。现有游戏代理学习流程依赖大规模演示，对动态游戏状态覆盖有限，且可扩展的交互训练环境难以获取。

Method: （1）扩展计算机使用动作空间：加入相对指针移动、持续输入与复合动作；（2）监督初始化：在6,044条精选游戏轨迹上微调计算机使用模型，轨迹将录制的动作与任务指令、视觉推理配对；（3）构建Player-1-Gym：围绕跨游戏共享的控制需求设计十六个合成2D与3D环境，提供可程序化验证的奖励以及随机化观测与控制动力学，支持基于PPO的多环境强化学习；（4）后训练得到Player-1-9B与Player-1-35B-A3B。

Result: Player-1-9B与Player-1-35B-A3B在MineDojo-300上相较各自SFT策略成功率分别提升27.8与17.7个百分点。Player-1-9B达到38.33%成功率，而最强基线为12.63%；在ViZDoom-10与GameWorld上也较SFT进一步提升。分析表明，更广的合成环境覆盖改善迁移能力，且RL过程中组合多种键鼠输入的复合动作使用增加。

Conclusion: 从合成经验中学习能显著提升游戏控制能力，超越监督初始化，且收益可泛化至RL训练套件之外。

Abstract: Computer-use agents have demonstrated strong capabilities in interpreting screens and executing keyboard and mouse actions. However, games, with their visually rich worlds and diverse mechanics, remain challenging and offer a demanding next step toward general digital control. Current game-agent learning pipelines often rely on large-scale demonstrations with limited coverage of dynamic game states, while scalable environments for interactive training remain difficult to obtain. We introduce Player-1, a post-training framework for learning game control beyond demonstrations through synthetic experience. We extend the computer-use action space with relative pointer motion, sustained inputs, and composite actions. For supervised initialization, we fine-tune computer-use models on 6,044 curated gameplay trajectories that pair recorded actions with task instructions and visual reasoning. We construct Player-1-Gym, sixteen synthetic 2D and 3D environments designed around control demands shared across games. Programmatically verifiable rewards and randomized observations and control dynamics support multi-environment reinforcement learning with PPO. The resulting Player-1-9B and Player-1-35B-A3B improve MineDojo-300 success over their SFT policies by 27.8 and 17.7 percentage points, respectively. Player-1-9B reaches 38.33% success compared with 12.63% for the strongest evaluated baseline, with further gains over SFT on ViZDoom-10 and GameWorld. Further analyses show that broader synthetic environment coverage improves transfer, while the use of composite actions that combine multiple keyboard and mouse inputs increases during RL. We demonstrate that learning from synthetic experience substantially improves game control beyond supervised initialization, with gains that generalize beyond the RL training suite.

</details>


### [107] [Any Time, Any Space: A World-State-Centric Framework for Agentic Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20571)
*Weijie Wang,Haomin Zhang,Ye Li,Akide Liu,Zeyu Zhang,Haoyu Zhao,Yefei He,Donny Y. Chen,Feng Chen,Bohan Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ATAS 将剧本编译为带显式地点、相机与事件时间引用的分镜请求，借助场景记忆与事件记忆检索证据，并在生成后经审核注册片段，从而在长时程智能体视频生成中实现跨镜头的空间与时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体视频生成需要把剧本转成在非连续请求间仍保持空间与时间一致的镜头。仅传递提示词或最近帧，无法为“重返同一场景”提供共享空间参照，也无法为“重放早前事件”提供带时间索引的记录。

Method: 提出 Any Time, Any Space (ATAS)：1）把剧本编译为含显式地点、相机、事件时间引用的分镜请求；2）生成前从两类记忆检索证据——注册静态几何的场景记忆，以及按场景、事件、时间索引的动态观测事件记忆；3）生成后重建并注册片段，仅当审核与状态检查通过时才更新记忆，且影片交付不依赖记忆准入。

Result: 在四种视频生成器上，ATAS 提升了所有报告的跨镜头一致性指标，并在每个骨干模型上取得最高综合基准分；在目标图像空间重返任务上优于对比智能体与 3D 引导方法，在事件重拍的三项标准中领先，并在全部六项人类评分标准上获得最高分。

Conclusion: 显式地点/相机/事件时间引用配合场景与事件双记忆的检索—生成—审核—注册闭环，可有效保障长时程智能体视频生成的跨镜头时空一致性，且交付与记忆准入解耦提升了鲁棒性。视频结果与代码见项目页 https://lhmd.top/atas/。

Abstract: Long-form agentic video generation must turn a screenplay into shots that remain spatially and temporally consistent across nonconsecutive requests. Passing prompts or recent frames between shots does not provide a shared spatial frame for revisiting a scene or a time-indexed record for replaying an earlier event. We present Any Time, Any Space (ATAS), which compiles a screenplay into shot requests with explicit location, camera, and event-time references. Before generation, it retrieves relevant evidence from a scene memory of registered static geometry and an event memory of dynamic observations indexed by scene, event, and time. After generation, ATAS reconstructs and registers the clip. It updates memory only when review and state checks accept the observation, while film delivery remains independent of memory admission. Across four video generators, ATAS improves all reported cross-shot consistency measures and achieves the highest aggregate benchmark score for each backbone. It also outperforms the compared agents and 3D-guided methods on target-image spatial revisit, leads three event-recapture criteria, and receives the highest human ratings on all six criteria. The video results and code are available on our project page: https://lhmd.top/atas/.

</details>


### [108] [VITAL: From Visual Motion to Precise Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20604)
*Ao Liang,Youquan Liu,Mingxiao Li,Wei Chow,Lingdong Kong,Yue Liao,Dongyue Lu,Yu Yang,Wei Yin,Runnan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VITAL 提出以视觉运动为主预测目标、小残差为补充的动作分词器，在 NAVSIM 上以单步去噪达到近十步性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 world-action 模型的动作专家通常在独立于视觉动态的轨迹表示上训练，动作目标与视觉动态脱节。作者希望直接从视觉运动推导动作目标，使视觉运动成为主要预测对象。

Method: 先研究从记录的未来图像重建轨迹的简化设定：无需未来预测，重建能捕捉主要机动但残留厘米级误差。据此提出 VITAL 动作分词器，将由视觉推导的运动骨架与紧凑残差耦合，残差编码骨架遗留的轨迹误差；主预测目标为视觉运动，微小度量修正被赋予独立归一化学习尺度。用 flow matching 让动作专家从当前图像、自车状态与导航指令联合预测两部分，推理时不生成未来图像；同一残差坐标支持有界强化学习后训练，在固定视觉骨架下精修执行。

Result: 仅用一次动作去噪步，VITAL 在 NAVSIM v1 navtest 上取得 90.8 PDMS，距其十步得分仅差 0.13 分，端到端推理速度为 3.29 倍。无需重训练，在 NAVSIM v2 navtest 上达 91.1 EPDMS，navhard 上达 38.6。

Conclusion: 将动作目标锚定于视觉推导的运动骨架、并用独立尺度编码残差误差，可显著提升推理效率并保持精度，且该残差表示天然兼容有界强化学习后训练，具备良好的跨基准泛化性。

Abstract: World-action models aim to turn visual anticipation into driving decisions, yet typically train action experts on trajectory representations defined independently of visual dynamics. This motivates deriving action targets directly from visual motion. We first examine a simpler setting in which trajectories are reconstructed from recorded future images. Even without the need for future prediction, reconstruction captures the dominant maneuver but leaves centimeter-scale errors. We therefore introduce VITAL (Vision-Induced Tokens for Action Learning), an action tokenizer that couples a visually derived motion scaffold with a compact residual encoding the trajectory error left by that scaffold. This representation makes visual motion the principal prediction target and gives small metric corrections a separate, normalized learning scale. Using flow matching, the action expert jointly predicts both components from the current image, ego state, and navigation command, without generating future images at inference. The same residual coordinates support bounded reinforcement post-training that refines execution while keeping the predicted visual scaffold fixed. With only one action denoising step, VITAL achieves 90.8 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 navtest, within 0.13 points of its ten-step score at 3.29x the end-to-end inference speed. Without retraining, it achieves 91.1 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2 navtest and 38.6 on navhard. Project page: https://worldbench.github.io/vital

</details>


### [109] [DynThinker: Unifying Static 3D and Dynamic 4D Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20598)
*Qihang Cao,Haoyuan Li,Zihan Guo,Sensen Gao,Zihui Gao,Huixin Zhang,Fan He,Jianhua Han,Hang Xu,Xiaodan Liang,Jia-Wang Bian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DynThinker统一框架，通过D-EMA与SDF缓解静态3D与动态4D间的空间-动态干扰，在9个基准10项指标上取得最优平均成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用空间智能需同时理解静态3D与动态4D，但现有模型难以兼顾：3D后训练会削弱动态理解，4D专用模型缺乏静态3D推理能力。作者分析发现这种空间-动态干扰在参数组间高度不均匀，其中静态与动态能力的平衡对后层MLP变化尤为敏感。

Method: 提出统一框架DynThinker，包含两个组件：（1）解耦指数移动平均（D-EMA），对干扰敏感的预训练参数按组进行可配置平均，约束参数漂移同时保留能力增强的灵活性；（2）空间-动态融合（SDF），在语义引导下，从动态几何编码器中选取空间与时间先验，融入VLM中间层表示，与D-EMA互补。

Result: 在覆盖9个静态3D与动态4D基准的10项指标上，DynThinker取得所有评估模型中最高的平均分：ReVSI 56.0，DSR-Bench 67.2，DSI-Bench在样本级与组级协议下分别为51.2与40.1，在四项评估中均刷新所比较开源模型的最优结果，且统一于单一模型。

Conclusion: 通过针对参数组不均匀干扰的D-EMA与语义引导的SDF，可有效缓解静态-动态能力冲突，在单一模型中同时强化静态3D与动态4D空间推理，实现统一的最优表现。

Abstract: Achieving general spatial intelligence requires a unified understanding of both static 3D and dynamic 4D. However, we find that existing models struggle to excel at both: 3D post-training can weaken dynamic understanding, while 4D-specialized models often lack strong static 3D reasoning. Our analysis reveals that this spatial--dynamic interference is highly non-uniform across parameter groups, with the balance between static and dynamic capabilities particularly sensitive to changes in late-layer MLPs. To address this interference, we propose DynThinker, a unified framework for jointly strengthening static and dynamic spatial reasoning. Decoupled Exponential Moving Average (D-EMA) applies configurable group-wise averaging to constrain drift in interference-sensitive pretrained parameters while preserving flexibility for capability enhancement. Spatial-Dynamic Fusion (SDF) complements D-EMA by selectively integrating spatial and temporal priors from a dynamic geometry encoder into intermediate VLM representations under semantic guidance. Across ten metrics spanning nine static 3D and dynamic 4D benchmarks, DynThinker achieves the highest average score among all evaluated models. Notably, DynThinker achieves 56.0 on ReVSI, 67.2 on DSR-Bench, and 51.2 and 40.1 under the sample-wise and group-wise evaluation protocols of DSI-Bench, respectively, setting new state-of-the-art results among the compared open-source models across all four evaluations within a unified model.

</details>


### [110] [GeoThinker-$F$: Endogenous Predictive World Representations for Fast Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20592)
*Haoyuan Li,Qihang Cao,Huanyuan Zhou,Xin Hu,Sijia Chen,Zihan Guo,Kun Xiang,Yunshuang Nie,Tao Tang,Xinlong Zheng,Jia-Wang Bian,Xianming Liu,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GeoThinker-$F$，通过将MLLM隐藏状态转化为预测性潜在世界表征，在去除外部3D编码器运行依赖的同时保持空间智能性能，实现最高3.6倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何感知多模态大模型依赖外部3D编码器提供结构先验，但在推理阶段额外执行编码器路径会带来显著的延迟与计算开销。

Method: 采用解耦的两阶段训练：第一阶段冻结MLLM主干，用轻量Latent Frame Prediction（LFP）头从离线3D几何专家和下一帧语义目标中学习；第二阶段移除外部专家，联合优化模型生成并通过Spatial-Grounded Temporal（SGT）Fusion模块选择性整合自预测先验。

Result: 在多个空间智能基准上保持有竞争力的性能，消除运行时对外部3D几何编码器的依赖，推理加速最高3.6倍，延迟从1.45s降至0.40s。

Conclusion: 中间语义表征足以预测当前几何与未来语义的有用表征，无需精确复现专用3D编码器特征，从而实现高效的空间感知内化。

Abstract: Existing geometry-aware Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) increasingly rely on external 3D encoders to provide structural priors, but executing these additional encoder paths at inference introduces substantial latency and computational overhead. We propose GeoThinker-$F$, an efficient framework that internalizes spatiotemporal perception by transforming causal MLLM hidden states into predictive latent world representations. Our key insight is that intermediate semantic representations contain sufficient information to predict useful representations of current geometry and future semantics, without requiring an exact reproduction of features from a dedicated 3D encoder. To realize this idea, GeoThinker-$F$ adopts a decoupled two-stage training strategy. In the first stage, the MLLM backbone is frozen while lightweight Latent Frame Prediction (LFP) heads learn from an offline 3D geometry expert and next-frame semantic targets. In the second stage, the external expert is removed, and the model is jointly optimized to generate and selectively integrate its self-predicted priors through a specialized Spatial-Grounded Temporal (SGT) Fusion module. Extensive evaluations show that GeoThinker-$F$ eliminates runtime dependence on external 3D geometry encoders while maintaining competitive performance across diverse spatial intelligence benchmarks. It achieves up to a $3.6\times$ inference speedup, reducing latency from $1.45\,\mathrm{s}$ to $0.40\,\mathrm{s}$ while retaining strong spatial reasoning performance.

</details>


### [111] [Representation Conditioning for Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20608)
*Saeed Razavi,Xingjian Leng,Liang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出表示条件化（RECON），通过将预训练视觉编码器token注入扩散Transformer的后层并在推理时复用MLP头作为代理，加速扩散模型训练收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示对齐方法（如REPA）仅通过反向传播对齐损失来监督中间层，将语义从视觉编码器引入扩散主干。虽然早期层能实现该目标，但后层仅由速度预测损失监督，无法保证能很好地获取这些语义。

Method: 提出表示条件化（RECON）。训练时，将预训练视觉编码器的token馈入后层；推理时由于不再有干净图像，复用MLP投影头（作为视觉编码器的代理），将其输出token投影到后层。该方法不增加任何参数。

Result: RECON收敛速度显著更快：比SiT快63倍，比REPA快25倍。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过在训练和推理阶段为后层提供视觉编码器语义，RECON在不增加参数的情况下改善了后层的语义表示，实现了更快的收敛，是一种简单有效的扩散Transformer训练加速方案。

Abstract: Representation-alignment methods effectively accelerate the diffusion transformer training. REPA, for example, uses a pretrained vision encoder to supervise an intermediate layer through an MLP projection head which is discarded after training. It aims to import semantics from the vision encoder to the diffusion backbone. While this objective is achieved at early layers by back-propagating the alignment loss, there is no guarantee that later layers, supervised by the velocity prediction loss alone, can well access such semantics. We propose a simple solution, representation conditioning (RECON), to improve semantics in later layers. During training, we feed the pretrained vision encoder tokens into later layers; during inference, because we no longer have the clean image, we reuse the MLP head (as a proxy to the vision encoder) and project its output tokens to the later layers. Our method adds no parameters and leads to much faster convergence: RECON is $63\times$ faster than SiT and $25\times$ faster than REPA. Code is available at https://github.com/saeidrazavi/RECON

</details>


### [112] [CommerceGen-Studio: An End-to-End Framework for Commercial-Grade Main-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20603)
*Xiaoli Xu,Zhengtao Wu,Zhengze Xu,Tao Liu,Yongchao Du,Zuan Gao,Xingjian Wang,Hao Yan,Mengting Chen,Wei Li,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 CommerceGen-Studio，一个面向电商 SKU 商业级主图生成的端到端框架，包含信息抽取、语义改写与多条件生成三阶段流水线，并发布 CommerceGen-Bench 基准，已在日产百万级主图上落地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商主图需同时满足主体保真、商标完整与字符级文本准确，而现有方法难以在商用规模下统一这些约束，且缺乏针对真实流量难例的评测基准。

Method: 框架分三阶段：(1) PERL-Agent 通过 VLM 与 LLM 角色的迭代闭环，并用无参考评分量表打分，将异构 SKU 元数据蒸馏为简洁卖点文本列表；(2) LTO 通过交替的领域策略优化，使改写器同时对齐文案与渲染图质量；(3) SKU-DiT 在 RDR 渐进训练下，联合以白底商品图、商标裁剪与结构化版式为条件，维持主体保真、商标完整与字符级文本准确。

Result: 框架在生产中日产数百万张主图，单图延迟仅数秒；在 CommerceGen-Bench 上，无论是否使用提示增强，两个编辑赛道均取得所评开源基线中最高总分，并可媲美更大的闭源模型，在决定图片能否发布的 Instruction Following 与 Product Integration 两项上超越部分闭源模型。

Conclusion: 该工作据称是首批实现商业规模运行的此类系统之一，并开源难例基准 CommerceGen-Bench，为电商主图生成提供了可落地的端到端方案与评测标准。

Abstract: We present CommerceGen-Studio, an end-to-end framework for generating commercial-grade main images for e-commerce Stock Keeping Units (SKUs). Our framework is organized along a three-stage pipeline of information extraction, semantic rewriting, and multi-condition generation: Prompt-Eval-Refine-Loop Agent (PERL-Agent) distills heterogeneous SKU metadata into concise selling-point text lists through an iterative closed loop of VLM and LLM roles scored by a reference-free rubric. Layout Thinker Optimization (LTO) aligns the rewriter with both caption and rendered-image quality via alternating domain-specific policy optimization. SKU-DiT, trained under Reinforce-Distill-Reinforce (RDR) progressive training, conditions jointly on the white-background product image, the trademark crop, and the structured layout to hold subject fidelity, trademark integrity, and character-level text accuracy together. The framework runs in production at a scale of millions of main images per day with a latency of a few seconds per image, which we believe makes it among the first systems of this kind to operate at commercial scale. We also release CommerceGen-Bench, a benchmark of hard cases collected from live traffic across all product categories. On it, our framework achieves the highest overall scores among the evaluated open-source baselines on both editing tracks, with and without prompt enhancement. It also matches much larger closed-source models, and beats several of them at Instruction Following and Product Integration - the two properties that determine whether an image can be published.

</details>


### [113] [Can Learning to Answer Spatial Questions Improve Novel-View Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20610)
*Jahyeok Koo,Hyeonseo Yu,Seungho Jang,Jaewoo Jung,Jiwon Kang,Seokju Cho,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Ask4NV，基于Mixture-of-Transformers的统一多模态模型，利用3D语言理解作为互补监督信号进行新视角生成，将相机几何引入VAE与ViT表示，并通过联合自注意力与模态感知注意力掩码在生成中利用理解特征。实验表明该方法在图像质量与几何一致性上表现优异，同时具备竞争力的3D语言理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限观测生成新视角需同时保持外观与空间结构。现有方法虽利用细粒度几何线索，但未显式建模对象身份、属性及对象间关系等高层语义与空间属性。作者探究3D语言理解能否为新视角生成提供互补监督。

Method: 提出Ask4NV，构建于预训练的统一多模态模型之上，采用Mixture-of-Transformers（MoT）架构；将相机几何融入VAE与ViT表示；通过联合自注意力与模态感知注意力掩码策略，在生成过程中利用理解特征。

Result: 实验显示新视角生成具备强图像质量与几何一致性，同时3D语言理解性能具有竞争力；进一步分析表明联合训练提升了VAE表示的跨视角对应性以及3D感知特征空间中的相似度。

Conclusion: 3D语言理解可作为新视角生成的有效互补监督，联合训练能同时改善生成质量与表示的空间一致性，为统一理解与生成任务提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Generating novel views from limited observations requires preserving both the appearance and spatial structure of a scene. Although recent approaches exploit fine-grained geometric cues, they do not explicitly model high-level semantic and spatial properties such as object identity, attributes, and inter-object relationships. In this work, we investigate whether 3D language understanding can provide complementary supervision for novel-view generation. We introduce Ask4NV, built on a pretrained unified multimodal model with a Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architecture. Our model incorporates camera geometry into both VAE and ViT representations and leverages understanding features during generation through joint self-attention with a modality-aware attention masking strategy. Experiments demonstrate strong image quality and geometric consistency in novel-view generation, alongside competitive 3D language understanding. Further analysis shows that joint training improves cross-view correspondence in VAE representations and similarity in a 3D-aware feature space.

</details>


### [114] [A$^3$PreCore: Robust Rigid Registration for Native FLAIR-Space Brain-Mask Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20561)
*Elmira Mirzabeigi,Zahra Khalilzad,Taylor Snowden-Richardson,Jesus Eduardo Lugo Arce,Jodie R. Gawryluk,Brian R. Christie,Jocelyn Faubert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出A³PreCore刚性配准策略实现T1脑掩膜向原生FLAIR空间的稳健迁移，并引入扩展定量质控(E-QC)框架，在78例配对数据中显著优于fsl_anat且与BET一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当T1到FLAIR的刚性配准不稳定时，将脑掩膜可靠迁移到原生FLAIR空间可能失败，影响下游分析。

Method: 开发A³PreCore：六自由度刚性配准，用归一化互信息(NMI)将全 bias-corrected T1加权图像对齐到原生FLAIR，同时保留既有的T1空间解剖掩膜；并提出扩展定量质控(E-QC)，刻画掩膜范围、视野相对位置、FLAIR强度支持、边界支持、下部形态及成对几何不一致性。在78名配对T1/FLAIR受试者中与fsl_anat和BET对比。

Result: fsl_anat出现严重刚体错配21/78(26.9%)，A³PreCore为0/78(McNemar p=9.54×10⁻⁷)。在57名无fsl_anat严重错配者中，A³PreCore保持高度一致(中位Dice=0.993；中位配对ΔNMI=-0.00007，p=0.429)。与BET总体一致(中位Dice=0.955)，代表性比较中下部和颅外延伸更少。方法盲法神经科学家在35/40受试者(87.5%)中将A³PreCore排第一。E-QC还揭示了全局描述子或质心位移无法发现的严重方向相关不一致。

Conclusion: A³PreCore是稳健的原生FLAIR脑掩膜迁移策略，E-QC可作为多维掩膜表征与受试者特异性几何质控的补充框架。

Abstract: Reliable brain-mask transfer into native fluid-attenuated inversion recovery (FLAIR) space can fail when T1-to-FLAIR rigid registration is unstable. We developed A$^3$PreCore, a six-degree-of-freedom rigid-registration strategy that aligns the full bias-corrected T1-weighted image to native FLAIR using normalized mutual information while preserving the established T1-space anatomical mask. We also introduced extended quantitative quality control (E-QC), which characterizes mask extent, field-of-view-relative position, FLAIR intensity support, boundary support, inferior morphology, and pairwise geometric disagreement. In 78 participants with paired T1-weighted and FLAIR imaging, A$^3$PreCore was compared with fsl_anat and the Brain Extraction Tool (BET). Gross rigid-body misregistration occurred in 21/78 fsl_anat outputs (26.9%) and 0/78 A$^3$PreCore outputs (exact two-sided McNemar p=9.54 x 10^-7). Among 57 participants without gross fsl_anat misregistration, A$^3$PreCore preserved close agreement (median Dice=0.993; median paired Delta NMI=-0.00007, p=0.429). A$^3$PreCore remained broadly concordant with BET (median Dice=0.955), with less inferior and extracranial extension in representative comparisons. Method-blinded neuroscientists ranked A$^3$PreCore first in 35/40 participants (87.5%). E-QC additionally exposed severe orientation-related disagreement not evident from global descriptors or centroid displacement alone. These findings support A$^3$PreCore as a robust native-FLAIR brain-mask transfer strategy and E-QC as a complementary framework for multidimensional mask characterization and participant-specific geometric quality control.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Gap Detection: Mitigating Homogenization through Demand and Defensibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20162)
*Xucheng Yu,Haibo Jin,Xiaoqi Wang,Runze Kong,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Gap Detection 框架，在共享文本嵌入空间中搜索“相关但未被占据”的方向（GapScore = Demand × Defensibility），将候选方向映射为概念词并生成多样化文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型对开放性问题常给出语义相似的答案，即使存在大量合理替代。生成有用替代需要同时判断查询所需内容与现有回答已覆盖的内容，但缺乏显式机制来定位“空白方向”。

Method: 定义 GapScore，由查询相关性（Demand）与对最近已有项的差异性（Defensibility）相乘构成；在单位球面上优化该乘积；用语言模型将候选方向映射为概念词；再通过任务特定过滤与受限提示将概念转化为文本。该框架还被实例化用于生成引擎优化（四个产品市场）与迭代合成数据增强。

Result: 在五个精选主题与 90 条 INFINITY-CHAT 查询上，输出 Collision 的平均相对降幅分别为 12.8% 与 10.8%。合成数据增强中（每方法 100 训练样本），Collision 从 0.324 降至 0.298（较无向累积），Vendi Score 从 27.16 升至 29.20，准确率在 40 样本测试集上仅差一例。组件消融揭示语义分离与相关性之间的权衡，并表明更好的方向分数并不一致地带来更好的下游指标。

Conclusion: 显式“空白搜索”是多样化内容生成的实用机制，但任务有效性需与几何新颖性分开验证。

Abstract: Language models often produce semantically similar answers to open-ended questions, even when many plausible alternatives exist. Generating useful alternatives requires identifying both what the query calls for and what existing responses already cover. We introduce Gap Detection, a framework that searches for relevant but unoccupied directions in a shared text embedding space. Its objective, GapScore, combines query relevance (Demand) with dissimilarity to the nearest existing item (Defensibility). The framework optimizes this product on the unit sphere, maps candidate directions to concept words with a language model, and uses task-specific filtering and constrained prompting to turn those concepts into text. On five curated topics and 90 INFINITY-CHAT queries, the reported mean relative reductions in output Collision are 12.8% and 10.8%, respectively. We also instantiate the framework for generative engine optimization over four product markets and for iterative synthetic-data augmentation. In the latter setting, with 100 training examples per method, Gap Detection reduces Collision from 0.324 to 0.298 relative to undirected accumulation and increases the Vendi Score from 27.16 to 29.20; the accuracy difference is one example on a 40-example test set. Component ablations expose the trade-off between semantic separation and relevance, while also showing that better direction scores do not uniformly yield better downstream metrics. These results support explicit gap search as a practical mechanism for diversifying content, with task validity checked separately from geometric novelty.

</details>


### [116] [Language Exposure Matters: Distribution and Order in Multilingual BabyLM Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20102)
*JiWoo Hwang,Lukas Edman,Alexander Fraser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在有限数据下，多语言预训练中语言的暴露量与暴露顺序都会影响BabyLM表现：混合训练优于课程学习，语言主导的混合配置可超过均衡配置，且以荷兰语为主的配置整体表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限数据条件下多语言预训练的两个关键因素：一是英语、荷兰语、中文之间语言暴露量如何分配，二是训练过程中暴露顺序如何安排。现有工作对"均衡暴露是否最优"以及"混合训练与课程训练孰优"在低资源多语言场景下尚缺乏系统比较。

Method: 对比两类变量：(1) 均衡多语言暴露 vs. 语言主导暴露（某一语言的训练数据多于其他语言）；(2) 混合训练（全程使用多语言数据）vs. 课程训练（先进行主导语言的单语暴露，再进行均衡多语言暴露）。在15组匹配配置上进行实验。

Result: 多语言BabyLM性能同时随语言暴露量和暴露顺序变化。混合训练在全部15组匹配配置上均优于课程学习，说明同步多语言暴露比分阶段暴露更有效。若干语言主导的混合训练配置得分高于均衡配置，表明均衡分布未必是表现最优的配置；其中以荷兰语为主的配置取得最佳整体性能。

Conclusion: 在有限数据多语言预训练中，语言暴露的顺序与数量均显著影响模型表现；同步混合暴露优于课程式分阶段暴露，且非均衡的语言主导策略（尤其荷兰语主导）可能带来更优结果，提示均衡数据分配并非普遍最优选择。

Abstract: We investigate two aspects of multilingual pre-training with limited data, namely how language exposure is distributed across English, Dutch, and Chinese, and how that exposure is ordered during training. In particular, we compare balanced multilingual exposure to language-dominant exposure, where one language is exposed with more training data than the others. We also compare mixed training, where multilingual training data is used throughout training, with curriculum training, where monolingual exposure of the dominant language is followed by balanced multilingual exposure. Our results show that multilingual BabyLM performance varies with both the amount and order of language exposure. Mixed training outperforms curriculum learning across all 15 matched configurations, indicating that simultaneous multilingual exposure is more effective than staged exposure in our setting. Several language-dominant mixed training configurations achieve higher scores than the balanced configuration, suggesting that a balanced distribution is not necessarily the highest-performing configuration in our setting. Interestingly, the Dutch-primary configurations achieve the best overall performance.

</details>


### [117] [AFCS: Atomic-Fact-Conditioned Synthesis for Factual Knowledge Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20086)
*Yunhao Wang,Feng Zhang,Tinghao Yu,Xing Wu,Jiang Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 AFCS（原子事实条件化合成）流水线，先提取源文档支持的原子事实，再以每条事实驱动生成，从而提升预训练语料的细粒度事实覆盖，在两个模型规模的多项事实基准上优于 WRAP 与 Nemotron-CC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识语料合成流水线（如 WRAP、Nemotron-CC）以源段落或文档片段为输入，生成改写或问答式样本，虽能提升表层形式与提示多样性，但不显式要求细粒度源事实（尤其是次级实体、列表/表格条目、精确时间属性）出现在合成语料中，导致事实覆盖不足。

Method: AFCS 包含四个环节：面向文档树的结构感知提取、基于源文的验证（source-grounded verification）、局部上下文扩展，以及多格式生成；每条被保留的原子事实作为生成条件驱动样本合成。

Result: 在相同 FineWiki 源语料上，用 OLMo-3-7B 与 32B base 做持续预训练，AFCS 在 SimpleQA、SimpleQA Verified、PopQA、HotpotQA 上均优于 WRAP 和 Nemotron-CC。粒度消融显示事实级合成优于块级、段落级与实体级；100B token 中期训练集成提升事实类基准，并使 12 项通用基准平均分提升 +1.09。

Conclusion: 以原子事实为条件的合成粒度比传统的片段级合成更有效，能够同时提升事实性与通用能力，为知识语料合成提供了一种细粒度可控的新范式。

Abstract: Synthetic data is increasingly used in language-model pretraining. Existing knowledge-corpus synthesis pipelines, including WRAP and Nemotron-CC, usually take a source passage or document segment as input and generate rewritten or QA-style samples from that text. This can improve surface form and prompt diversity, but it does not explicitly require fine-grained source facts -- especially secondary entities, list/table entries, and precise temporal attributes -- to appear in the synthesized corpus. We propose AFCS (Atomic-Fact-Conditioned Synthesis), a pipeline that first extracts source-supported atomic facts and then uses each retained fact to drive generation. AFCS uses structure-aware extraction over the document tree, source-grounded verification, local context expansion, and multi-format generation. In continued-pretraining experiments with OLMo-3-7B and a 32B base on the same FineWiki source corpus, AFCS outperforms WRAP and Nemotron-CC on SimpleQA, SimpleQA Verified, PopQA, and HotpotQA. Granularity ablations show that fact-level synthesis improves over chunk-level, paragraph-level, and entity-level synthesis; and a 100B-token mid-training integration improves factual benchmarks and raises the 12-task general benchmark average by +1.09.

</details>


### [118] [MemDuet: Learning Dual-Process Memory for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19928)
*Jiangcheng Song,Haodong Jing,Yulin Mu,Lesong Tao,Jixin Wang,Jin Cui,Tianjing Ren,Runxuan Xie,Yongqiang Ma,Nanning Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemDuet 提出一种双过程记忆框架，用小模型做快速并行证据选择（System 1）、大模型做记忆更新验证与按文档顺序更新（System 2），以三阶段训练实现长上下文推理中更优的精度-效率权衡，平均端到端推理加速 9.74x、输出 token 减少 73.91%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归记忆智能体使超长文档推理成为可能，但上下文长度增长会加剧推理性能与推理效率之间的权衡，亟需在保持长上下文推理能力的同时降低推理成本。

Method: 受 Kahneman 快慢思维理论启发，设计双过程记忆框架 MemDuet：System 1 为小语言模型，从原始上下文块中并行、快速地选择证据；System 2 为大语言模型，负责记忆更新的验证与按文档顺序进行记忆更新。训练分三阶段：先监督微调 System 1 进行证据选择，再用 Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）优化 System 2 的验证与记忆重写，最后对两个策略联合优化，以协调长上下文推理中的选择与更新。

Result: 在 RULER-HQA、LongBench v2 与 2WikiMultiHopQA 等多样化长上下文推理基准上，随上下文长度增长呈现良好的精度-效率权衡；三个数据集的评测设置平均而言，相较 RL-MemAgent 实现 9.74x 端到端推理加速与 73.91% 输出 token 减少。

Conclusion: 通过快慢双过程分工与三阶段（SFT + GRPO + 联合优化）训练，MemDuet 在长上下文推理中有效缓解性能-效率权衡，显著提升推理效率并减少输出开销。

Abstract: Reasoning over infinitely long documents is becoming a reality with recurrent memory agents. However, increasing context length exacerbates the trade-off between reasoning performance and inference efficiency. Inspired by Kahneman's account of fast and slow thinking, we propose MemDuet, a dual-process memory framework that learns to coordinate a small language model for fast, parallel evidence selection from raw context chunks (System 1) with a large language model for memory-update verification and memory updates in document order (System 2). We train MemDuet in three stages: supervised fine-tuning of System 1 for evidence selection, Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) of System 2 for verification and memory rewriting, and joint optimization of both policies to coordinate selection and updating for long-context reasoning. Experiments on diverse long-context reasoning benchmarks (RULER-HQA, LongBench v2, and 2WikiMultiHopQA) demonstrate a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off as context length grows. Averaged across evaluation settings on the three datasets, MemDuet achieves an end-to-end inference speedup of 9.74x and an output-token reduction of 73.91% relative to RL-MemAgent.

</details>


### [119] [Similar Models, Broken Odds: Where Luce's Axiom Fails in Chatbot Arena](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19095)
*Soovadeep Bakshi,Apurba Bose,Aishwarya Bose,Sandip Nandi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文检验 Chatbot Arena 的 Bradley-Terry 排名是否违反 Luce 选择公理，发现近似模型（近副本）之间的投票比分数预测更具决定性，并提出一个4–6参数的修正模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bradley-Terry 模型为每个模型仅赋予单一分数，隐含假设 Luce 选择公理成立；选择理论预测该假设会在"近似替身"模型间失效，因此需要实证检验并加以修正。

Method: 基于 180 万条 Arena 投票，按模型版本、同代不同规模、同实验室模型分组，估计决策性倍率（1.37、1.26、1.12）；进行平局处理、提示子集与话题、时间等稳健性检验；用回答措辞与格式相似度解释效应；构建含4–6参数的修正模型并与 Bradley-Terry 及数百参数模型在留出投票上比较；另用语言模型评委与三答案菜单做验证。

Result: 近副本投票比 BT 预测更具决定性；发布间隔一年的版本倍率一致，效应在多种稳健性检验下保持；措辞/格式相似度与该效应相关。修正模型在留出投票上优于 BT 和数百参数模型，增益主要来自对平局的建模；在预先固定的测试中冻结并迁移到新版本仍有效，但并未确立更好的头对头赔率。排行榜顺序几乎不变。语言模型评委三选一实验中，近副本主要从"孪生"模型夺票（合并提示后 log-odds +0.10，95% 区间 0.05–0.16），同一提示内位移较小且仅四位评委中两位显著。

Conclusion: Chatbot Arena 的单一分数排名违反了 Luce 选择公理，尤其在近副本模型间；加入少量参数的修正可更好预测留出投票（主要改善平局建模），但排行榜名次基本不变，头对头赔率优势未被证实。

Abstract: Chatbot Arena ranks language models with the Bradley-Terry model, which gives each model one score and so assumes Luce's choice axiom. Choice theory predicts where this fails: near-copies are told apart more decisively than their scores imply. On 1.8 million Arena votes, votes between versions of one model are 1.37 times as decisive as Bradley-Terry predicts, between sizes of one generation 1.26 times and between models of one lab 1.12 times; a release a year later gives consistent multipliers, and the effect survives alternative tie treatments, prompt subsets and topics, and timing checks. How alike two models' answers are worded and formatted tracks the effect. A correction with four to six parameters predicts held-out votes better than Bradley-Terry and than models with hundreds of parameters, mostly by modelling ties; frozen and carried to new releases in a test fixed in advance, its gains are concentrated in ties, and better head-to-head odds are not established. The leaderboard order barely moves. With language-model judges and three-answer menus, a near-copy takes votes mostly from its twin when choices are pooled over prompts (log-odds shift +0.10, 95% interval 0.05 to 0.16); within a prompt the shift is smaller and clear for two of four judges.

</details>


### [120] [Tail Competition Explains Scaling in Best-of-N Sampling for Verifiable Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19362)
*Taro Yano,Yoichi Ishibashi,Masafumi Oyamada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从极值理论视角将Best-of-N采样建模为正确与错误回复奖励分布上尾的竞争，刻画三种渐近机制并推导有限样本缩放律，提出尾部张力准则以预估采样何时有害，实验验证了理论与诊断行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Best-of-N是简单的推理时扩展策略，但在奖励模型不完美（incomplete reward model）时其行为理解不足；尤其需要解释何时带来单调增益、何时引发reward hacking导致性能退化。

Method: 将Best-of-N视为正确与错误回复极端奖励尾部的竞争；用极值理论（EVT）分析三种渐近机制（正确尾主导、错误尾主导、临界）；针对代表性奖励分布推导有限样本缩放律（含高斯奖励情形）；提出轻量级尾部张力（tail-tension）准则用于估计额外采样可能有害的时机。

Result: 成功不仅取决于采样到正确回复的概率，还取决于正确回复是否在上尾压过错误回复；高斯奖励下，当错误回复方差足够大时，即使均值更低也能主导选择；与oracle选择不同，不完美选择器的收敛可为多项式而非指数；尾部张力准则可诊断采样何时转为有害。

Conclusion: 尾部竞争为可验证问题上的Best-of-N采样提供了统一解释框架；合成数据与真实奖励模型分数实验与预测的机制、缩放律及诊断行为一致。

Abstract: Best-of-N sampling is a simple inference-time scaling strategy for large language models, yet its behavior under incomplete reward models remains poorly understood. We study Best-of-N as a competition between the extreme reward tails of correct and incorrect responses. This view shows that success depends not only on the probability of sampling a correct response, but also on whether correct responses dominate incorrect ones in the upper tail. Using extreme value theory, we characterize three asymptotic regimes: correct-tail-dominant, incorrect-tail-dominant, and critical explaining both monotonic gains and reward-hacking-induced degradation. We further derive finite-sample scaling laws for representative reward distributions, showing that, under Gaussian rewards, incorrect responses with sufficiently large variance can dominate selection even when their mean reward is lower. Unlike oracle selection, convergence with an incomplete selector can be polynomial rather than exponential. Finally, we propose a lightweight tail-tension criterion to estimate when additional sampling may become harmful. Experiments on synthetic data and real reward model scores are consistent with the theoretically predicted regimes, scaling laws, and diagnostic behavior, suggesting that tail competition provides a unified lens on Best-of-N sampling for verifiable problems.

</details>


### [121] [PersEvalBench: Can LLM Evaluators Judge Contextually Appropriate Use of Persona Information?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19314)
*Xinyi Liu,Hooshang Nayyeri,Emine Yilmaz,Dilek Hakkani-Tur,Charith Peris,Hari Thadakamalla*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 PersEvalBench 基准，测试 LLM 评测器能否可靠判断个性化助手对用户历史的使用是否恰当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 评测器被越来越多用于评估个性化助手，但其判断个性化是否可靠缺乏检验，缺少针对"用户信息应持续、迁移或失效"这一动态性的评测手段。

Method: 构建 PersEvalBench，含 403 个多轮情节场景，覆盖三大类九种受控错误子类型（欠个性化、过度个性化、状态误用），并辅以人工审核确认错误类型与用户信息使用的合理性；在五个人工标注子集上对比五种 LLM 评测器的识别表现，并测试高亮相关历史轮次对识别率的影响。

Result: 即便提供完整对话历史，LLM 评测器对受控个性化错误的识别仍然有限。在人工评测子集上，欠个性化差距最大：人类识别率 94.4%，最强评测器仅 63.9%。评测器常能察觉回答有问题但方向判断错误，把"未遵循适用偏好（UNDER）"误判为"使用不适用偏好（OVER）"。高亮相关历史轮次仅带来轻微提升。

Conclusion: LLM 评测器识别个性化错误的能力不足，瓶颈并非证据可及性，而在于判断用户信息在回答中应扮演何种角色；PersEvalBench 为诊断此类能力提供了受控基准。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) evaluators are increasingly used to assess personalized assistants, yet whether they can reliably judge personalization has rarely been tested. We introduce PersEvalBench, a benchmark testing whether an evaluator can recognize appropriate and inappropriate uses of user history. It contains 403 multi-episode scenarios where user information should persist, transfer, or cease to apply. The benchmark covers nine controlled error subtypes across three families: under-personalization, over-personalization, and misapplied-state errors. Human audits confirm that generated errors match their intended types and that the prescribed uses of user information are reasonable.
  Across five LLM evaluators, recognition of controlled personalization errors remains limited even with the full dialogue history. On the human-evaluated subset, under-personalization shows the largest gap: humans recognize 94.4% of these errors, compared with 63.9% for the strongest reported evaluator. Evaluators often recognize that a response is problematic but reverse the error direction, mistaking failure to follow an applicable preference (UNDER) for use of an inapplicable preference (OVER). Easier evidence access offers little help: highlighting the relevant history turns raises error recognition only slightly, suggesting that the remaining difficulty lies in judging what role that information should play in the response.

</details>


### [122] [HERO: Harnessing Evidence for Test-time Refinement and Prompt Optimization in Program Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19184)
*Mingyu Lu,Timothy Ossowski,Tristan Naumann,Hoifung Poon,Peniel Argaw*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出HERO框架，将代码生成中的可执行验证信号用于harness优化，结合离线提示更新学习与测试时实例级精炼，在SQL、Cypher、Python基准上取得最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有harness优化方法依赖LLM reflector提出更新，虽能修复已观察到的失败，却可能破坏原本正确的行为，缺乏可靠性保障，因此需要面向程序生成任务（如text-to-SQL、text-to-Python）的改进方案。

Method: HERO利用text-to-code任务可直接执行输出的特性，将执行结果作为验证信号。第一阶段离线优化：从经执行验证的成功与失败案例中学习可复用的prompt更新，并在采纳前验证候选修订；第二阶段测试时优化：将生成的代码视为实例专属harness，依据执行反馈进行精炼。

Result: 在SQL、Cypher、Python四个基准上达到最佳离线性能，pass@1相比初始prompt提升7.6个百分点；测试时精炼再提升2.0个百分点，并改善了执行效率。

Conclusion: 直接利用执行验证信号进行harness优化可有效规避reflector引入的破坏性问题，离线可复用更新与测试时实例级精炼形成互补，兼顾准确率与执行效率。

Abstract: Harness optimization improves LLMs by refining reusable prompts and scaffolding, yet requires further adaptation for program generation tasks such as text-to-SQL and text-to-Python. Existing methods often rely on LLM reflectors to propose harness updates, which can fix observed failures but also disrupt previously correct behavior. Text-to-code tasks offer a natural way to mitigate this issue: we can execute generated outputs, allowing us to evaluate and verify the effects of proposed updates directly. Building on this signal, we introduce \textsc{HERO} (\textbf{H}arnessing \textbf{E}vidence for \textbf{R}efinement and \textbf{O}ptimization), which combines offline and test-time harness optimization. At the first stage, \method{} learns reusable prompt updates from execution-verified successes and failures and validates candidate revisions before adoption. At test time, we treat the generated code as an instance-specific harness and refine it using execution feedback. Across four SQL, Cypher, and Python benchmarks, \method{} achieves the best offline performance, improving pass@1 by \(7.6\) points over the initial prompt, while test-time refinement adds another \(2.0\) points and improves execution efficiency.

</details>


### [123] [Crossing the Gender Binary: Modelling Gendered Reference in Science Fiction and General Fiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19028)
*Yuxuan Liu,Haim Dubossarsky,Ruth Ahnert*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文在同期科幻小说与一般小说语料中掩码 man/woman/person 等词，用 RoBERTa 预测替代词并按性别分类，发现两个语料库都双向跨越性别二元，但科幻小说的性别渗透性更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科幻小说常被认为具有"扰动性别"的作用，但这一主题性论断此前缺乏语言层面的分布证据；作者希望检验这种性别松动是否体现在词汇替代的可预测性上，并考察体裁是否造成差异。

Method: 在同期科幻小说与一般小说语料中掩码 man/men、woman/women、person/people，取 RoBERTa 给出的五个最可能替代词并按性别归类；采用置换检验、经五轮重采样评估保留率与替换率，并对底层句子做细读。

Result: 两个语料库均双向跨越性别二元，但并不对称：woman 被掩码时模型预测阳性词约为 man 被掩码时预测阴性词的两倍，每次错配都构成统计惊讶。相比一般小说，科幻小说性别渗透性更强，更常把 man 置于阴性语境而反向速率持平；其去性别化倾向也更强，将两个性别词拉向中性。此外，被掩码的 woman 比 man 更常引出只标社会范畴而无性别的替代词。

Conclusion: 该研究通过测量 man、woman、person 在语言分布中的性别渗透性并检验体裁影响，为科幻研究的主题性主张提供了语言学证据，也为计算文学研究提供了一条可迁移到种族、阶级等范畴词汇的分析流程。

Abstract: Science fiction is widely credited with unsettling gender. This article asks whether that holds at the linguistic level, in how readily gendered reference gives way to its counterpart, a neutral term, or other social category, and whether it separates the genre from general fiction. It does so by masking man/men, woman/women, and person/people across same-period science fiction and general fiction corpora and classifying RoBERTa's five likeliest substitutes by gender. Retention and displacement are permutation-tested over five resampling rounds, and the underlying sentences are read closely. Both corpora cross the gender binary in both directions, but unequally: where woman is masked, the model predicts a masculine term about twice as often as it predicts a feminine term where man is masked. Each mismatch marks a statistical surprise, a word the corpus did not expect there. Against this shared pattern, science fiction shows greater gender permeability than the general fiction corpus, placing man in feminine contexts more often than general fiction while the reverse rate holds steady. Its degendering tendency is stronger too, drawing both gendered terms toward the neutral. Beyond the binary, a masked woman also draws substitutes naming a social category and no gender far more often than a masked man does. In measuring the gender permeability of man, woman, and person in linguistic distribution and testing whether genre changes it, the study gives science fiction studies linguistic evidence for thematic claims and computational literary studies a pipeline applicable to other category vocabularies, such as race and class.

</details>


### [124] [Evaluating Large Language Models for Multilingual Abstractive Summarization: A Comprehensive Study of English and Bengali](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19003)
*Khan Mahi Al Atahar,Nazifa Tasneem,Ajwad Abrar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了8个闭源与开放权重LLM在规模匹配的英语与孟加拉语XL-Sum测试集上的零样本基准，结合词汇/语义指标、无参考事实性验证及多维LLM-as-a-Judge评估。结果显示跨语言差异显著，且不同评估方法给出不同模型排名，表明英语摘要优势未必迁移至孟加拉语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抽象式摘要跨语言表现存在语言质量与事实一致性可能背离的问题，在低资源语言中尤为突出；同时单一指标难以全面刻画多语言摘要质量，亟需标准化、多维度的评估框架。

Method: 在规模匹配的英语与孟加拉语XL-Sum测试集上，对8个闭源及开放权重LLM进行标准化零样本评测；评估体系融合词汇指标（ROUGE、BLEU）、语义指标、无参考事实性验证以及多维LLM-as-a-Judge评估。

Result: 跨语言差异显著：GPT-5.6 Luna在多项英语词汇指标上领先，但在孟加拉语上未保持优势；LLaMA 3.3 70B取得孟加拉语最高ROUGE与BLEU；Gemini 2.5 Flash获得观察到的最高孟加拉语事实性得分。不同评估方法导致模型排名不一致。

Conclusion: 英语摘要的强表现不必然迁移到孟加拉语，依赖单一指标评估多语言摘要存在局限；低资源语言需要综合性的多维度评估框架。

Abstract: Abstractive summarization remains challenging across languages, where linguistic quality and factual consistency may diverge, particularly in low-resource settings. We present a standardized zero-shot benchmark of eight closed-source and open-weight LLMs on size-matched English and Bengali XL-Sum test sets. Our evaluation combines lexical and semantic metrics with reference-free factuality verification and multidimensional LLM-as-a-Judge assessment, providing a comprehensive analysis of both surface-level quality and factual reliability in multilingual summarization. Results reveal substantial cross-lingual variation: GPT-5.6 Luna leads several English lexical metrics but does not maintain the same advantage in Bengali, where LLaMA 3.3 70B achieves the highest ROUGE and BLEU scores. Gemini 2.5 Flash obtains the highest observed Bengali factuality score. Furthermore, evaluation methods produce different model rankings, highlighting the limitations of relying on a single metric for multilingual summarization assessment. These findings demonstrate that strong English summarization performance does not necessarily transfer to Bengali and emphasize the need for comprehensive evaluation frameworks for low-resource languages.

</details>


### [125] [Share the Symbol, Not the Vector: Training-Free Fact Communication Across Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19696)
*Xin Li,Mengbing Liu,Hao Jiang,Xin Gao,Annan Wang,Xingwei Qu,Yichi Zhang,Chau Yuen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SYNC符号索引神经通信接口：发送方仅凭描述从共享词表选取词并发送索引，接收方用自身激活中拟合的“词方向”将事实写入隐状态，无需微调或学习型翻译器，即可在异构模型间实现选择性的多事实使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统通常以文本通信，近期也尝试交换模型内部状态，但跨架构共享状态往往依赖学习型翻译器或模型兼容性假设，难以在异构模型族之间直接迁移。

Method: SYNC采用词索引接口：发送方在看不到问题的情况下，针对每个对象从共享词表选择词并发送其索引；接收方用一次性从自身激活中拟合的本地词方向向量，把被描述事实写入其隐状态。评测采用交换两对象间某一属性的“选择性使用”任务，要求对交换前后、变化与未变化属性共四问均答对。在四个开发接收模型上选定早期写入层后固定；再用3个发送方与来自5个模型族的9个较新接收方测试。

Result: 在新场景上四问成功率72.0–98.3%，较中间层写入最高提升96个百分点，两个最大接收方与纯文本通信相当；一个无需任务的探针预先预测了每个新接收方可用的深度（45/45）。不经重调，列出全部事实的消息在新布局下保持89.6–100%成功率；在多词元符号的地标任务上，发送方消息达23.9–64.0%，低于文本但从无消息时的0%明显提升。

Conclusion: 共享词索引与接收方本地词方向可在不额外训练的情况下，支持跨模型族的选择性事实使用，为异构多智能体通信提供了免翻译器的可行路径。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems allow language models to solve tasks collaboratively by sharing complementary information. These systems typically communicate through text, while recent approaches also explore exchanging internal model states. However, sharing these states across architectures often requires learned translators or assumptions about model compatibility. We introduce SYNC (Symbol-Indexed Neural Communication), a word-indexed interface that connects heterogeneous models without fine-tuning or a learned translator. From descriptions only it can see, the sender selects words from a shared list and sends their indices for each object, without seeing the questions. The receiver writes the described facts into its hidden states using its own word directions, vectors fitted once from its activations. We test selective use by swapping one attribute between two objects and requiring correct answers about both changed and unchanged attributes before and after the swap, four answers in total. We choose an early write layer on four development receivers and then fix it. With three different senders and nine newer receivers from five families, four-answer success on new scenes reaches 72.0-98.3%, up to 96 percentage points above middle-layer writing, and the two largest receivers match plain text. A task-free probe predicted in advance which depths each new receiver can use (45 of 45 cases). Without retuning, messages listing every fact keep 89.6-100% success under new layouts, and sender messages reach 23.9-64.0% on a landmark task with multi-token symbols, below text but from 0% without a message. These results show that shared word indices and receiver-local directions can support selective fact use across model families without additional training.

</details>


### [126] [Evidence-Anchored Overdose Death Prediction from Death Investigation Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19556)
*Raavi Gupta,Ziqing Yang,Karli R. Hochstatter,Talia Nadel,Nicole D'Anna,Jason K. Graham,Hannah Johnson,Kelsey Opozda,Nabila El-Bassel,Anubhav Jangra,Smaranda Muresan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个从死亡调查报告自动识别疑似药物过量死亡的框架，并能生成由专家知识引导的支持性解释。通过专家引导的设计流程，研究比较了不同解释类型对预测性能的影响，以及"先解释后预测"的流水线模型与"预测解释联合"的自解释模型哪种架构更优。实验表明，基于输入报告内容的解释配合流水线模型表现最佳，且优于现行部署的规则系统15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国药物过量死亡的确认常因药物相关死亡认定的复杂性而被延迟。死亡调查报告是一种丰富但尚未充分利用的数据源，可用于预测某死亡是否为疑似过量（即在最终死因与死亡方式判定之前初步识别为过量）。利用该数据源有助于提前识别疑似过量死亡，缓解确认延迟问题。

Method: 采用专家引导的设计流程，构建自动分类框架，从死亡调查报告中识别疑似过量死亡并生成解释。研究两个问题：1）何种类型的解释最有利于模型预测性能；2）哪种架构更适合该任务——先进行推理再预测的流水线模型（pipeline），还是联合预测与推理的自解释模型（self-rationalizing）。

Result: 实验显示，解释内容扎根于输入死亡调查报告的流水线模型优于其他设置。最佳模型比当前部署的规则系统性能高出15%。

Conclusion: 扎根于输入死亡调查报告的解释配合流水线架构为识别疑似过量死亡的最优方案，且保留专家介入以进行最终决策的能力。该框架在性能上超越现行规则系统，表明其在实际应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Confirmation of overdose deaths in the United States is often delayed due to the complexities of certifying drug-involved deaths. Death investigation reports represent a rich but underutilized data source that could be used to predict whether a death is a suspected overdose (i.e., a death preliminarily identified as an overdose prior to the final cause and manner of death determination). We present an automated framework that classifies suspected overdoses from death investigation reports, while producing supporting explanations guided by expert knowledge. Through an expert-guided design process, we investigate 1) what type of explanations are best for model prediction performance, and 2) which architecture is better suited for this task: pipeline models that rationalize first and then predict, or self-rationalizing models that jointly predict and rationalize? Our experiments show that a pipeline model with explanations grounded in the input death investigation reports outperforms the other settings. Our best model also outperforms a rule-based system currently in deployment by 15%, while allowing expert interventions for final decisions.

</details>


### [127] [GraphRAG-LTM: A Layered Threat Modeling Framework for Attacks on Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19549)
*Yining Tang,Junyu Zhang,Haomiao Qiu,Yongchao Liu,Jiajun Zheng,Zhao Wang,Ziyue Qiao,Chao Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文提出GraphRAG-LTM分层威胁建模框架，在查询与图证据路径上测试三个嵌套攻击层级，揭示GraphRAG的证据路径安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GraphRAG依赖检索到的种子块与由其构建的图证据来支撑LLM回答，但其证据路径上的攻击研究不足，缺乏系统性的威胁建模与层级化攻击评测。

Method: 提出GraphRAG-LTM分层威胁建模框架，定义三级攻击权限：BB Query利用排序反馈在语义相似度约束下改写查询以压低相关种子块排名；WB Query利用检索器梯度实现同一目标；Graph-aware WB在白盒对抗查询基础上删除查询相关证据图中的桥接三元组，通过局部图编辑破坏多跳证据传播。

Result: 在多种数据集、检索器与LLM骨干上，仅凭排名反馈即可降低答案质量；梯度访问使查询攻击更强；桥接三元组删除进一步损害支撑事实的检索。

Conclusion: 种子检索与图证据共同导致GraphRAG的失效，攻击可分别作用于检索入口与图证据组装环节，需针对证据路径进行防护。

Abstract: Graph retrieval-augmented generation (GraphRAG) uses document-derived knowledge graphs to assemble evidence for large language model (LLM) answers. Its security depends on both the seed chunks retrieved for a query and the graph evidence assembled from them, yet attacks on this evidence path remain underexplored. We introduce GraphRAG-LTM, a Layered Threat Modeling framework that tests three nested levels of attacker access. BB Query uses ranking feedback to make query edits under a semantic-similarity constraint and demote relevant seed chunks. WB Query uses retriever gradients for the same objective. Graph-aware WB keeps the white-box adversarial query and deletes bridge triplets from the query-specific evidence graph, disrupting multi-hop evidence propagation through localized graph edits. Across datasets, retrievers, and LLM backbones, rank feedback alone reduces answer quality, gradient access strengthens query attacks, and bridge-triplet deletion further impairs supporting-fact retrieval. Further analyses show how seed retrieval and graph evidence jointly contribute to these failures.

</details>


### [128] [Typed Decision Models on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19536)
*Baowen Zhang,Hangting Ye,Hongyuan Zha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文在表格数据上比较了四种类型化决策模型与三种大语言模型（LLM），任务包括行分类、事实核查与异常检测，并对每个请求施加21种保持内容不变的改写。结果表明决策模型性能与LLM相当但成本更低，其中三种对问题表述的鲁棒性更强；然而没有任何模型对表格组织方式具有鲁棒性，且至少三分之一的决策会在某些改写下改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于：类型化决策模型能否作为生成式LLM在表格决策任务上的实用替代方案，以及这些模型在面对保持语义不变的问题/表格改写时的鲁棒性如何。

Method: 方法：在表格数据上对比四种类型化决策模型与三种大语言模型；任务涵盖行分类、事实核查和异常检测；每个请求以原始形式和21种保持内容的改写形式发送（如重命名选项、以不同格式书写表格）；并分析决策置信度与决策变化之间的关系。

Result: 结果：决策模型性能与LLM相当且成本更低；其中三种对问题表述方式的鲁棒性明显更强；但没有任何被测试模型对表格组织方式具有鲁棒性；对每个模型而言，至少三分之一的决定在表格或问题的某种改写下发生改变；决策模型给出低概率的决策最易发生变化。

Conclusion: 结论：类型化决策模型是表格决策的实用选择，但其鲁棒性仍是一个未解决的问题。

Abstract: Typed decision models are language models that answer a question with a fixed set of possible answers by returning a probability for each answer instead of generating text. We study them on tabular data and compare four decision models with three large language models (LLMs) on row classification, fact verification and anomaly detection. Every request is sent in its original form and under 21 types of rewrite that keep its content, such as renaming the options or writing the table in another format. The decision models perform as well as the LLMs at a lower cost. Three of them are also far more robust to how the question is formulated. No model we test is robust to how the table is organized. For every model, at least a third of the row decisions change under some rewrite of the table or the question. The decisions to which a decision model gives low probability are the ones most likely to change. Decision models are a practical choice for tabular decisions, but their robustness remains an open problem.

</details>


### [129] [A Unified Response Framework for Questions and Tutoring Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19532)
*Zhangqi Duan,Simon Woodhead,Maya Miller-Vedam,Andrew Lan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出统一框架，将学生答题与辅导对话中的子任务映射到同一IRT量表，实现难度预测与作答正确性预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有教育应用中的学生模型多聚焦于学生对问题的作答，忽视了大规模辅导情境下师生对话所蕴含的细粒度学生知识证据，因此需要同时建模答题与对话中的学习信号。

Method: 1) 识别辅导对话中的数学子任务，将其改写为自足问题，并判定学生对子任务作答的正确性；2) 构建IRT框架，从文本估计题目/子任务难度，跟踪学生在答题与对话交互中动态变化的能力；3) 提出利用相邻子任务间的辅导动作（tutor moves）来正则化难度值的新方法。

Result: 在两个包含人工辅导与AI辅导的真实数学教育数据集上，该框架在题目难度预测和作答正确性预测两项任务上均优于强基线。

Conclusion: 将答题与辅导对话统一建模到共享IRT量表是可行且有效的，辅导动作信息可作为难度正则化的有用信号，提升学生建模性能。

Abstract: Existing student models in educational applications largely focus on student responses to questions. However, as tutoring becomes more accessible at large scale, it is important to analyze dialogues between tutors and students, which may reveal fine-grained evidence on student knowledge. In this work, we propose a unified framework that models both student responses to questions and tutor-posed subtasks during dialogues on a shared item response theory (IRT) scale. We first identify mathematical subtasks in tutoring dialogues, rewrite them into self-contained questions, and identify the correctness of student responses to these subtasks. We then develop an IRT framework that estimates question/subtask difficulty from text, tracks students' evolving abilities across question-answering and dialogue interactions, and a novel method to leverages tutor moves between consecutive subtasks to regularize their difficulty values. We evaluate our framework on two real-world math education datasets containing both human- and AI-tutored dialogues. Results show that our framework outperforms strong baselines on both question difficulty prediction and response correctness prediction.

</details>


### [130] [One Block Ahead: Promoting Sampler State in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19510)
*Yangyi Shen,Pin-Jui Ku,Nicolai Oswald,Jean-Marie Lemercier,Yonggan Fu,Stefano Ermon,Jure Leskovec,Boris Ginsburg,Ante Jukić*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出免训练的 One Block Ahead (OBA) 解码算法，通过让下一个块的去噪与当前块并行进行，将"块间串行"的开销转化为并行收益；在 DiffusionGemma 上单次前向的 token 数提升 42.3%，单请求去噪吞吐提升 16.3%，仅有轻微精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块扩散语言模型虽可在块内并行去噪多个 token 位置，但块与块之间仍严格串行去噪，块内加速无法消除这一块间串行成本。作者希望直接针对块间串行这一瓶颈，探索块级并发解码的可行性。

Method: 提出 OBA，一种免训练解码算法：在当前块去噪的同时并行去噪下一个块。针对均匀状态扩散 LM（其块去噪进度无法从中间 token 观察），论证并发解码有效的条件——下一个块的 token 必须与对应的采样器状态（包括自条件信号与去噪时间）一起提前推进。同时推导了 break-even 判据，将 OBA 的收益与成对块前向传播的相对代价关联起来。

Result: 在 SOTA 扩散 LM DiffusionGemma 上，OBA 使单次前向传播的 token 数提升 42.3%（15.64→22.25），单请求去噪吞吐提升 16.3%（十个基准的平均值），伴随轻微的精度回退。

Conclusion: 块级并发解码在 uniform-state 扩散 LM 中可行且能带来可观的吞吐收益，其收益受成对块前向代价约束（由 break-even 判据刻画）；OBA 以免训练方式在近乎无损精度下实现了这一加速。

Abstract: Block-diffusion language models denoise a whole block of token positions in parallel so each forward pass can emit many tokens. However, blocks are still denoised in strict sequence, a cost that within-block acceleration leaves untouched. Here, we investigate decoding blocks concurrently and propose One Block Ahead (OBA), a training-free decoding algorithm that denoises the next block alongside the current one. We demonstrate that for uniform-state diffusion LMs, where a block's denoising progress is not visible in its intermediate tokens, concurrent decoding is advantageous if the next block's tokens are promoted together with the corresponding sampler state, including the self-conditioning signal and the denoising time. We also derive a break-even criterion that relates the benefit of OBA to the relative cost of a paired-block forward pass. On DiffusionGemma, a state-of-the-art diffusion LM, OBA increases the tokens per forward pass by 42.3% (from 15.64 to 22.25) and the single-request denoising throughput by 16.3%, averaged over ten benchmarks, with a small accuracy regression.

</details>


### [131] [Vision-Language Models Integrate Conflicting Evidence Like Biased Bayesian Observers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19407)
*Josh Barua,Kyle Mahowald,William Rudman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从贝叶斯视角提出双参数线索组合模型，解释视觉语言模型在文本与图像冲突时的偏好行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究仅用"模态偏好"（如"盲目相信文本"）描述视觉语言模型在文本与图像冲突时的行为，但无法解释为何这种偏好会随模型、领域和输入条件变化。

Method: 从贝叶斯角度建模，假设视觉语言模型将来自各模态的条件独立证据与先验信念相结合，并受模态特异性偏差影响，构建双参数线索组合模型。

Result: 该双参数模型能准确预测视觉语言模型在未见过的输入退化组合下的偏好，且无需重新拟合即可迁移到新的不可靠形式上。

Conclusion: 将证据强度与整合偏差分离，可解释视觉语言模型为何既能遵循文本又在更高权重上使用视觉证据，从而在单一组合规则下统一了文本主导与视觉主导的既有发现。

Abstract: Vision-language models must reconcile conflicting text and images. Prior work has characterized the resulting behavior in terms of modality biases--e.g., a "blind faith in text"--but why preferences vary across models, domains, and input conditions remains unclear. We develop a predictive account from a Bayesian perspective, positing that VLMs combine conditionally independent evidence from each modality with prior beliefs, subject to modality-specific biases. The resulting two-parameter cue combination model accurately predicts VLM preferences under unseen combinations of input degradation and transfers to new forms of unreliability without refitting. Separating evidence strength from integration bias explains how VLMs can follow text even while weighting visual evidence more heavily, reconciling reports of text and vision dominance under a single combination rule.

</details>


### [132] [Choosing the Number of Attention Heads from Intrinsic Dimension: A Small-Scale Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19966)
*Arjun Boya,Charan Sai Bandaru,Masthanaiah Pillimetla,Kavana Chidanandappa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种通过无标签估计token表征内在维度来确定Transformer注意力头数的启发式方法，在小规模实验中验证10头与32头性能统计等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力头数通常依赖惯例或网格搜索设定，缺乏有原则的预设方法，作者希望用表征内在维度作为设定头数的先验依据。

Method: 用嵌套dropout VAE在DistilBERT对WikiText-2的嵌入上估计内在维度，依据VAE率失真曲线拐点选取KL权重，得到k*=9.2（95% CI [8.6, 9.8]）；在从头训练的4层小型Transformer上对比10头与32头，并在SST-2与MRPC上用10个种子的配对TOST（±1分边界）检验，同时比较从头训练10头与训练32头再剪枝到10头的效果。

Result: 10头与32头在两个任务上统计等价，且10头模型注意力参数减少69%；从头训练10头略优于训练32头后剪枝到10头（高0.6分，p=0.008）。

Conclusion: 证据范围有限（两个分类任务、一个小架构、无预训练模型、内在维度与头数间无形式化关联），应将该结果视为值得在更大规模上检验的实用启发式，而非一般性原理。

Abstract: The number of attention heads in a transformer is usually set by convention or grid search. We test a simple heuristic for setting it in advance: estimate the intrinsic dimension of token representations without labels, and use roughly that many heads. We estimate intrinsic dimension with a nested-dropout VAE trained on DistilBERT embeddings of WikiText-2, choosing the KL weight at the knee of the VAE's rate-distortion curve rather than by hand. This gives $k^*=9.2$ (95% CI [8.6, 9.8]). In a small 4-layer transformer trained from scratch, 10 heads and 32 heads are statistically equivalent on SST-2 and on MRPC (paired TOST over 10 seeds, $\pm1$ point margin), with 69% fewer attention parameters for the 10-head model. Training 10 heads from the start also slightly outperforms training 32 heads and pruning to 10 (0.6 points, $p=0.008$). The evidence is narrow: two classification tasks, one small architecture, no pretrained models, and no formal link between intrinsic dimension and head count. We present the result as a practical heuristic worth testing at larger scale, not as a general principle.

</details>


### [133] [Whose Data, Whose Benchmark? A User-Centric Framework for Auditing Evaluation Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19268)
*Mir Tafseer Nayeem,Davood Rafiei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个以用户为中心的基准审计框架，区分“内容贡献者”与“结果所代表的目标用户”，并从基准内容、元数据、文档与评分流程四个层面操作化七个维度，对16个资源进行审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准分数在衡量模型能力时，常常掩盖了“谁的惯例、语境与判断”定义了成功表现这一问题，导致评估结果的代表性与主张的支持范围不透明。

Method: 构建以用户为中心的审计框架：区分源用户（贡献基准内容者）与目标用户（评估结果意图代表者）；在基准内容、元数据、文档、评分程序中操作化七个维度；对16个资源开展审计，并结合配对评分实验检验语义保持型答案变体对分数的影响。

Result: 11个文本基准中，美国中心惯例占匹配信号的62.4%-94.4%；5个图文资源的地理可见性集中，且与国家GDP份额强相关（Pearson r均值=0.780；Spearman ρ=0.778）。文档层面存在系统性“源-目标差距”：16个资源均操作化了源用户语境，但无一操作化目标用户语境。评分实验中，语义保持变体使DROP精确匹配从100.0%降至33.3%，TruthfulQA则出现指标依赖的参考相似度下降。

Conclusion: 该框架与文档检查清单使用户假设可审计，有助于明确基准分数究竟代表什么，以及评估主张在何处得到支持。

Abstract: Benchmarks shape how model capabilities are measured and interpreted, yet their scores can obscure whose conventions, contexts, and judgments define successful performance. We introduce a user-centric audit framework that distinguishes users who contribute benchmark content from those whose needs evaluation results are intended to represent. The framework operationalizes seven dimensions across benchmark content, metadata, documentation, and scoring procedures. Across 16 resources, we find that American-centered conventions account for 62.4-94.4% of matched signals in 11 text benchmarks, while geographic visibility in five image-text resources is concentrated and strongly associated with national GDP share (mean Pearson r = 0.780; Spearman p = 0.778). Documentation reveals a systematic source-target gap: all 16 resources operationalize source-user context, but none operationalize target-user context. Paired scoring experiments further show that meaning-preserving answer variants reduce DROP exact-match accuracy from 100.0% to 33.3%, while TruthfulQA exhibits metric-dependent reductions in reference similarity. Our framework and documentation checklist make user assumptions auditable, clarifying what benchmark scores represent and where evaluation claims are supported.

</details>


### [134] [Beyond Reachability: A Relation-Semantic Reasoning Framework for Biomedical Multi-hop QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19754)
*Ziyang Ling,Ziyi Chen,Ronald X. Xu,Mingzhai Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对生物医学知识图谱多跳推理中的"可达但无效"问题，提出ReSemReason框架，通过问题诱导的类型化关系模式分布共享候选发现与证据选择，并引入ReSemAttn实现关系条件化注意力与不确定性加权路径先验，从而提升答案排序与答案集质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物医学知识图谱多跳推理方法虽然能检索到正确答案，但在大量看似相关的候选中排序效果差；即使检索到正确答案，其证据与推理路径也可能无法支持问题所需的关系组合。作者将这种"答案可达性与证据有效性之间的脱节"称为 reachable-but-invalid（可达但无效）问题，旨在同时保证答案的正确性与证据有效性。

Method: 提出ReSemReason框架：1）在候选发现与证据选择之间共享一个由问题诱导的、针对类型化有向关系模式的分布，利用模式兼容性指导图搜索；2）设计ReSemAttn注意力机制，采用关系条件化的键/值投影与不确定性加权路径先验，对候选特定证据进行注意力计算；3）引入独立的绝对支持分数（absolute-support score），在所有检索路径都较弱时对归一化注意力进行补充。

Result: 在 BioHopR、PrimeKGQA 和 MedReason 三个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在答案排序和答案集质量上均有提升；并通过路径区分性分析考察了终点正确性与证据支持之间的区别，同时为搜索、注意力与模式不确定性设置了匹配的对照实验。

Conclusion: ReSemReason通过统一问题诱导关系模式分布、关系条件化注意力与绝对支持分数，有效缓解了生物医学多跳推理中"可达但无效"的问题，在保持答案可达的同时提升证据有效性与排序质量。

Abstract: Biomedical multi-hop reasoning over knowledge graphs requires identifying a semantically valid relation sequence that supports the answer. Existing methods can retrieve correct answers, yet often rank them poorly within a large pool of seemingly relevant candidates. Even when a correct answer is retrieved, its associated evidence and reasoning path may fail to support the relation composition required by the question. We term this disconnect between answer reachability and evidential validity the reachable-but-invalid problem. We present ReSemReason, which shares a question-induced distribution over typed, directed relation schemas between candidate discovery and evidence selection. Schema compatibility guides graph search; ReSemAttn then uses relation-conditioned key/value projections and an uncertainty-weighted path prior to attend to candidate-specific evidence. A separate absolute-support score complements normalized attention when all retrieved paths are weak. The reported experiments on BioHopR, PrimeKGQA, and MedReason show improvements in answer ranking and answer-set quality. Path-discrimination analyses examine the distinction between endpoint correctness and evidential support. We also specify matched controls for search, attention, and schema uncertainty.

</details>


### [135] [Does the Linguistic Register of Thinking Matter? Wenyan as a Compact Reasoning Register for Chinese LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19741)
*Minghan Li,Guodong Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Wenyan Thinking，将推理中间过程改写为文言文，同时保留简短直接答案，从而在有限解码预算下提升中文推理大模型的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文推理大模型的可见推理链通常以现代白话文书写，在有限解码预算下可能过于冗长，论文探究语言语体（register）能否用于提升推理效率。

Method: 从现代中文推理轨迹出发，仅将推理部分改写为文言文，并在改写后的监督数据上微调 Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507；同时用相同源数据、教师家族、基座模型和 SFT 配方构建紧凑现代中文对照组，以区分语体转换与通用压缩的贡献。

Result: 在 MMLU-ProX-Lite-ZH、CEVAL 和 AGIEval-CN 的预算敏感评测中，Wenyan Thinking 显著缩短推理长度并降低受限解码下的缺答率；紧凑改写可解释大部分效率收益，而文言文在主基准上稳定优于紧凑现代中文对照组，并在其他基准上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 语言语体是提升中文大模型高效可见推理的一个实用设计维度。

Abstract: Visible reasoning traces of Chinese reasoning-oriented LLMs are typically written in modern vernacular Chinese, which may be verbose under limited decoding budgets. We ask whether the linguistic register of reasoning can be used to improve efficiency. We propose Wenyan Thinking, which emits intermediate reasoning in Classical Chinese (wenyanwen) while retaining a short direct-answer output. Starting from modern-Chinese reasoning traces, we rewrite only the reasoning field into wenyan and fine-tune Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507 on the rewritten supervision. To distinguish register shift from generic compression, we construct a compact modern-Chinese control using the same source data, teacher family, base model, and SFT recipe. Across budget-sensitive evaluations on MMLU-ProX-Lite-ZH, CEVAL, and AGIEval-CN, Wenyan Thinking substantially reduces reasoning length and missing-answer rates under constrained decoding. Compact rewriting explains much of the efficiency gain, while wenyan consistently outperforms the compact modern-Chinese control on the primary benchmark and remains competitive across the additional benchmarks. These results identify linguistic register as a practical design axis for efficient visible reasoning in Chinese LLMs.

</details>


### [136] [MakSafe: A Spatiotemporal Semantic Framework for Formal Specification and Compliance Checking of Road Vehicle Safety Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19735)
*Maksym Labzhaniia,Julien Deantoni,Marie-Agn{è}s Peraldi-Frati,Fr{é}d{é}ric Mallet*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MakSafe：一个面向道路车辆安全规则的时空形式化框架，通过语义区域、基于动作的预测与迹树推理进行规则指定与检查，避免统一时间离散化，并给出分支特定判定与可扩展性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于场景的验证对自动驾驶车辆至关重要，但难以覆盖开放世界交通的全部变异性；且传统方法依赖统一时间离散化，在连续时空变化的交通场景中表达与推理能力受限。

Method: 构建形式化框架 MakSafe：形式化道路网络、车辆状态与占用区域（语义区域），采用基于动作的预测与迹树推理，建立可执行规则，包括前车识别规则与碰撞时间（TTC）验证规则。

Result: 评估包含依赖于轨迹的案例研究与针对交通密度 N、时域 H、分支因子 B 的可扩展性基准；MakSafe 能给出分支特定判定，从同一初始场景推导出安全与不安全结果，并可在控制更新后重新评估场景；在报告范围内单条规则检查耗时远低于 1 毫秒，时域越长 TTC 检查越慢。

Conclusion: MakSafe 可在不采用统一时间离散化的前提下对道路车辆安全规则进行形式化指定与高效检查，支持轨迹分支级别的安全判定与场景重评估，兼具表达力与毫秒级检查效率。

Abstract: Scenario-based validation is essential for automated road vehicles but cannot cover the full variability of openworld traffic. We present MakSafe, a formal spatiotemporal framework for specifying and checking road vehicle safety rules through semantic zones, action-based forecasting, and trace-tree reasoning, avoiding uniform time discretization. We formalize the road network, vehicle states, and occupancy zones, then build executable rules for front-vehicle identification and timeto-collision (TTC) verification. The evaluation combines a tracedependent case study with a scalability benchmark over traffic density (N ), horizon (H), and branch factor (B). MakSafe yields branch-specific verdicts, deriving safe and unsafe outcomes from the same initial scene, and re-evaluates a scenario after a control update. A single rule check stays well below a millisecond across the reported sweep, and longer horizons slow the TTC check.

</details>


### [137] [Recognizing Dialogue Ambiguity via Candidate Set Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19723)
*Shun Inadumi,Lee Sangmyeong,Koichiro Yoshino*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出候选集视觉定位（CSVG）任务与CandVG基准，评估视觉语言模型在多轮对话中维护候选指代对象集合的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实交互中理解歧义话语需累积上下文，且在视觉场景中指代对象时应在信息不唯一时持续维护并更新可能的候选指代集合，而非过早假定唯一指代。现有交互式视觉定位研究仅关注上下文充分后定位唯一对象，忽略了对候选集合的建模。

Method: 提出候选集视觉定位（CSVG）任务，估计与给定对话上下文一致的物体集合；构建CandVG基准，评估候选集合随对话轮次的变化；对多种视觉语言模型进行评测，并测试针对CSVG设计的指令提示的效果。

Result: 实验表明，更强的视觉定位能力并不必然带来更好的CSVG表现；模型难以预测完整候选集，主要受限于候选生成困难以及过早收敛到少量候选对象；使用针对CSVG设计的指令可在一定程度上缓解该问题。

Conclusion: CSVG任务与CandVG基准揭示了现有视觉语言模型在多轮交互中指代消解的关键缺陷，提示未来需关注候选集合的动态维护，而非仅追求唯一定位精度。

Abstract: Interpreting an ambiguous utterance in real-world interactions often requires context beyond a single utterance. When the utterance refers to objects in a visual scene, it is necessary to maintain and update the set of plausible referents as context accumulates, without assuming that the available information identifies a unique object. However, prior work on interactive visual grounding, which localizes objects referred to by linguistic expressions, has focused on identifying a unique object once sufficient dialogue context becomes available. To address this gap, we propose Candidate Set Visual Grounding (CSVG), a task for estimating the set of objects consistent with the given dialogue context. We further introduce CandVG, a benchmark that evaluates how the candidate set changes across dialogue turns. Our evaluation of vision-language models shows that stronger visual grounding capability does not necessarily yield better CSVG performance. In particular, models have difficulty predicting the complete candidate set, owing to challenges in candidate generation and premature commitment to a small set of candidates in early turns. Nevertheless, we find that this limitation can be partially mitigated in the evaluated large-scale models using instructions designed for CSVG.

</details>


### [138] [HACHIMI-II: From Role-Play to Learner Behavior Modeling for Faithful Student Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19654)
*Yilin Jiang,Fei Tan,Xuanyu Yin,Jing Leng,Aimin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出HACHIMI-II，将模拟学生从"角色扮演"改为"第三人称预测学习者下一步行为"，通过显式学习者状态建模显著提升模拟真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟学生可让研究者在真人学习者到位前测试辅导系统与教学策略，但前提是模拟须复现特定学习者在何处成功与失败。主流方法让大语言模型角色扮演学生，然而"角色"并不建模该学习者知道什么或下一步会做什么，模型自身能力会填补这一空缺，导致模拟学生表现水平由模型决定、彼此差异小、且很少犯真实学习者的错误。

Method: 将学生模拟重新表述为对学习者下一动作的第三人称预测。HACHIMI-II从学习者历史构建学生模型，推断其在当前题目或辅导轮次下的学习者状态，并将其编译为约束下一动作的"行为约束框架"（behavior harness）。

Result: 在三个题目作答数据集上，模拟与真实学生能力的Wasserstein-1距离比最强基线小2.4–8.4倍；在两个对话数据集上，复现真实学生错误（在知识类型、发生位置与机制上一致）的频率比最强基线高1.8–5.1倍；消融实验表明增益主要来自显式学习者状态。

Conclusion: 预测学习者而非角色扮演，能使能力强的模型真正可用作模拟学生。

Abstract: Simulated students let researchers test tutoring systems and teaching strategies before real learners are available, but only if the simulation reproduces where a particular learner succeeds and fails. The common approach asks a large language model to role-play a student, but a role does not model what this learner knows or will do next, so the model's own competence fills the gap. Simulated students perform at a level set by the model, differ little from one another, and seldom make the real learners' errors. We model the learner's behavior and recast student simulation as third-person prediction of the learner's next action. HACHIMI-II builds a student model from the learner's history, infers the learner's state for the current item or tutor turn, and compiles it into a behavior harness that constrains the next action. On three item-response datasets, the Wasserstein-1 distance between simulated and real student ability is 2.4--8.4$\times$ smaller than that of the strongest baseline. On two dialogue datasets, HACHIMI-II reproduces real student errors with the same knowledge type, locus, and mechanism 1.8--5.1$\times$ more often than the strongest baseline, and ablations trace the gains mainly to the explicit learner state. Predicting the learner makes capable models usable as simulated students.

</details>


### [139] [J-tau: A Fully Localized Japanese Benchmark for Tool-Agent-User Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19638)
*Chihiro Yano,Jun Hirako,Hiroaki Yamagiwa,Ryota Hirobuchi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 J-tau，首个公开的日语工具使用型 LLM 智能体客服场景评测基准，通过对 tau-bench 进行机器翻译、实体本地化与人工审校构建，并修复了原基准的无效任务与分数聚合错误两个缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: tau-bench 广泛用于评测 LLM 智能体，但以英语为中心，无法评测其他语言；现有多语言扩展没有公开日语版本，且主要依赖机器翻译，导致地址、货币等本地化实体与目标地区不一致，难以支撑可靠的日语评测。

Method: 对 tau-bench 进行本地化：机器翻译、实体适配（如地址、货币等区域特定实体）与人工审校；同时排查并修复原基准中的两个缺陷——无效任务和会虚高部分模型得分的分数聚合错误。

Result: 实验表明，J-tau 的评测错误少于朴素机器翻译版本，且所提出的修复措施有效解决了原基准的两个缺陷。

Conclusion: J-tau 为日语工具使用型 LLM 智能体提供了公开且可靠的交互式客服评测基准，并通过修复原基准缺陷提升了评测有效性。

Abstract: We present J-tau, the first publicly available Japanese benchmark for evaluating tool-using LLM agents in interactive customer-support scenarios. While tau-bench is widely used to evaluate LLM agents, it is English-centric and cannot evaluate agents in other languages. Existing multilingual extensions offer no public Japanese version and rely primarily on machine translation, leaving locale-specific entities such as addresses and currencies inconsistent with the target region. To enable reliable evaluation in Japanese, we carefully localize tau-bench through machine translation, entity adaptation, and human review. During this process, we also uncover and fix two flaws in the original tau-bench: invalid tasks and a score aggregation error that inflates certain models' results. Experiments show that J-tau yields fewer evaluation errors than a naively machine-translated counterpart and that our fixes resolve both flaws.

</details>


### [140] [Copy-Paste Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19247)
*Nathan Godey,Yoav Artzi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CPLMs，在传统LM head旁增加轻量Copy-Paste head，显式建模复制能力，提升训练效率与下游性能，并在长上下文和检索场景提供新工作点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管直觉上认为Transformer擅长从输入中复制，但本文指出LLM的上下文复制能力可通过显式建模进一步提升。

Method: 在常规语言建模（LM）head基础上，为模型添加一个轻量级的Copy-Paste head，构成Copy-Paste Language Models（CPLMs）。通过该额外路径显式建模复制行为，并实现部分缓解LM head的梯度瓶颈。

Result: 从135M到1.7B参数规模进行预训练验证，在所有规模和骨干网络上均取得提升，且训练与推理开销可忽略；在长上下文和检索设置中提供了新的性能工作点。

Conclusion: CPLMs通过显式复制建模提高了训练效率和下游性能；其成功部分归因于通过替代梯度路径缓解了LM head的梯度瓶颈。

Abstract: Transformers are intuitively considered to be efficient at copying from their inputs. We actually show that the ability of LLMs to copy in context can be improved through explicit modeling. We propose Copy-Paste Language Models (CPLMs), which add a lightweight Copy-Paste head to the conventional language modeling (LM) head. CPLMs improve training efficiency, downstream performance, and offer new operating points in long-context and retrieval setups.
