We show that these desiderata induce a one-to-one correspondence between valid answers and equivalence classes of clarifications, implying that any interpretation can be represented either by its answer or by the clarifications that uniquely express it. We leverage this correspondence to introduce \circleclar{}, a black-box method that estimates the set of answers available to a model by checking consistency along an answer--clarification--answer cycle. Across open-domain QA and executable Text-to-SQL, \circleclar{} achieves strong recovery of these answer sets. In QA, the recovered support yields the strongest overall performance in model-dependent ambiguity detection and enables effective selection among answering, clarifying, and abstaining. Together, these results demonstrate the practical value of a model-dependent view of ambiguity and its characterization through an answer-clarification correspondence.

</details>


### [214] [REWIND: Uncertainty-Guided Trajectory Repair for Test-Time Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19561)
*Yi Ding,Mengyu Wang,Min Zhou,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出免训练控制器REWIND，利用熵动态定位推理错误并做定向后缀重生成，在多模型多基准上超过所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推理中早期错误会约束后续所有推理；现有MCMC类重复生成方法随机选择重生成位置，效率受限，作者希望找到能指示推理错误的信号来指导重生成。

Method: 在MATH500上用Qwen2.5-Math-7B观察发现错误回答的平均token熵显著高于正确回答（问题内相对增加约112%），据此设计免训练控制器REWIND，依据熵动态提出定向的后缀重生成（suffix regeneration）。

Result: 在三个模型家族、五个基准上，REWIND优于所有基线，相较最强基线提升0.51-10.00个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明基于不确定性引导的定向轨迹修复（targeted uncertainty-guided trajectory repair）跨模型、跨任务均有效。

Abstract: An early mistake in the generated reasoning can constrain all subsequent reasoning. Repeated-generation methods, like MCMC-based approaches, attempt to improve output quality by sampling multiple generations, but their effectiveness can be limited by randomly selecting where to regenerate. On MATH500 with Qwen2.5-Math-7B, we find that incorrect responses have substantially higher mean token entropy than correct responses, with an average within-question relative increase of approximately 112%, suggesting that entropy can signal reasoning errors. Motivated by this finding, we introduce REWIND, a training-free controller that uses entropy dynamics to propose targeted suffix regenerations. Across three model families and five benchmarks, REWIND outperforms all baselines, with gains of 0.51-10.00 percentage points over the strongest baseline. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of targeted, uncertainty-guided trajectory repair across models and tasks. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/rewind-D310.

</details>


### [215] [CSWBench: Evaluating Context-Sensitive Code-Switching in Creative Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20272)
*Zewen Jiang,Yahan Li,Chenlu Guo,Diana Inkpen,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 CSWBench 基准与 CSWEval 评估框架，用于评测大语言模型在创意写作中语境敏感的语码转换能力，并发现现有模型表现存在明显但不均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽具备多语言能力，但有效的语码转换不止于多语言水平，还需要模型判断何时、何地、为何切换语言以契合语境。这种语境敏感能力目前缺乏充分评测。

Method: 提出 CSWBench 基准，包含聚焦叙事与扩展叙事两种互补设置；并提出理论驱动的 CSWEval 框架，含事件级评估（Licensing、Contextual Appropriateness、Functional Gain）与整篇级评估（聚焦叙事的整体文学与叙事质量、扩展叙事的多语言组织质量）。对十二个 LLM 进行评测。

Result: 模型在叙事语境中表现出有意义但不均衡的语码转换能力；模型差异在多语言文学整合方面尤为显著；多语言相关维度整体上比一般叙事维度呈现更强的难度效应；叙事显著性的转换伴随更强的语境与功能实现；事件级语码转换质量与整篇表现呈中等相关。

Conclusion: 语境敏感的语码转换是一类独立于多语言熟练度的能力，需通过事件级与整篇级多层次框架评估；CSWBench 与 CSWEval 可作为该方向的评测基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated multilingual capabilities across diverse language generation and understanding tasks. Yet effective code-switching requires more than multilingual proficiency: models must decide when, where, and why to switch languages in ways that fit the surrounding context. This context-sensitive ability remains insufficiently evaluated. We introduce CSWBench, a benchmark for evaluating context-sensitive code-switching in creative writing through two complementary settings: Focused Narrative and Extended Narrative. We further propose CSWEval, a theory-informed framework with two complementary levels: event-level evaluation of Licensing, Contextual Appropriateness, and Functional Gain, and whole-text evaluation of task-specific quality---overall literary and narrative quality in Focused Narrative and quality emphasizing multilingual organization in Extended Narrative. Evaluating twelve LLMs, we find that models exhibit meaningful but uneven code-switching competence across narrative contexts. Model differences are particularly pronounced in multilingual literary integration, while multilingual-focused dimensions show stronger difficulty effects overall than the general narrative dimensions; narratively salient switches are associated with stronger contextual and functional realization; and event-level CS quality is moderately associated with whole-text performance.

</details>


### [216] [ChronoCulture: Evaluating Cultural Awareness Across Time in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20472)
*Rusali Saha,Nitya Khurana,Ruihong Huang,Kuan-Hao Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文提出ChronoCulture基准，用72国纵向调查数据构建10,360个时间配对，检验LLM能否捕捉文化态度的历时变化；结果显示模型能预测某一时点的态度，却难以刻画其变化，即便提示年份也改善有限，表明模型对文化持近乎静态的刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向大语言模型的文化基准多把文化视为国家或地区的静态属性，忽视了文化态度与社会规范的历时演化。作者希望填补"跨时间文化意识"评估的空白。

Method: 构建ChronoCulture基准：基于72个国家的纵向调查数据，生成10,360个时间配对。每对包含同一国家、同一调查问题在较早年份与较晚年份的版本，并以完整的人口响应分布作为ground truth。随后在六个LLM上进行评测，并考察在提示中指定目标年份是否提升与调查响应的对齐度。

Result: 六个模型通常能较准确地预测某国在单一时间点的态度，但无法可靠捕捉态度的变化方向与幅度；模型对不同年份的预测彼此接近，未能覆盖观测到的多数变化。在提示中明确年份对整体对齐度提升甚微。

Conclusion: LLM倾向于呈现一种近乎静态的文化态度图景，缺乏对文化随时间演变的理解。作者据此指出，文化意识的评估应当纳入时间维度。

Abstract: Cultural attitudes and social norms evolve over time, yet most cultural benchmarks for large language models (LLMs) treat culture as a static attribute of a country or region. We introduce ChronoCulture, a benchmark of 10,360 temporal pairs built from longitudinal survey data across 72 countries. Each pair contains the same survey question asked in the same country in an older and a newer year, with full population response distributions as ground truth. Across six LLMs, we find that models can often predict a country's attitudes at one point in time but do not reliably capture how those attitudes change. Their predictions for different years tend to stay close together, failing to capture much of the observed change. Specifying the intended year in the prompt also offers little overall improvement in alignment with survey responses. These findings suggest that models often present a largely static picture of cultural attitudes, highlighting the need to evaluate cultural awareness across time.

</details>


### [217] [PrismaCoder: Pushing the Boundaries of Open Multimodal Code Intelligence through Decomposed Rubric Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20538)
*Qiushi Sun,Jingyang Gong,Yang Liu,Qiaosheng Chen,Zhangyue Yin,Zixian Huang,Hanxu Hu,Jing Xiong,Fangzhi Xu,Haochen Tan,Haoli Bai,Lifeng Shang,Xiaoguang Li,Ben Kao,Qipeng Guo,Lingpeng Kong,Jiahui Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出层次化数据流水线 PrismaForge（以合成评分量表为核心，将样本质量分解为可核查标准，兼作过滤与奖励），构建了迄今最大、最多样的多模态代码智能数据集 PrismaCoder-2M，并训练发布 PrismaCoder-9B 与 PrismaCoder-35B，性能大幅领先开源模型并可比肩领先闭源系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态代码智能需求增长，但闭源模型的训练数据与方法不公开，阻碍开源进展。开源受限于数据数量、质量及相应流水线。其中质量尤为困难：样本需同时满足程序正确、构建良好、渲染后视觉正确三个条件，而现有信号（执行反馈仅对错、代码相似度与视觉输出不对应、任务特定验证器不泛化）均无法同时覆盖这三方面。

Method: 从数据侧切入，提出 PrismaForge 层次化数据流水线，其核心是合成的评分量表（rubric），将质量因素分解为可核查的标准；该量表同时充当过滤器和奖励：先用于筛选监督语料，再驱动视觉编码任务的强化学习。基于该流水线构建 PrismaCoder-2M 数据集，并训练 PrismaCoder-9B 与 PrismaCoder-35B。

Result: 构建了迄今最多样、规模最大的多模态代码智能开源数据集 PrismaCoder-2M；训练并发布了 PrismaCoder-9B 和 PrismaCoder-35B，大幅领先开源最先进水平，并可媲美领先的闭源系统。分析表明，基于量表的流水线能提升不同骨干模型的多模态编码性能。

Conclusion: 基于评分量表的数据合成与过滤流水线可有效提升多模态编码性能，且具备跨模型骨干的通用性；代码与数据已在项目主页 https://qiushisun.github.io/PrismaCoder-Home/ 开源。

Abstract: Multimodal code intelligence connects the symbolic world of programs with the visual world their execution creates. Interest in this capability is burgeoning, as models are increasingly asked to produce charts, webpages, animations, and semi-structured documents. Recently, proprietary models have pushed far ahead, producing interactive artifacts even from a text description or a screenshot alone. Yet their data and training remain closed, and open progress is held back by the quantity and quality of available data and by the pipelines and methods for producing it. Quality is the harder of the two, since a sample must be correct as a program, well built, and above all visually right once rendered. No existing signal covers all three. Execution often returns just a pass or fail, code similarity does not map to the visual output, and task-specific verifiers do not generalize. To push open progress further, we start from the data side with PrismaForge, a hierarchical data pipeline centered on a synthesized rubric that decomposes these factors into checkable criteria. The same rubric serves as both filter and reward: it curates the supervised corpus, then drives reinforcement learning on visual-coding tasks. Leveraging PrismaForge for data synthesis and filtering, we build PrismaCoder-2M, the most diverse open dataset for multimodal code intelligence and the largest to date. On the model side, we train PrismaCoder-9B and PrismaCoder-35B on this data and release both. They lead the open state of the art by a wide margin and rival leading proprietary systems. Our analyses further show that the proposed rubric-based pipeline improves multimodal coding performance across model backbones. We release our code and data at https://qiushisun.github.io/PrismaCoder-Home/.

</details>


### [218] [The COUNT: Counting N-grams Unlocks Scaling on Host Memory and Training-Free Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20456)
*Ian Magnusson,Noah A. Smith,Pang Wei Koh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: COUNT 提出一种免学习参数的 n-gram 嵌入方法，将预计算的 n-gram 计数存于主机内存，通过语料频率加权 LM 自身 unembedding，实现独立于计算量的内存扩展，提升语言模型性能并支持推理时无训练域适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的算力-性能缩放关系已被广泛研究，近期研究开始关注内存缩放。增加 n-gram 嵌入可使内存扩展与算力扩展解耦（每次前向只读取少量嵌入行）。但现有方法从零训练嵌入，需要昂贵的 GPU 显存用于快速读写，而主机系统的普通内存廉价且在这些训练方法中未被充分利用。因此作者希望利用主机内存实现内存扩展。

Method: 提出 COUNT（Counting Offline Unlocks N-gram Transformers）：不添加任何可学习参数来构建 n-gram 嵌入，而是将预计算的 n-gram 计数存储在主机内存中。利用语料频率对语言模型自身的 unembedding（即最常跟随当前 n-gram 历史的下一个 token 的 unembedding）进行加权组合，作为额外的输入嵌入。

Result: 在固定算力预算下，COUNT 可作为额外输入嵌入加入 1B 参数语言模型的预训练，其验证损失改善幅度相当于算力多 57% 的模型。当 GPU 显存受限时，用 COUNT 让主干参数占满 GPU 显存，比将显存分配给额外可学习 n-gram 嵌入的表现更好。此外，COUNT 可在推理时切换到来自特定领域语料的不同 n-gram 计数表，无需任何训练即可适应新任务。

Conclusion: COUNT 通过将 n-gram 计数存于主机内存、复用模型自身 unembedding，实现了免学习参数且低成本的内存扩展，在固定算力下显著提升性能，显存受限时优于可学习 n-gram 嵌入，并支持零训练推理时域适应。

Abstract: The scaling relationship of compute and performance for language models (LMs) has been extensively studied; more recently, models are being designed to scale memory use, as well. In particular, adding embeddings for n-grams scales memory separately from compute use, because each pass only processes individual embedding rows, which are much smaller than the storage used for the whole embedding table. However, existing methods train embeddings from scratch, which needs expensive GPU memory for fast reading and writing. Meanwhile, the ordinary memory of the host system is cheap and underutilized by these training methods. To scale on host memory, we introduce COUNT (Counting Offline Unlocks N-gram Transformers), which builds n-gram embeddings without adding learned parameters by storing precomputed n-gram counts on host. We use the corpus frequencies to weight together the LM's own unembeddings of the next tokens most often following the current n-gram history. At a fixed compute budget, COUNT can be added as an additional input embedding for pretraining 1B-parameter LMs to improve validation loss as much as an LM with 57% more compute. If GPU memory is limited, using COUNT to let backbone parameters fill GPU memory gives better performance than splitting memory with additional learned n-gram embeddings. Beyond efficiency, COUNT allows switching in a different table of n-gram counts from a domain-specific corpus at inference time to adapt to a new task without any training.

</details>


### [219] [Cascaded Retrieval with Cross-Modal Query Refinement for Efficient Visual Document Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20367)
*Diana Turmakhan,Aseem Srivastava,Vivek Gupta,Iryna Gurevych,Thamar Solorio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SCOUT是一种级联双轨检索框架，通过轻量父检索器过滤候选池后重排，并结合离线层次摘要的语义轨与跨模态查询精炼融合，在4个基准上实现最高80.7倍延迟降低并超越SOTA检索性能最多7.8% NDCG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉丰富文档检索需匹配包含表格、图表及布局依赖结构的页面，现有视觉语言检索器查询时间成本随语料规模增长，难以规模化扩展。作者希望将检索性能与穷举搜索解耦。

Method: 提出SCOUT级联双轨检索框架：视觉轨先用轻量父检索器将语料过滤为紧凑候选池，再仅对候选应用更强的多向量重排器；语义轨并行地在离线生成的层次化页面与文档摘要上检索，提供与视觉匹配互补的信号；两轨输出通过跨模态查询精炼融合，用语义分数调整视觉查询表示后再最终排序。

Result: 在4个主要基准上与6个基线比较，查询时延相对最佳基线最高提升80.7倍（平均28.8倍），同时在所有基准上检索性能超越SOTA最多7.8% NDCG。

Conclusion: SCOUT通过级联双轨与跨模态查询精炼，同时显著改善检索效率与效果，验证了将检索性能与穷举搜索分离的可行性。

Abstract: Retrieving information from visually rich documents requires matching queries against pages that contain complex evidence in tables, figures, and layout-dependent structures. Current vision-language retrievers address this but incur query-time costs that grow with corpus size and are impractical to scale. We propose SCOUT, a cascaded two-track retrieval framework that separates retrieval performance from exhaustive search. The visual track first uses a lightweight parent retriever to filter the corpus to a compact candidate pool, then applies a stronger multi-vector reranker only to those candidates. In parallel, a semantic track retrieves over hierarchical page and document summaries generated offline to provide a signal complementary to visual matching. The outputs are fused through cross-modal query refinement, which uses semantic scores to adjust the visual query representation before final ranking. We evaluate SCOUT on 4 major benchmarks against 6 baselines and observe a significant query-time latency improvement up to 80.7x (28.8x on average) relative to the best baseline, while exceeding state-of-the-art retrieval performance by up to 7.8% NDCG across all benchmarks.

</details>


### [220] [AdaRSI: On-Demand Recursive Self-Improvement for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20474)
*Peidong Wang,Ying Chang,Zhiming Ma,Xiaocui Yang,Shi Feng,Daqi Liu,Yuzhe Yang,Xu Cao,Chuang Wang,Jian Su,Daling Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AdaRSI按需式具身递归自改进框架：先让编码智能体改进技能与harness，再通过从保存状态重试失败子步骤进行故障归因，仅对持续不可靠的子步骤进行微调，从而在RoboCasa365上以更低成本提升VLA长程任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）在未见过的长程任务中常中途失败。现有具身RSI方法存在两类局限：一类仅优化冻结执行器外围的harness，无法补充缺失的运动能力；另一类直接在持续失败上训练执行器，却不区分失败源于"执行器被调用的方式"还是"执行器自身能力"。因此需要回答：自改进具身系统何时应更新执行器权重。

Method: AdaRSI按需式RSI框架：编码智能体先改进自身技能与harness；随后通过对失败子步骤从保存状态重试进行故障归因；仅对仍不可靠的子步骤进行微调，训练示范由系统自行采集，并复用早前任务积累的经验。

Result: 在RoboCasa365全部50个目标任务上，以GR00T N1.5为执行器、不使用人类示范，AdaRSI在三个套件上均提升VLA，将未见复合任务成功率从4.6%提升至13.8%；相比逐子步骤全量微调，成功率略高，但仅用28%的GPU时间和22%的自采集示范；而无harness的微调训练更多却收益远低。

Conclusion: 具身自改进应按需更新执行器权重：先归因失败来源，仅对持续不可靠子步骤微调，可在更低算力与数据成本下获得更高长程任务成功率。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language-action models (VLAs) often fail midway through unseen long-horizon tasks. Recursive self-improvement (RSI) offers a remedy, but embodied RSI methods either refine the harness around a frozen executor and so cannot add a missing motion, or train the executor on persistent failures without asking whether a failure lies in how the executor is called or in what it can do. We ask when a self-improving embodied system should change the weights of its executor, and propose AdaRSI, an on-demand RSI framework for embodied agents. A coding agent first improves its skills and harness, then attributes persistent failures by retrying failing sub-steps from saved states, and fine-tunes only the sub-steps that remain unreliable, with demonstrations it collects itself and experience carried over from earlier tasks. On all 50 target tasks of RoboCasa365, with GR00T N1.5 as the executor and no human demonstrations, AdaRSI improves the VLA on all three suites and raises its success on unseen composite tasks from 4.6% to 13.8%. Compared with fine-tuning every sub-step, it reaches slightly higher success while using only 28% of the GPU time and 22% of the self-collected demonstrations, and fine-tuning without a harness trains more yet gains far less.

</details>


### [221] [Equal CER, Unequal Damage: Severity-Weighted Character Error Rate for OCR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20461)
*Nguyen Nguyen,Yongyi Zang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出semCER，一种保留CER对齐与分母、但用LLM评判器为每个字形编辑赋予严重程度的度量，将整体评分细化为可命名的编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CER对OCR和视觉语言模型而言，把拼写错误与关键错误（如人名写错）等同计费，无法反映不同编辑对读者体验的真实影响。

Method: semCER沿用CER的对齐方式与分母，不依赖分词，借助LLM评判器为每个字形编辑打一个严重程度分值；归一化CER保留整体转录得分，semCER再将其分解为具名编辑。

Result: 归一化CER在整体评分上与读者判断相当或更好，semCER可将其细化为具名编辑；编辑级评分在英、越、中三种语言中与读者评分一致，并将读者认为最严重的编辑排在列表顶部。在CER与semCER不一致的样本上，读者在英语和中文中更常支持semCER，与归一化CER持平的情况出现在越南语。跨16种语言，评判器对西班牙语错误变音符号几乎不计分，而泰卢固语中错误音调标记几乎等同于错误字母的严重度。在可统计真实词处，通用VLM的错误字母远高于随机水平，经典流水线则接近随机。

Conclusion: semCER作为更细粒度、更贴合读者感知的字形编辑严重度度量，提供了编辑级可解释性，并发布了6100万条评判编辑、聚合读者评分及蒸馏评分器。

Abstract: Character Error Rate (CER), the standard metric for OCR and vision-language models (VLMs), charges a typo as much as a wrong name. We introduce semCER, which keeps CER's alignment and denominator and charges each grapheme edit a severity from an LLM judge, with no word segmentation. Normalized CER keeps the score of a whole transcription, where it tracks readers as well as semCER or better, and semCER breaks that score down into named edits. The charges track readers' ratings edit by edit in English, Vietnamese, and Chinese and put the edits readers rate most severe near the top of a sorted list. On pairs drawn where CER and semCER disagreed, readers side with semCER more often than with normalized CER in English and Chinese, and equally often in Vietnamese. Across 16 languages, the judge charges a wrong diacritic or tone mark almost nothing in Spanish but nearly a wrong letter's severity in Telugu. Where we can count real words, a general VLM's wrong letter forms one far above chance while a classical pipeline's stays near it. We release the 61 million judged edits, the aggregated reader ratings, and the distilled scorer at https://github.com/nguyenx2t/semcer.

</details>


### [222] [Lexical Persistence Without Semantic Entrenchment in AI Agent Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20365)
*Chih-Han Lin,Yu-Yun Chang,Po-Ya Angela Wang,Shu-Kai Hsieh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究比较AI代理社区（MoltBook）与人类语言社区（PTT、Reddit）的语义时间结构，发现AI社区语义漂移更大、邻域稳定性更低、漂移轨迹缺乏长程时间依赖，表明词汇扩散并不必然带来历时语义固化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有观察显示AI代理社区能维持词汇持续性、热门词扩散与广泛采用，但尚不清楚其是否也能形成人类语言社区所特有的时间语义结构（如语义漂移的记忆性）。作者希望检验词汇层面的传播是否会伴随语义层面的历时固化，并区分"词汇扩散"与"语义整合"两种现象。

Method: 采用Compass-Aligned Distributional Embeddings (CADE)方法，对三类语料构建对齐的分布式嵌入：AI代理社区MoltBook（8周）、中文人类论坛PTT（19年）、英文人类论坛Reddit（匹配的8周窗口）。测量三项指标：语义漂移（B1）、邻域稳定性（B2）、以及逐词漂移轨迹的滞后1自相关（lag-1 autocorrelation）。并以2025年新成立的r/vibecoding作为社区年龄的对照。

Result: 各平台上滞后1自相关构成一条"记忆视界"连续谱：AI代理结果聚集于低端，人类平台表现出更长程的时间依赖。MoltBook的B1漂移约为两个人类平台的2倍，B2稳定性低20–30%。作为社区年龄的部分对照，r/vibecoding在其最初8周（0.082, p<.001）及15周（0.220, p<.001）均显示显著ACF，而MoltBook的ACF不显著（p=0.41），说明该差距不能仅归因于发展阶段。

Conclusion: 该模式表明仅靠词汇扩散不足以保证历时语义固化。结果符合这样一种对比：某些社区的语义轨迹累积了长程交互约束，而另一些社区的输出来自基本固定的预训练分布的反复再生。

Abstract: AI agent communities sustain lexical persistence, popular terms spread, recur, and achieve broad adoption, but whether they also develop the temporal semantic structure characteristic of human linguistic communities remains open. We compare MoltBook (AI agent community, 8 weeks), PTT (Chinese human BBS, 19 years), and Reddit (English human forum, in matched 8-week windows) using Compass-Aligned Distributional Embeddings (CADE), measuring semantic drift (B1), neighborhood stability (B2), and lag-1 autocorrelation of per-word drift trajectories. Across the platforms, lag-1 autocorrelation forms a memory-horizon continuum. AI agents' result clusters at the low end, while human platforms show longer-range temporal dependence. MoltBook exhibits approximately 2$\times$ the B1 drift and 20-30% lower B2 stability than both human platforms. As a partial control for community age, r/vibecoding (founded 2025-02-08) shows significant ACF in its first 8 weeks ($0.082$, $p<.001$) and over 15 weeks ($0.220$, $p<.001$); MoltBook's ACF remains non-significant ($p = 0.41$), consistent with the gap not reducing to developmental stage alone. We interpret this pattern as evidence that lexical diffusion alone does not guarantee diachronic semantic consolidation. The findings are consistent with a contrast between communities whose semantic trajectories accumulate long-range interaction constraints and communities whose outputs are repeatedly regenerated from largely fixed pretrained distributions.

</details>


### [223] [PerSo: Investigating Facets of LLMs' Social Intelligence in Real-World Persuasion Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20548)
*Ali Zamani,Soroush Esfahanian,Erfan Shahabi,Mohammad Taher Pilehvar,Azadeh Shakery,Yadollah Yaghoobzadeh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 PerSo 基准，基于 Reddit ChangeMyView 真实说服对话，联合评测大语言模型的认知社会智能（推断他人心理状态）与行为社会智能（选择合适社会行动），并采用 LLM-in-the-loop 流程构建任务、经人工标注验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会智能对 LLM 在人机交互与多智能体场景中的应用日益重要，包含认知与行为两方面；说服情境天然同时涉及二者，但已有基准多使用合成场景、且将两方面割裂评测，缺乏真实性与联合评估。

Method: 构建 PerSo 基准：数据取自 Reddit ChangeMyView 社区的真实说服对话；采用 LLM-in-the-loop 流程生成评测任务；通过人工标注对任务进行验证；对多种 LLM 在认知与行为两个维度上进行评估。

Result: 模型性能总体随规模增大而提升，但在不同维度上表现存在差异；在行为任务上最强 LLM 略微超过观测到的最佳人类表现，而在认知任务上最佳人类参与者仍保持领先。

Conclusion: PerSo 提供了基于真实说服对话的社会智能联合评测框架，揭示 LLM 在行为社会智能上已可媲美甚至略超人类，但在认知社会智能（心理状态推断）上仍落后于人类最佳水平。

Abstract: Understanding the social intelligence of large language models (LLMs) is increasingly important for applications involving human-AI interaction and multi-agent environments. Social intelligence comprises two aspects: cognitive intelligence, the ability to infer others' mental states, and behavioral intelligence, the ability to select appropriate social actions based on these inferences to achieve social goals. Persuasion provides a natural testbed in which both aspects are simultaneously at play, yet prior benchmarks typically evaluate them in isolation using synthetic scenarios. We introduce PerSo, a benchmark that evaluates both aspects on real-world persuasion dialogues from Reddit's ChangeMyView community, using an LLM-in-the-loop pipeline to construct the evaluation tasks, validated through human annotation. Our evaluation of diverse LLMs shows that performance generally scales with model size but varies across dimensions. The strongest LLM slightly surpasses the best observed human performance on the behavioral task, while the best human participant remains ahead on the cognitive task.

</details>


### [224] [Do AI Agents Gossip? Evaluative Talk in Human and AI-Agent Online Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20360)
*Yong-Hui Hua,Chih-Han Lin,Shu-Kai Hsieh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究比较AI智能体社区Moltbook与人类论坛PTT的八卦话语：AI社区确实自发涌现八卦，但流行度低2.6–6倍，且后续回应中质疑与挑战显著更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 八卦（评价不在场第三方的言谈）通常被视为人类独有的社会实践。作者希望检验：在自主AI智能体构成的社区中，是否也会自发出现可比的八卦话语，从而探讨AI群体能否复现人类社交言谈的结构性特征。

Method: 选取Moltbook（AI智能体所构成的Reddit式平台）与PTT（台湾大型人类电子布告栏）两个公开、异步论坛，采用同一操作性定义与相同沟通情境以降低设置差异。分析八卦的流行度、对象、首位回应者反应及是否以无据传闻呈现；并在控制帖子深度与版面构成的条件下，对至少含一条可编码回复的主题帖进行挑战率、共识是否被质疑等主题层面对比，另用两位人类标注者做重校准，并对以单条回复为单位的估计以区间形式报告。

Result: Moltbook上确实出现八卦，但流行度比PTT低2.6至6倍（取决于分母）。两平台八卦都常指向本地知名人物、倾向获得首位回应者认同、且少以无据传闻呈现。最明显差异在后续回应：含至少一条可编码回复的主题帖中，人类帖挑战率65.1%，智能体帖仅21.3%；出现早期认同式回应后，智能体帖有83.6%共识未受挑战，人类为44.7%。该主题层面差异在控制帖子深度与版面构成、并经两位人类标注者校准后依然稳健；基于单条回复的估计稳健性较低，故以区间报告。

Conclusion: AI智能体社区能够复现八卦的信息维度（传播关于不在场第三方的评价性信息），但八卦在人类中承担的关系性功能（如引发质疑、维持或修正共识）在AI社区中是否可比仍未解决。作者据此提出需进一步研究AI八卦的社会功能问题。

Abstract: Gossip, defined as evaluative talk about absent third parties, is widely seen as a uniquely human social practice. This study examines whether comparable discourse emerges spontaneously in a community of autonomous AI agents. We compare Moltbook, a Reddit-style platform populated by AI agents, with PTT, a large human-operated bulletin board in Taiwan. To reduce differences attributable to communicative setting, we analyze two public, asynchronous forums using the same operational definition of gossip. Gossip occurs on Moltbook, but its prevalence is 2.6 to 6 times lower than on PTT, depending on the denominator used. On both platforms, gossip frequently targets locally prominent figures, tends to elicit agreement from the first respondent, and is rarely presented as unsupported rumor. The clearest difference concerns how subsequent responses develop. Among threads containing at least one codable reply, challenges occur in 65.1% of human threads but only 21.3% of agent threads. Moreover, following an early aligning response, consensus remains unchallenged in 83.6% of agent threads, compared with 44.7% of human threads. This thread-level contrast remains after controlling for thread depth and board composition and under recalibration against two human annotators. Estimates based on individual reply turns are less robust and are therefore reported as bounds. These findings suggest that AI-agent communities reproduce the informational dimension of gossip, while the extent to which gossip performs comparable relational functions remains unresolved.

</details>


### [225] [Same Claim, Different Interpretations: How LLM Agents Resolve Ambiguity in Scientific Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20457)
*Delip Rao,Ahmed Muharram,Chris Callison-Burch*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"Claim Cards"框架，将科学声明验证中的隐含解释显式化为九个字段（标记为stated/inferred/default），并通过900次GPT-5.4实验证明单一解释字段的刻意修改可显著改变验证结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言科学声明常因表述不充分而存在多种合理解释。现有验证器通常暗中选定一种解释，基准测试却只对比结论与专家标签，既未记录专家或验证器的解释，也无法将分歧归因于证据还是解释，造成算力浪费于未经检验的解释。

Method: 引入Claim Cards，在证据收集前以九个字段记录验证器的解释，每字段标注为stated、inferred或default。对60个专家标注的材料科学与生物医学声明，用GPT-5.4代理各运行5次：带卡、不带卡、冻结卡（共900次）。另对24个材料科学声明结合计算工具测试更严格阈值对工具调用的影响。

Result: 超过40%的卡字段未被标记为stated；带卡仅增加约十分之一的token与时间，未显著改变与专家标签的一致性。代理自身解释在运行间存在波动，但同一声明内的波动未显著影响结论。然而，对冻结卡单一字段的刻意修改（使量词变为全称或提高支持阈值）在约三分之一的声明上改变了结论，而随机期望约为七分之一（各编辑p<0.001）。更严格阈值还改变了代理要求计算工具执行的内容。

Conclusion: 结论应与其解释一同报告，基准测试可发布每个标签背后的解释。解释虽在正常运行中影响有限，但在被刻意改变时对结论具有决定性作用，因此解释的透明化对验证的可追溯性与可靠性至关重要。

Abstract: Co-scientists and agentic scientific claim verifiers are increasingly used to assess claims across the sciences. Natural-language scientific claims, written for human experts, are often underspecified and so admit several defensible interpretations. Such verifiers usually pick one, often tacitly, and benchmarks score their verdicts against expert labels without recording the expert's interpretation or the verifier's in a comparable form. This leads to disagreements with expert labels that cannot be traced to the evidence or the interpretation, and to compute spent on unchecked interpretations. We introduce Claim Cards to make such interpretations explicit in the reasoning itself. A Claim Card records a verifier's interpretation as nine fields before evidence gathering, each marked stated, inferred, or default. To test whether an agent's interpretation can change its verdict, we ran a GPT-5.4 agent five times on each of 60 expert-labeled materials-science and biomedical claims with a card, without one, and with one card frozen across runs (900 runs). Over 40% of card fields were not marked stated, and a card added about a tenth to a run's GPT-5.4 tokens and time without measurably changing agreement with expert labels. The agent's own interpretations varied between runs, although within a claim this variation did not measurably track the verdict. A deliberate change to one field of the frozen card, however, making the quantifier universal or the threshold for support stricter, changed the verdict on about a third of the claims, against about one in seven expected by chance (p < 0.001 for each edit). On 24 materials-science claims run with computational tools, the stricter threshold also changed what the agent asked those tools to compute. We therefore argue that verdicts should be reported with their interpretations and that benchmarks could publish the interpretation behind each label.

</details>


### [226] [Latent Compaction with Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20582)
*Yingfa Chen,Xingyu Shen,Chaojun Xiao,Xu Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Latent Compaction，将 KV cache 视为可训练神经网络，通过测试时训练学习紧凑的文档专属缓存，在 32× 压缩下保持高检索准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文会放大 KV cache，增加内存占用与注意力计算成本。现有压缩方法需在未见问题上保留信息，但难以在激进压缩下维持检索精度。

Method: 通过测试时训练学习紧凑的文档专属 cache。初始化综合重建重要性、教师注意力与 key 空间多样性；先拟合全缓存的注意力输出与 log-normalizer，并固定重建选中的条目；再通过全缓存模型端到端蒸馏联合优化整个 cache。模型权重冻结，仅用文档的通用提示训练，不接触下游问答。

Result: 在 RULER、LongBench、Phonebook Lookup 上显著优于同预算压缩基线。RULER 上 32× 压缩达 96.7% 准确率，KVZip+ 为 40.0%；并跨模型规模与系列迁移，在 Qwen3 与 MiniCPM5 上检索均有提升。

Conclusion: 将 KV cache 视为神经网络并用测试时训练优化，可在激进压缩下保持检索准确率，验证了该范式的有效性。

Abstract: Long contexts enlarge key--value (KV) caches, increasing memory use and attention costs during generation. Compressing these caches must preserve information for unseen questions. We introduce **Latent Compaction**, which learns a compact document-specific cache through test-time training. We initialize cache entries using reconstruction importance, teacher attention, and key-space diversity. Training first fits attention outputs and log-normalizers to those of the full cache, with reconstruction-selected entries held fixed. It then jointly optimizes the entire cache through end-to-end distillation from the full-cache model. Model weights remain frozen throughout, and training uses only generic prompts about the document, without access to downstream questions or answers. Experiments on RULER, LongBench, and Phonebook Lookup demonstrate substantial gains over budget-matched compression baselines. On a RULER, for example, our method achieves 96.7% accuracy at 32$\times$ compression, compared with 40.0% for KVZip+. The method also transfers across model scales and families, with retrieval gains on both Qwen3 and MiniCPM5. These results show that treating the KV cache as a neural network and optimizing it with test-time training can preserve retrieval accuracy under aggressive compression. The code is released at https://www.github.com/THUNLP/latent-compaction

</details>


### [227] [VASPO: Self-Distillation with Privileged Value Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20516)
*Fei Wu,Zhenrong Zhang,Jianshu Zhang,Jun Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出VASPO方法，在PPO框架内整合特权信息引导的critic价值预测与自教师信号，用于推理模型的策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: on-policy自蒸馏通过特权自教师提供稠密监督，但该监督反映的是局部token偏好而非无引导学生续写的期望回报。已有方法将自蒸馏信号与GRPO结合，用GRPO定更新方向、教师做信号缩放，但整合方式不够有效。作者希望找到更优的整合机制。

Method: 发现互补的位置动态规律：特权信息在响应末段提升critic的价值预测精度，而自教师引导在生成初期强、中期下降、末期回升。据此提出VASPO：自教师与critic在学生生成的prefix上评估相同的特权信息，critic从预测续写回报计算token级PPO优势，自教师通过有界概率比调制选定的正向优势。

Result: 在六个数学推理基准上，VASPO相比PPO在Qwen3-4B与OLMo3-7B变体上分别提升平均准确率2.59和1.81个百分点，并在Knights and Knaves任务上取得所有评估方法中最高的平均准确率。

Conclusion: 将自蒸馏与PPO的critic价值预测相结合（而非GRPO）能更有效地利用特权信息的互补位置动态，显著提升推理模型性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation provides dense supervision for reasoning models via a privileged self-teacher. However, this supervision reflects local token preferences rather than the expected return of an unguided student continuation. Recent methods combine this signal with GRPO. They use GRPO for update directions and the teacher for signal scaling. However, we demonstrate a more effective integration within PPO. Specifically, we identify complementary positional dynamics: privileged information enhances a critic's value-prediction accuracy toward the response's end, whereas self-teacher guidance is strong initially, drops mid-generation, and rebounds finally. These insights motivate Value-Augmented Self-Distillation Policy Optimization (VASPO), wherein a self-teacher and critic evaluate identical privileged information at student-generated prefixes. The critic computes token-level PPO advantages from predicted continuation returns, while the self-teacher modulates selected positive advantages via bounded probability ratios. Across six mathematical reasoning benchmarks, VASPO improves mean accuracy over PPO by 2.59 and 1.81 percentage points on Qwen3-4B and OLMo3-7B variants, respectively, and achieves the highest mean accuracy among evaluated methods on Knights and Knaves. Our code is available at https://github.com/cii030/VASPO.

</details>


### [228] [ReaDy: Recurrent States as Dynamic Context for Speculative Drafting in Hybrid Linear Attention Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20503)
*Bohan Sun,Zunhai Su,Xialie Zhuang,Jianjin Li,Ngai Wong,Chuan-Wei Kuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReaDy 是首个利用混合线性注意力（HLA）循环状态进行位置特定上下文检索的推测解码方法，在多个基准上实现了平均 3.16× 的解码加速，并在吞吐量和接受长度上超越 DSpark。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于特征的草稿器依赖目标隐藏特征，而这些特征的上下文侧重可能与后续草稿位置的需求不一致，导致草稿质量受限。HLA 模型维护的紧凑循环状态累积了历史关联信息，可为推测解码提供高效且互补的上下文来源。

Method: ReaDy 为每个草稿位置配备学习到的查询，直接从目标模型已提交的循环状态中检索补充上下文；轻量级状态读取器通过残差更新将上下文注入草稿表示，并采用低秩状态读取与融合算子降低计算与 I/O 开销。方法基于 DSpark 的并行骨干与顺序预测头，配合轻量两级适配策略，并通过消融研究考察读取器容量、状态来源与残差位置。

Result: 在数学、代码生成与对话共七个基准上，ReaDy 在单张 NVIDIA B200 GPU 上使用 Qwen3.8-27B 实现相对自回归解码平均 3.16× 加速；在每个基准上均优于 DSpark，吞吐量平均提升 7.97%，接受长度平均提升 8.50%。

Conclusion: ReaDy 验证了将 HLA 循环状态用于位置特定上下文检索可有效提升推测解码的草稿质量与效率，是一种高效、通用且可扩展的加速方案。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by drafting multiple tokens per step, yet feature-based drafters rely on target hidden features whose contextual emphasis may not align with the needs of subsequent draft positions. Hybrid linear attention (HLA) models maintain compact recurrent states that accumulate historical associations, offering an efficient source of complementary context for speculative drafting. We introduce ReaDy, the first speculative decoding method, to the best of our knowledge, to leverage HLA recurrent states for position-specific context retrieval during drafting. ReaDy equips each draft position with a learned query to retrieve supplementary context directly from the target's committed recurrent states. A lightweight state reader integrates this context into draft representations through residual updates, while low-rank state reads and fused operators keep additional computation and I/O overhead low. Building on DSpark's parallel backbone and sequential prediction head, ReaDy employs a lightweight two-stage adaptation strategy, with reader capacity, state sources, and residual placement examined through ablation studies. Across seven benchmarks spanning mathematics, code generation, and dialogue, ReaDy achieves an average $3.16\times$ decoding speedup over autoregressive decoding with Qwen3.8-27B on a single NVIDIA B200 GPU. It outperforms DSpark on every benchmark, improving throughput by 7.97% and acceptance length by 8.50% on average. Our code is available at https://github.com/StartLuxLabs/ReaDy.

</details>


### [229] [Lost in Translation: Why Lower Bias Scores Don't Mean Fairer Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20479)
*Harsh Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文质疑多语言模型偏见审计中"英文平均刻板印象分数与他语言平均分之差即公平性差异"的常见做法，通过重述、逐项语言转换与注入扰动检验发现，跨语言分数下降中相当部分是噪声或项目层面的模式变化，而非真实公平性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语言偏见审计通常直接比较模型在英语与另一语言下的平均刻板印象分数，并将差值解读为公平性差异，但未检验该差异中有多少源自测量噪声、措辞差异或项目层面的分数模式变化，因而可能得出误导性结论。

Method: 审计10个开源模型、8种语言，共70个模型—语言对。方法包括：对英语测试项仅做重述以测量分数漂移；对语言转换进行逐项比较；检验测试能否捕捉刻意注入的偏移；并逐条判断翻译质量，区分不完美翻译与忠实翻译。此外记录三种会静默抹除偏见信号的常见流程错误。

Result: 全部70个模型—语言对的刻板印象分数均低于其英语水平；印地语、韩语和斯瓦希里语的降幅明显超过英语重述造成的漂移，且主要来自StereoSet项目。不完美翻译降幅更大，但仅用忠实翻译时降幅仍接近。项目英文分数对其翻译分数的预测力远弱于对重述分数的预测力：相关系数0.23（忠实翻译0.27），而重述为0.48。同时分数并未更接近中立，模型在翻译项目上彼此一致，印地语或西班牙语中某翻译项目的分数与其自身重述分数的相关达0.84和0.82。

Conclusion: 平均分下降无法区分"偏见更低"与"项目层面分数模式不同"两种情况，因此以跨语言平均分差推断公平性差异的做法不可靠；审计需进行项目级、重述对照及翻译质量检验，并避免会静默抹除偏见信号的流程错误。

Abstract: Bias audits of multilingual language models usually compare a model's average stereotype score in English with its average in another language and read the difference as a difference in fairness. We ask how much of that difference is noise. Auditing ten open models in eight languages, we measure how far scores move when English test items are merely reworded, test every language shift item by item, and check that the test catches shifts we inject on purpose. In all 70 model-language pairs the stereotype score falls below its English level, and for Hindi, Korean and Swahili the drop clearly exceeds what rewording English produces; most of it comes from StereoSet items. Judging every translation, we find that imperfect ones drop more, but faithful translations alone drop nearly as much. Still, a lower average does not show that a model is fairer. An item's English score predicts its translated score far less well than its reworded score (correlation 0.23, 0.27 on faithful translations, vs. 0.48), while scores stay as far from neutral, models agree on translated items, and within Hindi or Spanish a translated item's score correlates with its own rewording at 0.84 and 0.82. The average cannot distinguish lower bias from a different pattern of item-level scores. We also document three common pipeline errors that silently erase bias signal, and release our code.

</details>


### [230] [FOLD: Position-Free Far Memory with Local Order for Long-Context Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20392)
*Shuxuan Li,Jialiang Kang,Yanghao Zhou,Ziye Zhu,Xiao Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 FOLD，一种免训练方法，通过保留精确局部位置并将所有远端条目视为共享位置的远记忆，配合注意力锐化与总权重控制，在长上下文场景下显著提升投机解码的加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码在长输入场景下可摊薄 KV-cache 访问成本，具有潜力。但多数草稿模型在短序列上训练，长输入时性能退化，而长上下文训练代价高昂。现有免训练方法（限制上下文窗口、重缩放位置、检索增强近期上下文）各有局限。作者观察到草稿模型读取的隐状态已编码上下文与位置信息，且其轻量架构足以负担全历史注意力，故寻求更优的免训练方案。

Method: FOLD 是一种免训练方法：保留精确的局部位置编码，将所有远端条目视为"远记忆"并赋予共享位置；针对记忆增长导致的注意力稀释，在远记忆内部进行注意力锐化以突出相关条目，同时独立控制其相对局部窗口的总注意力权重。

Result: 在六组目标-草稿模型对及涵盖最高 256K token 的多样基准测试中，FOLD 多数情况下取得最高加速比，最优情况下相较自回归解码加速比翻倍以上，从 2.08× 提升至 4.22×。

Conclusion: FOLD 通过免训练的方式有效缓解草稿模型的长上下文退化问题，在长输入投机解码中显著优于现有方法，兼顾了性能与成本。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM generation through lightweight drafting and parallel verification. It is particularly promising for long inputs, where KV-cache access costs can be amortized over multiple accepted tokens. Yet most drafters are trained on short sequences and degrade on long inputs, while long-context training is costly. Existing training-free approaches restrict the context window, rescale positions, or augment recent context with retrieved distant entries. Drafters, however, read target hidden states that already encode contextual and positional information, and their lightweight architecture makes full-history attention affordable. We propose FOLD, a training-free method that preserves exact local positions and treats all distant entries as far memory at a shared position. To counter attention dilution as the memory grows, we sharpen attention within it to emphasize relevant entries while separately controlling its total weight relative to the local window. Across six target-drafter pairs and diverse benchmarks of up to 256K tokens, FOLD predominantly delivers the highest speedup, with the best case more than doubling the speedup over autoregressive decoding, from 2.08$\times$ to 4.22$\times$.

</details>


### [231] [Scaling Multilingual Mixture-of-Experts: How Language Coverage Changes Capacity and Compute Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20247)
*Joel Dag,Nikit Srivastava,Hamada M. Zahera,Axel-Cyrille Ngonga Ngomo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种考虑重复率的联合缩放定律，关联活跃参数量、各语言训练token数、专家数与语言覆盖度，用于分析多语言MoE模型的容量分配。基于64个预训练配置、26,300条单语言损失观测拟合，该定律在留出配置上较最强基线降低28%预测误差，并显示32专家相比稠密模型可将12→200语言的损失惩罚降低83%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言模型在各语言间共享容量，新增语言会损害既有语言表现；MoE虽能以极小额外每token计算扩展容量，但在给定额外容量下，增加专家是否能降低新增语言的代价尚不明确。

Method: 提出一种考虑重复率（repetition-aware）的联合缩放定律，将活跃参数量、各语言训练token数、专家数与语言覆盖度统一关联；基于64个预训练配置的26,300条单语言损失观测进行拟合，并与基线预测方法在留出配置上对比。

Result: 该定律较最强被评估基线将留出配置预测误差降低28%；在活跃参数量与各语言训练token数匹配时，32专家较稠密模型将语言数从12扩展至200的损失惩罚降低83%；并表明在更广的计算预算范围内，更广的语言覆盖度倾向于更多专家。

Conclusion: MoE的额外容量可显著缓解多语言扩展中的容量竞争，所提出的缩放定律能为多语言模型规模化过程中的专家/容量分配提供指导。

Abstract: Multilingual models share capacity across languages, so adding languages can hurt performance on existing ones. Mixture-of-experts models expand this capacity with little additional per-token computation. Given this extra capacity, does adding experts reduce what each additional language costs? To answer this question, we propose a repetition-aware joint scaling law relating number of active parameters, training tokens per language, number of experts, and language coverage. Fitted on 26,300 per-language loss observations from 64 pretrained configurations, our law reduces prediction error on held-out configurations by 28% relative to the strongest evaluated baseline. At matched number of active parameters and training tokens per language, 32 experts reduce the loss penalty of expanding from 12 to 200 languages by 83% compared with a dense model. Our law further shows that broader language coverage favors more experts across a wider range of compute budgets, guiding capacity allocation as multilingual models scale.

</details>


### [232] [Anuvani-Diarization: Zero Shot Speaker Diarization and Annotation Conventions Across 22 Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20327)
*Stephen Fernandes,Narayan Choudhary*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文面向印度22种官方语言的多人混合对话场景，构建了IndicDiarBench基准并提出Anuvani-Diarization系统，在1,164段录音上取得16.78%的时长加权DER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度22种官方语言的使用者常在同一轮次内混合多语言、自由重叠，并发生于电话、会议与媒体场景中，说话人日志（speaker diarization）是此类对话语音应用（多用户语音代理、会议与通话的说话人标注转写、呼叫中心分析、媒体监测）的关键基础，但此前缺乏覆盖全部22种语言的系统研究。

Method: 构建IndicDiarBench基准，覆盖印度的22种语言；提出Anuvani-Diarization，将自监督端到端分割模型适配到印度对话场景，并采用不确定性感知的银标注（silver supervision）监督方式；与九个近期日志系统进行零样本对比评测。

Result: 在全部1,164段录音上取得16.78%的时长加权DER，为所运行系统中最低，仅次于已发表最佳结果（16.0%）；在22种语言中的14种上领先三个最强对手；在六个国际基准上与父模型持平。此外，基准的话语级标注约定是一个隐藏变量，影响幅度达1.9至4.1个DER点（依系统而异），且从其他印度转写数据中学到的约定无法迁移。

Conclusion: Anuvani-Diarization在多语言印度对话日志任务上达到当前领先水平，同时揭示话语级标注约定对DER评测有显著且不可跨数据集迁移的影响，提示该约定应作为评测中的显式变量加以控制。

Abstract: India's 22 scheduled languages are spoken by over a billion people in conversations that mix languages, often within one turn, overlap freely, and take place over telephones, in meetings, and in media. Knowing who spoke when, the task of speaker diarization, is essential to speech applications for such conversation: voice agents conversing with several people at once, speaker attributed transcription of meetings and calls, contact center analytics, and media monitoring. We present a systematic study of speaker diarization across all 22 languages on IndicDiarBench and introduce Anuvani-Diarization, which adapts a self-supervised end-to-end segmentation model to Indian conversation with uncertainty aware silver supervision. Evaluated zero shot alongside nine recent diarization systems, it reaches a duration weighted diarization error rate (DER) of 16.78% on all 1,164 recordings, the lowest of all systems we ran and second only to the best published result (16.0%), and leads the three strongest rivals in 14 of 22 languages. It matches its parent on six international benchmarks. We also show that the benchmark's utterance level annotation convention is a hidden variable worth 1.9 to 4.1 DER points, depending on the system, and that a convention learned from other Indian transcriptions does not transfer.

</details>


### [233] [Taking the PULSE of Medical Reasoning: Step-Level Retrieval Timing for Knowledge-Grounded Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20292)
*Danyang Zhao,Sendong Zhao,Yanrui Du,Haochun Wang,Ming Ma,Wenhao Li,Jingbo Wang,Jiatong Liu,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PUlSE是一种轻量级探针方法，利用模型内部层间几何特征作为内在信号，实现医疗推理中的步骤级检索触发控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的医疗推理要求大语言模型动态访问外部生物医学证据以提升事实可信度与推理准确率，但无差别检索会引入噪声与延迟。核心问题是检索时机：在每个推理步骤，系统需判断模型内部知识是否可靠，还是需要外部证据。

Method: 通过后验轨迹分析发现错误步骤的层间波动性显著高于正确步骤，反映内部知识不稳定。基于此，PUlSE在层间几何特征上训练紧凑探针，在步骤完成时检测事实错误，并将其作为检索与步骤重生成的自适应触发器。

Result: 在四个医疗基准上，PUlSE定时检索的准确率与穷举式逐步检索相当，同时将检索调用减少72%；并可迁移至未见过的法律领域，显示潜在的领域无关通用性。

Conclusion: 该研究初步实现了检索触发设计从表层置信度估计向内部激活动态的转变，为自适应访问提供新思路。

Abstract: Reliable medical reasoning requires LLMs to dynamically access external biomedical evidence to improve factual trustworthiness and reasoning accuracy. However, indiscriminate retrieval can introduce noise and latency, raising a critical retrieval-timing problem: at each reasoning step, the system must decide whether the model's internal knowledge is reliable or external evidence is needed. We propose PUlSE, a lightweight probing method that uses the model's internal representations as intrinsic signals for step-level retrieval control. Our posterior trajectory analysis reveals that erroneous steps exhibit substantially higher inter-layer volatility than correct ones, suggesting unstable internal knowledge. Based on this finding, PUlSE trains a compact probe on inter-layer geometric features to detect factual errors upon step completion, and uses it as an adaptive trigger for retrieval and step regeneration. Across four medical benchmarks, PUlSE-timed retrieval matches exhaustive per-step retrieval in accuracy while reducing retrieval calls by 72%, and can transfer to an unseen legal domain, indicating potential domain-agnostic generality. Overall, our study provides an initial step toward shifting retrieval-trigger design from surface-level confidence estimates to internal activation dynamics for adaptive access.

</details>


### [234] [Neural POS Tagging for the Low-Resource Saraiki](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20279)
*Usman Nawaz,Muhammad Junaid Iqbal,Muhammad Asaf,Mariam Munsif Mir,Faisal Rehman,Tahir Alyas,Muhammad Hasnain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个手工标注的萨拉伊基语（Saraiki）通用词性（UPOS）标注数据集（17类标签），在统一微调设置下对比了三种预训练Transformer编码器，XLM-RoBERTa base取得最佳性能（测试准确率88.60%±0.21%），为低资源语言序列标注提供了基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 萨拉伊基语是使用波斯-阿拉伯字母、从右向左书写的印度-雅利安语，母语者约2889万（巴基斯坦2023年第七次人口普查），但仍是资源匮乏语言。词性标注是基础序列标注任务及众多下游NLP应用的重要组件，而该语言缺乏相应标注数据与基准。

Method: 从纯文本出发，人工标注构建萨拉伊基语通用词性（UPOS）数据集，共17个词性类别；在相同的微调配置下评估三种预训练Transformer编码器：XLM-RoBERTa base、Urdu RoBERTa small、multilingual DistilBERT；采用5个随机种子进行实验。

Result: XLM-RoBERTa base表现最佳，开发集准确率87.29%±0.21%，测试集准确率88.60%±0.21%；Urdu RoBERTa small与multilingual DistilBERT也取得有竞争力的结果，表明多语言及相关文字预训练编码器对萨拉伊基语UPOS标注有效。

Conclusion: 该工作提出的数据集、评估设置与基线结果为萨拉伊基语NLP及低资源序列标注的未来研究提供了基准。

Abstract: Part-of-speech tagging is a fundamental sequence labeling task and an important component for many downstream natural language processing applications. However, Saraiki, written from right to left in a Perso-Arabic script, remains an under-resourced Indo-Aryan language, despite being spoken as a mother tongue by 28,849,579 people (approximately 28.9 million) according to Pakistan 7th Population and Housing Census 2023. This paper presents a manually annotated Saraiki Universal POS tagging dataset developed from plain-text. We annotated the text with 17 POS categories. We evaluate three pretrained transformer encoders under the same fine-tuning setup: XLM-RoBERTa base, Urdu RoBERTa small, and multilingual DistilBERT. Experimental results over five random seeds show that XLM-RoBERTa base achieves the best performance, with 87.29% +/- 0.21% development accuracy and 88.60% +/- 0.21% test accuracy. Urdu RoBERTa small and multilingual DistilBERT also provide competitive results, suggesting that multilingual and related-script pretrained encoders are effective for Saraiki UPOS tagging. The dataset, evaluation setup, and baseline results introduced in this work provide a benchmark for future research on Saraiki NLP and low-resource sequence labeling.

</details>


### [235] [Anuvani-LID: Robust and Calibrated Spoken Language Identification for Indian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20263)
*Stephen Fernandes,Narayan Choudhary*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Anuvani LID——一个 115M 参数的专用印度语种识别 conformer 模型，基于 54,643 小时、覆盖 22 种印度语言及印度口音英语的语音训练，在 37 种测试条件下取得 0.914 平均 macro-F1，显著优于 Whisper、Gemini 3.5 Flash、GPT-audio 等通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言语音流水线的第一步是语种判定（LID），其错误会静默传播至识别、翻译与路由环节。印度场景尤为困难：22 种表列语言分属四个语系，音频短、含噪、窄带且夹杂语码转换。当前基础语音模型被广泛假设已具备内生的、可泛化的 LID 能力（自回归解码器输出语言 ID token，音频 LLM 可被提示说出语言），但该假设缺乏在相同音频上的严格验证。

Method: 作者在相同音频片段上检验通用模型的 LID 能力：Whisper 类模型仅覆盖 23 种语言中的 15 种；Gemini 3.5 Flash 在 1 秒输入上 macro-F1 为 0.52，GPT-audio 为 0.14。继而提出 Anuvani LID：一个 115M 参数的专用 LID conformer 模型，训练数据为 54,643 小时印度语音，覆盖 22 种印度语言与印度口音英语。评测采用包含 102 种条件、说话人与语料均留出（held-out）的受控基准，共 37 种条件。

Result: Anuvani LID 在 37 种条件下达到 0.914 平均 macro-F1，比 Gemini 3.5 Flash 高 5.2 分，并优于另外 14 个系统；在 1 秒输入上领先 Gemini 24 分；在全部 23 种语言上取得 0.897，而其他系统均不超过 0.713；校准误差 ECE 低至 0.008。

Conclusion: 通用语音/音频大模型的 LID 能力被高估，尤其在印度多语种、短时长、噪声与语码混合条件下覆盖不全且性能不足。可靠的印度语种识别仍需一个鲁棒、专用且经良好校准的模型。

Abstract: Every multilingual speech pipeline begins with a language decision, an error there propagates silently through recognition, translation and routing. In India it spans 22 scheduled languages across four language families, heard over short, noisy, narrowband and code-mixed audio. Foundational speech models increasingly present language identification (LID) as an inherent latent, generalized capability. Autoregressive decoders in speech models emit a special language ID token, and modern LLMs with audio capabilities can name the language when prompted. We test this assumption on identical clips and find that whisper and similar speech models has tokens for only 15 of the 23 languages, on one second inputs Gemini 3.5 Flash reaches 0.52 macro-F1 and GPT-audio 0.14. We present Anuvani LID, a dedicated 115M parameter LID conformer model trained on 54,643 hours of Indian speech spanning 22 Indian languages and Indian accented English. Along with designed and controlled studies on 102 condition benchmark with speaker and corpus held out tests. Across 37 conditions, it reaches 0.914 mean macro-F1, 5.2 points above Gemini 3.5 Flash and ahead of 14 other systems. It is 24 points ahead of Gemini at one second, scores 0.897 on all 23 languages where no other system exceeds 0.713, and is calibrated to an ECE of 0.008. Reliable Indian LID still requires a robust, dedicated and calibrated model.

</details>


### [236] [Scaling Multilingual Safety and Reasoning Evaluation through Reasoning-Guided Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20221)
*Zahraa Al Sahili,Ioannis Patras,Matthew Purver*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出推理引导的翻译框架，用于构建保留任务结构与语义的多语言评测基准，覆盖7个安全/对话/数学推理资源、11种语言，达到99.94%的条目-语言对覆盖率，并揭示开源LLM存在语言相关的拒绝行为不一致与低资源语言推理退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评测以英语为主，直接翻译已有基准虽可扩展多语言评测，但在有害请求、数学推理与对话交互等任务上难以保真原意，因此需要一种任务敏感、可控质量的基准构建方法。

Method: 提出推理引导的翻译框架：将翻译策略选择与大规模生成解耦，先进行推理引导的配置选择，再结合任务感知验证、定向修复与独立质量筛查。

Result: 构建了源对齐的多语言评测集合，覆盖7个安全、对话与数学推理资源、11种语言；在两个需修复子集中实现99.94%的目标条目-语言对覆盖率。对开源LLM的评测显示语言相关脆弱性：拒绝行为不一致，低资源语言推理能力显著下降。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了任务敏感基准构建与系统性质量评估对理解多语言模型行为的重要性，有助于超越英语视角评估LLM安全与推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed across languages, yet their safety and reasoning capabilities remain predominantly evaluated in English. Translating existing benchmarks offers a scalable approach to multilingual evaluation, but preserving task-specific meaning across languages presents significant challenges, particularly for harmful requests, mathematical reasoning, and conversational interactions. We introduce a reasoning-guided translation framework for constructing multilingual evaluations designed to preserve the structure and intended meaning of diverse benchmark tasks. Our approach separates translation strategy selection from large-scale generation, combining reasoning-guided configuration selection with task-aware validation, targeted repair, and independent quality screening. Using this framework, we develop a source-aligned evaluation collection spanning seven safety, conversational, and mathematical reasoning resources across 11 languages. The resulting collection achieves 99.94\% coverage of intended item--language pairs in the two subsets requiring repair. Evaluations of open-source LLMs reveal distinct language-dependent vulnerabilities, including inconsistent refusal behaviour and substantial reasoning degradation in lower-resource languages. These findings highlight the importance of task-sensitive benchmark construction and systematic quality assessment for understanding multilingual model behaviour beyond English.

</details>


### [237] [What Makes a Social Agent's Interactions Effective? Learning from Verifiable Outcomes in Paired Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20195)
*Caixin Kang,Ke Wang,Yuchuan Wu,Xiaoqiang Wang,Ling Li,Bingxue Qiu,Yongbin Li,Yoichi Sato*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 HICO-Bench 基准与 PaCPO 方法，用于评估并提升社交智能体在协作场景中达成"可验证方案"的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社交智能体评估主要依赖 LLM 评判者对对话打分，仅关注对话是否流畅或目标完成度，而不检验最终达成的方案是否真正可行。同时，这类评估无法区分"看似完成目标"与"实际满足双方私有条件"的差异，导致评估失真。

Method: 1) HICO-Bench：180 个日常协作场景（资源分配、日程安排、授权等），两名持有私有信息的智能体对话后各自独立记录达成方案；通过程序检验 Feasibility（两份记录一致且满足双方私有条件），通过 LLM 评判者检验 Integrity（对话支撑所记录方案），二者同时通过记为 Verified Success。每个场景有两个版本，共享公共语境但仅一处私有条件不同，正确方案随之不同。2) PaCPO（Paired-Credit Policy Optimization）：针对仅对话结束时才有反馈的问题，在每轮对话后要求智能体给出临时方案，并在两个版本中检验，对临时方案分别符合对应版本的轮次给予奖励。

Result: 在 13 个 LLM 上测试，36% 被判定为"目标完成度高"的对话未能通过 Verified Success。PaCPO 将 Qwen3-8B 的 Verified Success 从 20.4% 提升至 28.3%。

Conclusion: 仅靠 LLM 评判对话质量不足以衡量社交智能体的真实协作能力，需以最终方案的可验证性为判据；成对场景设计可有效区分表面完成与真实可行；PaCPO 通过回合级配对奖励缓解稀疏反馈问题，显著提升了方案的 Verified Success 率。

Abstract: Effective social interaction requires more than fluent responses: agents must reach plans that fit the participants' needs and circumstances. Existing evaluations of social agents mainly rely on LLM judges that rate the conversation, without checking the resulting plan. We introduce HICO-Bench, a benchmark of 180 everyday coordination scenarios, such as resource allocation, scheduling, and authorization, in which two agents with private information converse and then independently record the plan they agreed on. A program checks Feasibility (the two records match and satisfy both participants' private conditions), and an LLM judge checks Integrity (the conversation supports the recorded plan); passing both is a Verified Success. Each scenario has two versions that share the same public context but differ in one private condition, so the correct plan differs between them. Across thirteen LLMs, 36% of conversations rated highly for goal completion fail Verified Success. To learn from outcomes that arrive only at the end of a conversation, we propose Paired-Credit Policy Optimization (PaCPO). After each turn, PaCPO asks the agent for a tentative plan, checks it in both versions of the scenario, and rewards the turns after which the tentative plans fit their respective versions. PaCPO raises Qwen3-8B's Verified Success from 20.4% to 28.3%.

</details>


### [238] [Analyzing performance of Retrieval Augmented Generation models for Question Answering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20419)
*Fazle Mohammed Tasfiq,Sabab Attin,Sheikh Md. Tahmid Hossain,Raiat Reza,Tanzim Ahmmed,Nafis Chowdhury,Anika Ahmed,Moinul Haque,Farig Yousuf Sadeque*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文围绕南亚多语言文化问答场景，研究RAG系统的高性能构建因素，并比较RAG、无上下文与有上下文问答的表现差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 南亚地区语言与传统繁多且高度交织，文化根基深厚，对情境感知与文化背景化解释需求高。尽管LLM架构进步显著，但在原生语言下回答此类区域性文化问题时仍存挑战，因此需要提取高性能因素以指导优化混合RAG系统。

Method: 在南亚多语言设置下，对比分析RAG、无上下文问答（non-contexted）与有上下文问答（contexted）三种pipeline；考察RAG对文化性与非文化性问题的差异化影响，以及RAG对热门与非热门文化问题的增益差异。

Result: 摘要未给出具体量化结果，仅提出研究问题的探索性分析框架。

Conclusion: 研究不仅评估现有方法，还为未来更准确、高效的问答pipeline设计奠定基础。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) models have become almost a cornerstone of open-domain question answering tasks; in which the quality of answers generated in the process, greatly relies on the effectiveness of retrieval mechanisms. In the realm of LLM, the last two years have seen tremendous progress in RAG architectures, with many models reporting to have improved performance through various design innovations. The cultural question and answering systems of South Asia, in particular, present a specific challenge since the region is home to the vast number of languages and traditions, which are closely interrelated. Along with these deeply intertwined historical narratives increase the need for culturally grounded and context-aware interpretation. Despite major architectural advances in LLMs, claiming significant improvements, challenges remain when answering these region specific questions in native languages. In this study, we aim to extract high-performing factors to guide the development of optimized hybrid RAG systems. We further explore whether the integration of RAG changes performance differently for culturally grounded and non-cultural questions in a South Asian multilingual setup, and whether RAG produces a higher overall percentage of correct answers than contexted answering. We also examine how LLM performance changes when RAG methods are integrated into the question-answering pipeline in comparison with non-contexted and contexted answering and, evaluate whether the introduction of RAG benefits popular or non-popular culturally grounded questions more. Not only does our work evaluate the current methods but also carves a path for future designs of more accurate and efficient question-answering pipelines.

</details>


### [239] [Manacá-1B-Instruct-2: Reproducible Safety Alignment for a Native Brazilian-Portuguese LLM. Negative DPO Results, a Controlled Composition Scan, and a Silent Failure Mode of the LLM Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20570)
*Bruno Leonardo Santos Menezes,Carlos Leonardo Souza Cardoso,Fabio André Machado Porto*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍巴葡大模型 Manacá-1B-Instruct-2 的后训练对齐，公开了三次失败的 DPO 尝试，并证明安全能力主要来自专门的安全 SFT。最终模型在安全指标上以 Wilson 置信区间与 McNemar 检验显著优于四个外部巴葡模型，同时报告了 LLM 评委拒评导致有效样本量被静默削减的方法学问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴西葡萄牙语在开源语言模型领域仍缺乏覆盖，已有的少数安全对齐模型大多不记录失败尝试，也缺乏统计显著性验证的对比。作者希望填补这一空白，并提高对齐过程的可复现性与评估严谨性。

Method: 基于从零训练的 Manacá-1B（1.72B 参数）进行第二代后训练对齐，使用 291,223 条 SFT 语料；记录三次 DPO 尝试（均为负结果，其中一次训练奖励边际达 1.0 但自由生成无变化）；采用 LLM-jp AnswerCarefully 风格的安全 SFT 并迭代三轮；对安全 SFT 混合中通用数据比例（10%–60%）进行受控扫描；用 Wilson 置信区间与配对 McNemar 检验（Holm-Bonferroni 校正）与四个外部巴葡模型对比；补充 n-gram 去污染检查与 Chinchilla 锚定的算力预算表。

Result: 三次 DPO 尝试均未带来收益，其中一次存在训练奖励边际与生成行为脱钩的现象；安全能力主要来自安全 SFT 而非 DPO。通用数据比例扫描显示过拒曲线非单调，拐点在 40%–50%。最终模型安全区间下界（94.1%）高于四个基线的上界，差异显著。同时发现 MT-Bench-PT 六条安全条目中有两条评委无条件拒评，导致此前报告中该类别有效 N 被静默降低。通用能力差距被归因于预训练规模而非对齐方法。

Conclusion: 论文强调应公开失败的对齐尝试并保证统计显著性验证；安全对齐的关键来自专门的安全 SFT 及其配比调优，而非 DPO；同时提示 LLM 评委评估中拒评会污染有效样本量，需显式检查。全部代码、原始数据、日志与脚本已公开。

Abstract: Brazilian Portuguese remains underserved by open-source language models, and the few that are safety-aligned rarely document what did not work or validate comparisons with statistical significance. We present Manacá-1B-Instruct-2, the second-generation post-training alignment of Manacá-1B (1.72B parameters, trained from scratch for PT-BR), with an SFT corpus of 291,223 examples. We document three DPO attempts, all negative, including a case where the training reward margin reaches 1.0 while free generation does not change at all, and show that safety came from a dedicated safety-SFT (LLM-jp AnswerCarefully style), refined over three iterations until closing the safety-helpfulness frontier. A controlled scan of the fraction of general data in the safety-SFT mix (10% to 60%) reveals a non-monotonic over-refusal curve with a knee point at 40-50%. The final model is compared against four external PT-BR models on the same items, with Wilson confidence intervals and paired McNemar tests with Holm-Bonferroni correction: the lower bound of its safety interval (94.1%) lies above the upper bound of all four baselines. We also report a methodological finding for LLM-judge evaluation: on two of six safety items in MT-Bench-PT, the judge categorically refuses to assign a score regardless of the evaluated response, silently reducing the effective N of that category in every prior report of the project. We complement this with an n-gram decontamination check and a Chinchilla-anchored compute-budget table that attributes the gap in general capability to pre-training scale rather than alignment methodology. All code, raw data, logs, and scripts that produce every reported number are publicly released.

</details>


### [240] [MolLean: A Process-Verifiable Language for Chemical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20459)
*Jiatong Li,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 MolLean，一种以显式分子状态为基础、可过程验证的化学推理语言，并配合 MolLean-MCP 有状态工具接口，实现单次轨迹执行、整轨迹精化与交互式推理三种模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学思维链（chain-of-thought）可能包含幻觉性的结构与性质断言，缺乏可验证性，导致推理过程停留在自由文本解释层面而无法被检查与修正。

Method: 构建基于分层 Lean 内核的 MolLean 语言，对分子状态转移与断言（从原子图编辑到化合物转化）进行检查；通过类型化见证协议区分原生结构检查与外部提供的测量值；并引入 MolLean-MCP 有状态工具接口，在生成过程中暴露转移、检查与读出，使模型依据反馈决定下一步动作，被拒绝的动作不改变状态。

Result: 在覆盖分子生成、编辑、优化与反应共 12 项任务、8,800 个匹配样例上，对比直接思维链评估三种模式；使用 Qwen3.6-27B 时，整轨迹精化在全部 12 项任务上优于直接思维链；MolLean-MCP 通过逐步验证反馈补充整轨迹精化，在反应类任务上取得更优性能。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可执行的化学推理基础框架，使显式化学承诺可被检查与修订，而非仅存在于自由形式的解释之中。

Abstract: Chemical chain-of-thought can contain hallucinated structural and property claims. We thereby introduce MolLean, a process-verifiable language that grounds chemical reasoning in an explicit molecular state. A layered Lean-based kernel checks state transitions and assertions over molecular readouts, from primitive graph edits to compound transformations. Meanwhile, a typed witness protocol separates native structural checks from externally supplied measurements. We further introduce MolLean-MCP, a stateful tool interface that exposes these transitions, checks, and readouts during generation: the model receives feedback to decide its next action, and rejected actions leave the state unchanged. MolLean supports one-pass trace execution, whole-trace refinement, and interactive reasoning through MolLean-MCP. We evaluate these three modes with direct chain-of-thought on 8,800 matched examples across 12 tasks spanning molecular generation, editing, optimization, and reactions. With Qwen3.6-27B, whole-trace refinement outperforms direct chain-of-thought on all 12 tasks. MolLean-MCP complements whole-trace refinement with stepwise verification feedback, reaching better performance in reaction tasks. Together, these results highlight an executable foundation for chemical reasoning, in which explicit chemical commitments can be checked and revised rather than remaining solely in free-form explanations.

</details>


### [241] [From Unreliable to Usable: Decomposing Failure Modes and Confidence Signals in LLM Entity Extraction - Evidence from Cyber Threat Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20417)
*Jiahong Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对两款新一代低成本LLM与一款旗舰模型在354个事件语料上的抽取评估显示：不可靠性跨层级持续存在，主要失效并非幻觉而是背景性提及与范围缺口，置信度只能排序、不能验证真伪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究报告了全长文档LLM抽取的严重不可靠性，但留下两个未解问题：失败究竟由什么构成，以及置信度信号能否将不可靠输出转化为可用输出。本文通过预注册、完全冻结的评估来回答这两点，并划出部署边界。

Method: 预注册、完全冻结的评估设计。模型：DeepSeek-V4-Flash、GLM-5.3-Flash 两款低成本模型，加旗舰级对照 GPT-6.1-Sol。数据：公开语料354个事件campaign（315份唯一报告，630次模型-报告评估）。评估维度：以金标打分的抽取质量、置信度校准、对单句扰动的稳健性。分析手段：完整提及普查＋对360个抽样候选做三层标注（两名来自被评模型集之外厂商的独立LLM标注员，在预注册验收线上Cohen's kappa=0.79，完全披露）；零成本自一致性频率分层与文本频率×SC频率组合；对全45个目标做关系级反事实测试。

Result: (1) 不可靠性跨层级持续：微精度0.03-0.34，言语化校准误差ECE 0.53-0.94，两个数据划分均如此，旗舰层级也未能缩小差距。(2) 主要失效不是幻觉：反向投影得65.6%为背景性提及、19.9%为金标范围缺口，仅1.2%为无支撑候选。(3) 置信度能排序但不能验证：零成本自一致性频率分层在全部六个测试划分的模型-类型单元中通过预注册的前30%精度增益门槛（训练集为4/6），文本频率×SC频率组合在各单元中3胜2平1负；但全45个目标的关系级反事实显示，单句错误归属在95.6-100%的情况下翻转高置信判断，甚至明确标注无关的背景句仍在至多26.7%的情况下绑定出虚假脆弱性。

Conclusion: 部署边界明确：置信度分层可用于设定人工复核优先级，但绝不能作为真实性判定依据。抽取不可靠的根源在于背景提及与范围界定，而非幻觉，且该问题不会随模型层级提升而消失。

Abstract: Audits of large language model (LLM) extraction from full-length documents report severe unreliability, leaving two questions open: what the failures actually consist of, and whether confidence signals can turn unreliable output into usable output. We run a preregistered, fully frozen evaluation of two new-generation low-cost LLMs (DeepSeek-V4-Flash, GLM-5.3-Flash) with a flagship-tier control (GPT-6.1-Sol) on a public corpus of 354 incident campaigns (315 unique reports, 630 model-report evaluations), scoring extraction quality against gold, confidence calibration, and robustness to single-sentence perturbations. Three findings emerge. (1) Unreliability persists across tiers: micro precision is 0.03-0.34 and verbalized calibration error (ECE) 0.53-0.94 across both splits; the gap does not close at the flagship tier. (2) The dominant failure is not hallucination: a full mention census plus a three-tier annotation of 360 sampled candidates (two independent LLM annotators from vendors outside the evaluated set, Cohen's kappa = 0.79 on the preregistered acceptance line, fully disclosed) back-projects to 65.6% background mentions and 19.9% gold-scope gaps, with only 1.2% unsupported candidates. (3) Confidence ranks but does not verify: zero-cost self-consistency-frequency tiering passes a preregistered top-30% precision-gain bar in all six test-split model-type cells (4/6 on train), the zero-cost text-frequency x SC frequency combination wins 3, ties 2, loses 1 across cells, yet relation-level counterfactuals on all 45 targets show that a single misattribution sentence flips high-confidence judgments in 95.6-100% of cases, and even a background sentence explicitly marked as unrelated still binds a fake vulnerability in up to 26.7% of them. We conclude with a deployment boundary: confidence tiering may set review priorities, never truth.

</details>


### [242] [Cortex: Consolidating Context into Parametric Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20399)
*Jintian Zhang,Feng He,Xinnian Liang,Ziwen Xu,Haoming Xu,Haojing Huang,Shuangzhi Wu,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 受生物记忆巩固启发，论文提出"记忆巩固（Memory Consolidation, MC）"问题，即把上下文带来的性能增益内化到模型参数中，同时保留非目标能力。作者提出五类任务族测试台，评测单次、批量和顺序更新下的巩固与保留表现，并提出 Cortex 方法：生成前瞻性查询以预测上下文用途，将上下文条件下的输出蒸馏为可合并的低秩或结构化前馈网络更新。在 4B–120B 的稠密与 MoE、文本与多模态骨干网络上，Cortex 多数设置下位于巩固—保留帕累托前沿。在 14B 模型上完成 50 次顺序事实编辑后，Cortex-Structural 恢复 88.9% 的上下文增益，同时保留 99.8% 的原始非目标性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文可提升大语言模型表现，但移除上下文后收益往往丢失。受生物系统"巩固"机制启发，作者将上下文指定编辑、技能学习与行为引导统一到同一目标下，试图在参数中内化上下文增益，同时不损害非目标能力。

Method: 提出五任务族测试台，衡量单次、批量、顺序更新下的巩固与保留。提出 Cortex：生成前瞻性查询以预判上下文的使用方式，并把上下文条件下的输出蒸馏为可合并的低秩或结构化 FFN 参数更新。

Result: 在 4B–120B 稠密与 MoE 骨干（含纯文本与多模态模型）上，Cortex 在多数评测设置中处于巩固—保留帕累托前沿。14B 模型上经 50 次顺序事实编辑后，Cortex-Structural 恢复 88.9% 的上下文增益，并保留 99.8% 的原始非目标性能。

Conclusion: Cortex 展示了将上下文收益内化进参数、并兼顾非目标能力保留的可行性，为上下文指定编辑、技能学习和行为引导提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Context can improve large language model performance, but these gains may be lost after context removal. Inspired by biological systems consolidation, we formulate Memory Consolidation (MC) as internalizing these gains while preserving non-target capabilities, connecting context-specified editing, skill learning, and behavioral steering under a common objective. We introduce a five-task-family testbed measuring consolidation and retention under single, batch, and sequential updates. We propose Cortex, which generates prospective queries to anticipate uses of context and distills context-conditioned outputs into mergeable low-rank or structural feed-forward network updates. Across 4B--120B dense and mixture-of-experts backbones, including both text-only and multimodal models, Cortex lies on the empirical consolidation--retention Pareto frontier in most evaluated settings. After 50 sequential factual edits on a 14B model, Cortex-Structural recovers 88.9% of the context-induced gain while retaining 99.8% of original non-target performance.

</details>


### [243] [From Triage to Impact: A Multi-Hop Reasoning Benchmark for Explainable Persian Hate Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20370)
*Ghazal Kalhor,Sima Kamandloo,Mehrsa Shamsian,Behnam Bahrak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个面向波斯语仇恨言论的可解释、细粒度多跳基准，覆盖内容分级、目标识别、仇恨策略与道德基础分析、文本证据抽取、有害叙事推断及严重性评估，并系统评测了当前先进大语言模型，发现任务越细粒度推理越难，专有模型普遍优于开源模型，引入中间推理步骤可显著提升严重性预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨言论在社交媒体上日益普遍，其检测、解读与治理受到关注。波斯语属于低资源语言，缺乏足够的标注数据与计算资源，难以开展细粒度仇恨言论分析，因此需要一个可解释、细粒度且多跳的评测基准来推动该领域研究。

Method: 作者构建了一个细粒度、多跳的可解释波斯语仇恨言论分析基准，任务涵盖内容分级、目标识别、仇恨策略与道德基础分析、文本证据抽取、有害叙事推断和严重性评估；随后对当前先进大语言模型进行评测，并对比专有模型与开源模型、推理导向模型与非推理模型，同时研究引入中间推理步骤对严重性预测的影响。

Result: 评测显示，随着任务对细粒度语义与推理能力要求提高，模型性能总体下降；专有模型普遍优于开源权重模型，而推理导向模型并未表现出一致优势；与一次性评估相比，引入中间推理步骤能显著提升严重性预测效果。分析还发现，“煽动暴力或伤害”与更高严重性关联最强，而政治派别类目标尤其与颠覆型道德框架相关。

Conclusion: 该工作为基于大语言模型的仇恨言论分析提供了洞见，并支持对波斯语社交媒体进行更可问责的内容治理。基准已公开于 https://github.com/kalhorghazal/From-Triage-to-Impact。

Abstract: Hate speech has become increasingly prevalent on social media, raising concerns about its detection, interpretation, and moderation. Persian, a relatively low-resource language, lacks sufficient annotated data and computational resources for fine-grained hate speech analysis. In this study, we introduce a fine-grained, multi-hop benchmark for explainable Persian hate speech analysis, covering content triage, target identification, hate tactic and moral foundation analysis, textual evidence extraction, harmful narrative inference, and severity assessment. By evaluating state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs), we find that performance generally declines as tasks require more fine-grained semantic and inferential reasoning. Proprietary models generally outperform open-weight models, while reasoning-oriented models show no consistent advantage. Incorporating intermediate reasoning steps substantially improves severity prediction compared with one-shot assessment. Our analysis further reveals that Incitement to Violence or Harm is most strongly associated with higher severity, while political-affiliation targets are particularly associated with subversion-based moral framing. Overall, this work provides insights into LLM-based hate speech analysis and supports more accountable moderation of Persian-language social media. The benchmark is publicly available at https://github.com/kalhorghazal/From-Triage-to-Impact.

</details>


### [244] [MMLA-RSI: Recursive Self-Improvement through RTT and RTMA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20294)
*Junyi Zou,Avrova Donz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MMLA-RSI递归自我改进方案，含RTT（改策略状态Φ）与RTMA（改权威记忆M）两条实现路径，均将自适应方法本身作为改进对象，并给出有界可执行规则语言与统一算法，部分实验验证了各声明组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使未解问题中的智能体能通过改变行为方式与所保留经验来提升表现，并让自适应方法本身（数值更新器、记忆管理器）成为可被改进的对象，从而实现递归自我改进。

Method: 在Memory-Mediated Learning Architecture中设计MMLA-RSI，包含两条路径：RTT改变策略状态Φ，RTMA改变权威记忆M。每代的当前更新器/记忆管理器训练出其后继者，经独立准入的后继者在下一代接任同一角色。提供有界可执行规则语言、共享任务与提案接口，以及覆盖单路径或其组合的算法。RTT实例调整裁剪动量更新中的标量调度，RTMA实例在NULL与含逐出可能的整行写入之间选择。由固定解释器、可信提交者和独立评估器限定可变范围，并以状态版本、读闭包和条件属性刻画因果执行、已接受转移与有限工作量。

Result: 四候选程序选择中，保留LARC反馈更新使两个训练初始化的期望误差分别降低24.65和36.65个百分点；按时间顺序的持续集成回放中，反馈绑定为正效应而保留会增加损失；已完成的SQL研究较未自适应基板显著改善，但未确立所需的额外在线收益。

Conclusion: 结果仅刻画了所声明的各组件。两条递归路径及其联合实现已在本文中给出规范，但其有益递归链与发现效率仍有待评估。

Abstract: An agent can improve its behavior within an unresolved problem by changing how it acts and which experience it retains. We propose MMLA-RSI, a recursive self-improvement scheme with two implementations in Memory-Mediated Learning Architecture: Reasoning-Time Training (RTT) changes the policy state $Φ$, while Reasoning-Time Memory Adaptation (RTMA) changes authoritative memory $M$. Each route also makes its adaptation method an object of improvement. A current numerical updater trains the policy proposing its successor; a current memory manager chooses the records available to the process proposing its successor. An independently admitted successor takes over the same role in the next generation. We give bounded executable rule languages, shared task/proposal interfaces, and one algorithm for either route or their composition. The RTT instance changes scalar schedules in a clipped-momentum update. The RTMA instance chooses NULL or a complete-row write with possible eviction. A fixed interpreter, trusted committer, and independent evaluator bound what can change. State versions, read closures, and conditional properties describe causal execution, accepted transitions, and finite work. Existing numerical-component studies motivate the construction. In four-candidate program selection, retaining LARC feedback updates reduces expected error by 24.65 and 36.65 percentage points for two trained initializations. In chronological continuous-integration replay, feedback binding is positive while retention increases loss. Completed SQL studies improve substantially over an unadapted substrate but do not establish the required additional online benefit. These results characterize their declared components. The two recursive routes and their joint realization are specified here; their beneficial recursive chains and discovery efficiency remain to be evaluated.

</details>


### [245] [Repairing Speech Access After Text Updates with Frozen Affine Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20253)
*Ming-Hao Hsu,Yuxiang Wang,Yingda Shen,Zhizheng Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"更新后语音访问修复"问题：文本更新获得的知识在语音查询下可能无法访问；提出AFFIRM算子（冻结主干、单次前向修复语音访问），在合成数据上优势不明显，但在真实录音数据集上无需重拟合即可迁移并提升ROUGE-L 0.0941。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具备语音能力的语言模型主要继承文本丰富主干的知识，持续维护也以文本更新为主。但通过文本更新获得的知识，在同一查询以语音形式提出时可能仍难以访问，即文本习得与语音访问之间存在错配，需要单独验证与修复。

Method: 将问题形式化为"更新后语音访问修复"：承载知识的检查点保持冻结，由可拆卸算子在一次模型前向中修复纯语音访问。提出AFFIRM（跨模态接口修复的仿射场）：先学习并冻结针对语音隐状态的基校正，再在算子训练查询上以零均值方式加入查询特定校正；由配对的语音与文本状态提供坐标，使校正以每个查询为条件。同时与全局SALSA steering和低秩ReFT干预在相同"冻结单状态"约束下对比。

Result: 在平衡的合成语音PopQA上，配对比较未显示AFFIRM相对全局SALSA steering或低秩ReFT的明确优势。在带文本段落的真实录音SLUE-SQA-5问题上，全规模PopQA拟合的AFFIRM算子无需重拟合即可迁移，ROUGE-L较配对的无修复基线提升0.0941；低秩ReFT干预同样正向迁移，而匹配的SALSA变体则低于其配对基线0.0707。

Conclusion: 文本习得与语音访问在更新后可能出现分离，应当分别加以验证与修复；AFFIRM展示了跨数据集、无需重拟合的可迁移修复能力，但合成场景下相对现有干预方法优势尚不明确。

Abstract: Speech-capable language models inherit most of their knowledge from text-rich language backbones, and continued model maintenance is likewise dominated by text updates. Knowledge acquired through a text update can nevertheless remain difficult to access when the same query is spoken. We formulate this mismatch as post-update speech access repair, where the knowledge-bearing checkpoint stays frozen and a detachable operator repairs speech-only access in one model pass. We introduce Affine Fields for Interface Repair across Modalities (AFFIRM), which first learns and freezes a base correction for speech hidden states, then adds query-specific corrections with zero mean over the operator-training queries. Paired speech and text states provide the coordinates that condition these corrections on each query. On balanced synthetic-speech PopQA, paired comparisons do not establish a clear advantage for AFFIRM over global SALSA steering or low-rank ReFT interventions under the same frozen single-state constraint. On recorded SLUE-SQA-5 questions with text passages, a full-scale PopQA-fitted AFFIRM operator transfers without refitting and improves ROUGE-L by 0.0941 over its paired No Repair baseline. Low-rank ReFT interventions also transfer positively, whereas the matched SALSA variant falls 0.0707 below its paired baseline. These results show that text acquisition and speech access can diverge after an update and should be verified and repaired separately.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [246] [LLM-Based Policy Compliance Verification of Loan Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20155)
*Florentina Voboril*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于LLM的两步式贷款政策合规检查原型：先从内部政策文档自动抽取规则并结构化，再据此评估贷款申请，识别政策例外、不合规项与需人工复核的案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银行依赖大量内部政策框架确保公司贷款决策符合监管要求与内部风险标准，但贷款申请的政策审查高度依赖人工与专业知识，易遗漏政策违规或例外情形，故需自动化辅助手段。

Method: 两步架构：第一步从政策文档中自动抽取合规相关规则并转换为结构化、机器可读的表示；第二步将贷款申请与抽取出的规则逐条比对评估，输出政策例外、不合规发现、信息缺失及需人工审查的情形。

Result: 规则抽取在多次运行中高度稳定，且能一致覆盖领域专家认定关键的规则；在11份具有已知政策例外的真实贷款申请上，系统成功检出多数例外，并额外发现人工审查中曾被忽略的合规问题。

Conclusion: 该方法存在假阳性且无法完全替代人工判断，但表明基于LLM的政策合规核查可为贷款申请审查提供有价值的支持，通过提示潜在合规风险辅助人工决策。

Abstract: Banks rely on extensive internal policy frameworks to ensure that corporate lending decisions comply with regulatory requirements and internal risk standards. Reviewing loan applications against these policies is a largely manual and knowledge-intensive process, which can lead to overlooked policy violations or exceptions. This report presents a prototype of an LLM-based policy checker that automatically verifies loan applications against internal lending policies.
  The proposed approach follows a two-step architecture. First, compliance-relevant rules are automatically extracted from policy documents and converted into structured, machine-readable representations. Second, loan applications are evaluated against the extracted rules to identify policy exceptions, non-compliant findings, missing information, and cases requiring manual review.
  Evaluation results demonstrate that the rule extraction process is highly stable across multiple runs and consistently captures rules identified as critical by a domain expert. When evaluated on 11 real loan applications with known policy exceptions, the system successfully detected the majority of these exceptions and identified additional compliance issues that had previously been overlooked during manual reviews. While the approach produces false positives and cannot fully replace human judgement, the results indicate that LLM-based policy compliance verification can provide valuable support in the loan application verification process by highlighting potential compliance risks.

</details>


### [247] [Seeing, Maintaining, and Learning: The Evolution of Multimodal Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19366)
*Minghao Guo,Qingyue Jiao,Xiujin Liu,Jianhong Pang,Ruimeng Yang,Naihao Xu,Liwei Che,Haochen Xue,Ziqi Wang,Xinying Cai,Jiaxuan Luo,Jiaxin Deng,Xuesong Ye,Jintao Qu,Wenyao Gao,Jingwen Gu,Chenyang Zhao,Shichun Liu,Guibin Zhang,Zefang Liu,Siyin Wang,Wujiang Xu,Danrui Li,Zeru Shi,Woongyeong Yeo,Shuang Liu,Wei-Chieh Huang,Weizhi Zhang,Tianyu Yang,Kangsan Kim,Yebowen Hu,Bowen Wen,Bowen Jiang,Haohao Su,Ming Yin,Sheldon Yu,Mian Zhang,Yuyan Chen,Hanrong Zhang,Kai Mei,Zihao Zhang,Mingyu Jin,Xi Zhu,Hengyi Wang,Tianxin Wei,Linyong Nan,Zhenwen Liang,Zhendong Chu,Ziqi Huang,Yongye Su,Sixun Dong,Shujian Liu,Kaiqu Liang,Jiaojiao Han,Zhaoye Fei,Kun Wang,Shihan Dou,Zhichun Guo,Xingjian Diao,Zeqi Gu,Heng Wang,Tao Feng,Cheng Qian,Hamid Palangi,Silei Xu,Xi Yun,Yi Zhu,Shilong Liu,Qingsong Wen,Sung Ju Hwang,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang,Yiyu Shi,Dimitris N. Metaxas,Jiaxuan You,Julian McAuley,Mengdi Wang,Jiawei Han,Philip S. Yu,Ziyi Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多模态智能体记忆的两轴分析框架：轴一关注多模态信息的形成与表示（保留什么、如何编码），轴二关注记忆管理随经验积累的适应方式，并划分为 T1 静态记忆、T2 动态记忆、T3 自演化记忆三个层级，据此归纳设计模式与原则并展望未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型与智能体通过整合多模态信息，使AI系统能更像人类一样从真实世界学习与交互。然而，当多模态智能体面向长时程任务与终身学习场景时，如何从复杂异构的多模态数据中持续获取有用信息仍是重大挑战。应对该挑战需要有效的记忆机制：跨会话保持连续性、适应用户与环境变化，并通过积累经验优化记忆管理策略。

Method: 文章提出一个两轴分析框架。轴一（形成与表示）刻画保留哪些信息以及如何编码，涵盖模态特定嵌入、文本与结构化记录、潜在表示与参数化表示等，以支持后续推理与行动。轴二（记忆管理）区分三个层级：T1 静态记忆复用固定记忆；T2 动态记忆通过稳定策略更新记忆；T3 自演化记忆通过不断演化的策略更新记忆。基于该框架，作者综合归纳反复出现的设计模式。

Result: 通过两轴框架的梳理，文章提炼出若干关键原则：在源信息保真度与抽象化之间取得平衡；维持时间有效性与来源溯源（provenance）；将记忆使用建立在可恢复的证据之上；以及随时间演化记忆策略。此外，还系统讨论了多模态智能体记忆机制面临的挑战与潜在未来方向。

Conclusion: 该工作以“形成与表示”和“记忆管理”两轴构建了多模态智能体记忆的分析框架，并以 T1/T2/T3 三层次刻画记忆管理的自适应程度，为理解长时程与终身场景下的记忆设计提供了统一视角，指出了兼顾保真、时序有效性、证据可追溯与策略演化的重要性，并展望了未来研究方向。
